(3.9)

\displaystyle\Omega(p):=\sqrt{E(p)(E(p)+2\lambda\widehat{v}(p))}

denote the dispersion function for acoustic excitations, and let

(3.10)

\displaystyle a_{p}^{+}(t)

\displaystyle:=

\displaystyle{\mathcal{V}}_{HFB}^{*}(t)a_{p}^{+}{\mathcal{V}}_{HFB}(t)\,,

(3.11)

\displaystyle a_{p}(t)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle{\mathcal{V}}_{HFB}^{*}(t)a_{p}{\mathcal{V}}_{HFB}(t)\,.

Moreover, let

(3.12)

\displaystyle{\mathcal{M}}(p):=\begin{pmatrix}-E(p)-\lambda\widehat{v}(p)&-\lambda\widehat{v}(p)\\
\lambda\widehat{v}(p)&E(p)+\lambda\widehat{v}(p)\end{pmatrix}\,.

Then,

(3.13)

\displaystyle\begin{pmatrix}a_{p}^{+}(t)\\
a_{-p}(t)\end{pmatrix}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\Big[\cos(t\Omega(p))\,{\bf 1}-i\frac{\sin(t\Omega(p))}{\Omega(p)}{\mathcal{M}}(p)\Big]\begin{pmatrix}a_{p}^{+}\\
a_{-p}\end{pmatrix}\,.

Proof.

We obtain the following system of ODEs,

(3.14)

\displaystyle i\partial_{t}a_{p}^{+}(t)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle{\mathcal{V}}_{HFB}^{*}(t)\,[a_{p}^{+},{\mathcal{H}}_{HFB}^{\phi_{0}}]\,{\mathcal{V}}_{HFB}(t)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-(E(p)+\lambda\widehat{v}(p))a^{+}_{p}(t)-\lambda\widehat{v}(p)a_{-p}(t)\,,

(3.15)

\displaystyle i\partial_{t}a_{-p}(t)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle{\mathcal{V}}_{HFB}^{*}(t)\,[a_{-p},{\mathcal{H}}_{HFB}^{\phi_{0}}]\,{\mathcal{V}}_{HFB}(t)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle(E(p)+\lambda\widehat{v}(p))a_{-p}(t)+\lambda\widehat{v}(p)a_{p}^{+}(t)\,,

where
E(p)=\frac{p^{2}}{2}
, so that

(3.16)

\displaystyle i\partial_{t}\begin{pmatrix}a_{p}^{+}(t)\\
a_{-p}(t)\end{pmatrix}={\mathcal{M}}(p)\begin{pmatrix}a_{p}^{+}(t)\\
a_{-p}(t)\end{pmatrix}\,

with

(3.17)

\displaystyle{\mathcal{M}}(p):=\begin{pmatrix}-E(p)-\lambda\widehat{v}(p)&-\lambda\widehat{v}(p)\\
\lambda\widehat{v}(p)&E(p)+\lambda\widehat{v}(p)\end{pmatrix}\,.

We observe that
{\mathcal{M}}(p)
satisfies

(3.18)

\displaystyle{\mathcal{M}}^{2}(p)=\Omega^{2}(p)\,{\bf 1}\,,

where

(3.19)

\displaystyle\Omega(p):=\sqrt{E(p)(E(p)+2\lambda\widehat{v}(p))}

denotes the dispersion function of acoustic excitations; see, for instance,
[
112
,
274
]
. In particular, we have
{\mathcal{M}}^{-1}(p)=\frac{1}{\Omega^{2}(p)}{\mathcal{M}}(p)
.

Hence,

(3.20)

\displaystyle\begin{pmatrix}a_{p}^{+}(t)\\
a_{-p}(t)\end{pmatrix}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\exp\left(-it\begin{pmatrix}-E(p)-\lambda\widehat{v}(p)&-\lambda\widehat{v}(p)\\
\lambda\widehat{v}(p)&E(p)+\lambda\widehat{v}(p)\end{pmatrix}\right)\begin{pmatrix}a_{p}^{+}\\
a_{-p}\end{pmatrix}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\Big(\cos(t\Omega(p))\,{\bf 1}-i\sin(t\Omega(p))\frac{{\mathcal{M}}(p)}{\Omega(p)}\Big)\begin{pmatrix}a_{p}^{+}\\
a_{-p}\end{pmatrix}\,.

This finishes the proof.
∎

Corollary 3.3
.

Assume
q\in[1,\infty]
and
n\in\{0,1,2,3\}
.
Moreover, recall the assumptions
\widehat{v}\geq 0
with
\widehat{v}(0)=0
from Section
2.2
.

V_{1},V_{2}
, defined by

(3.21)

\displaystyle a_{p}^{+}(t)\,=\,\big(e^{i\Omega(p)t}+i\lambda^{2}\sin(\Omega(p)t)V_{1}(p)^{2}\big)a_{p}^{+}+i\lambda\sin(\Omega(p)t)V_{2}(p)a_{-p}\,,

are given by

(3.22)

\displaystyle V_{1}(p)^{2}

\displaystyle:=

\displaystyle\frac{\hat{v}(p)^{2}}{\Omega(p)(\Omega(p)+E(p)+\lambda\hat{v}(p))}\,,

(3.23)

\displaystyle V_{2}(p)

\displaystyle:=

\displaystyle\frac{\hat{v}(p)}{\Omega(p)}\,.

They satisfy the bounds

(3.24)

\displaystyle\|D^{n}V_{1}^{2}\|_{q}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C\|\hat{v}\|_{2(\left\lfloor\frac{r}{2}\right\rfloor+1),w,c}^{n+2}\,,

(3.25)

\displaystyle\|D^{n}V_{2}\|_{q}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C\|\hat{v}\|_{2(\left\lfloor\frac{r}{2}\right\rfloor+1),w,c}^{n+1}\,.

Similarly,

(3.26)

\displaystyle u_{\lambda}(t,p)

\displaystyle:=

\displaystyle e^{i\Omega(p)t}+i\lambda^{2}\sin(\Omega(p)t)V_{1}(p)^{2}

(3.27)

\displaystyle v_{\lambda}(t,p)

\displaystyle:=

\displaystyle i\lambda\sin(\Omega(p)t)V_{2}(p)

satisfy

(3.28)

\displaystyle\|u_{\lambda}(t,\cdot)\|_{\ell^{\infty}({\Lambda^{*}})}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C(1+\lambda^{2}\|\hat{v}\|_{w,d}^{2})\,,

(3.29)

\displaystyle\|v_{\lambda}(t,\cdot)\|_{d}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C\lambda\|\hat{v}\|_{w,d}

for all
q\in[1,\infty]
, all
\lambda\in(0,1]
, and all
t>0
.

Proof.

Lemma
3.2
implies

(3.30)

\displaystyle a_{p}^{+}(t)\,=\,\cos(t\Omega(p))a_{p}^{+}-i\sin(t\Omega(p))\Big(\frac{-E(p)-\lambda\hat{v}(p)}{\Omega(p)}a_{p}^{+}-\lambda\frac{\hat{v}(p)}{\Omega(p)}a_{-p}\Big)\,.

Define
a:=E(p)
and
b:=E(p)+2\lambda\hat{v}(p)
. Setting

(3.31)

\displaystyle V_{1}(p)^{2}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{1}{\lambda^{2}}\Big(\frac{a+b}{2\sqrt{ab}}-1\Big)\,,

(3.32)

\displaystyle V_{2}(p)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{1}{\lambda}\frac{b-a}{2\sqrt{ab}}\,=\,\frac{\hat{v}(p)}{\Omega(p)}\,,

we have that

(3.33)

\displaystyle a_{p}^{+}(t)\,=\,\big(e^{i\Omega(p)t}+i\lambda^{2}\sin(\Omega(p)t)V_{1}(p)^{2}\big)a_{p}^{+}+i\lambda\sin(\Omega(p)t)V_{2}(p)a_{-p}\,.

We start by establishing pointwise bounds. We have

(3.34)

\displaystyle\frac{a+b}{2\sqrt{ab}}-1

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{(\sqrt{a}-\sqrt{b})^{2}}{2\sqrt{ab}}=\frac{(b-a)^{2}}{2\sqrt{ab}(\sqrt{a}+\sqrt{b})^{2}}\,.

In particular, we get

(3.35)

\displaystyle V_{1}(p)^{2}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{2\hat{v}(p)^{2}}{\Omega(p)(\sqrt{E(p)}+\sqrt{E(p)+2\lambda\hat{v}(p)})^{2}}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{\hat{v}(p)^{2}}{\Omega(p)(\Omega(p)+E(p)+\lambda\hat{v}(p))}.

Using
\hat{v}\geq 0
, we have the pointwise bounds

(3.36)

\displaystyle|V_{1}^{2}|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle 2\frac{\hat{v}^{2}}{E^{2}}\,,

(3.37)

\displaystyle|V_{2}|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{\hat{v}}{E}\,.

We thus have that

\displaystyle\|V_{1}^{2}\|_{d}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C\|\hat{v}\|_{w,d}^{2}\,,

(3.38)

\displaystyle\|V_{2}\|_{d}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C\|\hat{v}\|_{w,d}\,.

Finally, set

(3.39)

\displaystyle h_{1}(x)

\displaystyle:=

\displaystyle\frac{2x^{2}}{\sqrt{1+2\lambda x}\big(1+\sqrt{1+2\lambda x}\big)^{2}}\,,

(3.40)

\displaystyle h_{2}(x)

\displaystyle:=

\displaystyle\frac{x}{\sqrt{1+2\lambda x}}\,.

We have that

(3.41)

\displaystyle V_{1}(p)^{2}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle h_{1}\big(\frac{\hat{v}(p)}{E(p)}\big)\,,

(3.42)

\displaystyle V_{2}(p)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle h_{2}\big(\frac{\hat{v}(p)}{E(p)}\big)\,.

Using Lemma
C.3
with
n\in\{1,2,3\}
, we obtain

(3.43)

\displaystyle\|D^{n}V_{1}^{2}\|_{q}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C\|h_{1}\|_{C^{n}([0,\|\hat{v}\|_{2(\left\lfloor\frac{r}{2}\right\rfloor+1),w,c}])}\|\hat{v}\|_{2(\left\lfloor\frac{r}{2}\right\rfloor+1),w,c}^{n}\,,

(3.44)

\displaystyle\|D^{n}V_{2}\|_{q}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C\|h_{2}\|_{C^{n}([0,\|\hat{v}\|_{2(\left\lfloor\frac{r}{2}\right\rfloor+1),w,c}])}\|\hat{v}\|_{2(\left\lfloor\frac{r}{2}\right\rfloor+1),w,c}^{n}\,.

A straight-forward calculation yields

(3.45)

\displaystyle\|h_{1}\|_{C^{n}([0,\|\hat{v}\|_{2(\left\lfloor\frac{r}{2}\right\rfloor+1),w,c}])}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C\|\hat{v}\|_{2(\left\lfloor\frac{r}{2}\right\rfloor+1),w,c}^{2}\,,

(3.46)

\displaystyle\|h_{2}\|_{C^{n}([0,\|\hat{v}\|_{2(\left\lfloor\frac{r}{2}\right\rfloor+1),w,c}])}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C\|\hat{v}\|_{2(\left\lfloor\frac{r}{2}\right\rfloor+1),w,c}\,.

Combining (
3.43
), (
3.44
), (
3.45
), and (
3.46
) finishes the proof.
∎

4.
Trace estimates

In this chapter, we omit the subscript
{\Lambda^{*}}
from
\int_{\Lambda^{*}}
in our notation, as we will only consider
\int\equiv\int_{\Lambda^{*}}
as sums over
{\Lambda^{*}}
in the sense of (
1.51
).

4.1.
Computation of the partition function

We establish estimates on the partition function on finite particle number subspaces of the Fock space
{\mathcal{F}}
. The estimates derived here are needed to control the error terms coming from the expansion in Lemma
3.1
.

For the next lemma, let us introduce some notation. Let us denote

(4.1)

\displaystyle a^{(1)}_{p}\,:=\,a^{+}_{p}\quad\mbox{and\ }\quad a^{(-1)}_{p}\,:=\,a_{p}\,.

Let

(4.2)

\displaystyle{\mathcal{P}}[a,a^{+}]

\displaystyle:=

\displaystyle\Big\{\int\,d\mathbf{p}_{k}h(\mathbf{p}_{k},\vec{\sigma}_{k})\prod_{j=1}^{k}a_{p_{j}}^{(\sigma_{j})}\mid k\in{\mathbb{N}},\,h(\cdot,\vec{\sigma}_{k})\in{\mathcal{S}}^{\prime}({\mathbb{R}}^{3}),

\displaystyle\vec{\sigma}_{k}=(\sigma_{1},\ldots,\sigma_{k})\in\{\pm 1\}^{k}\Big\}

denote all monomials in
a,a^{+}
. Here and henceforth, we define ordered operator products via multiplication from the right, by

(4.3)

\displaystyle\prod_{j=1}^{n+1}A_{j}

\displaystyle:=

\displaystyle\Big(\prod_{j=1}^{n}A_{j}\Big)A_{n+1}\,,\,n\in{\mathbb{N}}_{0}\,,

(4.4)

\displaystyle\prod_{j=1}^{0}A_{j}

\displaystyle:=

\displaystyle{\bf 1}\,.

Let us denote

(4.5)

\displaystyle\osg(A)\,:=\,\sum_{j=1}^{k}\sigma_{j}

whenever
A=\int\,d\mathbf{p_{k}}h(\mathbf{p}_{k},\vec{\sigma}_{k})\prod_{j=1}^{k}a_{p_{j}}^{(\sigma_{j})}
. Note that we have

(4.6)

\displaystyle[\mathcal{N}_{b},\prod_{j=1}^{k}a_{p_{j}}^{(\sigma_{j})}]\,=\,\sum_{j=1}^{k}\sigma_{j}\prod_{j=1}^{k}a_{p_{j}}^{(\sigma_{j})}\,,

which, in turn, gives

(4.7)

\displaystyle[\mathcal{N}_{b},A]\,=\,\osg(A)A

whenever
A=\int\,d\mathbf{p_{k}}h(\mathbf{p_{k}},\mathbf{\sigma_{k}})\prod_{j=1}^{k}a_{p_{j}}^{(\sigma_{j})}
. Using the spectral decomposition

(4.8)

\displaystyle P_{n}\,=\,\frac{1}{2\pi i}\oint_{\partial B_{\frac{1}{2}}(n)}\frac{dz}{\mathcal{N}_{b}-z}\,,

with counter-clockwise contour, it follows that

(4.9)

\displaystyle P_{n}A\,=\,AP_{n-\osg(A)}

for all
A\in{\mathcal{P}}[a,a^{+}]
. We will refer to (
4.9
) as the
Pull-Through Formula
for projectors, see
[
35
]
.

Moreover, if
\osg(A)\neq 0
, (
4.7
) implies that

(4.10)

\displaystyle\nu(A)\,=\,\frac{1}{\osg(A)}\nu([\mathcal{N}_{b},A])\,=\,0\,.

Lemma 4.1

(Moments of the number operator)
.

We have for all
\ell\in{\mathbb{N}}_{0}
that

(4.11)

\nu_{0}((\mathcal{N}_{b}+1)^{\frac{\ell}{2}})\,\leq\,C_{\ell,\|f_{0}\|_{d}}|\Lambda|^{\frac{\ell}{2}}\,.

Proof.

Let
{\mathcal{K}}_{\mu}:=\int_{{\Lambda^{*}}}dp\,(K(p)-\mu)a_{p}^{+}a_{p}
. Then we obtain that

(4.12)

\displaystyle Z_{0}(\mu)

\displaystyle:=

\displaystyle\operatorname{Tr}(e^{-{\mathcal{K}}_{\mu}})

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\prod_{p\in{\Lambda^{*}}}\sum_{n=0}^{\infty}e^{-n(K(p)-\mu)}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\prod_{p\in{\Lambda^{*}}}\frac{1}{1-e^{-(K(p)-\mu)}}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle e^{-|\Lambda|\int_{{\Lambda^{*}}}dp\log\big(1-e^{-(K(p)-\mu)}\big)}

for any
\mu<\kappa_{0}
. Let

(4.13)

\kappa_{n}\,:=\,-(-\partial_{\mu})^{n}\big|_{\mu=0}\int_{{\Lambda^{*}}}dp\log\big(1-e^{-(K(p)-\mu)}\big)

denote the
n^{th}
cumulant for
n\in{\mathbb{N}}
.
Let
{n\choose\mathbf{r}_{\ell}}
denote the multinomial coefficient, and
R(\ell):=\{\mathbf{r}_{\ell}\in{\mathbb{N}}_{0}^{\ell}\mid\sum_{n=1}^{\ell}nr_{n}=\ell\}
. Then the Faà di Bruno formula, see
[
295
]
, yields

\displaystyle\nu_{0}(\mathcal{N}_{b}^{\ell})\,=

\displaystyle\,\frac{(-\partial_{\mu})^{\ell}\big|_{\mu=0}Z_{0}(\mu)}{Z_{0}(0)}

(4.14)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\,\ell!\sum_{\mathbf{r}_{\ell}\in R(\ell)}\prod_{n=1}^{\ell}\frac{1}{r_{n}!}\Big(\frac{|\Lambda|\kappa_{n}}{n!}\Big)^{r_{n}}\,.

Observe that we have

(4.15)

\kappa_{1}\,=\,\int_{{\Lambda^{*}}}dp\,\frac{1}{e^{K(p)}-1}\,=\,\int_{{\Lambda^{*}}}dpf_{0}(p)\,,

recalling
f_{0}
from (
1.57
). More generally, Lemma
A.2
implies

(4.16)

\kappa_{n}\,\leq\,C_{n,\|f_{0}\|_{d}}

for all
n\in{\mathbb{N}}
. As a consequence of (
4.14
) and (
4.16
), we find that

(4.17)

\nu_{0}((\mathcal{N}_{b}+1)^{\ell})\,\leq\,C_{\ell,\|f_{0}\|_{d}}|\Lambda|^{\ell}\,,

where we used that
|\Lambda|\geq 1
. Using

(4.18)

\displaystyle\nu_{0}((\mathcal{N}_{b}+1)^{\frac{\ell}{2}})\,\leq\,\nu_{0}((\mathcal{N}_{b}+1)^{\ell-1})^{\frac{1}{2}}\nu_{0}((\mathcal{N}_{b}+1)^{\ell+1})^{\frac{1}{2}}

yields the half-integer case. This concludes the proof.
∎

Remark 4.2
.

We use an argument from
[
117
]
. Using the fact that

(4.19)

\displaystyle e^{-{\mathcal{K}}}a_{p}^{+}e^{{\mathcal{K}}}\,=\,e^{-K(p)}a_{p}^{+}\,,

cyclicity of the trace implies

(4.20)

\displaystyle f_{0}(p)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{\operatorname{Tr}\big(e^{-{\mathcal{K}}}a_{p}^{+}a_{p}\big)}{\operatorname{Tr}e^{-{\mathcal{K}}}|\Lambda|}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle e^{-K(p)}\frac{\operatorname{Tr}\big(a_{p}^{+}e^{-{\mathcal{K}}}a_{p}\big)}{\operatorname{Tr}e^{-{\mathcal{K}}}|\Lambda|}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle e^{-K(p)}\frac{\operatorname{Tr}\big(e^{-{\mathcal{K}}}a_{p}a_{p}^{+}\big)}{\operatorname{Tr}e^{-{\mathcal{K}}}|\Lambda|}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle e^{-K(p)}(f_{0}(p)\,+\,1)\,,

Thus, we have

(4.21)

\displaystyle f_{0}(p)\,=\,\frac{1}{e^{K(p)}-1}\,.

Lemma 4.3

(Operator product bound)
.

Let
A_{j}\in{\mathcal{P}}[a,a^{+}]
be monomials in
a,a^{+}
,
\gamma_{j}>0
, and
k_{j}\in{\mathbb{N}}
be such that

(4.22)

\displaystyle\|P_{m}A_{j}P_{m-\osg(A_{j})}\|\,\leq\,\gamma_{j}(m+|\Lambda|)^{k_{j}/2}

for all
j\in\{1,\ldots,\ell\}
and all
m\in{\mathbb{N}}_{0}
. Then we have that

(4.23)

\displaystyle|\nu(\prod_{j=1}^{\ell}A_{j})|\,\leq\,\Big(\prod_{j=1}^{\ell}\gamma_{j}\Big)\nu\Big(\big({\mathcal{N}}+\sum_{m=1}^{\ell}|\osg(A_{m})|+|\Lambda|\big)^{\sum_{j=1}^{\ell}k_{j}/2}\Big)

for any state
\nu
.

Proof.

Observe that

(4.24)

\displaystyle\osg(A)\,=\,\sum_{j=1}^{\ell}\osg(A_{j})\,.

In addition,

(4.25)

\displaystyle\sum_{n=0}^{\infty}P_{n}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle{\bf 1}\,,

(4.26)

\displaystyle P_{n}f(\mathcal{N}_{b})

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle P_{n}f(n)

holds for any measurable function
f
. Hence and by applying (
4.9
), we have that

(4.27)

\displaystyle|\nu(A)|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\sum_{n=0}^{\infty}|\nu(P_{n}AP_{n-\osg(A)})|

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\sum_{n=0}^{\infty}|\nu\Big(P_{n}\big(\prod_{j=1}^{\ell}P_{n-\sum_{m=1}^{j-1}\osg(A_{m})}A_{j}P_{n-\sum_{m=1}^{j}\osg(A_{m})}\big)P_{n-\osg(A)}\Big)|\,.

Denoting
B_{n}:=\prod_{j=1}^{\ell}P_{n-\sum_{m=1}^{j-1}\osg(A_{m})}A_{j}P_{n-\sum_{m=1}^{j}\osg(A_{m})}
, we can apply Cauchy-Schwarz to
\nu
to obtain the upper bound

(4.28)

\displaystyle|\nu(A)|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\sum_{n=0}^{\infty}\nu(P_{n})^{\frac{1}{2}}\nu(P_{n-\osg(A)}B_{n}^{+}B_{n}P_{n-\osg(A)})^{\frac{1}{2}}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\sum_{n=0}^{\infty}\|B_{n}\|\nu(P_{n})^{\frac{1}{2}}\nu(P_{n-\osg(A)})^{\frac{1}{2}}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\Big(\sum_{n=0}^{\infty}\|B_{n}\|\nu(P_{n})\Big)^{\frac{1}{2}}\Big(\sum_{n=0}^{\infty}\|B_{n}\|\nu(P_{n-\osg(A)})\Big)^{\frac{1}{2}}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\Big(\sum_{n=0}^{\infty}\|B_{n}\|\nu(P_{n})\Big)^{\frac{1}{2}}\Big(\sum_{n=\osg(A)_{-}}^{\infty}\|B_{n+\osg(A)}\|\nu(P_{n})\Big)^{\frac{1}{2}}\,.

Here, we used Cauchy-Schwarz to
\sum_{n=0}^{\infty}
, followed by the fact that
P_{n-\osg(A)}\equiv 0
for all
n<\osg(A)_{+}
, as
\mathcal{N}_{b}\geq 0
in the sense of quadratic forms. Notice that we have

(4.29)

\displaystyle\|B_{n}\|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\prod_{j=1}^{\ell}\|P_{n-\sum_{m=1}^{j-1}\osg(A_{m})}A_{j}P_{n-\sum_{m=1}^{j}\osg(A_{m})}\|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\prod_{j=1}^{\ell}\gamma_{j}\Big(\big(n-\sum_{m=1}^{j-1}\osg(A_{m})\big)_{+}+|\Lambda|\Big)^{k_{j}/2}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\Big(\prod_{j=1}^{\ell}\gamma_{j}\Big)\Big(n+\sum_{m=1}^{\ell}|\osg(A_{m})|+|\Lambda|\Big)^{\sum_{j=1}^{\ell}k_{j}/2}

by assumption (
4.22
). Similarly, we have that

(4.30)

\displaystyle\|B_{n+\osg(A)}\|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\prod_{j=1}^{\ell}\gamma_{j}\Big(\big(n+\osg(A)-\sum_{m=1}^{j-1}\osg(A_{m})\big)_{+}+|\Lambda|\Big)^{k_{j}/2}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\prod_{j=1}^{\ell}\gamma_{j}\Big(\big(n+\sum_{m=j}^{\ell}\osg(A_{m})\big)_{+}+|\Lambda|\Big)^{k_{j}/2}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\Big(\prod_{j=1}^{\ell}\gamma_{j}\Big)\Big(n+\sum_{m=1}^{\ell}|\osg(A_{m})|+|\Lambda|\Big)^{\sum_{j=1}^{\ell}k_{j}/2}

due to (
4.24
). Collecting (
4.28
)–(
4.30
) and applying (
4.26
) again, we then obtain

(4.31)

\displaystyle|\nu(A)|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\Big(\prod_{j=1}^{\ell}\gamma_{j}\Big)\sum_{n=0}^{\infty}\Big(n+\sum_{m=1}^{\ell}|\osg(A_{m})|+\max_{1\leq j\leq\ell}k_{j}\Big)^{\sum_{j=1}^{\ell}k_{j}/2}\nu(P_{n})

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\Big(\prod_{j=1}^{\ell}\gamma_{j}\Big)\nu\Big(\big({\mathcal{N}}+\sum_{m=1}^{\ell}|\osg(A_{m})|+|\Lambda|\big)^{\sum_{j=1}^{\ell}k_{j}/2}\Big)

∎

4.2.
Operator estimates w.r.t.
\mathcal{N}_{b}

In our analysis, we will bound various operators relative to powers of the particle number operator
\mathcal{N}_{b}
, in conjunction with previously established estimates on the partition function.

Lemma 4.4

(
{\mathcal{H}}_{cub}(t)
bound)
.

We have that

(4.32)

\displaystyle{\mathcal{H}}_{cub}(t)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\sum_{k=1}^{16}A_{cub}^{(k)}(t)

with monomials
A_{cub}^{(k)}(t)\in{\mathcal{P}}[a,a^{+}]
such that

(4.33)

\displaystyle\|A_{cub}^{(k)}(t)P_{M}\|\,\leq\,C\|\hat{v}\|_{w,d}(1+\lambda\|\hat{v}\|_{w,d})^{6}\frac{\lambda(M+|\Lambda|)^{\frac{3}{2}}}{\sqrt{N}}

for any
M\in{\mathbb{N}}_{0}
, and
|\osg(A_{cub}^{(k)}(t))|\leq 3
.

Proof.

Corollary
3.3
and evenness of
\hat{v}
imply that

(4.34)

\displaystyle{\mathcal{H}}_{cub}(t)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{\lambda}{\sqrt{N}}\int_{({\Lambda^{*}})^{3}}d\mathbf{p}_{3}\,\hat{v}(p_{2})\delta(p_{1}+p_{2}-p_{3})a_{p_{1}}^{+}(t)a_{p_{2}}^{+}(t)a_{p_{3}}(t)\,+\,h.c.

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{\lambda}{\sqrt{N}}\sum_{\vec{\sigma}_{3}\in\{\pm 1\}^{3}}\int_{({\Lambda^{*}})^{3}}d\mathbf{p}_{3}\,\delta(\sum_{j=1}^{3}\sigma_{j}p_{j})\hat{v}(p_{2})\prod_{j=1}^{3}(g_{j}(p_{j},\sigma_{j})a_{p_{j}}^{(\sigma_{j})})\,+\,h.c.\,,

where
\vec{\sigma}_{3}=(\sigma_{1},\sigma_{2},\sigma_{3})
, and

(4.35)

\displaystyle g_{j}(p_{j},\sigma_{j})

\displaystyle:=

\displaystyle\delta_{\sigma_{j},1}u_{\lambda}(t,p_{j})+\delta_{\sigma_{j},-1}v_{\lambda}(t,p_{j})\,,\quad j\in\{1,2\}\,,

(4.36)

\displaystyle g_{3}(p_{3},\sigma_{3})

\displaystyle:=

\displaystyle\delta_{\sigma_{3},-1}\overline{u_{\lambda}}(t,p_{3})+\delta_{\sigma_{3},1}\overline{v_{\lambda}}(t,p_{3})\,.

Abbreviating

(4.37)

\displaystyle A_{cub}(\vec{\sigma}_{3})\,:=\,\int d\mathbf{p}_{3}\,\delta(\sum_{j=1}^{3}\sigma_{j}p_{j})\hat{v}(p_{2})\prod_{j=1}^{3}(g_{j}(p_{j},\sigma_{j})a_{p_{j}}^{(\sigma_{j})})\,,

it is sufficient to prove (
4.33
) for
A_{cub}(\vec{\sigma}_{3})
, since the adjoint satisfies

(4.38)

\displaystyle\|A_{cub}(\vec{\sigma}_{3})^{*}P_{M}\|

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\|P_{M-\sum_{j=1}^{3}\sigma_{j}}A_{cub}(\vec{\sigma}_{3})^{*}P_{M}\|

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\|A_{cub}(\vec{\sigma}_{3})P_{M-\sum_{j=1}^{3}\sigma_{j}}\|

due to the Pull-Through formula (
4.9
).

Using
Lemma A.3

(Wick’s Theorem)
.
in Appendix
A
, we have that

(4.39)

\displaystyle a^{(\sigma_{1})}_{p_{1}}a^{(\sigma_{2})}_{p_{2}}a^{(\sigma_{3})}_{p_{3}}\delta(\sum_{j=1}^{3}\sigma_{j}p_{j})

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle:a^{(\sigma_{1})}_{p_{1}}a^{(\sigma_{2})}_{p_{2}}a^{(\sigma_{3})}_{p_{3}}:\delta(\sum_{j=1}^{3}\sigma_{j}p_{j})

\displaystyle+\,\delta(p_{1}-p_{2})\delta(p_{3})\delta_{\sigma_{1},-1}\delta_{\sigma_{2},1}a_{0}^{(\sigma_{3})}

\displaystyle+\,\delta(p_{1}-p_{3})\delta(p_{2})\delta_{\sigma_{1},-1}\delta_{\sigma_{3},1}a_{0}^{(\sigma_{2})}

\displaystyle+\,\delta(p_{2}-p_{3})\delta(p_{1})\delta_{\sigma_{2},-1}\delta_{\sigma_{3},1}a_{0}^{(\sigma_{1})}\,.

We want to apply Lemma
A.4
to each of the terms associated with the terms in (
4.39
). For that, we need to ensure integrability for each of those terms. The terms involving
a_{0}^{(\sigma)}
contain 2 momentum-
\delta
where the only free momentum comes with a coefficient
v_{\lambda}
resp.
\overline{v_{\lambda}}
. Using

(4.40)

\displaystyle\|a_{0}^{+}P_{M}\|\,=\,\|a_{0}P_{M+1}\|\,,

Lemma
A.4
then implies

(4.41)

\displaystyle\|a_{0}^{(\sigma)}P_{M}\|

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\delta_{\sigma,1}\|a_{0}^{+}P_{M}\|+\delta_{\sigma,-1}\|a_{0}P_{M}\|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\sqrt{|\Lambda|(M+1)}\,.

For the cubic term in (
4.39
) and if not all
\sigma_{j}=1
or all
\sigma_{j}=-1
, Lemma
A.4
implies

(4.42)

\displaystyle\|:A_{cub}(\vec{\sigma}_{3}):P_{M}\|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\||\hat{v}(p_{2})|^{\frac{1}{2}}\prod_{j=1}^{2}|v_{\lambda}(p_{j})|^{\delta_{\sigma_{j},-1}}|u_{\lambda}(p_{3})|^{\delta_{\sigma_{3},-1}}\delta(\sum_{j=1}^{3}p_{j}\sigma_{j})^{\frac{1}{2}}\|_{L^{\infty}_{\mathbf{p}_{J_{+}}}L^{2}_{\mathbf{p}_{J_{-}}}}

\displaystyle\||\hat{v}(p_{2})|^{\frac{1}{2}}\prod_{j=1}^{2}|u_{\lambda}(p_{j})|^{\delta_{\sigma_{j},1}}|v_{\lambda}(p_{3})|^{\delta_{\sigma_{3},1}}\delta(\sum_{j=1}^{3}p_{j}\sigma_{j})^{\frac{1}{2}}\|_{L^{\infty}_{\mathbf{p}_{J_{-}}}L^{2}_{\mathbf{p}_{J_{+}}}}

\displaystyle(M+\sum_{j=1}^{3}\sigma_{j})_{|J_{+}|}^{\frac{1}{2}}(M)_{|J_{-}|}^{\frac{1}{2}}\,,

using the notation in Lemma
A.4
with
n=3
. Integrability of
\hat{v}(p_{2})
is sufficient to yield that the RHS is, indeed, finite. If
\sigma_{j}=1
for all
j
, we have that

(4.43)

\displaystyle\|:A_{cub}(1,1,1):P_{M}\|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\big(\|\hat{v}(p_{2})u_{\lambda}(t,p_{1})u_{\lambda}(t,p_{2})\overline{v_{\lambda}}(t,p_{3})\|_{L^{2}_{p_{2}}L^{\infty}_{p_{1},p_{3}}}^{2}(M-2)

\displaystyle+\|\hat{v}(p_{2})u_{\lambda}(t,p_{1})u_{\lambda}(t,p_{2})\overline{v_{\lambda}}(t,p_{1}+p_{2})\|_{L^{2}_{\mathbf{p}_{2}}}^{2}|\Lambda|\big)^{\frac{1}{2}}\sqrt{M(M-1)}\,.

Similarly, we find that

(4.44)

\displaystyle\|:A_{cub}(-1,-1,-1):P_{M}\|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\big(\|\hat{v}(p_{2})v_{\lambda}(t,p_{1})v_{\lambda}(t,p_{2})\overline{u_{\lambda}}(t,p_{3})\|_{L^{2}_{p_{2}}L^{\infty}_{p_{1},p_{3}}}^{2}(M+1)

\displaystyle+\|\hat{v}(p_{2})v_{\lambda}(t,p_{1})v_{\lambda}(t,p_{2})\overline{u_{\lambda}}(t,p_{1}+p_{2})\|_{L^{2}_{p_{2}}L^{\infty}_{p_{1}}}|\Lambda|\big)^{\frac{1}{2}}\sqrt{(M+3)(M+2)}

Observe that

(4.45)

\displaystyle\|H\|_{L^{2}({\Lambda^{*}})}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{1}{\sqrt{|\Lambda|}}\|H\|_{\ell^{2}({\Lambda^{*}})}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\|H\|_{d}\,.

Since we are summing over a lattice, there are terms for which momentum requires the summation over
\hat{v}(2p)
or
v_{\lambda}(2p)
resp.
\overline{v_{\lambda}}(2p)
. In order to use the upper bound
\|\hat{v}\|_{w,d}
, we employ the fact that

(4.46)

\displaystyle\Big|\sum_{p\in{\Lambda^{*}}}H(2p)\Big|\,\leq\,\|H\|_{\ell^{1}({\Lambda^{*}})}\,.

Collecting (
4.38
), (
4.41
), (
4.42
), (
4.43
), (
4.44
), (
4.45
), and employing Corollary
3.3
, we have shown that

(4.47)

\displaystyle\|A_{cub}(\vec{\sigma}_{3})P_{M}\|\,\leq\,C\|\hat{v}\|_{w,d}(1+\lambda\|\hat{v}\|_{w,d})^{6}(M+|\Lambda|)^{\frac{3}{2}}\,,

where we also used the fact that
|\Lambda|\geq 1
. Together with (
4.34
), this finishes the proof.
∎

Lemma 4.5

(
{\mathcal{H}}_{quart}(t)
bound)
.

We have that

(4.48)

\displaystyle{\mathcal{H}}_{quart}(t)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\sum_{k=1}^{16}A_{quart}^{(k)}(t)

with monomials
A_{quart}^{(k)}(t)\in{\mathcal{P}}[a,a^{+}]
such that

(4.49)

\displaystyle\|A_{quart}^{(k)}(t)P_{M}\|\,\leq\,C\|\hat{v}\|_{w,d}(1+\lambda\|\hat{v}\|_{w,d})^{8}\frac{\lambda(M+|\Lambda|)^{2}}{N}

for any
M\in{\mathbb{N}}_{0}
, and
|\osg(A_{quart}^{(k)}(t))|\leq 4
.

Proof.

We follow the steps of the proof of Lemma
4.4
. We have that

(4.50)

\displaystyle{\mathcal{H}}_{quart}(t)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{\lambda}{2N}\int d\mathbf{p}_{4}\,\hat{v}(p_{1}-p_{3})\delta(p_{1}+p_{2}-p_{3}-p_{4})a_{p_{1}}^{+}(t)a_{p_{2}}^{+}(t)a_{p_{3}}(t)a_{p_{4}}(t)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{\lambda}{2N}\sum_{\vec{\sigma}_{4}\in\{\pm 1\}^{4}}\int d\mathbf{p}_{4}\,\delta(\sum_{j=1}^{4}\sigma_{j}p_{j})\hat{v}(\sigma_{1}p_{1}+\sigma_{3}p_{3})\prod_{j=1}^{4}(g_{j}(p_{j},\sigma_{j})a_{p_{j}}^{(\sigma_{j})})\,,

where
\vec{\sigma}_{4}=(\sigma_{1},\sigma_{2},\sigma_{3},\sigma_{4})
, and

(4.51)

\displaystyle g_{j}(p_{j},\sigma_{j}):=\begin{cases}u_{\lambda}(t,p_{j})^{\delta_{\sigma_{j},1}}v_{\lambda}(t,p_{j})^{\delta_{\sigma_{j},-1}}\,,\quad j\in\{1,2\}\,,\\
\overline{u_{\lambda}}(t,p_{j})^{\delta_{\sigma_{j},-1}}\overline{v_{\lambda}}(t,p_{j})^{\delta_{\sigma_{j},1}}\,,\quad j\in\{3,4\}\,.\end{cases}

Analogously to above, we denote

(4.52)

\displaystyle A_{quart}(\vec{\sigma}_{4})\,:=\,\int d\mathbf{p}_{4}\,\delta(\sum_{j=1}^{4}\sigma_{j}p_{j})\hat{v}(\sigma_{1}p_{1}+\sigma_{3}p_{3})\prod_{j=1}^{4}(g_{j}(p_{j},\sigma_{j})a_{p_{j}}^{(\sigma_{j})})\,.

Wick-ordering using Lemma
A.3
yields

(4.53)

\displaystyle\delta(\sum_{j=1}^{4}\sigma_{j}p_{j})\prod_{j=1}^{4}a_{p_{j}}^{(\sigma_{j})}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\delta(\sum_{j=1}^{4}\sigma_{j}p_{j}):\prod_{j=1}^{4}a_{p_{j}}^{(\sigma_{j})}:\,+\,\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}j_{1}<j_{2},\\
j_{3}<j_{4}\end{subarray}}\delta(p_{j_{1}}-p_{j_{2}})\delta_{\sigma_{j_{1}},-1}\delta_{\sigma_{j_{2}},1}

\displaystyle\big(\delta(\sigma_{j_{3}}p_{j_{3}}+\sigma_{j_{4}}p_{j_{4}}):a_{p_{j_{3}}}^{(\sigma_{j_{3}})}a_{p_{j_{4}}}^{(\sigma_{j_{4}})}:\,+\,|\Lambda|\delta(p_{j_{3}}-p_{j_{4}})\delta_{\sigma_{j_{3}},-1}\delta_{\sigma_{j_{4}},1}\big)\,,

where
\{j_{1},\ldots,j_{4}\}=\{1,\ldots,4\}
.

We start with the 0-order terms. These occur whenever two pairs
aa^{+}
exist, each with an annihilation operator to the left of a creation operator. This implies an integrable coefficient
v_{\lambda}
resp.
\overline{v_{\lambda}}
for each of the corresponding momenta. Together with the deltas coming from the contraction, the resulting terms are integrable and are bounded by

(4.54)

\displaystyle|\Lambda|\Big\|\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}j_{1}<j_{2},\\
j_{3}<j_{4}\end{subarray}}\int d\mathbf{p}_{4}\,\hat{v}(\sigma_{1}p_{1}+\sigma_{3}p_{3})\prod_{j=1}^{4}g_{j}(p_{j},\sigma_{j})

\displaystyle\delta(p_{j_{1}}-p_{j_{2}})\delta(p_{j_{3}}-p_{j_{4}})\delta_{\sigma_{j_{1}},-1}\delta_{\sigma_{j_{2}},1}\delta_{\sigma_{j_{3}},-1}\delta_{\sigma_{j_{4}},1}P_{M}\Big\|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C\lambda^{2}|\Lambda|\|\hat{v}\|_{w,d}^{3}(1+\lambda^{2}\|\hat{v}\|_{w,d}^{2})^{2}\,,

where, as above
\{j_{1},\ldots,j_{4}\}=\{1,\ldots,4\}
.

For terms quadratic in
a
,
a^{+}
, there again remain two independent momenta in the integration after integrating out the
\delta^{\prime}s
coming from momentum conservation and the single contraction. Observe that one of these
\delta
’s comes from a contraction with an annihilation operator left to a creation operator. Thus, the corresponding momentum comes with an integrable factor
v_{\lambda}(t,p_{j_{1}})
resp.
\overline{v_{\lambda}}(t,p_{j_{1}})
. The other momentum comes with a coefficient
v_{\lambda}(t,p_{j_{3}})^{\delta_{\sigma_{j_{3}},-1}}:a_{p_{j_{3}}}^{(\sigma_{j_{3}})}a_{-\sigma_{j_{3}}\sigma_{j_{4}}p_{j_{3}}}^{(\sigma_{j_{4}})}:
, unless
j_{3}=3
. In that case, we have a coefficient
v(\sigma_{1}p_{1}+\sigma_{3}p_{3}):a_{p_{3}}^{(\sigma_{3})}a_{-\sigma_{3}\sigma_{4}p_{3}}^{(\sigma_{4})}:
. Then, integrating first over
p_{j_{2}},p_{j_{4}}
and then
p_{j_{3}}
, Lemma
A.4
implies

(4.55)

\displaystyle\Big\|\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}j_{1}<j_{2},\\
j_{3}<j_{4}\end{subarray}}\int d\mathbf{p}_{4}\,\hat{v}(\sigma_{1}p_{1}+\sigma_{3}p_{3})\prod_{j=1}^{4}g_{j}(p_{j},\sigma_{j})

\displaystyle\delta(p_{j_{1}}-p_{j_{2}})\delta(\sigma_{j_{3}}p_{j_{3}}+\sigma_{j_{4}}p_{j_{4}})\delta_{\sigma_{j_{1}},-1}\delta_{\sigma_{j_{2}},1}:a_{p_{j_{3}}}^{(\sigma_{j_{3}})}a_{p_{j_{4}}}^{(\sigma_{j_{4}})}:P_{M}\Big\|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C\|\hat{v}\|_{w,d}(1+\lambda^{2}\|\hat{v}\|_{w,d})^{4}(M+|\Lambda|)\,.

Finally, if not all
\sigma_{j}=1
or
\sigma_{j}=-1
, Lemma
A.4
implies

(4.56)

\displaystyle\|:A_{quart}(\vec{\sigma}_{4}):P_{M}\|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C\||\hat{v}(\sigma_{1}p_{1}+\sigma_{3}p_{3})|^{\frac{1}{2}}\prod_{j=1}^{2}|v_{\lambda}(t,p_{j})|^{\delta_{\sigma_{j},-1}}|u_{\lambda}(t,p_{j+2})|^{\delta_{\sigma_{j+2},-1}}\delta(\sum_{j=1}^{4}\sigma_{j}p_{j})^{\frac{1}{2}}\|_{L^{\infty}_{p_{J_{+}}L^{2}_{p_{J_{-}}}}}

\displaystyle\||\hat{v}(\sigma_{1}p_{1}+\sigma_{3}p_{3})|^{\frac{1}{2}}\prod_{j=1}^{2}|u_{\lambda}(t,p_{j})|^{\delta_{\sigma_{j},1}}|v_{\lambda}(t,p_{j+2})|^{\delta_{\sigma_{j+2},1}}\delta(\sum_{j=1}^{4}\sigma_{j}p_{j})^{\frac{1}{2}}\|_{L^{\infty}_{p_{J_{-}}L^{2}_{p_{J_{+}}}}}

\displaystyle(M+1)^{2}\,.

By flipping the roles of
(1,2)
and
(3,4)
, it is sufficient to show boundedness of only one of the norms. If
1\in J_{-}
, we can use integrability of
|v_{\lambda}(t,p_{1})|
, if
2\in J_{-}
, there is a factor
|v_{\lambda}(t,p_{2})|
. Integrability w.r.t. to
p_{3}
and
p_{4}
is always given due to the factor
|\hat{v}(\sigma_{1}p_{1}+\sigma_{3}p_{3})|\delta(\sum_{j=1}^{4}\sigma_{j}p_{j})
. In case
J_{-}=\emptyset
, Lemma
A.4
implies

(4.57)

\displaystyle\|:A_{quart}(1,1,1,1):P_{M}\|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle(\|\hat{v}(p_{1}+p_{3})v_{\lambda}(t,p_{1})v_{\lambda}(t,p_{2})\overline{u_{\lambda}}(t,p_{3})\overline{u_{\lambda}}(t,p_{4})\|_{L^{2}_{\mathbf{p}_{2}}L^{\infty}_{p_{3},p_{4}}}^{2}(M-3)

\displaystyle+\,\|\hat{v}(p_{1}+p_{3})v_{\lambda}(t,p_{1})v_{\lambda}(t,p_{2})\overline{u_{\lambda}}(t,p_{3})\overline{u_{\lambda}}(t,p_{1}+p_{2}-p_{3})\|_{L^{2}_{\mathbf{p}_{3}}}^{2}|\Lambda|)^{\frac{1}{2}}

\displaystyle\sqrt{M(M-1)(M-2)}\,.

\|:A_{quart}(-1,-1,-1,-1):P_{M}\|
can be similarly bounded. Collecting (
4.54
), (
4.55
), (
4.56
) and (
4.57
), and using
|\Lambda|\geq 1
, we have shown that

(4.58)

\displaystyle\|A_{quart}(\vec{\sigma}_{4})P_{M}\|\,\leq\,C\|\hat{v}\|_{w,d}(1+\lambda\|\hat{v}\|_{w,d})^{8}(M+|\Lambda|)^{2}\,.

Using (
4.50
), this concludes the proof.
∎

Proposition 4.6
.

Let
A\in{\mathcal{P}}[a,a^{+}]
be a monomial in
a,a^{+}
,
\gamma>0
and
\ell\in{\mathbb{N}}
be such that

(4.59)

\displaystyle\|AP_{M}\|\,\leq\,\gamma(M+|\Lambda|)^{\frac{\ell}{2}}\,,

and that
|\osg(A)|\leq\ell
. Let
\mathbf{s}_{j+k}\in\Delta[t,j+k]
and
t\geq 0
. For the moment, we abbreviate by
A{\mathcal{H}}_{cub}^{j}{\mathcal{H}}_{quart}^{k}(\mathbf{s}_{j+k})
all terms that contain one factor
A
,
j
factors
{\mathcal{H}}_{cub}
,
k
factors
{\mathcal{H}}_{quart}
, all at possibly different times
s_{m}
. Then we have that

(4.60)

\displaystyle|\nu(A{\mathcal{H}}_{cub}^{j}{\mathcal{H}}_{quart}^{k}(\mathbf{s}_{j+k}))|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{\gamma(C\lambda\|\hat{v}\|_{w,d})^{j+k}(1+\lambda\|\hat{v}\|_{w,d})^{6j+8k}}{N^{\frac{j}{2}+k}}

\displaystyle\nu\Big(\big(\mathcal{N}_{b}+3j+4k+\ell+|\Lambda|\big)^{\frac{3j+4k+\ell}{2}}\Big)\,.

for any state
\nu
and all
\lambda>0
small enough.

Proof.

Decomposing
{\mathcal{H}}_{cub}
and
{\mathcal{H}}_{quart}
into monomials as in Lemmata
4.4
and
4.5
, we apply Lemmata
4.3
,
4.4
,
4.5
together with assumption (
4.59
) on
A
, we obtain that

(4.61)

\displaystyle|\nu(AA_{cub}^{j}A_{quart}^{k}(\mathbf{s}_{j+k}))|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{\gamma(C\lambda\|\hat{v}\|_{w,d})^{j+k}(1+\lambda\|\hat{v}\|_{w,d})^{6j+8k}}{N^{\frac{j}{2}+k}}

\displaystyle\nu\Big(\big(\mathcal{N}_{b}+3j+4k+\ell+|\Lambda|\big)^{\frac{3j+4k+\ell}{2}}\Big)\,.

As a consequence, we obtain that

(4.62)

\displaystyle|\nu(A{\mathcal{H}}_{cub}^{j}{\mathcal{H}}_{quart}^{k}(\mathbf{s}_{j+k}))|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{\gamma(C\lambda\|\hat{v}\|_{w,d})^{j+k}(1+\lambda\|\hat{v}\|_{w,d})^{6j+8k}}{N^{\frac{j}{2}+k}}

\displaystyle\nu\Big(\big(\mathcal{N}_{b}+3j+4k+\ell+|\Lambda|\big)^{\frac{3j+4k+\ell}{2}}\Big)\,.

This finishes the proof.
∎

Lemma 4.7
.

Given a test function
J\in\ell^{2}({\Lambda^{*}})\cap\ell^{\infty}({\Lambda^{*}})
, we recall that

(4.63)

\displaystyle f[J]

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\int_{\Lambda^{*}}\,dp\,J(p)a_{p}^{+}a_{p}\,,

(4.64)

\displaystyle g[J]

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\int_{\Lambda^{*}}\,dp\,J(p)a_{-p}a_{p}\,.

Then we have

(4.65)

\displaystyle\|P_{m}f[J]P_{n}\|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\delta_{m,n}\|J\|_{\ell^{\infty}({\Lambda^{*}})}m\,,

(4.66)

\displaystyle\|P_{m}g[J]P_{n}\|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\delta_{n,m+2}\|J\|_{2\cap\infty,d}(m+1+|\Lambda|)\,.

Proof.

Due to
[{\mathcal{N}},f[J]]=0
, we have that

(4.67)

\displaystyle P_{m}f[J]P_{n}\,=\,\delta_{m,n}\int d\mathbf{p}_{2}\,J(p_{1})\delta(p_{1}-p_{2})a_{p_{1}}^{+}a_{p_{2}}P_{n}\,.

Then Lemma
A.4
in Appendix
A
implies

(4.68)

\displaystyle\|P_{m}f[J]P_{n}\|\,\leq\,\delta_{m,n}\|J\|_{\infty}m

For
g[J]
, we have that

(4.69)

\displaystyle P_{m}g[J]P_{n}\,=\,\delta_{n,m+2}\int d\mathbf{p}_{2}\,J(p_{1})\delta(p_{1}+p_{2})a_{p_{1}}a_{p_{2}}

Then Lemma
A.4
implies

(4.70)

\displaystyle\|P_{m}g[J]P_{n}\|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\delta_{n,m+2}\sqrt{\|J\|_{\infty}^{2}(m+1)+|\Lambda|\|J\|_{2}^{2}}\sqrt{m+2}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\delta_{n,m+2}(\|J\|_{L^{2}({\Lambda^{*}})}+\|J\|_{\infty})(m+1+|\Lambda|)

This finishes the proof.
∎

Lemma 4.8

(Propagation of moments in HFB evolution)
.

The HFB evolution
{\mathcal{V}}_{HFB}(t)
satisfies

(4.71)

\displaystyle\big\|(\mathcal{N}_{b}+|\Lambda|)^{\frac{\ell}{2}}{\mathcal{V}}_{HFB}(t)(\mathcal{N}_{b}+|\Lambda|)^{-\frac{\ell}{2}}\big\|\,\leq\,e^{K_{\ell}\|\hat{v}\|_{w,d}\lambda t}

for all
\ell\in{\mathbb{N}}
and some positive constants
K_{\ell}>0
.

Proof.

Let
\psi\in{\mathcal{F}}
. We have that

(4.72)

\displaystyle i\partial_{t}\langle{\mathcal{V}}_{HFB}(t)\psi,(\mathcal{N}_{b}+|\Lambda|)^{\ell}{\mathcal{V}}_{HFB}(t)\psi\rangle

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\langle{\mathcal{V}}_{HFB}(t)\psi,[(\mathcal{N}_{b}+|\Lambda|)^{\ell},{\mathcal{H}}_{HFB}^{\phi_{0}}]{\mathcal{V}}_{HFB}(t)\psi\rangle

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\sum_{n=0}^{\infty}(n+|\Lambda|)^{\ell}\langle{\mathcal{V}}_{HFB}(t)\psi,[P_{n},{\mathcal{H}}_{cor}^{\phi_{0}}]{\mathcal{V}}_{HFB}(t)\psi\rangle\,.

Employing (
4.9
) and recalling (
1.34
), we have that

(4.73)

\displaystyle[P_{n},{\mathcal{H}}_{HFB}^{\phi_{0}}]

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\lambda(P_{n}g[\hat{v}]P_{n+2}-P_{n-2}g[\hat{v}]P_{n}-h.c.)\,.

As a consequence, we have that

(4.74)

\displaystyle\big|\partial_{t}\langle{\mathcal{V}}_{HFB}(t)\psi,(\mathcal{N}_{b}+|\Lambda|)^{\ell}{\mathcal{V}}_{HFB}(t)\psi\rangle\big|

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle 2\lambda\Big|\sum_{n=0}^{\infty}(n+|\Lambda|)^{\ell}\mathrm{Im}\langle{\mathcal{V}}_{HFB}(t)\psi,(P_{n}g[\hat{v}]P_{n+2}-P_{n-2}g[\hat{v}]P_{n}){\mathcal{V}}_{HFB}(t)\psi\rangle\Big|

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle 2\lambda\Big|\sum_{n=0}^{\infty}[(n+|\Lambda|)^{\ell}-(n+2+|\Lambda|)^{\ell}]\mathrm{Im}\langle{\mathcal{V}}_{HFB}(t)\psi,P_{n}g[\hat{v}]P_{n+2}{\mathcal{V}}_{HFB}(t)\psi\rangle\Big|\,.

Using the Mean-Value Theorem and Cauchy-Schwarz, the last inequality implies

(4.75)

\displaystyle\big|\partial_{t}\langle{\mathcal{V}}_{HFB}(t)\psi,(\mathcal{N}_{b}+|\Lambda|)^{\ell}{\mathcal{V}}_{HFB}(t)\psi\rangle\big|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle K_{\ell}\lambda\sum_{n=0}^{\infty}(n+2+|\Lambda|)^{\ell-1}\|P_{n}{\mathcal{V}}_{HFB}(t)\psi\|\|P_{n}g[\hat{v}]P_{n+2}{\mathcal{V}}_{HFB}(t)\psi\|\,.

Applying Lemma
4.7
followed by Young’s inequality, we find

(4.76)

\displaystyle\big|\partial_{t}\langle{\mathcal{V}}_{HFB}(t)\psi,(\mathcal{N}_{b}+|\Lambda|)^{\ell}{\mathcal{V}}_{HFB}(t)\psi\rangle\big|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle K_{\ell}\lambda\|\hat{v}\|_{w,d}\sum_{n=0}^{\infty}(n+2+|\Lambda|)^{\ell-1}(n+1+|\Lambda|)

\displaystyle\big(\langle{\mathcal{V}}_{HFB}(t)\psi,P_{n}{\mathcal{V}}_{HFB}(t)\psi\rangle+\langle{\mathcal{V}}_{HFB}(t)\psi,P_{n+2}{\mathcal{V}}_{HFB}(t)\psi\rangle\big)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle K_{\ell}\lambda\|\hat{v}\|_{w,d}\langle{\mathcal{V}}_{HFB}(t)\psi,(\mathcal{N}_{b}+|\Lambda|)^{\ell}{\mathcal{V}}_{HFB}(t)\psi\rangle\,.

Employing Gronwall’s Lemma concludes the proof.
∎

Remark 4.9
.

Using Corollary
3.3
, we find that

(4.77)

\displaystyle{\mathcal{V}}_{HFB}^{*}(t)\mathcal{N}_{b}{\mathcal{V}}_{HFB}(t)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\int dp\,\big(|u_{\lambda}(t,p)|^{2}a_{p}^{+}a_{p}+|v_{\lambda}(t,p)|^{2}a_{-p}a_{-p}^{+}

\displaystyle+\,u_{\lambda}(t,p)\overline{v_{\lambda}}(t,p)a_{p}^{+}a_{-p}^{+}+\overline{u_{\lambda}}(t,p)v_{\lambda}(t,p)a_{p}a_{-p}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\int dp\,\big((|u_{\lambda}(t,p)|^{2}+|v_{\lambda}(t,p)|^{2})a_{p}^{+}a_{p}+|\Lambda||v_{\lambda}(t,p)|^{2}

\displaystyle+\,u_{\lambda}(t,p)\overline{v_{\lambda}}(t,p)a_{p}^{+}a_{-p}^{+}+\overline{u_{\lambda}}(t,p)v_{\lambda}(t,p)a_{p}a_{-p}\,,

where we used the CCR together with the fact that
v_{\lambda}
is even in
p
. Lemma
4.7
then implies

(4.78)

\displaystyle\big\|{\mathcal{V}}_{HFB}^{*}(t)(\mathcal{N}_{b}+|\Lambda|)^{\frac{1}{2}}{\mathcal{V}}_{HFB}(t)(\mathcal{N}_{b}+|\Lambda|)^{-\frac{1}{2}}\big\|\,\leq\,C_{\|\hat{v}\|_{w,d}}\,.

As an immediate consequence of Lemmata
4.8
and
B.2
, we obtain the following statement.

Corollary 4.10

(Propagation of moments)
.

Let
|\Lambda|\geq 1
. There exist constants
C_{\ell,\mu_{0}},K_{\ell}>0
such that for any
\ell\in{\mathbb{N}}
, we have that

(4.79)

\displaystyle\nu_{t}\big((\mathcal{N}_{b}+|\Lambda|)^{\frac{\ell}{2}}\big)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C_{\ell,\|f_{0}\|_{d}}e^{K_{\ell}\|\hat{v}\|_{w,d}\lambda|\Lambda|t}|\Lambda|^{\frac{\ell}{2}}\,.

Proof.

Assume that
\frac{\ell}{2}\in{\mathbb{N}}
. Define

(4.80)

\displaystyle\widetilde{\mathcal{U}}_{N}(t)\,:=\,{\mathcal{W}}^{*}[\sqrt{N|\Lambda|}\phi_{0}]e^{-i{\mathcal{H}}_{N}t}{\mathcal{W}}[\sqrt{N|\Lambda|}\phi_{0}]\,,

and let

(4.81)

\displaystyle\mu_{t}(A)\,:=\,\nu_{0}\big(\widetilde{{\mathcal{U}}}_{N}^{*}(t)A\widetilde{{\mathcal{U}}}_{N}(t)\big)\,.

There exist
\lambda_{k}\geq 0
,
\sum_{k=1}^{\infty}\lambda_{k}=1
, and
\Psi_{k}\in{\mathcal{F}}
such that

(4.82)

\displaystyle\mu_{t}(A)\,=\,\sum_{k=1}^{\infty}\lambda_{k}\langle\Psi_{k},A\Psi_{k}\rangle\,.

Then we have that

(4.83)

\displaystyle\nu_{t}\big((\mathcal{N}_{b}+1)^{\frac{\ell}{2}}\big)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\mu_{t}\Big({\mathcal{V}}_{HFB}^{*}(t)(\mathcal{N}_{b}+1)^{\frac{\ell}{2}}{\mathcal{V}}_{HFB}(t)\Big)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\sum_{k=1}^{\infty}\lambda_{k}\langle\Psi_{k},{\mathcal{V}}_{HFB}^{*}(t)(\mathcal{N}_{b}+1)^{\frac{\ell}{2}}{\mathcal{V}}_{HFB}(t)\Psi_{k}\rangle

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle e^{K_{\ell}\|\hat{v}\|_{w,d}\lambda t}\sum_{k=1}^{\infty}\lambda_{k}\langle\Psi_{k},(\mathcal{N}_{b}+1)^{\frac{\ell}{2}}\Psi_{k}\rangle

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle e^{K_{\ell}\|\hat{v}\|_{w,d}\lambda t}\mu_{t}\big((\mathcal{N}_{b}+1)^{\frac{\ell}{2}}\big)\,.

using Lemma
4.8
. Similarly, we write

(4.84)

\displaystyle\nu_{0}(A)=\sum_{k=1}^{\infty}\alpha_{k}\langle\Phi_{k},A\Phi_{k}\rangle

for some
\alpha_{k}\geq 0
,
\sum_{k=1}^{\infty}\alpha_{k}=1
,
\Phi_{k}\in{\mathcal{F}}
, and we obtain that

(4.85)

\displaystyle\mu_{t}\big((\mathcal{N}_{b}+1)^{\frac{\ell}{2}}\big)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\sum_{k=1}^{\infty}\alpha_{k}\langle\Phi_{k},\widetilde{{\mathcal{U}}}_{N}^{*}(t)(\mathcal{N}_{b}+1)^{\frac{\ell}{2}}\widetilde{{\mathcal{U}}}_{N}(t)\Phi_{k}\rangle

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C_{\ell}e^{K_{\ell}\|\hat{v}\|_{w,d}\lambda|\Lambda|t}\sum_{k=1}^{\infty}\mu_{k}\langle\Phi_{k},(\mathcal{N}_{b}+1)^{\frac{\ell}{2}}\big(1+\frac{\mathcal{N}_{b}}{N|\Lambda|}\big)\Phi_{k}\rangle

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle C_{\ell}e^{K_{\ell}\|\hat{v}\|_{w,d}\lambda|\Lambda|t}\nu_{0}\Big((\mathcal{N}_{b}+1)^{\frac{\ell}{2}}\big(1+\frac{\mathcal{N}_{b}}{N|\Lambda|}\big)\Big)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C_{\ell,\|f_{0}\|_{d}}e^{K_{\ell}\|\hat{v}\|_{w,d}\lambda|\Lambda|t}|\Lambda|^{\frac{\ell}{2}}\,,

where we used Lemma
B.2
followed by Lemma
4.1
. Collecting (
4.83
) and (
4.85
) and using
|\Lambda|\geq 1
, we obtain that

(4.86)

\displaystyle\nu_{t}\big((\mathcal{N}_{b}+1)^{\frac{\ell}{2}}\big)\,\leq\,C_{\ell,\|f_{0}\|_{d}}e^{K_{\ell}\|\hat{v}\|_{w,d}\lambda|\Lambda|t}|\Lambda|^{\frac{\ell}{2}}\,.

The half-integer case follows analogously to (
4.18
). This concludes the proof.
∎

5.
Control of error terms in the expansion

Again, we write
\int\equiv\int_{\Lambda^{*}}
for brevity, to account for lattice sums over
{\Lambda^{*}}
in the sense of (
1.51
).

Proposition 5.1

(Tail estimates)
.

Let
T>0
and
t\leq T\lambda^{-2}
,
\lambda\in(0,1)
,
|\Lambda|\geq 1
, and
J\in\ell^{1}({\Lambda^{*}})\cap\ell^{\infty}({\Lambda^{*}})
. Then the following holds true.

(1)

(5.1)

\displaystyle\Phi_{t}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-i\int_{0}^{t}\,ds\,\frac{\nu_{0}\big([a_{0},{\mathcal{H}}_{cub}(s)]\big)}{|\Lambda|}\,+\,\operatorname{Rem}_{2}(t;\Phi)

with

(5.2)

\displaystyle|\operatorname{Rem}_{2}(t;\Phi)|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C_{\|\hat{v}\|_{w,d},\|f_{0}\|_{d}}T^{2}e^{C\|\hat{v}\|_{w,d}|\Lambda|/\lambda T}\frac{|\Lambda|^{\frac{3}{2}}}{N\lambda^{2}}\Big(1+\frac{|\Lambda|}{N}\Big)\,.

(2)

(5.3)

\displaystyle\int dp\,J(p)(f_{t}(p)-f_{0}(p))

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-i\int_{0}^{t}\,ds\,\frac{\nu_{0}\big([f[J],{\mathcal{H}}_{quart}(s)]\big)}{|\Lambda|}

\displaystyle-\int_{\Delta[t,2]}\,d\mathbf{s}_{2}\frac{\nu_{0}\big([[f[J],{\mathcal{H}}_{cub}(s_{1})],{\mathcal{H}}_{cub}(s_{2})]\big)}{|\Lambda|}

\displaystyle+\operatorname{Rem}_{2}(t;f[J])

with

(5.4)

\displaystyle|\operatorname{Rem}_{2}(t;f[J])|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C_{\|\hat{v}\|_{w,d},\|f_{0}\|_{d}}\langle T\rangle^{4}\|J\|_{\ell^{\infty}({\Lambda^{*}})}\frac{e^{C\|\hat{v}\|_{w,d}|\Lambda|/\lambda T}|\Lambda|^{6}}{\lambda^{4}N^{2}}\Big(1+\frac{|\Lambda|}{N}\Big)^{2}\,.

(3)

(5.5)

\displaystyle\int dp\,J(p)g_{t}(p)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-i\int_{0}^{t}\,ds\,\frac{\nu_{0}\big([g[J],{\mathcal{H}}_{quart}(s)]\big)}{|\Lambda|}

\displaystyle-\int_{\Delta[t,2]}\,d\mathbf{s}_{2}\frac{\nu_{0}\big([[g[J],{\mathcal{H}}_{cub}(s_{1})],{\mathcal{H}}_{cub}(s_{2})]\big)}{|\Lambda|}

\displaystyle+\operatorname{Rem}_{2}(t;g[J])

with

(5.6)

\displaystyle|\operatorname{Rem}_{2}(t;g[J])|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C_{\|\hat{v}\|_{w,d},\|f_{0}\|_{d}}\langle T\rangle^{4}\|J\|_{2\cap\infty,d}\frac{e^{C\|\hat{v}\|_{w,d}|\Lambda|/\lambda T}|\Lambda|^{6}}{\lambda^{4}N^{2}}\Big(1+\frac{|\Lambda|}{N}\Big)^{2}\,.

Proof.

Recall that
\nu_{0}
being number conserving implies

(5.7)

\displaystyle\nu_{0}(a_{0})\,=\,\nu_{0}(a_{p}a_{q})\,=\,0\,.

Let
A\in{\mathcal{P}}[a,a^{+}]
, be a monomial with data such that

(5.8)

\displaystyle\|P_{n}AP_{n-\osg(A)}\|\,\leq\,\gamma(n+|\Lambda|)^{\frac{\ell}{2}}

with
\ell,|\osg(A)|\leq 2
. Lemma
3.1
implies

(5.9)

\displaystyle\nu_{t}(A)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\nu_{0}(A)-i\int_{0}^{t}\,ds\,\nu_{0}\big([A,{\mathcal{H}}_{I}(s)]\big)

\displaystyle-\int_{\Delta[t,2]}\,d\mathbf{s}_{2}\nu_{s_{2}}\big([[A,{\mathcal{H}}_{I}(s_{1})],{\mathcal{H}}_{I}(s_{2})]\big)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\nu_{0}(A)-i\int_{0}^{t}\,ds\,\nu_{0}\big([A,{\mathcal{H}}_{I}(s)]\big)

\displaystyle-\int_{\Delta[t,2]}\,d\mathbf{s}_{2}\nu_{0}\big([[A,{\mathcal{H}}_{I}(s_{1})],{\mathcal{H}}_{I}(s_{2})]\big)

\displaystyle+i\int_{\Delta[t,3]}\,d\mathbf{s}_{3}\nu_{0}\big([[[A,{\mathcal{H}}_{I}(s_{1})],{\mathcal{H}}_{I}(s_{2})],{\mathcal{H}}_{I}(s_{3})]\big)

(5.10)

\displaystyle+\int_{\Delta[t,4]}\,d\mathbf{s}_{4}\nu_{s_{4}}\big([[[[A,{\mathcal{H}}_{I}(s_{1})],{\mathcal{H}}_{I}(s_{2})],{\mathcal{H}}_{I}(s_{3})],{\mathcal{H}}_{I}(s_{4})]\big)\,,

where

(5.11)

\displaystyle{\mathcal{H}}_{I}(s)\,=\,{\mathcal{H}}_{cub}(s)+{\mathcal{H}}_{quart}(s)\,,

see (
3.6
). In order to simplify notation, we shall abbreviate by
A{\mathcal{H}}_{cub}^{j}{\mathcal{H}}_{quart}^{k}(\mathbf{s}_{j+k})
all terms that contain one factor
A
,
j
factors
{\mathcal{H}}_{cub}(s_{\ell})
,
k
factors
{\mathcal{H}}_{quart}(s_{m})
, all at possibly different times. Let
\mathbf{s}_{j+k}\in\Delta[t,j+k]
,
t\leq T\lambda^{-2}
,
\lambda\in(0,1)
. Proposition
4.6
then implies

(5.12)

\displaystyle|\nu_{s_{j+k}}\big(A{\mathcal{H}}_{cub}^{j}{\mathcal{H}}_{quart}^{k}(\mathbf{s}_{j+k})\big)|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{C_{j,k}\gamma\|\hat{v}\|_{w,d}^{j+k}\lambda^{j+k}(1+\lambda\|\hat{v}\|_{w,d})^{6j+8k}}{N^{\frac{j}{2}+k}}\nu_{s_{j+k}}\Big((\mathcal{N}_{b}+|\Lambda|)^{\frac{3j+4k+\ell}{2}}\Big)

for all
N>0
large enough. By Corollary
4.10
, we obtain

(5.13)

\displaystyle|\nu_{s_{j+k}}\big(A{\mathcal{H}}_{cub}^{j}{\mathcal{H}}_{quart}^{k}(\mathbf{s}_{j+k})\big)|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C_{\|\hat{v}\|_{w,d},\|f_{0}\|_{d},j,k}\gamma e^{K_{j,k}\|\hat{v}\|_{w,d}|\Lambda|/\lambda T}\frac{\lambda^{j+k}|\Lambda|^{\frac{3j+4k+\ell}{2}}}{N^{\frac{j}{2}+k}}\,.

Similarly to (
5.12
), we have that Lemma
4.1
implies

(5.14)

\displaystyle|\nu_{0}\big(A{\mathcal{H}}_{cub}^{j}{\mathcal{H}}_{quart}^{k}(\mathbf{s}_{j+k})\big)|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C_{\|\hat{v}\|_{w,d},\|f_{0}\|_{d},j,k}\gamma\frac{\lambda^{j+k}|\Lambda|^{\frac{3j+4k+\ell}{2}}}{N^{\frac{j}{2}+k}}\,.

Due to
\nu_{0}
being number conserving, see (
4.10
), we have that

(5.15)

\displaystyle|\nu_{0}\big(A{\mathcal{H}}_{cub}^{j}{\mathcal{H}}_{quart}^{k}(\mathbf{s}_{j+k})\big)|\,=\,0\,,

whenever the total number of creation and annihilation operators in
A{\mathcal{H}}_{cub}^{j}{\mathcal{H}}_{quart}^{k}(\mathbf{s}_{j+k})
is odd.

In the case
A=a_{0}
, due to Corollary
3.3
and using
\hat{v}(0)=0
, we have that

(5.16)

\displaystyle a_{0}\,=\,a_{0}(s)

for all
s\in{\mathbb{R}}
. With that, an easy computation yields

(5.17)

\displaystyle[a_{0},{\mathcal{H}}_{cub}(s)]

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle[a_{0},{\mathcal{H}}_{cub}^{\phi_{0}}](s)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{\lambda}{\sqrt{N}}\int\,dp\,\hat{v}(p)\big(a_{p}^{+}(s)a_{p}(s)+a_{p}(s)a_{-p}(s)\big)\,.

Using Corollary
3.3
, we obtain

(5.18)

\displaystyle[a_{0},{\mathcal{H}}_{cub}(s)]

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle f[J_{1}(s)]+g[J_{2}(s)]+g^{*}[J_{3}(s)]+J_{4}(s)\,,

where, with the notation of Corollary
3.3
,

(5.19)

\displaystyle J_{1}(s,p)

\displaystyle:=

\displaystyle\frac{\lambda\hat{v}(p)}{\sqrt{N}}\big(|u_{\lambda}(s,p)|^{2}+|v_{\lambda}(s,p)|^{2}+2\overline{v_{\lambda}}(s,p)\overline{u_{\lambda}}(s,p)\big)\,,

(5.20)

\displaystyle J_{2}(s,p)

\displaystyle:=

\displaystyle\frac{\lambda\hat{v}(p)}{\sqrt{N}}\big(v_{\lambda}(s,p)+\overline{u_{\lambda}}(s,p)\big)\overline{u_{\lambda}}(s,p)\,,

(5.21)

\displaystyle J_{3}(s,p)

\displaystyle:=

\displaystyle\frac{\lambda\hat{v}(p)}{\sqrt{N}}\big(u_{\lambda}(s,p)+\overline{v_{\lambda}}(s,p)\big)\overline{v_{\lambda}}(s,p)\,,

(5.22)

\displaystyle J_{4}(s)

\displaystyle:=

\displaystyle\frac{\lambda}{\sqrt{N}}\int\,dp\,\hat{v}(p)\big(|v_{\lambda}(s,p)|^{2}+\overline{u_{\lambda}}(s,p)\overline{v_{\lambda}}(s,p)\big)\,.

Note, that in (
5.18
), we used the notation

(5.23)

\displaystyle g^{*}[J]\,=\,\int\,dp\,J(p)a_{p}^{+}a_{-p}^{+}\,.

Using Corollary
3.3
again, we find that

(5.24)

\displaystyle\|J_{i}(s)\|_{d}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C_{\|\hat{v}\|_{w,d}}\frac{\lambda}{\sqrt{N}}\quad\mbox{for\ }i\in\{1,2,3\}\,,

(5.25)

\displaystyle|J_{4}(s)|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C_{\|\hat{v}\|_{w,d}}\frac{\lambda}{\sqrt{N}}

for all
s\leq T\lambda^{-2}
.

Similarly, we compute

(5.26)

\displaystyle[a_{0},{\mathcal{H}}_{quart}(s)]

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle[a_{0},{\mathcal{H}}_{quart}](s)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{\lambda}{N}\int\,dp\,\hat{v}(p_{2})(a_{p_{1}+p_{2}}^{+}a_{p_{2}}a_{p_{1}})(s)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{1}{\sqrt{N}}{\mathcal{H}}_{cub}^{(2)}(s)\,.

Here, we used the notation

(5.27)

\displaystyle{\mathcal{H}}_{cub}(s)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{\lambda}{\sqrt{N}}\int\,dp\,\hat{v}(p_{2})\big((a_{p_{1}}^{+}a_{p_{2}}^{+}a_{p_{1}+p_{2}})(s)+(a_{p_{1}+p_{2}}^{+}a_{p_{2}}a_{p_{1}})(s)\big)

\displaystyle=:

\displaystyle{\mathcal{H}}_{cub}^{(1)}(s)+{\mathcal{H}}_{cub}^{(2)}(s)\,.

Notice that bounds of the form (
5.12
)–(
5.14
) hold with
{\mathcal{H}}_{cub}
replaced by
{\mathcal{H}}_{cub}^{(2)}
, due to the proof of Proposition
4.6
.

Using (
5.9
) and (
5.26
), we have that

(5.28)

\displaystyle\Phi_{t}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-\frac{i}{|\Lambda|}\int_{0}^{t}\,ds\,\nu_{0}\big([a_{0},{\mathcal{H}}_{cub}(s)]\big)\,+\,\operatorname{Rem}_{2}(t;\Phi)\,,

where

(5.29)

\displaystyle\operatorname{Rem}_{2}(t;\Phi)

\displaystyle:=

\displaystyle-\frac{i}{2\sqrt{N}}\int_{0}^{t}\,ds\,\frac{\nu_{0}({\mathcal{H}}_{cub}^{(2)}(s))}{|\Lambda|}

\displaystyle-\int_{\Delta[t,2]}\,d\mathbf{s}_{2}\frac{\nu_{s_{2}}\big([f[J_{1}(s_{1})]+g[J_{2}(s_{1})]+g^{*}[J_{3}(s_{1})],{\mathcal{H}}_{I}(s_{2})]\big)}{|\Lambda|}

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{2\sqrt{N}}\int_{\Delta[t,2]}\,d\mathbf{s}_{2}\,\frac{\nu_{s_{2}}\big([{\mathcal{H}}_{cub}^{(2)}(s_{1}),{\mathcal{H}}_{I}(s_{2})]\big)}{|\Lambda|}\,.

Recall that, by definition (
5.23
),

(5.30)

\displaystyle|\nu_{s_{2}}([g^{*}[J_{3}(s_{1})],{\mathcal{H}}_{I}(s_{2})])|\,=\,|\nu_{s_{2}}([g[\overline{J_{3}}(s_{1})],{\mathcal{H}}_{I}(s_{2})])|\,.

Then Lemma
4.7
together with the bounds (
5.13
) and (
5.24
) imply

(5.31)

\displaystyle\frac{\big|\nu_{s_{2}}\big([f[J_{1}(s_{1})],{\mathcal{H}}_{I}(s_{2})]\big)\big|}{|\Lambda|}\,,\,\frac{\big|\nu_{s_{2}}\big([g[J_{2}(s_{1})],{\mathcal{H}}_{I}(s_{2})]\big)\big|}{|\Lambda|}\,,

\displaystyle\frac{\big|\nu_{s_{2}}\big([g^{*}[J_{3}(s_{1})],{\mathcal{H}}_{I}(s_{2})]\big)\big|}{|\Lambda|}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{C_{\|\hat{v}\|_{w,d},\|f_{0}\|_{d}}e^{C\|\hat{v}\|_{w,d}|\Lambda|/\lambda T}\lambda^{2}|\Lambda|^{\frac{3}{2}}}{N}\Big(1+\sqrt{\frac{|\Lambda|}{N}}\Big)\,.

(
5.15
) with
A={\bf 1}
implies

(5.32)

\displaystyle\frac{|\nu_{0}({\mathcal{H}}_{cub}^{(2)}(s))|}{|\Lambda|\sqrt{N}}\,=\,0\,.

(
5.13
) with
A={\bf 1}
implies

(5.33)

\displaystyle\frac{\big|\nu_{s_{2}}\big([{\mathcal{H}}_{cub}^{(2)}(s_{1}),{\mathcal{H}}_{I}(s_{2})]\big)\big|}{|\Lambda|\sqrt{N}}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{C_{\|\hat{v}\|_{w,d},\|\hat{v}\|_{w,d}}e^{C\|\hat{v}\|_{w,d}|\Lambda|/\lambda T}\lambda^{2}|\Lambda|^{2}}{N^{\frac{3}{2}}}\Big(1+\sqrt{\frac{|\Lambda|}{N}}\Big)\,.

Collecting (
5.29
), (
5.31
), (
5.32
), and (
5.33
), yields the bound

(5.34)

\displaystyle|\operatorname{Rem}_{2}(t;\Phi)|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C_{\|\hat{v}\|_{w,d},\|f_{0}\|_{d}}e^{C\|\hat{v}\|_{w,d}|\Lambda|/\lambda T}\frac{\lambda^{2}t^{2}|\Lambda|^{\frac{3}{2}}}{N}\Big(1+\sqrt{\frac{|\Lambda|}{N}}\Big)^{2}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C_{\|\hat{v}\|_{w,d},\|f_{0}\|_{d}}T^{2}e^{C\|\hat{v}\|_{w,d}|\Lambda|/\lambda T}\frac{|\Lambda|^{\frac{3}{2}}}{N\lambda^{2}}\Big(1+\frac{|\Lambda|}{N}\Big)

for all
t\leq T\lambda^{-2}
,
\lambda\in(0,1)
.

In the cases
A=f[J]
and
A=g[J]
, we have that

(5.35)

\displaystyle\frac{\nu_{t}(A)}{|\Lambda|}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{\nu_{0}(f[J])}{|\Lambda|}\mathds{1}_{A=f[J]}-i\int_{0}^{t}\,ds\,\frac{\nu_{0}\big([A,{\mathcal{H}}_{quart}(s)]\big)}{|\Lambda|}

\displaystyle-\int_{\Delta[t,2]}\,d\mathbf{s}_{2}\frac{\nu_{0}\big([[A,{\mathcal{H}}_{cub}(s_{1})],{\mathcal{H}}_{cub}(s_{2})]\big)}{|\Lambda|}

\displaystyle+\operatorname{Rem}_{2}(t;A)\,,

where, using (
5.7
) and (
5.10
),

(5.36)

\displaystyle\operatorname{Rem}_{2}(t;A)

\displaystyle:=

\displaystyle-i\int_{0}^{t}\,ds\,\frac{\nu_{0}\big([A,{\mathcal{H}}_{cub}(s)]\big)}{|\Lambda|}

\displaystyle-\int_{\Delta[t,2]}\,d\mathbf{s}_{2}\frac{\nu_{0}\big([[A,{\mathcal{H}}_{cub}(s_{1})],{\mathcal{H}}_{quart}(s_{2})]\big)}{|\Lambda|}

\displaystyle-\int_{\Delta[t,2]}\,d\mathbf{s}_{2}\frac{\nu_{0}\big([[A,{\mathcal{H}}_{quart}(s_{1})],{\mathcal{H}}_{I}(s_{2})]\big)}{|\Lambda|}

\displaystyle+i\int_{\Delta[t,3]}\,d\mathbf{s}_{3}\frac{\nu_{0}\big([[[A,{\mathcal{H}}_{I}(s_{1})],{\mathcal{H}}_{I}(s_{2})],{\mathcal{H}}_{I}(s_{3})]\big)}{|\Lambda|}

\displaystyle+\int_{\Delta[t,4]}\,d\mathbf{s}_{4}\frac{\nu_{s_{4}}\big([[[[A,{\mathcal{H}}_{I}(s_{1})],{\mathcal{H}}_{I}(s_{2})],{\mathcal{H}}_{I}(s_{3})],{\mathcal{H}}_{I}(s_{4})]\big)}{|\Lambda|}\,.

Employing (
5.14
), Lemma
4.7
yields

(5.37)

\displaystyle\frac{\big|\nu_{0}\big(A{\mathcal{H}}_{quart}^{2}(\mathbf{s}_{2})\big)\big|}{|\Lambda|}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C_{\|\hat{v}\|_{w,d},\|f_{0}\|_{d}}\frac{\|J\|_{A}\lambda^{2}|\Lambda|^{4}}{N^{2}}\,,

(5.38)

\displaystyle\frac{\big|\nu_{0}\big(A{\mathcal{H}}_{cub}^{2}{\mathcal{H}}_{quart}(\mathbf{s}_{3})\big)\big|}{|\Lambda|}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C_{\|\hat{v}\|_{w,d},\|f_{0}\|_{d}}\frac{\|J\|_{A}\lambda^{3}|\Lambda|^{5}}{N^{2}}\,,

(5.39)

\displaystyle\frac{\big|\nu_{0}\big(A{\mathcal{H}}_{quart}^{3}(\mathbf{s}_{3})\big)\big|}{|\Lambda|}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C_{\|\hat{v}\|_{w,d},\|f_{0}\|_{d}}\frac{\|J\|_{A}\lambda^{3}|\Lambda|^{6}}{N^{3}}\,,

where we abbreviated

(5.40)

\displaystyle\|J\|_{A}\,:=\,\|J\|_{\ell^{\infty}({\Lambda^{*}})}\mathds{1}_{A=f[J]}\,+\,\|J\|_{2\cap\infty,d}\mathds{1}_{A=g[J]}\,.

Using (
5.15
), we have that

(5.41)

\displaystyle\frac{\big|\nu_{0}\big([A,{\mathcal{H}}_{cub}(s)]\big)\big|}{|\Lambda|}\,=\,\frac{\big|\nu_{0}\big(A{\mathcal{H}}_{cub}{\mathcal{H}}_{quart}(\mathbf{s}_{2})\big)\big|}{|\Lambda|}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{\big|\nu_{0}\big(A{\mathcal{H}}_{cub}^{3}(\mathbf{s}_{3})\big)\big|}{|\Lambda|}\,=\,\frac{\big|\nu_{0}\big(A{\mathcal{H}}_{cub}{\mathcal{H}}_{quart}^{2}(\mathbf{s}_{3})\big)\big|}{|\Lambda|}\,=\,0\,.

(
5.12
) together with Lemma
4.7
implies

(5.42)

\displaystyle\frac{\big|\nu_{s_{2}}\big(A{\mathcal{H}}_{I}^{4}(\mathbf{s}_{4}\big)\big|}{|\Lambda|}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C_{\|\hat{v}\|_{w,d},\|f_{0}\|_{d}}e^{C\|\hat{v}\|_{w,d}|\Lambda|/\lambda T}\frac{\|J\|_{A}\lambda^{4}|\Lambda|^{6}}{N^{2}}\Big(1+\sqrt{\frac{|\Lambda|}{N}}\Big)^{4}\,.

Collecting (
5.37
)-(
5.42
), we arrive at

(5.43)

\displaystyle|\operatorname{Rem}_{2}(t;A)|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{C_{\|\hat{v}\|_{w,d},\|f_{0}\|_{d}}e^{C\|\hat{v}\|_{w,d}|\Lambda|/\lambda T}\|J\|_{A}\lambda^{2}t^{2}|\Lambda|^{4}}{N^{2}}\Big[1\,+\,\lambda t|\Lambda|\Big(1+\frac{|\Lambda|}{N}\Big)

\displaystyle+\,\lambda^{2}t^{2}|\Lambda|^{2}\Big(1+\sqrt{\frac{|\Lambda|}{N}}\Big)^{4}\Big]

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C_{\|\hat{v}\|_{w,d},\|f_{0}\|_{d}}\langle T\rangle^{4}\|J\|_{A}\frac{e^{C\|\hat{v}\|_{w,d}|\Lambda|/\lambda T}|\Lambda|^{6}}{\lambda^{4}N^{2}}\Big(\big(1+\frac{|\Lambda|}{N}\big)^{2}+\frac{\lambda^{2}}{|\Lambda|^{2}}\Big)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C_{\|\hat{v}\|_{w,d},\|f_{0}\|_{d}}\langle T\rangle^{4}\|J\|_{A}\frac{e^{C\|\hat{v}\|_{w,d}|\Lambda|/\lambda T}|\Lambda|^{6}}{\lambda^{4}N^{2}}\Big(1+\frac{|\Lambda|}{N}\Big)^{2}

for all
t\leq T\lambda^{-2}
,
\lambda\in(0,1)
,
|\Lambda|\geq 1
. This finishes the proof.
∎

Up until now, all calculations did not further distinguish the cases of fixed
|\Lambda|
and
|\Lambda|
growing with
N
. In the latter situation, we will approximate lattice sums over
{\Lambda^{*}}
with integrals over
{\mathbb{R}}^{3}
. It is crucial to note that oscillatory and dispersive properties differ fundamentally in these two cases.

5.1.
Fixed,
N
-independent lattice
{\Lambda^{*}}\cong{\mathbb{Z}}^{3}

5.1.1.
Notation

For the next result, we define

(5.44)

\displaystyle\Delta_{cub}H(\mathbf{p_{2}})

\displaystyle:=

\displaystyle H(p_{1})+H(p_{2})-H(p_{1}+p_{2})\,,

(5.45)

\displaystyle\widetilde{h}

\displaystyle:=

\displaystyle 1+h\,,

(5.46)

\displaystyle p_{12}

\displaystyle:=

\displaystyle p_{1}+p_{2}\,.

Recall from Corollary
3.3
that

(5.47)

\displaystyle V_{1}(p)^{2}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{\hat{v}(p)^{2}}{\Omega(p)(\Omega(p)+E(p)+\lambda\hat{v}(p))}\,,

(5.48)

\displaystyle V_{2}(p)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{\hat{v}(p)}{\Omega(p)}\,.

In order to describe the dynamics of
\Phi
, we introduce the condensate operator

(5.49)

\displaystyle\operatorname{Con}_{d}(h)(T;\lambda)

\displaystyle:=

\displaystyle-i\int_{0}^{T}\,dS\,\int_{\Lambda^{*}}\,dp\,\hat{v}(p)h_{\frac{S}{\lambda^{2}}}(p)\,.

For the dynamics of
f
, we define the generalized Boltzmann operators

(5.50)

\displaystyle\operatorname{Bol}_{d}(h)[J](T;\lambda)

\displaystyle:=

\displaystyle\frac{1}{\lambda^{2}}\int_{\Delta[T,2]}\,d\mathbf{S}_{2}\,\int_{({\Lambda^{*}})^{2}}\,d\mathbf{p}_{2}\,\cos\big(\frac{\Delta_{cub}\Omega(\mathbf{p}_{2})(S_{1}-S_{2})}{\lambda^{2}}\big)

\displaystyle(\hat{v}(p_{1})+\hat{v}(p_{2}))^{2}\Delta_{cub}J(\mathbf{p}_{2})\big(\widetilde{h}_{{\frac{S_{2}}{\lambda^{2}}}}(p_{1})\widetilde{h}_{\frac{S_{2}}{\lambda^{2}}}(p_{2})h_{\frac{S_{2}}{\lambda^{2}}}(p_{1}+p_{2})

\displaystyle-h_{\frac{S_{2}}{\lambda^{2}}}(p_{1})h_{\frac{S_{2}}{\lambda^{2}}}(p_{2})\widetilde{h}_{\frac{S_{2}}{\lambda^{2}}}(p_{1}+p_{2})\big)\,,

(5.51)

\displaystyle\operatorname{bol}^{(1)}(h)[J](\mathbf{S}_{2}/\lambda^{2})

\displaystyle:=

\displaystyle\frac{2}{\lambda^{2}}\mathrm{Im}\int_{({\Lambda^{*}})^{2}}\,d\mathbf{p}_{2}\,(\hat{v}(p_{1})+\hat{v}(p_{2}))(\hat{v}(p_{1})+\hat{v}(p_{12}))

\displaystyle V_{2}(p_{1})e^{-i(\Omega(p_{2})-\Omega(p_{12}))(S_{1}-S_{2})/\lambda^{2}}

\displaystyle\Big(\big(-J(-p_{1})+J(p_{2})-J(p_{12})\big)e^{i\Omega(p_{1})S_{2}/\lambda^{2}}\sin(\Omega(p_{1})S_{1}/\lambda^{2})

\displaystyle\big(\widetilde{h}_{\frac{S_{2}}{\lambda^{2}}}(-p_{1})h_{\frac{S_{2}}{\lambda^{2}}}(p_{2})\widetilde{h}_{\frac{S_{2}}{\lambda^{2}}}(p_{12})-h_{\frac{S_{2}}{\lambda^{2}}}(-p_{1})\widetilde{h}_{\frac{S_{2}}{\lambda^{2}}}(p_{2})h_{\frac{S_{2}}{\lambda^{2}}}(p_{12})\big)

\displaystyle+\,{\Delta_{cub}J}(\mathbf{p}_{2})e^{i\Omega(p_{1})S_{1}/\lambda^{2}}\sin(\Omega(p_{1})S_{2}/\lambda^{2})

\displaystyle\big(h_{\frac{S_{2}}{\lambda^{2}}}(p_{1})h_{\frac{S_{2}}{\lambda^{2}}}(p_{2})\widetilde{h}_{\frac{S_{2}}{\lambda^{2}}}(p_{12})-\widetilde{h}_{\frac{S_{2}}{\lambda^{2}}}(p_{1})\widetilde{h}_{\frac{S_{2}}{\lambda^{2}}}(p_{2})h_{\frac{S_{2}}{\lambda^{2}}}(p_{12})\big)\Big)\,,

(5.52)

\displaystyle\operatorname{Bol}_{d}^{(1)}(h)[J](T;\lambda)

\displaystyle:=

\displaystyle\int_{\Delta[T,2]}d\mathbf{S}_{2}\,\operatorname{bol}^{(1)}(h)[J](\mathbf{S}_{2}/\lambda^{2})\,,

(5.53)

\displaystyle\operatorname{bol}^{(2,1)}(h)[J](\mathbf{S}_{2};\lambda)

\displaystyle:=

\displaystyle\frac{1}{\lambda^{2}}\mathrm{Im}\int_{({\Lambda^{*}})^{2}}\,d\mathbf{p}_{2}\,(\hat{v}(p_{1})+\hat{v}(p_{2}))^{2}{\Delta_{cub}J}(\mathbf{p}_{2})e^{i\Omega(\mathbf{p}_{2})(S_{1}-S_{2})/\lambda^{2}}

\displaystyle\Big(V_{1}(p_{1})^{2}\big(\sin(\Omega(p_{1})S_{1}/\lambda^{2})e^{-i\Omega(p_{1})S_{1}/\lambda^{2}}-\sin(\Omega(p_{1})S_{2}/\lambda^{2})e^{i\Omega(p_{1})S_{2}/\lambda^{2}}\big)

\displaystyle+V_{1}(p_{2})^{2}\big(\sin(\Omega(p_{2})S_{1}/\lambda^{2})e^{-i\Omega(p_{2})S_{1}/\lambda^{2}}-\sin(\Omega(p_{2})S_{2}/\lambda^{2})e^{i\Omega(p_{2})S_{2}/\lambda^{2}}\big)

\displaystyle-V_{1}(p_{12})^{2}\big(\sin(\Omega(p_{12})S_{1}/\lambda^{2})e^{i\Omega(p_{12})S_{1}/\lambda^{2}}-\sin(\Omega(p_{12})S_{2}/\lambda^{2})e^{i\Omega(p_{12})S_{2}/\lambda^{2}}\big)\Big)

\displaystyle\big(h_{\frac{S_{2}}{\lambda^{2}}}(p_{1})h_{\frac{S_{2}}{\lambda^{2}}}(p_{2})\widetilde{h}_{\frac{S_{2}}{\lambda^{2}}}(p_{12}))-\widetilde{h}_{\frac{S_{2}}{\lambda^{2}}}(p_{1})\widetilde{h}_{\frac{S_{2}}{\lambda^{2}}}(p_{2})h_{\frac{S_{2}}{\lambda^{2}}}(p_{12})\big)\,,

(5.54)

\displaystyle\operatorname{bol}^{(2,2)}(h)[J](\mathbf{S}_{2}/\lambda^{2})

\displaystyle:=

\displaystyle\frac{2}{\lambda^{2}}\mathrm{Re}\int_{({\Lambda^{*}})^{2}}\,d\mathbf{p}_{2}\,(\hat{v}(p_{1})+\hat{v}(p_{2}))

\displaystyle\Big[\sin(\Omega(p_{1})S_{1}/\lambda^{2})\sin(\Omega(p_{12})S_{1}/\lambda^{2})

\displaystyle e^{i\Omega(p_{2})(S_{1}-S_{2})/\lambda^{2}+i(\Omega(p_{1})-\Omega(p_{12}))S_{2}/\lambda^{2}}

\displaystyle V_{2}(p_{1})V_{2}(p_{12})(\hat{v}(p_{2})+\hat{v}(p_{12}))

\displaystyle(-J(-p_{1})+J(p_{2})+J(-p_{12}))

\displaystyle\big(h_{\frac{S_{2}}{\lambda^{2}}}(-p_{1})\widetilde{h}_{\frac{S_{2}}{\lambda^{2}}}(p_{2})\widetilde{h}_{\frac{S_{2}}{\lambda^{2}}}(-p_{12})-\widetilde{h}_{\frac{S_{2}}{\lambda^{2}}}(-p_{1})h_{\frac{S_{2}}{\lambda^{2}}}(p_{2})f(-p_{12})\big)

\displaystyle+\,\sin(\Omega(p_{1})S_{1}/\lambda^{2})e^{i\Omega(p_{2})(S_{1}-S_{2})/\lambda^{2}-i\Omega(p_{12})S_{1}/\lambda^{2}}V_{2}(p_{1})

\displaystyle(-J(-p_{1})+J(p_{2})-J(p_{12}))

\displaystyle\Big((\hat{v}(p_{1})+\hat{v}(p_{2}))V_{2}(p_{1})\sin(\Omega(p_{1})S_{2}/\lambda^{2})e^{i\Omega(p_{12})S_{2}/\lambda^{2}}

\displaystyle+\,(\hat{v}(p_{2})+\hat{v}(p_{12}))V_{2}(p_{12})\sin(\Omega(p_{12})S_{2}/\lambda^{2})e^{-i\Omega(p_{1})S_{2}/\lambda^{2}}\Big)

\displaystyle\big(h_{\frac{S_{2}}{\lambda^{2}}}(-p_{1})\widetilde{h}_{\frac{S_{2}}{\lambda^{2}}}(p_{2})h_{\frac{S_{2}}{\lambda^{2}}}(p_{12})-\widetilde{h}_{\frac{S_{2}}{\lambda^{2}}}(-p_{1})h_{\frac{S_{2}}{\lambda^{2}}}(p_{2})\widetilde{h}_{\frac{S_{2}}{\lambda^{2}}}(p_{12})\big)

\displaystyle+\Big(\frac{1}{2}e^{-i(\Omega(p_{1})+\Omega(p_{2}))S_{2}/\lambda^{2}}\sin(\Omega(p_{12})S_{2}/\lambda^{2})V_{2}(p_{12})(\hat{v}(p_{1})+\hat{v}(p_{2}))

\displaystyle+\,e^{-i(\Omega(p_{1})+\Omega(p_{12}))s_{2}}\sin(\Omega(p_{2})S_{2}/\lambda^{2})V_{2}(p_{2})(\hat{v}(p_{1})+\hat{v}(p_{12}))\Big)

\displaystyle\big(\widetilde{h}_{\frac{S_{2}}{\lambda^{2}}}(p_{1})\widetilde{h}_{\frac{S_{2}}{\lambda^{2}}}(p_{2})\widetilde{h}_{\frac{S_{2}}{\lambda^{2}}}(-p_{12})-h_{\frac{S_{2}}{\lambda^{2}}}(p_{1})h_{\frac{S_{2}}{\lambda^{2}}}(p_{2})h_{\frac{S_{2}}{\lambda^{2}}}(-p_{12})\big)\Big]\,,

(5.55)

\displaystyle\operatorname{bol}^{(2)}(h)[J](\mathbf{S}_{2}/\lambda^{2})

\displaystyle:=

\displaystyle\operatorname{bol}^{(2,1)}(h)[J](\mathbf{S}_{2};\lambda)+\operatorname{bol}^{(2,2)}(h)[J](\mathbf{S}_{2}/\lambda^{2})\,,

(5.56)

\displaystyle\operatorname{Bol}_{d}^{(2)}(h)[J](T;\lambda)

\displaystyle:=

\displaystyle\int_{\Delta[T,2]}d\mathbf{S}_{2}\,\operatorname{bol}^{(2)}(h)[J](\mathbf{S}_{2}/\lambda^{2})\,.

5.1.2.
Results

Proposition 5.2
.

Let
T>0
,
\lambda\in(0,1)
,
|\Lambda|\geq 1
, and
J\in L^{\infty}({\mathbb{R}}^{3};{\mathbb{R}})
. Then the following holds.

(1)

(5.57)

\displaystyle-i\int_{0}^{T\lambda^{-2}}\,ds\,\frac{\nu_{0}\big([a_{0},{\mathcal{H}}_{cub}(s)]\big)}{|\Lambda|}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{1}{N^{\frac{1}{2}}\lambda}\operatorname{Con}_{d}(f_{0})(T;\lambda)\,+\,\operatorname{err}^{(Bog)}_{1,d}(\frac{T}{\lambda^{2}};\Phi)\,,

where

(5.58)

\displaystyle|\operatorname{err}^{(Bog)}_{1,d}(\frac{T}{\lambda^{2}};\Phi)|\,\leq\,\frac{C_{\|\hat{v}\|_{w,d},\|f_{0}\|_{d}}T}{N^{\frac{1}{2}}}\,,

(2)

(5.59)

\displaystyle\nu_{0}([f[J],{\mathcal{H}}_{quart}(s)])\,=\,0\,,

(3)

(5.60)

\displaystyle-\int_{\Delta[T\lambda^{-2},2]}\,d\mathbf{s}_{2}\frac{\nu_{0}\big([[f[J],{\mathcal{H}}_{cub}(s_{1})],{\mathcal{H}}_{cub}(s_{2})]\big)}{|\Lambda|}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{1}{N}\big(\operatorname{Bol}_{d}(f_{0})[J](T;\lambda)\,+\,\lambda\operatorname{Bol}_{d}^{(1)}(f_{0})[J](T;\lambda)\,+\,\lambda^{2}\operatorname{Bol}_{d}^{(2)}(f_{0})[J](T;\lambda)\big)

\displaystyle+\,J(0)|\Phi_{\frac{T}{\lambda^{2}}}|^{2}\,+\,\operatorname{err}^{(Bog,\operatorname{Bol})}_{2,d}(\frac{T}{\lambda^{2}};f[J])\,+\,J(0)\operatorname{err}^{(Bog,\operatorname{Con})}_{2,d}(\frac{T}{\lambda^{2}};f)\,,

where

(5.61)

\displaystyle|\operatorname{err}^{(Bog,\operatorname{Bol})}_{2,d}(\frac{T}{\lambda^{2}};f[J])|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C_{\|\hat{v}\|_{w,d},\|f_{0}\|_{d}}\|J\|_{\ell^{\infty}({\Lambda^{*}})}T^{2}\frac{\lambda}{N}\,,

(5.62)

\displaystyle|\operatorname{err}^{(Bog,\operatorname{Con})}_{2,d}(t;f)|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C_{\|\hat{v}\|_{w,d},\|f_{0}\|_{d}}e^{C\|\hat{v}\|_{w,d}|\Lambda|/\lambda T}

\displaystyle\frac{\langle T\rangle^{4}|\Lambda|^{\frac{3}{2}}}{\lambda^{3}N^{\frac{3}{2}}}\Big(1+\frac{|\Lambda|}{N}\Big)^{2}\Big(1+\frac{|\Lambda|^{\frac{3}{2}}}{\lambda N^{\frac{1}{2}}}\Big)\,,

(4)

(5.63)

\displaystyle-i\int_{0}^{T\lambda^{-2}}\,ds\,\frac{\nu_{0}(g[J],{\mathcal{H}}_{quart}(s))}{|\Lambda|}\,=\,\frac{1}{N}\int_{0}^{T}\,dS\,\mathrm{abs}_{quart,d}(f_{0})[J](S/\lambda^{2})\,,

The absorption operator
\mathrm{abs}_{quart,d}(f_{0})[J](S/\lambda^{2})
consists of terms of the form

(5.64)

\displaystyle(-i)^{\ell}\lambda^{j}\int\,dp\,dk\,e^{-i(m_{1}\Omega(p)+m_{2}\Omega(k))S/\lambda^{2}}(1+f(p)+f(-p))J(p)

\displaystyle V_{1}(p)^{2\alpha_{1}}V_{2}(p)^{\alpha_{2}}\hat{v}(p-k)V_{1}(k)^{2\alpha_{3}}V_{2}(k)^{\alpha_{4}}(f_{0}(k)+\iota)\,,

with
\ell\in{\mathbb{N}}_{0}
,
\ell\leq 3
,
j\in{\mathbb{N}}_{0}
,
j\leq 7
,
m_{1},m_{2}\in\{0,\pm 2\}
,
\alpha_{j}\in\{0,1,2\}
and
\iota\in\{0,1\}
. The integrand contains a factor
f_{0}(k)
or a factor
V_{2}(k)
.

(5)

(5.65)

\displaystyle-\int_{\Delta[T\lambda^{-2},2]}\,d\mathbf{s}_{2}\frac{\nu_{0}\big([[g[J],{\mathcal{H}}_{cub}(s_{1})],{\mathcal{H}}_{cub}(s_{2})]\big)}{|\Lambda|}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{1}{N\lambda^{2}}\int_{\Delta[T,2]}\,d\mathbf{S}_{2}\,\big(\operatorname{col}_{d}(f_{0})[J](\mathbf{S}_{2}/\lambda^{2})+\mathrm{abs}_{cub,d}(f_{0})[J](\mathbf{S}_{2}/\lambda^{2})\big)

\displaystyle+\,J(0)\Big(\Phi_{T\lambda^{-2}}\Big)^{2}\,+\,J(0)\operatorname{err}^{(Bog,\operatorname{Con})}_{2}(\frac{T}{\lambda^{2}};g)\,.

Here, the collision operator
\operatorname{col}_{d}(f_{0})[J](\mathbf{s}_{2})
consists of terms of the form

(5.66)

\displaystyle(-i)^{\ell_{0}}\lambda^{j_{0}}\int_{({\Lambda^{*}})^{3}}\,d\mathbf{p}_{3}\,e^{-i\sum_{\ell=1}^{2}s_{\ell}\sum_{k=1}^{3}\sigma_{k,\ell}\Omega(p_{k})}\delta(p_{1}+p_{2}-p_{3})

\displaystyle J(p_{j_{1}})\hat{v}(p_{2})\hat{v}(p_{j_{2}})\prod_{k=1}^{3}V_{1}(p_{k})^{2\alpha_{\ell_{k}}}V_{2}(p_{k})^{\beta_{\ell_{k}}}

\displaystyle\Big(\prod_{k=1}^{3}\big(f_{0}(\tau_{k,1}p_{k})+\frac{1+\tau_{k,2}}{2}\big)\,-\,\prod_{k=1}^{3}\big(f_{0}(-\tau_{k,1}p_{k})+\frac{1-\tau_{k,2}}{2}\big)\Big)\,,

where
\ell_{0}\in{\mathbb{N}}_{0}
,
\ell_{0}\leq 3
,
j_{0}\in{\mathbb{N}}_{0}
,
j_{0}\leq 12
,
\sigma_{k,\ell},\tau_{k,\ell}\in\{\pm 1\}
,
j_{1},j_{2}\in\{1,2,3\}
and
\alpha_{\ell_{k}},\beta_{\ell_{k}}\in\{0,1,2\}
. Any term contains a product of at least two of the functions
\hat{v}
,
f_{0}
,
V_{1}
, and
V_{2}
depending on at least two of the momenta
\{p_{1},p_{2},p_{3}\}
, which implies that the integrand in (
5.66
) exhibits the necessary regularity properties used in Propositions
5.7
and
5.9
below. The absorption operator
\mathrm{abs}_{cub,d}(f_{0})[J](\mathbf{s}_{2})
consists of terms of the form

(5.67)

\displaystyle(-i)^{\ell}\lambda^{j}\int_{({\Lambda^{*}})^{2}}\,dp\,dk\,e^{-is_{1}m_{1}\Omega(p)-is_{2}m_{2}\Omega(k)}J(p)(1+f_{0}(p)+f_{0}(-p))

\displaystyle\hat{v}(p)V_{1}(p)^{2\alpha_{1}}V_{2}(p)^{\alpha_{2}}\hat{v}(k)V_{1}(k)^{2\alpha_{3}}V_{2}(k)^{\alpha_{4}}(f_{0}(k)+\iota)\,,

where
\ell\in{\mathbb{N}}_{0}
,
\ell\leq 3
,
j\in{\mathbb{N}}_{0}
,
j\leq 12
,
m_{1},m_{2}\in\{0,\pm 2\}
,
\alpha_{j}\in\{0,1,2\}
and
\iota\in\{0,1\}
.
|\operatorname{err}^{(Bog,\operatorname{Con})}_{2}(\frac{T}{\lambda^{2}};g)|
satisfies the same bound as
|\operatorname{err}^{(Bog,\operatorname{Con})}_{2}(\frac{T}{\lambda^{2}};f)|
.

Proof.

Set
t=T\lambda^{-2}
. Since here all integrals range over
{\Lambda^{*}}
in the sense of (
1.51
), we will omit the domain of integration in this proof. Using that
\nu_{0}(Q)=0
for
[Q,\mathcal{N}_{b}]\neq 0
, (
5.18
), and recalling
J_{j}
from (
5.19
) and (
5.22
), we start by observing that

(5.68)

\displaystyle-i\int_{0}^{t}\,ds\,\frac{\nu_{0}\big([a_{0},{\mathcal{H}}_{cub}(s)]\big)}{|\Lambda|}\,=\,-i\int_{0}^{t}\,ds\,\big(\int\,dp\,f_{0}(p)J_{1}(s,p)\,+\,\frac{J_{4}(s)}{|\Lambda|}\big)\,.

Here, we also used the fact that translation-invariance implies

Recall that, by Corollary
3.3
,

(5.70)

\displaystyle J_{1}(s,p)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{\lambda\hat{v}(p)}{\sqrt{N}}\big(|u_{\lambda}(s,p)|^{2}\,+\,|v_{\lambda}(s,p)|^{2}\,+\,2\overline{v_{\lambda}}(s,p)\overline{u_{\lambda}}(s,p)\big)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{\lambda\hat{v}(p)}{\sqrt{N}}\Big(1\,+\,2\lambda^{2}\mathrm{Re}\big(e^{-i\Omega(p)s}i\sin(\Omega(p)s)V_{1}(p)\big)

\displaystyle+\,\lambda^{4}\sin^{2}(\Omega(p)s)V_{1}(p)^{4}\,+\,|v_{\lambda}(s,p)|^{2}

\displaystyle+\,2\overline{v_{\lambda}}(s,p)\overline{u_{\lambda}}(s,p)\Big)\,,

(5.71)

\displaystyle J_{4}(s)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{\lambda}{\sqrt{N}}\int\,dp\,\hat{v}(p)\big(|v_{\lambda}(s,p)|^{2}+\overline{u_{\lambda}}(s,p)\overline{v_{\lambda}}(s,p)\big)\,.

Thus, again by Corollary
3.3
, we obtain

(5.72)

\displaystyle\|J_{1}(s)+\frac{\lambda\hat{v}}{\sqrt{N}}\|_{\infty}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C\frac{\lambda\|\hat{v}\|_{\infty}}{\sqrt{N}}\Big(\lambda^{2}\|V_{1}\|_{\infty}^{2}\,+\,\lambda^{4}\|V_{1}\|_{\infty}^{4}\,+\,\|v_{\lambda}(s,\cdot)\|_{\infty}^{2}

\displaystyle+\,\|v_{\lambda}(s,\cdot)\|_{\infty}\|u_{\lambda}(t,\cdot)\|_{\infty}\Big)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C\frac{\lambda^{2}\|\hat{v}\|_{w,d}^{2}(1+\lambda\|\hat{v}\|_{w,d})^{3}}{\sqrt{N}}\,,

(5.73)

\displaystyle|J_{4}(s)|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C\frac{\lambda\|\hat{v}\|_{1}}{\sqrt{N}}\big(\|v_{\lambda}(s,\cdot)\|_{\infty}^{2}\,+\,\|v_{\lambda}(s,\cdot)\|_{\infty}\|u_{\lambda}(t,\cdot)\|_{\infty}\big)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C\frac{\lambda^{2}\|\hat{v}\|_{w,d}^{2}(1+\lambda\|\hat{v}\|_{w,d})^{3}}{\sqrt{N}}

for all
s\geq 0
. Then (
5.68
), Lemma
4.7
, and the definition (
5.57
) of
\operatorname{err}^{(Bog)}_{1,d}(t;\Phi)
yield

(5.74)

\displaystyle|\operatorname{err}^{(Bog)}_{1,d}(t;\Phi)|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C\frac{\lambda^{2}t\|\hat{v}\|_{w,d}^{2}(1+\lambda\|\hat{v}\|_{w,d})^{3}(|\Lambda|^{-1}+\|f_{0}\|_{1})}{N^{\frac{1}{2}}}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{C_{\|\hat{v}\|_{w,d},\|f_{0}\|_{d}}T}{N^{\frac{1}{2}}}\,.

Collecting (
5.68
), (
5.72
), (
5.73
), and (
5.74
), we have proved (
5.57
).

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-i\int_{0}^{t}\,ds\,\frac{\nu_{0}([a_{0},{\mathcal{H}}_{cub}(s)])}{|\Lambda|}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\Phi_{t}\,-\,\operatorname{Rem}_{2}(t;\Phi)\,.

In particular, we have that the condensate term is given by

(5.83)

\displaystyle J(0)|\Phi_{t}|^{2}+J(0)\operatorname{err}^{(Bog,\operatorname{Con})}_{2,d}(t;f)\,,

where

(5.84)

\displaystyle\operatorname{err}^{(Bog,\operatorname{Con})}_{2,d}(t;f)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-2\mathrm{Re}\Big((-i)\int_{0}^{t}\,ds\,\frac{\nu_{0}([a_{0},{\mathcal{H}}_{cub}(s)])}{|\Lambda|}\overline{\operatorname{Rem}_{2}(t;\Phi)}\Big)

\displaystyle-\,|\operatorname{Rem}_{2}(t;\Phi)|^{2}\,.

Using (
5.68
), (
5.72
), and (
5.73
), we have that

(5.85)

\displaystyle\Big|\int_{0}^{t}\,ds\,\frac{\nu_{0}([a_{0},{\mathcal{H}}_{cub}(s)])}{|\Lambda|}\Big|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C_{\|\hat{v}\|_{w,d}}\frac{\lambda t(1+\|f_{0}\|_{d})}{\sqrt{N}}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{C_{\|\hat{v}\|_{w,d},\|f_{0}\|_{d}}T}{N^{\frac{1}{2}}\lambda}

for all
\lambda\in(0,1)
. Thus, Proposition
5.1
and (
5.85
) yield

(5.86)

\displaystyle|\operatorname{err}^{(Bog,\operatorname{Con})}_{2,d}(t;f)|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C_{\|\hat{v}\|_{w,d},\|f_{0}\|_{d}}\Big[\frac{T}{N^{\frac{1}{2}}\lambda}T^{2}e^{C\|\hat{v}\|_{w,d}|\Lambda|/\lambda T}\frac{|\Lambda|^{\frac{3}{2}}}{N\lambda^{2}}\Big(1+\frac{|\Lambda|}{N}\Big)

\displaystyle+\,T^{4}e^{C\|\hat{v}\|_{w,d}|\Lambda|/\lambda T}\frac{|\Lambda|^{3}}{N^{2}\lambda^{4}}\Big(1+\frac{|\Lambda|}{N}\Big)^{2}\Big]

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C_{\|\hat{v}\|_{w,d},\|f_{0}\|_{d}}e^{C\|\hat{v}\|_{w,d}|\Lambda|/\lambda T}\frac{\langle T\rangle^{4}|\Lambda|^{\frac{3}{2}}}{\lambda^{3}N^{\frac{3}{2}}}\Big(1+\frac{|\Lambda|}{N}\Big)^{2}\Big(1+\frac{|\Lambda|^{\frac{3}{2}}}{\lambda N^{\frac{1}{2}}}\Big)\,.

Next, we compute the Boltzmann term for
f
. Observe that with the decomposition (
5.27
), we have that

(5.87)

\displaystyle{\mathcal{H}}_{cub}(t)\,=\,{\mathcal{H}}_{cub}^{(1)}(t)\,+\,{\mathcal{H}}_{cub}^{(2)}(t)\,.

Notice that
{\mathcal{H}}_{cub}^{(1)}(t)
and
{\mathcal{H}}_{cub}^{(2)}(t)
are formal adjoints. In particular, we have that

(5.88)

\displaystyle\nu_{0}\big([[f[J],{\mathcal{H}}_{cub}(s_{1})],{\mathcal{H}}_{cub}(s_{2})]\big)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle 2\mathrm{Re}\nu_{0}\big([[f[J],{\mathcal{H}}_{cub}^{(1)}(s_{1})],{\mathcal{H}}_{cub}^{(1)}(s_{2})]\big)

\displaystyle+\,2\mathrm{Re}\nu_{0}\big([[f[J],{\mathcal{H}}_{cub}^{(1)}(s_{1})],{\mathcal{H}}_{cub}^{(2)}(s_{2})]\big)

With that, we sort the Boltzmann contractions of
\nu_{0}\big([[f[J],{\mathcal{H}}_{cub}(s_{1})],{\mathcal{H}}_{cub}(s_{2})]\big)
by powers of
\lambda
, i.e.,

(5.89)

\displaystyle-\frac{1}{|\Lambda|}[[\hbox to5.97pt{\vbox to8.89pt{\pgfpicture\makeatletter\hbox{\hskip 2.98613pt\lower-4.44444pt\hbox to0.0pt{\lxSVG@begingroup@{_scopebegin} \lxSVG@begingroup@{stroke} \lxSVG@begingroup@{fill} \lxSVG@setlinewidth{\the\pgflinewidth}\lxSVG@begingroup@{stroke-width} \lx@inpgf@ignorespaces\nullfont\hbox to0.0pt{\lxSVG@begingroup@{_scopebegin}
