\lxSVG@closescope \hbox to0.0pt{}{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}\hss}\lxSVG@discardpath\lxSVG@closescope \hss}}\lxSVG@closescope\endpgfpicture}}_{k_{1}}]\vbox to19.00002pt{}{}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-2\mathrm{Re}\int d\mathbf{p}_{2}e^{i\Delta_{cub}\Omega(\mathbf{p}_{2})(s_{1}-s_{2})}\hat{v}(p_{2})

\displaystyle(\hat{v}(p_{1})+\hat{v}(p_{2}))\Delta_{cub}J(\mathbf{p}_{2})

\displaystyle\big(f_{0}(p_{1})f_{0}(p_{2})\widetilde{f_{0}}(p_{12})-\widetilde{f_{0}}(p_{1})\widetilde{f_{0}}(p_{2})f_{0}(p_{12})\big)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\mathrm{Re}\int d\mathbf{p}_{2}e^{i\Delta_{cub}\Omega(\mathbf{p}_{2})(s_{1}-s_{2})}(\hat{v}(p_{1})+\hat{v}(p_{2}))^{2}\Delta_{cub}J(\mathbf{p}_{2})

\displaystyle\big(\widetilde{f_{0}}(p_{1})\widetilde{f_{0}}(p_{2})f_{0}(p_{12})-f_{0}(p_{1})f_{0}(p_{2})\widetilde{f_{0}}(p_{12})\big)\,.

Here, we used (
) followed by symmetry
p_{1}\leftrightarrow p_{2}
.

Recalling Corollary
3.3
,
\operatorname{bol}^{(1)}(f_{0})[J](s_{1},s_{2})
is given by the Boltzmann contractions of

(5.92)

\displaystyle-\frac{2}{|\Lambda|}\mathrm{Re}\,i\int\,d\mathbf{p}_{2}d\mathbf{k}_{2}\,\hat{v}(p_{2})\hat{v}(k_{2})e^{i{\Delta_{cub}\Omega}(\mathbf{p}_{2})s_{1}+i{\Delta_{cub}\Omega}(\mathbf{k}_{2})s_{2}}

\displaystyle\Big(e^{-i\Omega(p_{1})s_{1}}\sin(\Omega(p_{1})s_{1})V_{2}(p_{1})\nu_{0}\big([[f[J],a_{-p_{1}}a_{p_{2}}^{+}a_{p_{12}}],a_{k_{1}}^{+}a_{k_{2}}^{+}a_{k_{12}}]\big)

\displaystyle+\,e^{-i\Omega(p_{2})s_{1}}\sin(\Omega(p_{2})s_{1})V_{2}(p_{2})\nu_{0}\big([[f[J],a_{p_{1}}^{+}a_{-p_{2}}a_{p_{12}}],a_{k_{1}}^{+}a_{k_{2}}^{+}a_{k_{12}}]\big)

\displaystyle+\,e^{-i\Omega(k_{1})s_{2}}\sin(\Omega(k_{1})s_{2})V_{2}(k_{1})\nu_{0}\big([[f[J],a_{p_{1}}^{+}a_{p_{2}}^{+}a_{p_{12}}],a_{-k_{1}}a_{k_{2}}^{+}a_{k_{12}}]\big)

\displaystyle+\,e^{-i\Omega(k_{2})s_{2}}\sin(\Omega(k_{2})s_{2})V_{2}(k_{2})\nu_{0}\big([[f[J],a_{p_{1}}^{+}a_{p_{2}}^{+}a_{p_{12}}],a_{k_{1}}^{+}a_{-k_{2}}a_{k_{12}}]\big)\Big)

Using (
5.90
), obtain, similarly to above,

(5.93)

\displaystyle 2\mathrm{Im}\int\,d\mathbf{p}_{2}\,\hat{v}(p_{2})\Big[\big(-J(-p_{1})+J(p_{2})-J(p_{12})\big)

\displaystyle(\hat{v}(p_{1})+\hat{v}(p_{12}))e^{i(\Omega(p_{2})-\Omega(p_{12}))(s_{1}-s_{2})+i\Omega(p_{1})s_{2}}\sin(\Omega(p_{1})s_{1})V_{2}(p_{1})

\displaystyle\big(\widetilde{f_{0}}(-p_{1})f_{0}(p_{2})\widetilde{f_{0}}(p_{12})-f_{0}(-p_{1})\widetilde{f_{0}}(p_{2})f_{0}(p_{12})\big)

\displaystyle+\,\big(J(p_{1})-J(-p_{2})-J(p_{12})\big)(\hat{v}(p_{2})+\hat{v}(p_{12}))

\displaystyle e^{i(\Omega(p_{1})-\Omega(p_{12}))(s_{1}-s_{2})+i\Omega(p_{2})s_{2}}\sin(\Omega(p_{2})s_{1})V_{2}(p_{2})

\displaystyle\big(f_{0}(p_{1})\widetilde{f_{0}}(-p_{2})\widetilde{f_{0}}(p_{12})-\widetilde{f_{0}}(p_{1})f_{0}(-p_{2})f_{0}(p_{12})\big)

\displaystyle+\,\Big(e^{i(\Omega(p_{1})-\Omega(p_{12}))(s_{1}-s_{2})+i\Omega(p_{2})s_{1}}\sin(\Omega(p_{2})s_{2})V_{2}(p_{2})(\hat{v}(p_{2})+\hat{v}(p_{12}))

\displaystyle+\,e^{i(\Omega(p_{2})-\Omega(p_{12}))(s_{1}-s_{2})+i\Omega(p_{1})s_{1}}\sin(\Omega(p_{1})s_{2})V_{2}(p_{1})(\hat{v}(p_{1})+\hat{v}(p_{12}))\Big)

\displaystyle{\Delta_{cub}J}(\mathbf{p}_{2})\big(f_{0}(p_{1})f_{0}(p_{2})\widetilde{f_{0}}(p_{12})-\widetilde{f_{0}}(p_{1})\widetilde{f_{0}}(p_{2})f_{0}(p_{12})\big)\Big]\,.

Here, we used the fact that
\hat{v}
, and thus
\Omega
, are even functions. Using symmetry in
p_{1}\leftrightarrow p_{2}
, we can further simplify this expression to

(5.94)

\displaystyle\operatorname{bol}^{(1)}(f_{0})[J](\mathbf{s}_{2})

\displaystyle:=

\displaystyle 2\mathrm{Im}\int\,d\mathbf{p}_{2}\,(\hat{v}(p_{1})+\hat{v}(p_{2}))(\hat{v}(p_{1})+\hat{v}(p_{12}))

\displaystyle V_{2}(p_{1})e^{i(\Omega(p_{2})-\Omega(p_{12}))(s_{1}-s_{2})}

\displaystyle\Big(\big(-J(-p_{1})+J(p_{2})-J(p_{12})\big)e^{i\Omega(p_{1})s_{2}}\sin(\Omega(p_{1})s_{1})

\displaystyle\big(\widetilde{f_{0}}(-p_{1})f_{0}(p_{2})\widetilde{f_{0}}(p_{12})-f_{0}(-p_{1})\widetilde{f_{0}}(p_{2})f_{0}(p_{12})\big)

\displaystyle+\,{\Delta_{cub}J}(\mathbf{p}_{2})e^{i\Omega(p_{1})s_{1}}\sin(\Omega(p_{1})s_{2})

\displaystyle\big(f_{0}(p_{1})f_{0}(p_{2})\widetilde{f_{0}}(p_{12})-\widetilde{f_{0}}(p_{1})\widetilde{f_{0}}(p_{2})f_{0}(p_{12})\big)\Big)\,.

Recalling (
5.52
), we have that

(5.95)

\displaystyle\int_{\Delta[T\lambda^{-2},2]}d\mathbf{s}_{2}\,\operatorname{bol}^{(1)}(f_{0})[J](\mathbf{s}_{2})\,=\,\operatorname{Bol}_{d}^{(1)}(f_{0})[J](T;\lambda)\,.

Next, we compute the corrections with coefficient
\frac{\lambda^{4}}{N}
. These come either from a correction to the same momentum,
a_{p}^{+}\to i\lambda^{2}V_{1}(p)^{2}\sin(\Omega(p)t)a_{p}^{+}
, or from two momentum flips,
a_{p}^{+}\to i\lambda V_{2}(p)\sin(\Omega(p)t)a_{-p}
. In the first case, computations analogous to (
5.91
) yield

(5.96)

\displaystyle-2\mathrm{Re}\,i\int\,d\mathbf{p}_{2}\,\hat{v}(p_{2})(\hat{v}(p_{1})+\hat{v}(p_{2})){\Delta_{cub}J}(\mathbf{p}_{2})e^{i{\Delta_{cub}\Omega}(\mathbf{p}_{2})(s_{1}-s_{2})}

\displaystyle\Big(V_{1}(p_{1})^{2}\big(\sin(\Omega(p_{1})s_{1})e^{-i\Omega(p_{1})s_{1}}-\sin(\Omega(p_{1})s_{2})e^{i\Omega(p_{1})s_{2}}\big)

\displaystyle+\,V_{1}(p_{2})^{2}\big(\sin(\Omega(p_{2})s_{1})e^{-i\Omega(p_{2})s_{1}}-\sin(\Omega(p_{2})s_{2})e^{i\Omega(p_{2})s_{2}}\big)

\displaystyle-\,V_{1}(p_{12})^{2}\big(\sin(\Omega(p_{12})s_{1})e^{i\Omega(p_{12})s_{1}}-\sin(\Omega(p_{12})s_{2})e^{i\Omega(p_{12})s_{2}}\big)\Big)

\displaystyle\big(f_{0}(p_{1})f_{0}(p_{2})\widetilde{f_{0}}(p_{12})-\widetilde{f_{0}}(p_{1})\widetilde{f_{0}}(p_{2})f_{0}(p_{12})\big)\,.

Using symmetry in
p_{1}\leftrightarrow p_{2}
again, we obtain

(5.97)

\displaystyle\operatorname{bol}^{(2)}_{1}(f_{0})[J](\mathbf{s}_{2})

\displaystyle:=

\displaystyle\mathrm{Im}\int\,d\mathbf{p}_{2}\,(\hat{v}(p_{1})+\hat{v}(p_{2}))^{2}{\Delta_{cub}J}(\mathbf{p}_{2})e^{i{\Delta_{cub}\Omega}(\mathbf{p}_{2})(s_{1}-s_{2})}

\displaystyle\Big(V_{1}(p_{1})^{2}\big(\sin(\Omega(p_{1})s_{1})e^{-i\Omega(p_{1})s_{1}}-\sin(\Omega(p_{1})s_{2})e^{i\Omega(p_{1})s_{2}}\big)

\displaystyle+\,V_{1}(p_{2})^{2}\big(\sin(\Omega(p_{2})s_{1})e^{-i\Omega(p_{2})s_{1}}-\sin(\Omega(p_{2})s_{2})e^{i\Omega(p_{2})s_{2}}\big)

\displaystyle-\,V_{1}(p_{12})^{2}\big(\sin(\Omega(p_{12})s_{1})e^{i\Omega(p_{12})s_{1}}-\sin(\Omega(p_{12})s_{2})e^{i\Omega(p_{12})s_{2}}\big)\Big)

\displaystyle\big(f_{0}(p_{1})f_{0}(p_{2})\widetilde{f_{0}}(p_{12})-\widetilde{f_{0}}(p_{1})\widetilde{f_{0}}(p_{2})f_{0}(p_{12})\big)\,.

In the case of two momentum flips, the correction terms are given by the Boltzmann contractions of

\displaystyle-\frac{2}{|\Lambda|}\mathrm{Re}\int\,d\mathbf{p}_{2}d\mathbf{k}_{2}\,\hat{v}(p_{2})\hat{v}(k_{2})e^{i{\Delta_{cub}\Omega}(\mathbf{p}_{2})s_{1}-i{\Delta_{cub}\Omega}(\mathbf{k}_{2})s_{2}}\,

\displaystyle\Big(e^{-i(\Omega(p_{1})-\Omega(p_{12}))s_{1}}i\sin(\Omega(p_{1})s_{1})(-i)\sin(\Omega(p_{12})s_{1})V_{2}(p_{1})V_{2}(p_{12})

\displaystyle(-J(-p_{1})+J(p_{2})+J(-p_{12}))\nu_{0}([a_{-p_{1}}a_{p_{2}}^{+}a_{-p_{12}}^{+},a_{k_{12}}^{+}a_{k_{2}}a_{k_{1}}])

\displaystyle+\,e^{-i(\Omega(p_{2})-\Omega(p_{12}))s_{1}}i\sin(\Omega(p_{2})s_{1})(-i)\sin(\Omega(p_{12})s_{1})V_{2}(p_{2})V_{2}(p_{12})

\displaystyle(J(p_{1})-J(-p_{2})+J(-p_{12}))\nu_{0}([a_{p_{1}}^{+}a_{-p_{2}}a_{-p_{12}}^{+},a_{k_{12}}^{+}a_{k_{2}}a_{k_{1}}])

\displaystyle+\,e^{-i\Omega(p_{1})s_{1}+i\Omega(k_{2})s_{2}}i\sin(\Omega(p_{1})s_{1})(-i)\sin(\Omega(k_{2})s_{2})V_{2}(p_{1})V_{2}(k_{2})

\displaystyle(-J(-p_{1})+J(p_{2})-J(p_{12}))\nu_{0}([a_{-p_{1}}a_{p_{2}}^{+}a_{p_{12}},a_{k_{12}}^{+}a_{-k_{2}}^{+}a_{k_{1}}])

\displaystyle+\,e^{-i\Omega(p_{2})s_{1}+i\Omega(k_{2})s_{2}}i\sin(\Omega(p_{2})s_{1})(-i)\sin(\Omega(k_{2})s_{2})V_{2}(p_{2})V_{2}(k_{2})

\displaystyle(J(p_{1})-J(-p_{2})-J(p_{12}))\nu_{0}([a_{p_{1}}^{+}a_{-p_{2}}a_{p_{12}},a_{k_{12}}^{+}a_{-k_{2}}^{+}a_{k_{1}}])

\displaystyle+\,e^{-i\Omega(p_{1})s_{1}+i\Omega(k_{1})s_{2}}i\sin(\Omega(p_{1})s_{1})(-i)\sin(\Omega(k_{1})s_{2})V_{2}(p_{1})V_{2}(k_{1})

\displaystyle(-J(-p_{1})+J(p_{2})-J(p_{12}))\nu_{0}([a_{-p_{1}}a_{p_{2}}^{+}a_{p_{12}},a_{k_{12}}^{+}a_{k_{2}}a_{-k_{1}}^{+}])

\displaystyle+\,e^{-i\Omega(p_{2})s_{1}+i\Omega(k_{1})s_{2}}i\sin(\Omega(p_{2})s_{1})(-i)\sin(\Omega(k_{1})s_{2})V_{2}(p_{2})V_{2}(k_{1})

\displaystyle(J(p_{1})-J(-p_{2})-J(p_{12}))\nu_{0}([a_{p_{1}}^{+}a_{-p_{2}}a_{p_{12}},a_{k_{12}}^{+}a_{k_{2}}a_{-k_{1}}^{+}])

\displaystyle+\,e^{i\Omega(p_{12})s_{1}-i\Omega(k_{12})s_{2}}(-i)\sin(\Omega(p_{12})s_{1})i\sin(\Omega(k_{12})s_{2})V_{2}(p_{12})V_{2}(k_{12})

(5.98)

\displaystyle(J(p_{1})+J(p_{2})+J(-p_{12}))\nu_{0}([a_{p_{1}}^{+}a_{p_{2}}^{+}a_{-p_{12}}^{+},a_{-k_{12}}a_{k_{2}}a_{k_{1}}])\Big)\,.

This expression equals

(5.99)

\displaystyle-2\mathrm{Re}\int\,d\mathbf{p}_{2}\,\hat{v}(p_{2})\Big[\sin(\Omega(p_{1})s_{1})\sin(\Omega(p_{12})s_{1})e^{i\Omega(p_{2})(s_{1}-s_{2})+i(\Omega(p_{1})-\Omega(p_{12}))s_{2}}

\displaystyle V_{2}(p_{1})V_{2}(p_{12})(\hat{v}(p_{2})+\hat{v}(p_{12}))(-J(-p_{1})+J(p_{2})+J(-p_{12}))

\displaystyle\big(\widetilde{f_{0}}(-p_{1})f_{0}(p_{2})f(-p_{12})-f_{0}(-p_{1})\widetilde{f_{0}}(p_{2})\widetilde{f_{0}}(-p_{12})\big)

\displaystyle+\,\sin(\Omega(p_{2})s_{1})\sin(\Omega(p_{12})s_{1})e^{i\Omega(p_{1})(s_{1}-s_{2})+i(\Omega(p_{2})-\Omega(p_{12}))s_{2}}

\displaystyle V_{2}(p_{2})V_{2}(p_{12})(\hat{v}(p_{1})+\hat{v}(p_{12}))(J(p_{1})-J(-p_{2})+J(-p_{12}))

\displaystyle\big(f_{0}(p_{1})\widetilde{f_{0}}(-p_{2})f(-p_{12})-\widetilde{f_{0}}(p_{1})f_{0}(-p_{2})\widetilde{f_{0}}(-p_{12})\big)

\displaystyle+\,\sin(\Omega(p_{1})s_{1})e^{i\Omega(p_{2})(s_{1}-s_{2})-i\Omega(p_{12})s_{1}}V_{2}(p_{1})(-J(-p_{1})+J(p_{2})-J(p_{12}))

\displaystyle\big(\hat{v}(p_{1})V_{2}(p_{1})\sin(\Omega(p_{1})s_{2})e^{i\Omega(p_{12})s_{2}}+\hat{v}(p_{12})V_{2}(p_{12})\sin(\Omega(p_{12})s_{2})e^{-i\Omega(p_{1})s_{2}}\big)

\displaystyle\big(\widetilde{f_{0}}(-p_{1})f_{0}(p_{2})\widetilde{f_{0}}(p_{12})-f_{0}(-p_{1})\widetilde{f_{0}}(p_{2})f_{0}(p_{12})\big)

\displaystyle+\,\sin(\Omega(p_{2})s_{1})e^{i\Omega(p_{1})(s_{1}-s_{2})-i\Omega(p_{12})s_{1}}V_{2}(p_{2})(J(p_{1})-J(-p_{2})-J(p_{12}))

\displaystyle\big(\hat{v}(p_{2})V_{2}(p_{2})\sin(\Omega(p_{2})s_{2})e^{i\Omega(p_{12})s_{2}}+\hat{v}(p_{12})V_{2}(p_{12})\sin(\Omega(p_{12})s_{2})e^{-i\Omega(p_{2})s_{2}}\big)

\displaystyle\big(f_{0}(p_{1})\widetilde{f_{0}}(-p_{2})\widetilde{f_{0}}(p_{12})-\widetilde{f_{0}}(p_{1})f_{0}(-p_{2})f_{0}(p_{12})\big)

\displaystyle+\,\sin(\Omega(p_{1})s_{1})e^{i\Omega(p_{2})(s_{1}-s_{2})-i\Omega(p_{12})s_{1}}V_{2}(p_{1})\hat{v}(p_{2})(-J(-p_{1})+J(p_{2})-J(p_{12}))

\displaystyle\big(V_{2}(p_{1})\sin(\Omega(p_{1})s_{2})e^{i\Omega(p_{12})s_{2}}+V_{2}(p_{12})\sin(\Omega(p_{12})s_{2})e^{i\Omega(p_{1})s_{2}}\big)

\displaystyle\big(\widetilde{f_{0}}(-p_{1})f_{0}(p_{2})\widetilde{f_{0}}(p_{12})-f_{0}(-p_{1})\widetilde{f_{0}}(p_{2})f_{0}(p_{12})\big)

\displaystyle+\,\sin(\Omega(p_{2})s_{1})e^{i\Omega(p_{1})(s_{1}-s_{2})-i\Omega(p_{12})s_{1}}V_{2}(p_{2})\hat{v}(p_{1})(J(p_{1})-J(-p_{2})-J(p_{12}))

\displaystyle\big(V_{2}(p_{2})\sin(\Omega(p_{2})s_{2})e^{i\Omega(p_{12})s_{2}}+V_{2}(p_{12})\sin(\Omega(p_{12})s_{2})e^{i\Omega(p_{2})s_{2}}\big)

\displaystyle\big(f_{0}(p_{1})\widetilde{f_{0}}(-p_{2})\widetilde{f_{0}}(p_{12})-\widetilde{f_{0}}(p_{1})f_{0}(-p_{2})f_{0}(p_{12})\big)

\displaystyle+V_{2}(p_{2})e^{i(\Omega(p_{1})+\Omega(p_{2}))s_{1}}\sin(\Omega(p_{12})s_{1})\big(J(p_{1})+J(p_{2})+J(-p_{12})\big)

\displaystyle+\,\Big(e^{-i(\Omega(p_{1})+\Omega(p_{2}))s_{2}}\sin(\Omega(p_{12})s_{2})V_{2}(p_{12})(\hat{v}(p_{1})+\hat{v}(p_{2}))

\displaystyle+\,e^{-i(\Omega(p_{1})+\Omega(p_{12}))s_{2}}\sin(\Omega(p_{2})s_{2})V_{2}(p_{2})(\hat{v}(p_{1})+\hat{v}(p_{12}))

\displaystyle+\,\sin(\Omega(p_{1})s_{2})e^{-i(\Omega(p_{2})+\Omega(p_{12}))s_{2}}V_{2}(p_{1})(\hat{v}(p_{2})+\hat{v}(p_{12}))\Big)

\displaystyle\big(f_{0}(p_{1})f_{0}(p_{2})f_{0}(-p_{12})-\widetilde{f_{0}}(p_{1})\widetilde{f_{0}}(p_{2})\widetilde{f_{0}}(-p_{12})\big)\Big]\,.

Using symmetry in
p_{1}\leftrightarrow p_{2}
, this can be reduced to

(5.100)

\displaystyle-2\mathrm{Re}\int\,d\mathbf{p}_{2}\,(\hat{v}(p_{1})+\hat{v}(p_{2}))

\displaystyle\Big[\sin(\Omega(p_{1})s_{1})\sin(\Omega(p_{12})s_{1})e^{i\Omega(p_{2})(s_{1}-s_{2})+i(\Omega(p_{1})-\Omega(p_{12}))s_{2}}

\displaystyle V_{2}(p_{1})V_{2}(p_{12})(\hat{v}(p_{2})+\hat{v}(p_{12}))(-J(-p_{1})+J(p_{2})+J(-p_{12}))

\displaystyle\big(\widetilde{f_{0}}(-p_{1})f_{0}(p_{2})f(-p_{12})-f_{0}(-p_{1})\widetilde{f_{0}}(p_{2})\widetilde{f_{0}}(-p_{12})\big)

\displaystyle+\,\sin(\Omega(p_{1})s_{1})e^{i\Omega(p_{2})(s_{1}-s_{2})-i\Omega(p_{12})s_{1}}V_{2}(p_{1})(-J(-p_{1})+J(p_{2})-J(p_{12}))

\displaystyle\big(\hat{v}(p_{1})V_{2}(p_{1})\sin(\Omega(p_{1})s_{2})e^{i\Omega(p_{12})s_{2}}+\hat{v}(p_{12})V_{2}(p_{12})\sin(\Omega(p_{12})s_{2})e^{-i\Omega(p_{1})s_{2}}\big)

\displaystyle\big(\widetilde{f_{0}}(-p_{1})f_{0}(p_{2})\widetilde{f_{0}}(p_{12})-f_{0}(-p_{1})\widetilde{f_{0}}(p_{2})f_{0}(p_{12})\big)

\displaystyle+\,\sin(\Omega(p_{1})s_{1})e^{i\Omega(p_{2})(s_{1}-s_{2})-i\Omega(p_{12})s_{1}}V_{2}(p_{1})\hat{v}(p_{2})(-J(-p_{1})+J(p_{2})-J(p_{12}))

\displaystyle\big(V_{2}(p_{1})\sin(\Omega(p_{1})s_{2})e^{i\Omega(p_{12})s_{2}}+V_{2}(p_{12})\sin(\Omega(p_{12})s_{2})e^{i\Omega(p_{1})s_{2}}\big)

\displaystyle\big(\widetilde{f_{0}}(-p_{1})f_{0}(p_{2})\widetilde{f_{0}}(p_{12})-f_{0}(-p_{1})\widetilde{f_{0}}(p_{2})f_{0}(p_{12})\big)

\displaystyle\Big(\frac{1}{2}e^{-i(\Omega(p_{1})-\Omega(p_{2}))s_{2}}\sin(\Omega(p_{12})s_{2})V_{2}(p_{12})(\hat{v}(p_{1})+\hat{v}(p_{2}))

\displaystyle+\,e^{-i(\Omega(p_{1})-\Omega(p_{12}))s_{2}}\sin(\Omega(p_{2})s_{2})V_{2}(p_{2})(\hat{v}(p_{1})+\hat{v}(p_{12}))\Big)

\displaystyle\big(f_{0}(p_{1})f_{0}(p_{2})f_{0}(-p_{12})-\widetilde{f_{0}}(p_{1})\widetilde{f_{0}}(p_{2})\widetilde{f_{0}}(-p_{12})\big)\Big]\,.

This can be further be simplified to

(5.101)

\displaystyle\operatorname{bol}^{(2)}_{2}(f_{0})[J](\mathbf{s}_{2})

\displaystyle:=

\displaystyle 2\mathrm{Re}\int\,d\mathbf{p}_{2}\,(\hat{v}(p_{1})+\hat{v}(p_{2}))

\displaystyle\Big[\sin(\Omega(p_{1})s_{1})\sin(\Omega(p_{12})s_{1})e^{i\Omega(p_{2})(s_{1}-s_{2})+i(\Omega(p_{1})-\Omega(p_{12}))s_{2}}

\displaystyle V_{2}(p_{1})V_{2}(p_{12})(\hat{v}(p_{2})+\hat{v}(p_{12}))(-J(-p_{1})+J(p_{2})+J(-p_{12}))

\displaystyle\big(f_{0}(-p_{1})\widetilde{f_{0}}(p_{2})\widetilde{f_{0}}(-p_{12})-\widetilde{f_{0}}(-p_{1})f_{0}(p_{2})f(-p_{12})\big)

\displaystyle+\,\sin(\Omega(p_{1})s_{1})e^{i\Omega(p_{2})(s_{1}-s_{2})-i\Omega(p_{12})s_{1}}V_{2}(p_{1})

\displaystyle(-J(-p_{1})+J(p_{2})-J(p_{12}))

\displaystyle\Big((\hat{v}(p_{1})+\hat{v}(p_{2}))V_{2}(p_{1})\sin(\Omega(p_{1})s_{2})e^{-i\Omega(p_{12})s_{2}}

\displaystyle+\,(\hat{v}(p_{2})+\hat{v}(p_{12}))V_{2}(p_{12})\sin(\Omega(p_{12})s_{2})e^{-i\Omega(p_{1})s_{2}}\Big)

\displaystyle\big(f_{0}(-p_{1})\widetilde{f_{0}}(p_{2})f_{0}(p_{12})-\widetilde{f_{0}}(-p_{1})f_{0}(p_{2})\widetilde{f_{0}}(p_{12})\big)

\displaystyle\Big(\frac{1}{2}e^{-i(\Omega(p_{1})+\Omega(p_{2}))s_{2}}\sin(\Omega(p_{12})s_{2})V_{2}(p_{12})(\hat{v}(p_{1})+\hat{v}(p_{2}))

\displaystyle+\,e^{-i(\Omega(p_{1})+\Omega(p_{12}))s_{2}}\sin(\Omega(p_{2})s_{2})V_{2}(p_{2})(\hat{v}(p_{1})+\hat{v}(p_{12}))\Big)

\displaystyle\big(\widetilde{f_{0}}(p_{1})\widetilde{f_{0}}(p_{2})\widetilde{f_{0}}(-p_{12})-f_{0}(p_{1})f_{0}(p_{2})f_{0}(-p_{12})\big)\Big]\,.

In addition, let

(5.102)

\displaystyle\operatorname{bol}^{(2)}(f_{0})[J](\mathbf{s}_{2})\,:=\,\operatorname{bol}^{(2)}_{1}(f_{0})[J](\mathbf{s}_{2})\,+\,\operatorname{bol}^{(2)}_{2}(f_{0})[J](\mathbf{s}_{2})\,.

Recalling definitions (
5.53
), (
5.54
), and (
5.56
), we obtain that

(5.103)

\displaystyle\int_{\Delta[T\lambda^{-2},2]}d\mathbf{s}_{2}\,\operatorname{bol}^{(2)}(f_{0})[J](\mathbf{s}_{2})\,=\,\operatorname{Bol}_{d}^{(2)}(f_{0})[J](T;\lambda)\,.

In order to bound
\operatorname{bol}^{(3)}(f_{0})[J](\mathbf{s}_{2})
, we need to, first, look at integrability of the terms. Notice that, for Boltzmann contractions, the order of the creation/annihilation operators within a single argument of a commutator do not matter. In particular, we are interested in evaluating expressions involving the Boltzmann contractions of

(5.104)

\displaystyle[a_{\sigma_{1}p_{1}}^{(\sigma_{1})}a_{\sigma_{2}p_{2}}^{(\sigma_{2})}a_{\sigma_{3}p_{12}}^{(-\sigma_{3})},a_{\tau_{1}k_{1}}^{(\tau_{1})}a_{\tau_{2}k_{2}}^{(\tau_{2})}a_{\tau_{3}k_{12}}^{(-\tau_{3})}]\,,

which contain a factor

(5.105)

\displaystyle\big(f_{0}(\sigma_{1}p_{1})+\frac{1-\sigma_{1}}{2}\big)\big(f_{0}(\sigma_{2}p_{2})+\frac{1-\sigma_{2}}{2}\big)\big(f_{0}(\sigma_{3}p_{12})+\frac{1+\sigma_{3}}{2}\big)

\displaystyle-\big(f_{0}(\sigma_{1}p_{1})+\frac{1+\sigma_{1}}{2}\big)\big(f_{0}(\sigma_{2}p_{2})+\frac{1+\sigma_{2}}{2}\big)\big(f_{0}(\sigma_{3}p_{12})+\frac{1-\sigma_{3}}{2}\big)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\sigma_{3}f_{0}(\sigma_{1}p_{1})f_{0}(\sigma_{2}p_{2})\,-\,\sigma_{1}f_{0}(\sigma_{2}p_{2})f_{0}(\sigma_{3}p_{12})\,-\,\sigma_{2}f_{0}(\sigma_{1}p_{1})f_{0}(\sigma_{3}p_{12})

\displaystyle+\,(\sigma_{3}-\sigma_{2})f_{0}(\sigma_{1}p_{1})\,+\,(\sigma_{3}-\sigma_{1})f_{0}(\sigma_{2}p_{2})\,-\,(\sigma_{1}+\sigma_{2})f_{0}(\sigma_{3}p_{12})\,.

Observe that there is a global coefficient
\hat{v}(p_{2})
. After evaluating all
\delta
coming from contractions between
p
and
k
momenta, we want to verify integrability w.r.t.
dp_{1}\,dp_{2}
. We are thus left with verifying integrability for the terms involving
(\sigma_{3}-\sigma_{1})f_{0}(\sigma_{2}p_{2})
. This term occurs only if
\sigma_{3}=-\sigma_{1}
. Another global factor then is

(5.106)

\displaystyle V_{2}(p_{1})^{\frac{1-\sigma_{1}}{2}}V_{2}(p_{12})^{\frac{1-\sigma_{3}}{2}}=V_{2}(p_{1})^{\frac{1-\sigma_{1}}{2}}V_{2}(p_{12})^{\frac{1+\sigma_{1}}{2}}\,,

where we recall from (
5.88
) that we only need to consider
{\mathcal{H}}_{cub}^{(1)}(s_{1})
in the first argument of the commutator. In particular, there is an integrable factor w.r.t.
dp_{1}
. With that, we have the estimate

(5.107)

\displaystyle|\operatorname{bol}^{(3)}(f_{0})[J](\mathbf{s}_{2})|\,\leq\,C_{\|\hat{v}\|_{w,d},\|f_{0}\|_{d}}\|J\|_{\ell^{\infty}({\Lambda^{*}})}\,.

As a consequence, we have that

(5.108)

\displaystyle|\operatorname{err}^{(Bog,\operatorname{Bol})}_{2,d}(t;f[J])|

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\Big|\frac{\lambda^{5}}{N}\int_{\Delta[t,2]}\,d\mathbf{s}_{2}\,\operatorname{bol}^{(3)}(f_{0})[J](\mathbf{s}_{2})\Big|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C_{\|\hat{v}\|_{w,d},\|f_{0}\|_{d}}\|J\|_{\ell^{\infty}({\Lambda^{*}})}\frac{\lambda^{5}t^{2}}{N}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C_{\|\hat{v}\|_{w,d},\|f_{0}\|_{d}}\|J\|_{\ell^{\infty}({\Lambda^{*}})}T^{2}\frac{\lambda}{N}\,.

This concludes the proof of (
5.60
).

Finally, we compute the dynamics of
g
. We write

(5.109)

\displaystyle-i\int_{0}^{t}\,\frac{ds}{|\Lambda|}[\hbox to5.13pt{\vbox to6.25pt{\pgfpicture\makeatletter\hbox{\>\lower-3.12498pt\hbox to0.0pt{\lxSVG@begingroup@{_scopebegin} \lxSVG@begingroup@{stroke} \lxSVG@begingroup@{fill} \lxSVG@setlinewidth{\the\pgflinewidth}\lxSVG@begingroup@{stroke-width} \lx@inpgf@ignorespaces\nullfont\hbox to0.0pt{\lxSVG@begingroup@{_scopebegin}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{\lambda^{2}}{N}\int_{0}^{t}\,ds\,\mathrm{abs}_{quart,d}(f_{0})[J](s)\,,

where
\mathrm{abs}_{quart,d}(f_{0})[J](s)
consists of terms of the form

(5.110)

\displaystyle(-i)^{\ell}\lambda^{j}\int\,dp\,dk\,e^{-is(m_{1}\Omega(p)+m_{2}\Omega(k))}J(p)

\displaystyle[(1+f_{0}(p))(1+f_{0}(-p))-f_{0}(p)f_{0}(-p)]

\displaystyle V_{1}(p)^{2\alpha_{1}}V_{2}(p)^{\alpha_{2}}\hat{v}(p\pm k)V_{1}(k)^{2\alpha_{3}}V_{2}(k)^{\alpha_{4}}(f_{0}(k)+\iota)\,,

where
\ell\in{\mathbb{N}}_{0}
,
\ell\leq 3
,
j\in{\mathbb{N}}_{0}
,
j\leq 7
,
m_{1},m_{2}\in\{0,\pm 2\}
,
\alpha_{j}\in\{0,1,2\}
and
\iota\in\{0,1\}
. Here, we already employed the fact that
\hat{v}(0)=0
. Using symmetry of the integrand w.r.t.
p\leftrightarrow-p
, we can further simplify the expression (
5.110
) to

(5.111)

\displaystyle(-i)^{\ell}\lambda^{j}\int\,dp\,dk\,e^{-is(m_{1}\Omega(p)+m_{2}\Omega(k))}(1+f_{0}(p)+f_{0}(-p))J(p)

\displaystyle V_{1}(p)^{2\alpha_{1}}V_{2}(p)^{\alpha_{2}}\hat{v}(p-k)V_{1}(k)^{2\alpha_{3}}V_{2}(k)^{\alpha_{4}}(f_{0}(k)+\iota)\,.

Observe that the only terms contributing to (
5.109
) are of the form
[aa,(a^{+})^{3}a]
with any permutation of
(a^{+})^{3}a
, which is why the terms contain at least one momentum flip
a_{p}\to i\lambda V_{2}(p)\sin(\Omega(p)s)a_{-p}
. This justifies the extra factor
\lambda
on the RHS of (
5.109
). In the case
\iota=1
, we need to have at least one annihilation operator left of a creation operator in the second argument of the commutator. This yields an additional factor
V_{2}(k)
. In particular, the integrand in (
5.110
) contains
\hat{v}(p\pm k)f_{0}(k)
or
\hat{v}(p\pm k)V_{2}(k)
.

Next, we compute

(5.112)

\displaystyle\frac{(-i)^{2}}{|\Lambda|}\int_{\Delta[t,2]}\,d\mathbf{s}_{2}\,\nu_{0}([[g[J],{\mathcal{H}}_{cub}(s_{1})],{\mathcal{H}}_{cub}(s_{2})])

\displaystyle=

}{{{{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}}\lxSVG@begingroup@{_scopebegin} \lxSVG@transformcm{1.0}{0.0}{0.0}{1.0}{-9.90924pt}{-2.66666pt}\lxSVG@begingroup@{transform} \pgfsys@hbox{60}\lxSVG@closescope }}}
\lxSVG@closescope }}}
\lxSVG@closescope \hbox to0.0pt{}{{
{}{}{}}}{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}\hss}\lxSVG@discardpath\lxSVG@closescope \hss}}\lxSVG@closescope\endpgfpicture}}\hbox to0pt{\vbox to0pt{\pgfpicture\makeatletter\hbox{\hskip 0.0pt\lower 0.0pt\hbox to0.0pt{\lxSVG@begingroup@{_scopebegin} \lxSVG@begingroup@{stroke} \lxSVG@begingroup@{fill} \lxSVG@setlinewidth{\the\pgflinewidth}\lxSVG@begingroup@{stroke-width} \lx@inpgf@ignorespaces\nullfont\hbox to0.0pt{\lxSVG@begingroup@{_scopebegin} {
{}{}{}{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}}{}{{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}{}}{}{{}}{}{

{}{}{
{}{}{}{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}}{}{{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}{}}{{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}{}}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle J(0)\Phi_{t}^{2}+J(0)\operatorname{err}^{(Bog,\operatorname{Con})}_{2,d}(t;g)\,.

where, analogously to (
5.84
),

(5.116)

\displaystyle\operatorname{err}^{(Bog,\operatorname{Con})}_{2,d}(t;g)

\displaystyle:=

\displaystyle-2\Big(-i\int_{0}^{t}\,ds\,\frac{\nu_{0}([a_{0},{\mathcal{H}}_{cub}(s)])}{|\Lambda|}\operatorname{Rem}_{2}(t;\Phi)\Big)

\displaystyle-\,\big(\operatorname{Rem}_{2}(t;\Phi)\big)^{2}\,.

Using analogous estimates as for (
5.86
), we find that
|\operatorname{err}^{(Bog,\operatorname{Con})}_{2,d}(t;g)|
satisfies the same bounds as
|\operatorname{err}^{(Bog,\operatorname{Con})}_{2,d}(t;f)|
.

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{\lambda^{2}}{N}\int_{\Delta[t,2]}\,d\mathbf{s}_{2}\,\operatorname{col}_{d}(f_{0})[J](\mathbf{s}_{2})\,.

The expressions in
\operatorname{col}_{d}(f_{0})[J](\mathbf{s}_{2})
are of the form

(5.118)

\displaystyle(-i)^{\ell_{0}}\lambda^{j_{0}}\int\,d\mathbf{p}_{3}\,e^{-i\sum_{\ell=1}^{2}s_{\ell}\sum_{k=1}^{3}\sigma_{k,\ell}\Omega(p_{k})}\delta(p_{1}+p_{2}-p_{3})

\displaystyle J(p_{j_{1}})\hat{v}(p_{2})\hat{v}(p_{j_{2}})\prod_{k=1}^{3}V_{1}(p_{k})^{2\alpha_{\ell_{k}}}V_{2}(p_{k})^{\beta_{\ell_{k}}}

\displaystyle\Big(\prod_{k=1}^{3}\big(f_{0}(\tau_{k,1}p_{k})+\frac{1+\tau_{k,2}}{2}\big)\,-\,\prod_{k=1}^{3}\big(f_{0}(-\tau_{k,1}p_{k})+\frac{1-\tau_{k,2}}{2}\big)\Big)\,,

where
\ell_{0}\in{\mathbb{N}}_{0}^{\leq 3}
,
j_{0}\in{\mathbb{N}}_{0}
,
j_{0}\leq 12
,
\sigma_{k,\ell},\tau_{k,\ell}\in\{\pm 1\}
,
j_{1},j_{2}\in\{1,2,3\}
and
\alpha_{\ell_{k}},\beta_{\ell_{k}}\in\{0,1,2\}
. We need to ensure integrability of each of these terms. More precisely, we will show that any term contains a product of at least two of the functions
\hat{v}
,
f_{0}
,
V_{1}
, and
V_{2}
depending on at least two of the momenta
p_{1}
,
p_{2}
, or
p_{3}
. We have that

(5.119)

\displaystyle\int\,dp\,J(p)a_{p}a_{-p}\,=\,\int\,dp\,J(p)a_{-p}a_{p}\,=\,\int\,dp\,J(-p)a_{p}a_{-p}\,,

where we used the CCR followed by substitution. In particular, we may assume without loss of generality that
J
is even. Then the CCR imply

(5.120)

\displaystyle[g[J],a_{\sigma_{1}p_{1}}^{(\sigma_{1})}a_{\sigma_{2}p_{2}}^{(\sigma_{2})}a_{\sigma_{3}p_{3}}^{(-\sigma_{3})}]

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\delta_{\sigma_{1},1}J(p_{1})a_{-p_{1}}a_{\sigma_{2}p_{2}}^{(\sigma_{2})}a_{\sigma_{3}p_{3}}^{(-\sigma_{3})}

\displaystyle+\,\delta_{\sigma_{2},1}J(p_{2})a_{\sigma_{1}p_{1}}^{(\sigma_{1})}a_{-p_{2}}a_{\sigma_{3}p_{3}}^{(-\sigma_{3})}

\displaystyle+\,\delta_{\sigma_{3},-1}J(p_{3})a_{\sigma_{1}p_{1}}^{(\sigma_{1})}a_{\sigma_{2}p_{2}}^{(\sigma_{2})}a_{p_{3}}\,.

We will discuss the expressions related to one these three terms in detail; the remaining follow with analogous computations. Consider the Boltzmann contractions of

(5.121)

\displaystyle\nu_{0}\big([a_{-p_{1}}a_{\sigma_{2}p_{2}}^{(\sigma_{2})}a_{\sigma_{3}p_{3}}^{(-\sigma_{3})},a_{\tau_{1}k_{1}}^{(\tau_{1})}a_{\tau_{2}k_{2}}^{(\tau_{2})}a_{\tau_{3}k_{3}}^{(-\tau_{3})}]\big)\,,

which is why, again, the order of the operators
a
and
a^{+}
does not matter. Observe that it is sufficient to have a factor
\hat{v}
,
f_{0}
,
V_{1}
, or
V_{2}
with momentum
p_{1}
or
p_{12}
, since, due to (
5.118
), we always have a coefficient
\hat{v}(p_{2})
. The Boltzmann contractions in (
5.121
) yield a factor

(5.122)

\displaystyle\big(f_{0}(-p_{1})+1\big)\big(f_{0}(\sigma_{2}p_{2})+\frac{1-\sigma_{2}}{2}\big)\big(f_{0}(\sigma_{3}p_{12})+\frac{1+\sigma_{3}}{2}\big)

\displaystyle-f_{0}(-p_{1})\big(f_{0}(\sigma_{2}p_{2})+\frac{1+\sigma_{2}}{2}\big)\big(f_{0}(\sigma_{3}p_{12})+\frac{1-\sigma_{3}}{2}\big)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\sigma_{3}f_{0}(-p_{1})f_{0}(\sigma_{2}p_{2})\,+\,f_{0}(\sigma_{2}p_{2})f_{0}(\sigma_{3}p_{12})\,-\,\sigma_{2}f_{0}(-p_{1})f_{0}(\sigma_{3}p_{12})

\displaystyle+\,(\sigma_{3}-\sigma_{2})f_{0}(-p_{1})\,+\,(1+\sigma_{3})f_{0}(\sigma_{2}p_{2})\,+\,(1-\sigma_{2})f_{0}(\sigma_{3}p_{12})\,.

The only term in (
5.122
) that does not already involve a factor depending on a momentum other than
p_{2}
, see also (
5.118
), is
(1+\sigma_{3})f_{0}(\sigma_{2}p_{2})
. This term only appears if
\sigma_{3}=1
, which we now want to consider.

Due to momentum flips in
p_{j}
, the corresponding term associated with
{\mathcal{H}}_{cub}^{(2)}(s_{1})
in the first argument of the commutator has a coefficient
V_{2}(p_{1})V_{2}(p_{3})
, yielding the remaining integrability w.r.t.
dp_{1}
.

Thus, let us consider the terms associated with
{\mathcal{H}}_{cub}^{(2)}(s_{1})
. If we contract
a_{\tau_{2}k_{2}}^{(\tau_{2})}
in with
a_{-\sigma_{1}p_{1}}^{(-\sigma_{1})}
or
a_{\sigma_{3}p_{3}}^{(-\sigma_{3})}
, we obtain a factor
\hat{v}(p_{1})
or
\hat{v}(p_{12})
, yielding integrability w.r.t.
dp_{1}
.

So, it remains to consider the case when
a_{\tau_{2}k_{2}}^{(\tau_{2})}
is contracted with
a_{\sigma_{2}p_{2}}^{(\sigma_{2})}
. The remaining contractions yield either
(\tau_{1},-\tau_{3})=(1,1)
or
(-\tau_{3},\tau_{1})=(1,1)
. In those cases, we obtain an additional factor
V_{2}(p_{12})
or
V_{2}(p_{1})
. This concludes the argument.

We are left with evaluating the pair absorption terms

\displaystyle-\frac{1}{|\Lambda|}\int_{\Delta[t,2]}\,d\mathbf{s}_{2}\,[[\hbox to5.13pt{\vbox to6.25pt{\pgfpicture\makeatletter\hbox{\>\lower-3.12498pt\hbox to0.0pt{\lxSVG@begingroup@{_scopebegin} \lxSVG@begingroup@{stroke} \lxSVG@begingroup@{fill} \lxSVG@setlinewidth{\the\pgflinewidth}\lxSVG@begingroup@{stroke-width} \lx@inpgf@ignorespaces\nullfont\hbox to0.0pt{\lxSVG@begingroup@{_scopebegin}
{{}}\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces\hbox{\hbox{{\lxSVG@begingroup@{_scopebegin} {{}{}{{
{}{}}}{
{}{}}
{{}{{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}}}{{}{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}}{}{{}{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}}
{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces

}{{{{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}}\lxSVG@begingroup@{_scopebegin} \lxSVG@transformcm{1.0}{0.0}{0.0}{1.0}{-2.34375pt}{-2.15277pt}\lxSVG@begingroup@{transform} \pgfsys@hbox{60}\lxSVG@closescope }}}

\lxSVG@closescope \hbox to0.0pt{}{{

{}{}{

{}{}{}{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}}{}{{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}{}}
{}{}{}\lxSVG@stroke\lxSVG@drawpath@unclipped{M 0 7.13 L 0 19.16 L 0 19.16 L 0 7.13}{fill:none} \lx@inpgf@ignorespaces
\lxSVG@closescope \hbox to0.0pt{}{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}\hss}\lxSVG@discardpath\lxSVG@closescope \hss}}\lxSVG@closescope\endpgfpicture}}_{2})]\vbox to19.00002pt{}{}

(5.123)

\displaystyle=\frac{\lambda^{2}}{N}\int_{\Delta[t,2]}\,d\mathbf{s}_{2}\,\mathrm{abs}_{cub,d}(f_{0})[J](\mathbf{s}_{2})\,.

\mathrm{abs}_{cub,d}(f_{0})[J](\mathbf{s}_{2})
consists of terms of the form

(5.124)

\displaystyle(-i)^{\ell}\lambda^{j}\int\,dp\,dk\,e^{-is_{1}m_{1}\Omega(p)-is_{2}m_{2}\Omega(k)}J(p)(1+f_{0}(p)+f_{0}(-p))\hat{v}(p)

\displaystyle V_{1}(p)^{2\alpha_{1}}V_{2}(p)^{\alpha_{2}}\hat{v}(k)V_{1}(k)^{2\alpha_{3}}V_{2}(k)^{\alpha_{4}}(f_{0}(k)+\iota)\,,

where
\ell\in{\mathbb{N}}_{0}
,
\ell\leq 3
,
j\in{\mathbb{N}}_{0}
,
j\leq 12
,
m_{1},m_{2}\in\{0,\pm 2\}
,
\alpha_{j}\in\{0,1,2\}
and
\iota\in\{0,1\}
. Here again, we take into account that terms involving
\hat{v}(0)=0
vanish. This concludes the proof.
∎

Lemma 5.3
.

We have the following expansions.

(1)

Identifying the RHS with its continuous extension, we have that

(5.125)

\displaystyle\int_{\Delta[t,2]}\,d\mathbf{s}_{2}\,e^{i(\omega_{1}s_{1}+\omega_{2}s_{2})}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{2}{\omega_{2}}\Big(\frac{\sin^{2}\big((\omega_{1}+\omega_{2})t/2\big)}{\omega_{1}+\omega_{2}}\,-\,\frac{\sin^{2}\big(\omega_{1}t/2\big)}{\omega_{1}}\Big)

\displaystyle-\,\frac{i}{\omega_{2}}\Big(\frac{\sin\big((\omega_{1}+\omega_{2})t\big)}{\omega_{1}+\omega_{2}}\,-\,\frac{\sin(\omega_{1}t)}{\omega_{1}}\Big)

for all
\omega_{1},\omega_{2}\in{\mathbb{R}}
.

(2)

The Bogoliubov dispersion
\Omega
in Lemma
3.2
satisfies

(5.126)

\displaystyle\Omega

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle E\,+\,\lambda\hat{v}_{Bog}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle E\,+\,\lambda\hat{v}\,-\,\lambda^{2}\frac{\hat{v}^{2}}{2E}\,+\,\lambda^{3}{\operatorname{err}}_{Bog}\,,

where
\hat{v}_{Bog}:=\frac{2\hat{v}}{1+\sqrt{1+2\lambda\frac{\hat{v}}{E}}}
and
{\operatorname{err}}_{Bog}
satisfies

(5.127)

\displaystyle\|{\operatorname{err}}_{Bog}\|_{\infty}\,\leq\,C\|\hat{v}\|_{w,d}^{3}

for all
\lambda>0
.

Proof.

For the first part, let
\omega_{1},\omega_{2},\omega_{1}+\omega_{2}\neq 0
. Then

(5.128)

\displaystyle\int_{\Delta[t,2]}\,d\mathbf{s}_{2}\,e^{i(\omega_{1}s_{1}+\omega_{2}s_{2})}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{t}\,ds\,\frac{e^{i\omega_{1}s}}{i\omega_{2}}(e^{i\omega_{2}s}\,-\,1)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-\frac{1}{\omega_{2}}\Big(\frac{e^{i(\omega_{1}+\omega_{2})t}-1}{\omega_{1}+\omega_{2}}\,-\,\frac{e^{i\omega_{1}t}-1}{\omega_{1}}\Big)\,.

Using
1-\cos(x)=2\sin(x/2)
, we have shown the first statement.

For the second part, we expand
\Omega
using

(5.129)

\displaystyle\Omega

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle E\,+\,\frac{\Omega^{2}-E^{2}}{E+\Omega}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle E\,+\,\frac{2\lambda\hat{v}}{1+\sqrt{1+2\lambda\frac{\hat{v}}{E}}}\,.

We Taylor expand

(5.130)

\displaystyle\frac{1}{1+\sqrt{1+x}}\,=\,\frac{1}{2}\,-\,\frac{x}{8}\,+\,R(x)

for some
R(x)
. An easy computation yields

(5.131)

\displaystyle\frac{|R(x)|}{x^{2}}\,\leq\,C

for all
x>0
, and in the limit
x\searrow 0
. This concludes the proof.
∎

Remark 5.4

(Talbot effect)
.

Lemma
5.3
shows that the leading term in
\operatorname{Bol}_{d}(f_{0})[J]
is a sum of the form

(5.132)

\displaystyle\frac{T}{\lambda^{2}}\int_{({\Lambda^{*}})^{2}}\,d\mathbf{p}_{2}\,\frac{\sin^{2}\Big(\frac{T({\Delta_{cub}E}\,+\,\lambda\Delta_{cub}\hat{v}_{Bog})}{2\lambda^{2}}\Big)}{\Big(\frac{T({\Delta_{cub}E}\,+\,\lambda\Delta_{cub}\hat{v}_{Bog})}{2\lambda^{2}}\Big)^{2}}{\Delta_{cub}J}(\mathbf{p}_{2})H(\mathbf{p}_{2})

for some
H\sim 1
with
\hat{v}_{Bog}
as defined in Lemma
5.3
. Thus, as a function of
T
, the modulus of this sum oscillates between
0
and
O(\lambda^{-2})
. The size of these oscillations is dependent on the the interaction profile
\hat{v}
, the lattice
\Lambda
, and the chosen sequence
\lambda=\lambda(N)
. A similar phenomenon occurs for
\operatorname{Bol}_{d}^{(j)}
, which thereby also oscillate in modulus between
0
and
O(\lambda^{j-2})
, depending on
T
, and we also observe it for
\operatorname{col}_{d}
,
\mathrm{abs}_{quart,d}
, and
\mathrm{abs}_{cub,d}
. Therefore,
\operatorname{Bol}_{d}^{(j)}
can dominate
\operatorname{Bol}_{d}
, depending on whether
\frac{T}{4\pi\lambda^{2}}{\Delta_{cub}\Omega}(\mathbf{p}_{2})
lies in a small vicinity of
\frac{1}{4}+{\mathbb{Z}}
for some
\mathbf{p}_{2}\in({\Lambda^{*}})^{2}
. This is reminiscent of the Talbot effect, see
[
128
,
190
]
. As heuristically explained above,
\operatorname{Bol}_{d}^{(j)}
are negligible in the case of large system length
L\sim\lambda^{-2-}
. Next, we will present a proof of this fact.

5.2.
Continuum approximation
{\Lambda^{*}}\to{\mathbb{R}}^{3}

In this section, we will write out the summation over the lattice
{\Lambda^{*}}
explicitly. In contrast, all integrals will be understood as integrals over
{\mathbb{R}}^{d}
in the respective dimension
d
.

Lemma 5.5
.

Let
F_{1}(\mathbf{p}_{2}),F_{2}(\mathbf{p}_{2})\in\{\pm\Omega(p_{1})\pm\Omega(p_{2})\pm\Omega(p_{1}+p_{2}),0\}
,
H\in C^{\infty}_{c}({\mathbb{R}}^{6})
,
\chi\in C^{\infty}_{c}({\mathbb{R}}^{3})
, and
\tau_{1},\tau_{2}\in{\mathbb{R}}
. Let
\langle x\rangle:=(1+x^{2})^{\frac{1}{2}}
. Abbreviate

\displaystyle{\operatorname{err}}_{disc}^{(1)}

\displaystyle:=

\displaystyle\lambda^{2}\int_{0}^{T\lambda^{-2}}\,ds\,\Big(\frac{1}{|\Lambda|^{2}}\sum_{\mathbf{p}_{2}\in({\Lambda^{*}})^{2}}e^{i(\tau_{1}\Omega(p_{1})+\tau_{2}\Omega(p_{2}))s}H(\mathbf{p}_{2})

\displaystyle-\,\frac{1}{(2\pi)^{6}}\int\,d\mathbf{p}_{2}\,e^{i(\tau_{1}\Omega(p_{1})+\tau_{2}\Omega(p_{2}))s}H(\mathbf{p}_{2})\Big)\,,

(5.133)

\displaystyle{\operatorname{err}}_{disc}^{(2)}

\displaystyle:=

\displaystyle\lambda^{2}\int_{\Delta[T\lambda^{-2},2]}\,d\mathbf{s}_{2}\,\Big(\frac{1}{|\Lambda|^{2}}\sum_{\mathbf{p}_{2}\in({\Lambda^{*}})^{2}}e^{iF_{1}(\mathbf{p}_{2})s_{1}+iF_{2}(\mathbf{p}_{2})s_{2}}H(\mathbf{p}_{2})

\displaystyle-\,\frac{1}{(2\pi)^{6}}\int\,d\mathbf{p}_{2}\,e^{iF_{1}(\mathbf{p}_{2})s_{1}+iF_{2}(\mathbf{p}_{2})s_{2}}H(\mathbf{p}_{2})\Big)\,.

Then we have for any
r>6
and
\lambda>0
the following.

(1)

(5.134)

\displaystyle\Big|\frac{1}{|\Lambda|}\sum_{p\in{\Lambda^{*}}}\chi(p)\,-\,\frac{1}{(2\pi)^{3}}\int_{{\mathbb{R}}^{3}}\,dp\,\chi(p)\Big|\,\leq\,C_{r}\frac{\||\nabla|^{\frac{r}{2}}\chi\|_{1}}{|\Lambda|^{\frac{r}{6}}}\,,

(2)

(5.135)

\displaystyle|{\operatorname{err}}_{disc}^{(1)}|\,\leq\,C_{\|\hat{v}\|_{2(\left\lfloor\frac{r}{2}\right\rfloor+1),w,c},r}\frac{\langle T\rangle^{r+1}}{\lambda^{2r}|\Lambda|^{\frac{r}{3}}}\sum_{n=0}^{2(\left\lfloor\frac{r}{2}\right\rfloor+1)}\Big\|\langle|\mathbf{p}_{2}|\rangle^{n}D^{2(\left\lfloor\frac{r}{2}\right\rfloor+1)-n}H\Big\|_{1}\,,

(3)

if
F_{1}=F_{2}\equiv 0
,

(5.136)

\displaystyle|{\operatorname{err}}_{disc}^{(2)}|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C_{\|\hat{v}\|_{2(\left\lfloor\frac{r}{2}\right\rfloor+1),w,c},r}\frac{T^{2}}{\lambda^{2}|\Lambda|^{\frac{r}{3}}}\||\nabla|^{r}H\|_{1}\,,

(4)

if
(F_{1},F_{2})\not\equiv(0,0)
,

(5.137)

\displaystyle|{\operatorname{err}}_{disc}^{(2)}|\,\leq\,C_{\|\hat{v}\|_{2(\left\lfloor\frac{r}{2}\right\rfloor+1),w,c},r}\frac{\langle T\rangle^{r+2}}{\lambda^{2r+2}|\Lambda|^{\frac{r}{3}}}\sum_{n=0}^{2(\left\lfloor\frac{r}{2}\right\rfloor+1)}\Big\|\langle|\mathbf{p}_{2}|\rangle^{n}D^{2(\left\lfloor\frac{r}{2}\right\rfloor+1)-n}H\Big\|_{1}\,.

Proof.

Let

(5.138)

\displaystyle{\tau}\,:=\,\frac{2\pi}{|\Lambda|^{\frac{1}{3}}}\,,

so that

(5.139)

\displaystyle\int_{\Lambda^{*}}\,dp\,\tilde{H}(p)\,=\,\frac{{\tau}^{3}}{(2\pi)^{3}}\sum_{p\in{\Lambda^{*}}}\tilde{H}(p)\,.

Denote

(5.140)

\displaystyle G_{\lambda}(\mathbf{p}_{2})\,:=\,\lambda^{2}\int_{\Delta[T\lambda^{-2},2]}\,d\mathbf{s}_{2}\,e^{iF_{1}(\mathbf{p}_{2})s_{1}+iF_{2}(\mathbf{p}_{2})s_{2}}\,.

Poisson summation implies

(5.141)

\displaystyle{\tau}^{6}\sum_{\mathbf{p}_{2}\in({\Lambda^{*}})^{2}}G_{\lambda}(\mathbf{p}_{2})H(\mathbf{p}_{2})

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle{\tau}^{6}\sum_{\mathbf{P}_{2}\in{\mathbb{Z}}^{6}}G_{\lambda}(\tau\mathbf{P}_{2})H({\tau}\mathbf{P}_{2})

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\sum_{\mathbf{X}_{2}\in{\mathbb{Z}}^{6}}{\tau}^{6}\int_{{\mathbb{R}}^{6}}\,d\mathbf{P}_{2}\,e^{2\pi i\mathbf{P}_{2}\cdot\mathbf{X}_{2}}G_{\lambda}(\tau\mathbf{P}_{2})H({\tau}\mathbf{P}_{2})

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\sum_{\mathbf{X}_{2}\in{\mathbb{Z}}^{6}}\int_{{\mathbb{R}}^{6}}\,d\mathbf{p}_{2}\,e^{2\pi i\mathbf{p}_{2}\cdot\mathbf{X}_{2}/{\tau}}G_{\lambda}(\mathbf{p}_{2})H(\mathbf{p}_{2})\,.

As a consequence of (
5.141
), we obtain that

(5.142)

\displaystyle{\operatorname{err}}_{disc}^{(2)}\,=\,\frac{1}{(2\pi)^{6}}\sum_{\mathbf{X}_{2}\in{\mathbb{Z}}^{6}\setminus\{0\}}\int_{{\mathbb{R}}^{6}}\,d\mathbf{p}_{2}\,e^{2\pi i\mathbf{p}_{2}\cdot\mathbf{X}_{2}/{\tau}}G_{\lambda}(\mathbf{p}_{2})H(\mathbf{p}_{2})\,.

Next, we have that

\displaystyle|\mathbf{X}_{2}|^{r}\int_{{\mathbb{R}}^{6}}\,d\mathbf{p}_{2}\,e^{2\pi i\mathbf{p}_{2}\cdot\mathbf{X}_{2}/{\tau}}G_{\lambda}(\mathbf{p}_{2})H(\mathbf{p}_{2})

(5.143)

\displaystyle=\,\frac{{\tau}^{r}}{(2\pi)^{r}}\int_{{\mathbb{R}}^{6}}\,d\mathbf{p}_{2}\,e^{2\pi i\mathbf{p}_{2}\cdot\mathbf{X}_{2}/{\tau}}|\nabla_{\mathbf{p}_{2}}|^{r}\big(G_{\lambda}(\mathbf{p}_{2})H(\mathbf{p}_{2})\big)\,.

In particular, we have that

(5.144)

\displaystyle|{\operatorname{err}}_{disc}^{(2)}|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C_{r}{\tau}^{r}\sum_{\mathbf{X}_{2}\in{\mathbb{Z}}^{6}\setminus\{0\}}\frac{1}{|\mathbf{X}_{2}|^{r}}\||\nabla|^{r}\big(G_{\lambda}H\big)\|_{1}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{C_{r}}{|\Lambda|^{\frac{r}{3}}}\||\nabla|^{r}\big(G_{\lambda}H\big)\|_{1}

due to
r>6
. With analogous steps, we obtain

(5.145)

\displaystyle\Big|{\tau}^{3}\sum_{p\in{\Lambda^{*}}}\chi(p)\,-\,\int_{{\mathbb{R}}^{3}}\,dp\,\chi(p)\Big|

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{{\tau}^{\frac{r}{2}}}{(2\pi)^{\frac{r}{2}}}\Big|\sum_{X\in{\mathbb{Z}}^{3}\setminus\{0\}}\frac{1}{|X|^{\frac{r}{2}}}\int_{{\mathbb{R}}^{3}}\,dp\,e^{2\pi ip\cdot X/{\tau}}|\nabla|^{\frac{r}{2}}\chi(p)\Big|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{C_{r}}{|\Lambda|^{\frac{r}{6}}}\||\nabla|^{\frac{r}{2}}\chi\|_{1}\,.

Interpolation implies

(5.146)

\displaystyle\||\nabla|^{r}\big(G_{\lambda}H\big)\|_{1}\,\leq\,C\|(-\Delta)^{\left\lfloor\frac{r}{2}\right\rfloor+1}\big(G_{\lambda}H\big)\|_{1}^{\frac{\frac{r}{2}}{\left\lfloor\frac{r}{2}\right\rfloor+1}}\|G_{\lambda}H\|_{1}^{\frac{1+\left\lfloor\frac{r}{2}\right\rfloor-\frac{r}{2}}{\left\lfloor\frac{r}{2}\right\rfloor+1}}\big)\,,

where
t_{0}>0
will be fixed below. The Leibniz rule implies for
k\in{\mathbb{N}}_{0}
that

(5.147)

\displaystyle|(-\Delta)^{k}G_{\lambda}H|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C_{k}\sum_{n=0}^{2k}|D^{n}G_{\lambda}||D^{2k-n}H|\,.

We start with the case
F_{1}=F_{2}\equiv 0
. Lemma
5.3
yields
G_{\lambda}=C\frac{T^{2}}{2}\lambda^{-2}
, which, employing (
5.144
), implies

(5.148)

\displaystyle|{\operatorname{err}}_{disc}^{(2)}|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C_{r}T^{2}\lambda^{-2}{\tau}^{r}\||\nabla|^{r}H\|_{1}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C_{r}\frac{T^{2}}{\lambda^{2}|\Lambda|^{\frac{r}{3}}}\||\nabla|^{r}H\|_{1}\,.

Next, let
F_{1}\equiv 0\wedge F_{2}\not\equiv 0
,
F_{1}\not\equiv 0\wedge F_{2}\equiv 0
, or
F_{1}=-F_{2}\not\equiv 0
. Let
F_{j}\not\equiv 0
for some
j\in\{1,2,3\}
. Then, by Lemma
5.3
, we obtain that

(5.149)

\displaystyle G_{\lambda}(\mathbf{p}_{2})\,=\,\frac{T^{2}}{\lambda^{2}}G(F_{j}(\mathbf{p}_{2})T/\lambda^{2})

for some continuous function
G\in C^{\infty}_{b}({\mathbb{R}})
. Moreover, the Faà di Bruno formula, see
[
295
]
, implies

(5.150)

\displaystyle|D^{n}G(F_{j}T/\lambda^{2})|\,\leq\,C_{n}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}\mathbf{r}_{n}\in\\
R(n)\end{subarray}}|G^{(S(\mathbf{r}_{n}))}|(F_{j}T/\lambda^{2})\prod_{\ell=1}^{n}\Big[\frac{T}{\lambda^{2}}|D^{\ell}F_{j}|\Big]^{r_{\ell}}\,,

where

(5.151)

\displaystyle R(n)

\displaystyle:=

\displaystyle\{\mathbf{r}_{n}\in{\mathbb{N}}_{0}^{n}\mid\sum_{\ell=1}^{n}\ell r_{\ell}=n\}\,,

(5.152)

\displaystyle S(\mathbf{r}_{n})

\displaystyle:=

\displaystyle\sum_{\ell=1}^{n}r_{\ell}\,.

Observe that due to Lemma
5.3
, we have that

(5.153)

\displaystyle|\nabla F_{j}|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C_{\|\hat{v}\|_{2(\left\lfloor\frac{r}{2}\right\rfloor+1),w,c}}\langle|\mathbf{p}_{2}|\rangle\,,

(5.154)

\displaystyle|D^{\ell}F_{j}|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C_{\|\hat{v}\|_{2(\left\lfloor\frac{r}{2}\right\rfloor+1),w,c}}

for all
\lambda\in(0,1)
, and all
\ell\geq 2
. Then (
5.150
) together with (
5.153
), (
5.154
), and the fact that
1\leq S(\mathbf{r}_{n})\leq n
imply

(5.155)

\displaystyle|D^{n}G(F_{j}T/\lambda^{2})|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C_{n}\frac{T\langle T\rangle^{n-1}\langle|\nabla F_{j}|\rangle^{n}}{\lambda^{2n}}\Big(\sum_{\ell=1}^{n}|G^{(\ell)}|\Big)(F_{j}T/\lambda^{2})

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C_{\|\hat{v}\|_{2(\left\lfloor\frac{r}{2}\right\rfloor+1),w,c},n}\frac{T\langle T\rangle^{n-1}\langle|\mathbf{p}_{2}|\rangle^{n}}{\lambda^{2n}}\Big(\sum_{\ell=1}^{n}|G^{(\ell)}|\Big)(F_{j}T/\lambda^{2})

for all
\lambda\in(0,1)
. Collecting (
5.147
),
5.155
, we have that

(5.156)

\displaystyle\|(-\Delta)^{k}\big(G_{\lambda}H\big)\|_{1}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C_{\|\hat{v}\|_{2(\left\lfloor\frac{r}{2}\right\rfloor+1),w,c},r}\sum_{n=0}^{2k}\frac{T^{3}\langle T\rangle^{n-1}}{\lambda^{2n+2}}\Big\|\Big(\sum_{\ell=1}^{n}|G^{(\ell)}|\Big)(F_{j}T/\lambda^{2})\langle|\mathbf{p}_{2}|\rangle^{n}D^{2k-n}H\Big\|_{1}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C_{\|\hat{v}\|_{2(\left\lfloor\frac{r}{2}\right\rfloor+1),w,c},r}\frac{\langle T\rangle^{2k+2}}{\lambda^{4k+2}}\Big\|\Big(\sum_{\ell=0}^{2k}|G^{(\ell)}|\Big)\Big\|_{\infty}\sum_{n=0}^{2k}\Big\|\langle|\mathbf{p}_{2}|\rangle^{n}D^{2k-n}H\Big\|_{1}

for all
k\in\{0,1,2,\ldots,\left\lfloor\frac{r}{2}\right\rfloor+1\}
. Then (
5.146
) and (
5.156
) yield

(5.157)

\displaystyle\||\nabla|^{r}\big(G_{\lambda}H\big)\|_{1}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C_{\|\hat{v}\|_{2(\left\lfloor\frac{r}{2}\right\rfloor+1),w,c},r}\frac{\langle T\rangle^{r+2}}{\lambda^{2r+2}}\sum_{n=0}^{2(\left\lfloor\frac{r}{2}\right\rfloor+1)}\Big\|\langle|\mathbf{p}_{2}|\rangle^{n}D^{2(\left\lfloor\frac{r}{2}\right\rfloor+1)-n}H\Big\|_{1}\,,

where we also used the fact that
\|H\|_{1}\leq\|\langle\mathbf{p}_{2}\rangle^{2(\left\lfloor\frac{r}{2}\right\rfloor+1)}H\|_{1}
. (
5.144
) and (
5.157
) yield

(5.158)

\displaystyle|{\operatorname{err}}_{disc}^{(2)}|\,\leq\,C_{\|\hat{v}\|_{2(\left\lfloor\frac{r}{2}\right\rfloor+1),w,c},r}\frac{\langle T\rangle^{r+2}}{\lambda^{2r+2}|\Lambda|^{\frac{r}{3}}}\sum_{n=0}^{2(\left\lfloor\frac{r}{2}\right\rfloor+1)}\Big\|\langle|\mathbf{p}_{2}|\rangle^{n}D^{2(\left\lfloor\frac{r}{2}\right\rfloor+1)-n}H\Big\|_{1}\,.

Now, let
F_{j}\not\equiv 0
for all
j\in\{1,2,3\}
. In this case, Lemma
5.3
implies

(5.159)

\displaystyle G_{\lambda}(\mathbf{p}_{2})\,=\,\frac{T^{2}}{\lambda^{2}}G\big(\frac{T}{\lambda^{2}}(F_{1}(\mathbf{p}_{2}),F_{2}(\mathbf{p}_{2}))\big)

for some smooth function
G\in C^{\infty}_{b}({\mathbb{R}}^{2})
. The Faà di Bruno formula implies

(5.160)

\displaystyle\big|D^{n}G\big(\frac{T}{\lambda^{2}}(F_{1}(\mathbf{p}_{2}),F_{2}(\mathbf{p}_{2}))\big)\big|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C_{n}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}\mathbf{r}_{n}\in\\
R(n)\end{subarray}}|D^{S(\mathbf{r}_{n})}G|\big(\frac{T}{\lambda^{2}}(F_{1}(\mathbf{p}_{2}),F_{2}(\mathbf{p}_{2}))\big)

\displaystyle\prod_{\ell=1}^{n}\Big[\frac{T}{\lambda^{2}}\sum_{j=1}^{2}|D^{\ell}F_{j}|\Big]^{r_{\ell}}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C_{n}\frac{T\langle T\rangle^{n-1}\langle\sum_{j=1}^{2}|\nabla F_{j}|\rangle^{n}}{\lambda^{2n}}

\displaystyle\Big(\sum_{\ell=1}^{n}|D^{\ell}G|\Big)\big(\frac{T}{\lambda^{2}}(F_{1}(\mathbf{p}_{2}),F_{2}(\mathbf{p}_{2}))\big)\,.

Collecting (
5.147
), (
5.153
), (
5.154
), and
5.160
, we have that

(5.161)

\displaystyle\|(-\Delta)^{k}\big(G_{\lambda}H\big)\|_{1}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C_{\|\hat{v}\|_{2(\left\lfloor\frac{r}{2}\right\rfloor+1),w,c},r}\sum_{n=0}^{2k}\frac{T^{3}\langle T\rangle^{n-1}}{\lambda^{2n+2}}

\displaystyle\Big\|\Big(\sum_{\ell=1}^{n}|D^{\ell}G|\Big)\big(\frac{T}{\lambda^{2}}(F_{1},F_{2})\big)\langle\sum_{j=1}^{2}|\nabla F_{j}|\rangle^{n}D^{2k-n}H\Big\|_{1}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C_{\|\hat{v}\|_{2(\left\lfloor\frac{r}{2}\right\rfloor+1),w,c},r}\frac{\langle T\rangle^{2k+2}}{\lambda^{4k+2}}\Big\|\Big(\sum_{\ell=0}^{2k}|D^{\ell}G|\Big)\Big\|_{\infty}\sum_{n=0}^{2k}\Big\|\langle|\mathbf{p}_{2}|\rangle^{n}D^{2k-n}H\Big\|_{1}

for all
k\in\{0,1,2,\ldots,\left\lfloor\frac{r}{2}\right\rfloor+1\}
. As a consequence of (
5.146
), we thus obtain

(5.162)

\displaystyle\||\nabla|^{r}\big(G_{\lambda}H)\|_{1}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C_{\|\hat{v}\|_{2(\left\lfloor\frac{r}{2}\right\rfloor+1),w,c},r}\frac{\langle T\rangle^{r+2}}{\lambda^{2r+2}}\sum_{n=0}^{2(\left\lfloor\frac{r}{2}\right\rfloor+1)}\Big\|\langle|\mathbf{p}_{2}|\rangle^{n}D^{2(\left\lfloor\frac{r}{2}\right\rfloor+1)-n}H\Big\|_{1}\,.

(
5.144
) and (
5.162
) imply

(5.163)

\displaystyle|{\operatorname{err}}_{disc}^{(2)}|\,\leq\,C_{\|\hat{v}\|_{2(\left\lfloor\frac{r}{2}\right\rfloor+1),w,c},r}\frac{\langle T\rangle^{r+2}}{\lambda^{2r+2}|\Lambda|^{\frac{r}{3}}}\sum_{n=0}^{2(\left\lfloor\frac{r}{2}\right\rfloor+1)}\Big\|\langle|\mathbf{p}_{2}|\rangle^{n}D^{2(\left\lfloor\frac{r}{2}\right\rfloor+1)-n}H\Big\|_{1}\,.

Finally, let

(5.164)

\displaystyle\mathring{G}_{\lambda}(\mathbf{p}_{2})\,:=\,\lambda^{2}\int_{0}^{T\lambda^{-2}}\,ds\,e^{i(\tau_{1}\Omega(p_{1})+\tau_{2}\Omega(p_{2}))s}\,.

A simple computation shows

(5.165)

\displaystyle\mathring{G}_{\lambda}(\mathbf{p}_{2})\,=\,T\mathring{G}\big((\tau_{1}\Omega(p_{1})+\tau_{2}\Omega(p_{2}))\frac{T}{\lambda^{2}}\big)

for some smooth
\mathring{G}\in C^{\infty}_{b}({\mathbb{R}})
. If
\tau_{1}=\tau_{2}=0
, we have
\mathring{G}\equiv C
. If
(\tau_{1},\tau_{2})\neq(0,0)
, a computation analogous to (
5.155
) yields

(5.166)

\displaystyle|D^{n}\mathring{G}\big((\tau_{1}\Omega(p_{1})+\tau_{2}\Omega(p_{2}))T/\lambda^{2}\big)|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C_{\|\hat{v}\|_{2(\left\lfloor\frac{r}{2}\right\rfloor+1),w,c},n}\frac{T\langle T\rangle^{n-1}\langle|\mathbf{p}_{2}|\rangle^{n}}{\lambda^{2n}}\Big(\sum_{\ell=1}^{n}|\mathring{G}^{(\ell)}|\Big)((\tau_{1}\Omega(p_{1})+\tau_{2}\Omega(p_{2}))T/\lambda^{2})

for all
n\in\{0,1,2,\ldots,2(\left\lfloor\frac{r}{2}\right\rfloor+1)\}
. In particular, with analogous steps that led to (
5.158
), we obtain

(5.167)

\displaystyle|{\operatorname{err}}_{disc}^{(1)}|\,\leq\,C_{\|\hat{v}\|_{2(\left\lfloor\frac{r}{2}\right\rfloor+1),w,c},r}\frac{\langle T\rangle^{r+1}}{\lambda^{2r}|\Lambda|^{\frac{r}{3}}}\sum_{n=0}^{2(\left\lfloor\frac{r}{2}\right\rfloor+1)}\Big\|\langle|\mathbf{p}_{2}|\rangle^{n}D^{2(\left\lfloor\frac{r}{2}\right\rfloor+1)-n}H\Big\|_{1}\,.

This concludes the proof.
∎

Remark 5.6
.

Observe that Lemma
5.5
implies that

(5.168)

\displaystyle\int_{\Lambda^{*}}\,dp\,f_{0}(p)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\|f_{0}\|_{1}+C_{r}\frac{\||\nabla|^{\frac{r}{2}}f_{0}\|_{1}}{|\Lambda|^{\frac{r}{6}}}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C_{r}\|f_{0}\|_{2(\left\lfloor\frac{r}{2}\right\rfloor+1),c}

for any
r>6
as in Theorem
2.2
, where
C_{r}
is independent of
|\Lambda|\geq 1
. In particular, we have that

(5.169)

\displaystyle\|f_{0}\|_{d}\,\leq\,C_{r}\|f_{0}\|_{2(\left\lfloor\frac{r}{2}\right\rfloor+1),c}\,.

This is allows us to use the previous estimates to prove Theorem
2.2
as well. Analogously, we have that

(5.170)

\displaystyle\|\hat{v}\|_{w,d}\,\leq\,C_{r}\|\hat{v}\|_{2(\left\lfloor\frac{r}{2}\right\rfloor+1),w,c}\,.

in the assumption of Theorem
2.1
, where, again
C_{r}
is independent of
|\Lambda|\geq 1
.

Likewise, we have that

(5.171)

\displaystyle\|J\|_{2\cap\infty,d}\,\leq\,C_{r}\|J\|_{2(\left\lfloor\frac{r}{2}\right\rfloor+1),c}\,.

For the next statement, define the continuous analogues
\operatorname{Con}_{c}
,
\operatorname{Bol}_{c}
, etc. of
\operatorname{Con}_{d}
,
\operatorname{Bol}_{d}
, etc. in (
5.49
)–(
5.56
), and (
5.63
), (
5.65
) by replacing the lattice sums
\int_{\Lambda^{*}}
over
{\Lambda^{*}}
in the sense of (
1.51
) by Lebesgue integrals
\frac{1}{(2\pi)^{3}}\int_{{\mathbb{R}}^{3}}
over
{\mathbb{R}}^{3}
.

Proposition 5.7
.

Let
T>0
,
J\in L^{\infty}({\mathbb{R}}^{3};{\mathbb{R}})
and
r>6
. Then the following holds for all
\lambda>0
small enough, dependent on
\|\hat{v}\|_{2(\left\lfloor\frac{r}{2}\right\rfloor+1),w,c}
.

(1)

(5.172)

\displaystyle-i\int_{0}^{T\lambda^{-2}}\,ds\,\frac{\nu_{0}\big([a_{0},{\mathcal{H}}_{cub}(s)]\big)}{|\Lambda|}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{1}{N^{\frac{1}{2}}\lambda}\operatorname{Con}_{c}(f_{0})(T;\lambda)\,+\,\operatorname{err}^{(Bog)}_{1,c}(\frac{T}{\lambda^{2}};\Phi)\,,

where

(5.173)

\displaystyle|\operatorname{err}^{(Bog)}_{1,c}(\frac{T}{\lambda^{2}};\Phi)|\,\leq\,\frac{C_{\|\hat{v}\|_{2(\left\lfloor\frac{r}{2}\right\rfloor+1),w,c},\|f_{0}\|_{2(\left\lfloor\frac{r}{2}\right\rfloor+1),c},r}T}{N^{\frac{1}{2}}\lambda}\Big(\lambda\,+\,\frac{1}{|\Lambda|^{\frac{r}{6}}}\Big)\,,

(2)

(5.174)

\displaystyle-\int_{\Delta[t,2]}\,d\mathbf{s}_{2}\frac{\nu_{0}\big([[f[J],{\mathcal{H}}_{cub}(s_{1})],{\mathcal{H}}_{cub}(s_{2})]\big)}{|\Lambda|}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{1}{N}\operatorname{Bol}_{c}(f_{0})[J](T;\lambda)\,+\,J(0)|\Phi_{\frac{T}{\lambda^{2}}}|^{2}

\displaystyle+\,\operatorname{err}^{(Bog,\operatorname{Bol})}_{2,c}(\frac{T}{\lambda^{2}};f[J])\,+\,J(0)\operatorname{err}^{(Bog,\operatorname{Con})}_{2,c}(\frac{T}{\lambda^{2}};f)\,,

where

\displaystyle|\operatorname{err}^{(Bog,\operatorname{Bol})}_{2,c}(\frac{T}{\lambda^{2}};f[J])|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{C_{\|\hat{v}\|_{2(\left\lfloor\frac{r}{2}\right\rfloor+1),w,c},\|f_{0}\|_{2(\left\lfloor\frac{r}{2}\right\rfloor+1),c},r}\langle T\rangle^{r+2}\|J\|_{W^{2\left\lfloor\frac{r}{2}\right\rfloor+2,\infty}}}{N}

\displaystyle\Big(\frac{1}{\lambda^{2(r+1)}|\Lambda|^{\frac{r}{3}}}+\lambda|\log(\lambda)|\Big)\,,

(5.175)

\displaystyle|\operatorname{err}^{(Bog,\operatorname{Con})}_{2,c}(\frac{T}{\lambda^{2}};f[J])|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C_{\|\hat{v}\|_{2(\left\lfloor\frac{r}{2}\right\rfloor+1),w,c},\|f_{0}\|_{2(\left\lfloor\frac{r}{2}\right\rfloor+1),c},r}e^{C_{r}\|\hat{v}\|_{2(\left\lfloor\frac{r}{2}\right\rfloor+1),w,c}|\Lambda|/\lambda T}

\displaystyle\frac{\langle T\rangle^{4}|\Lambda|^{\frac{3}{2}}}{\lambda^{3}N^{\frac{3}{2}}}\Big(1+\frac{|\Lambda|}{N}\Big)^{2}\Big(1+\frac{|\Lambda|^{\frac{3}{2}}}{\lambda N^{\frac{1}{2}}}\Big)\,,

(3)

\displaystyle-i\int_{0}^{T\lambda^{-2}}\,ds\,\frac{\nu_{0}(g[J],{\mathcal{H}}_{quart}(s))}{|\Lambda|}

(5.176)

\displaystyle=\,\frac{1}{N}\int_{0}^{T}\,dS\,\mathrm{abs}_{quart,c}(f_{0})[J](S/\lambda^{2})\,+\,\operatorname{err}^{(dis)}_{1,c}(\frac{T}{\lambda^{2}};g[J])

with

(5.177)

\displaystyle|\operatorname{err}^{(dis)}_{1,c}(\frac{T}{\lambda^{2}};g[J])|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{C_{\|\hat{v}\|_{2(\left\lfloor\frac{r}{2}\right\rfloor+1),w,c},\|f_{0}\|_{2(\left\lfloor\frac{r}{2}\right\rfloor+1),c},r}\langle T\rangle^{r+1}\|J\|_{W^{2\left\lfloor\frac{r}{2}\right\rfloor+2,\infty}}}{N\lambda^{2r}|\Lambda|^{\frac{r}{3}}}\,,

(4)

\displaystyle-\int_{\Delta[T\lambda^{-2},2]}\,d\mathbf{s}_{2}\frac{\nu_{0}\big([[g[J],{\mathcal{H}}_{cub}(s_{1})],{\mathcal{H}}_{cub}(s_{2})]\big)}{|\Lambda|}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{N\lambda^{2}}\int_{\Delta[T,2]}\,d\mathbf{S}_{2}\,\big(\operatorname{col}_{c}(f_{0})[J](\mathbf{S}_{2}/\lambda^{2})+\mathrm{abs}_{cub,c}(f_{0})[J](\mathbf{S}_{2}/\lambda^{2})\big)

(5.178)

\displaystyle+\,\operatorname{err}^{(dis)}_{2,c}(\frac{T}{\lambda^{2}};g[J])\,+\,J(0)\Big(\Phi_{T\lambda^{-2}}\Big)^{2}\,+\,J(0)\operatorname{err}^{(Bog,\operatorname{Con})}_{2,c}(\frac{T}{\lambda^{2}};g)\,.

where

(5.179)

\displaystyle|\operatorname{err}^{(dis)}_{2,c}(\frac{T}{\lambda^{2}};g[J])|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{C_{\|\hat{v}\|_{2(\left\lfloor\frac{r}{2}\right\rfloor+1),w,c},\|f_{0}\|_{2(\left\lfloor\frac{r}{2}\right\rfloor+1),c},r}\langle T\rangle^{r+2}\|J\|_{W^{2\left\lfloor\frac{r}{2}\right\rfloor+2,\infty}}}{N\lambda^{2(r+1)}|\Lambda|^{\frac{r}{3}}}

and
\operatorname{err}^{(Bog,\operatorname{Con})}_{2,c}(\frac{T}{\lambda^{2}};g)
satisfies the same bound as
\operatorname{err}^{(Bog,\operatorname{Con})}_{2,c}(\frac{T}{\lambda^{2}};f)
.

Proof.

Recall from Proposition
5.2
that

(5.180)

\displaystyle-i\int_{0}^{T\lambda^{-2}}\,ds\,\frac{\nu_{0}\big([a_{0},{\mathcal{H}}_{cub}(s)]\big)}{|\Lambda|}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{1}{N^{\frac{1}{2}}\lambda}\operatorname{Con}_{d}(f_{0})(T;\lambda)\,+\,\operatorname{err}^{(Bog)}_{1,d}(\frac{T}{\lambda^{2}};\Phi)

with

(5.181)

\displaystyle|\operatorname{err}^{(Bog)}_{1,d}(\frac{T}{\lambda^{2}};\Phi)|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{C_{\|\hat{v}\|_{w,d},\|f_{0}\|_{d}}T}{N^{\frac{1}{2}}}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{C_{\|\hat{v}\|_{2(\left\lfloor\frac{r}{2}\right\rfloor+1),w,c},\|f_{0}\|_{2(\left\lfloor\frac{r}{2}\right\rfloor+1),c},r}T}{N^{\frac{1}{2}}}

due to Remark
5.6
.
Let

(5.182)

\displaystyle\operatorname{err}^{(dis)}_{1,c}(\frac{T}{\lambda^{2}};\Phi)\,:=\,\frac{1}{N^{\frac{1}{2}}\lambda}\Big(\operatorname{Con}_{d}(f_{0})(T;\lambda)-\operatorname{Con}_{c}(f_{0})(T;\lambda)\Big)\,.

Then Lemma
5.5
implies

(5.183)

\displaystyle|\operatorname{err}^{(dis)}_{1,c}(\frac{T}{\lambda^{2}};\Phi)|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{T}{N^{\frac{1}{2}}\lambda}\Big|\frac{(2\pi)^{3}}{|\Lambda|}\sum_{p\in{\Lambda^{*}}}\hat{v}(p)f_{0}(p)\,-\,\int_{{\mathbb{R}}^{3}}\,dp\,\hat{v}(p)f_{0}(p)\Big|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C_{r}\frac{T}{N^{\frac{1}{2}}\lambda|\Lambda|^{\frac{r}{6}}}\||\nabla|^{\frac{r}{2}}\hat{v}f_{0}\|_{1}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{C_{\|\hat{v}\|_{2(\left\lfloor\frac{r}{2}\right\rfloor+1),w,c},\|f_{0}\|_{2(\left\lfloor\frac{r}{2}\right\rfloor+1),c},r}T}{N^{\frac{1}{2}}\lambda|\Lambda|^{\frac{r}{6}}}\,.

Here, we used interpolation as in (
5.146
), together with the Leibniz rule. Applying (
5.181
), (
5.183
) implies

(5.184)

\displaystyle|\operatorname{err}^{(Bog)}_{1,c}(\frac{T}{\lambda^{2}};\Phi)|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle|\operatorname{err}^{(Bog)}_{1,d}(\frac{T}{\lambda^{2}};\Phi)|\,+\,|\operatorname{err}^{(dis)}_{1,c}(\frac{T}{\lambda^{2}};\Phi)|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{C_{\|\hat{v}\|_{2(\left\lfloor\frac{r}{2}\right\rfloor+1),w,c},\|f_{0}\|_{2(\left\lfloor\frac{r}{2}\right\rfloor+1),c},r}T}{N^{\frac{1}{2}}\lambda}\Big(\lambda\,+\,\frac{1}{|\Lambda|^{\frac{r}{6}}}\Big)\,.

Next, Proposition
5.2
together with Remark
5.6
yield that

(5.185)

\displaystyle-\int_{\Delta[T\lambda^{-2},2]}\,d\mathbf{s}_{2}\frac{\nu_{0}\big([[f[J],{\mathcal{H}}_{cub}(s_{1})],{\mathcal{H}}_{cub}(s_{2})]\big)}{|\Lambda|}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{1}{N}\big(\operatorname{Bol}_{d}(f_{0})[J](T;\lambda)\,+\,\lambda\operatorname{Bol}_{d}^{(1)}(f_{0})[J](T;\lambda)\,+\,\lambda^{2}\operatorname{Bol}_{d}^{(2)}(f_{0})[J](T;\lambda)\big)

\displaystyle+\,J(0)|\Phi_{\frac{T}{\lambda^{2}}}|^{2}\,+\,\operatorname{err}^{(Bog,\operatorname{Bol})}_{2,d}(\frac{T}{\lambda^{2}};f[J])\,+\,J(0)\operatorname{err}^{(Bog,\operatorname{Con})}_{2,d}(\frac{T}{\lambda^{2}};f)\,,

where

(5.186)

\displaystyle|\operatorname{err}^{(Bog,\operatorname{Bol})}_{2,d}(\frac{T}{\lambda^{2}};f[J])|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C_{\|\hat{v}\|_{2(\left\lfloor\frac{r}{2}\right\rfloor+1),w,c},\|f_{0}\|_{2(\left\lfloor\frac{r}{2}\right\rfloor+1),c},r}T^{2}\|J\|_{\ell^{\infty}({\Lambda^{*}})}\frac{\lambda}{N}\,,

(5.187)

\displaystyle|\operatorname{err}^{(Bog,\operatorname{Con})}_{2,d}(t;f)|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C_{\|\hat{v}\|_{2(\left\lfloor\frac{r}{2}\right\rfloor+1),w,c},\|f_{0}\|_{2(\left\lfloor\frac{r}{2}\right\rfloor+1),c},r}e^{C_{r}\|\hat{v}\|_{2(\left\lfloor\frac{r}{2}\right\rfloor+1),w,c}|\Lambda|/\lambda T}

\displaystyle\frac{\langle T\rangle^{4}|\Lambda|^{\frac{3}{2}}}{\lambda^{3}N^{\frac{3}{2}}}\Big(1+\frac{|\Lambda|}{N}\Big)^{2}\Big(1+\frac{|\Lambda|^{\frac{3}{2}}}{\lambda N^{\frac{1}{2}}}\Big)\,.

Let

(5.188)

\displaystyle\operatorname{err}^{(dis)}_{2,0}(\frac{T}{\lambda^{2}};f[J])

\displaystyle:=

\displaystyle\frac{1}{N}\Big(\operatorname{Bol}_{d}(f_{0})[J](T;\lambda)\,-\,\operatorname{Bol}_{c}(f_{0})[J](T;\lambda)\Big)\,,

(5.189)

\displaystyle\operatorname{err}^{(dis)}_{2,1}(\frac{T}{\lambda^{2}};f[J])

\displaystyle:=

\displaystyle\frac{\lambda}{N}\Big(\operatorname{Bol}_{d}^{(1)}(f_{0})[J](T;\lambda)\,-\,\operatorname{Bol}_{c}^{(1)}(f_{0})[J](T;\lambda)\Big)\,,

(5.190)

\displaystyle\operatorname{err}^{(dis)}_{2,2}(\frac{T}{\lambda^{2}};f[J])

\displaystyle:=

\displaystyle\frac{\lambda^{2}}{N}\Big(\operatorname{Bol}_{d}^{(2)}(f_{0})[J](T;\lambda)\,-\,\operatorname{Bol}_{c}^{(2)}(f_{0})[J](T;\lambda)\Big)\,.

Recalling (
5.50
), (
5.52
), (
5.56
), (
5.53
), and (
5.54
), Lemma
5.5
then implies

(5.191)

\displaystyle\operatorname{err}^{(dis)}_{2,j}(\frac{T}{\lambda^{2}};f[J])

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{\lambda^{j}}{N\lambda^{2r+2}|\Lambda|^{\frac{r}{3}}}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{C_{\|\hat{v}\|_{2(\left\lfloor\frac{r}{2}\right\rfloor+1),w,c},\|f_{0}\|_{2(\left\lfloor\frac{r}{2}\right\rfloor+1),c},r}\langle T\rangle^{r+2}\|J\|_{W^{2\left\lfloor\frac{r}{2}\right\rfloor+2,\infty}}}{N\lambda^{2r+2}|\Lambda|^{\frac{r}{3}}}

for
j\in\{0,1,2\}
. Lemma
C.5
yields

(5.192)

\displaystyle\frac{\lambda^{j}}{N}|\operatorname{Bol}_{c}^{(j)}(f_{0})[J](T;\lambda)|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C_{\|\hat{v}\|_{2(\left\lfloor\frac{r}{2}\right\rfloor+1),w,c},\|f_{0}\|_{2(\left\lfloor\frac{r}{2}\right\rfloor+1),c},r}T(1+\log(T))\|J\|_{C^{1}}\frac{\lambda|\log(\lambda)|}{N}

for
j\in\{1,2\}
, and all
\lambda>0
small enough, dependent on
\|\hat{v}\|_{2(\left\lfloor\frac{r}{2}\right\rfloor+1),w,c}
Let

(5.193)

\displaystyle\frac{1}{N}\operatorname{Bol}_{c}(f_{0})[J](T;\lambda)\,+\,\operatorname{err}^{(Bog,\operatorname{Bol})}_{2,c}(\frac{T}{\lambda^{2}};f[J])

\displaystyle:=

\displaystyle\frac{1}{N}\big(\operatorname{Bol}_{d}(f_{0})[J](T;\lambda)\,+\,\lambda\operatorname{Bol}_{d}^{(1)}(f_{0})[J](T;\lambda)\,

\displaystyle+\,\lambda^{2}\operatorname{Bol}_{d}^{(2)}(f_{0})[J](T;\lambda)\big)\,+\,\operatorname{err}^{(Bog,\operatorname{Bol})}_{2,d}(\frac{T}{\lambda^{2}};f[J])\,.

Collecting (
5.186
), (
5.191
), and (
5.192
), we thus proved that

(5.194)

\displaystyle|\operatorname{err}^{(Bog,\operatorname{Bol})}_{2,c}(\frac{T}{\lambda^{2}};f[J])|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C_{\|\hat{v}\|_{2(\left\lfloor\frac{r}{2}\right\rfloor+1),w,c},\|f_{0}\|_{2(\left\lfloor\frac{r}{2}\right\rfloor+1),c},r}\Big(\frac{T^{2}\|J\|_{\ell^{\infty}({\Lambda^{*}})}\lambda}{N}

\displaystyle+\,\frac{\langle T\rangle^{r+2}\|J\|_{W^{2\left\lfloor\frac{r}{2}\right\rfloor+2,\infty}}}{N\lambda^{2(r+1)}|\Lambda|^{\frac{r}{3}}}

\displaystyle+\,\frac{\lambda|\log(\lambda)|T(1+\log(T))\|J\|_{W^{1,\infty}}}{N}\Big)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{C_{\|\hat{v}\|_{2(\left\lfloor\frac{r}{2}\right\rfloor+1),w,c},\|f_{0}\|_{2(\left\lfloor\frac{r}{2}\right\rfloor+1),c},r}\langle T\rangle^{r+2}\|J\|_{W^{2\left\lfloor\frac{r}{2}\right\rfloor+2,\infty}}}{N}

\displaystyle\Big(\frac{1}{\lambda^{2(r+1)}|\Lambda|^{\frac{r}{3}}}+\lambda|\log(\lambda)|\Big)\,.

Finally, we compute the discretization error in the dynamics of
g
. Let

(5.195)

\displaystyle\operatorname{err}^{(dis)}_{1,c}(\frac{T}{\lambda^{2}};g[J])

\displaystyle:=

\displaystyle\frac{1}{N}\int_{0}^{T}\,dS\,\big(\mathrm{abs}_{quart,d}(f_{0})[J](S/\lambda^{2})\,-\,\mathrm{abs}_{quart,c}(f_{0})[J](S/\lambda^{2})\big)\,.

Lemma
5.5
together with Proposition
5.2
implies

(5.196)

\displaystyle|\operatorname{err}^{(dis)}_{1,c}(\frac{T}{\lambda^{2}};g[J])|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{C_{\|\hat{v}\|_{2(\left\lfloor\frac{r}{2}\right\rfloor+1),w,c},\|f_{0}\|_{2(\left\lfloor\frac{r}{2}\right\rfloor+1),c},r}\langle T\rangle^{r+1}\|J\|_{W^{2\left\lfloor\frac{r}{2}\right\rfloor+2,\infty}}}{N\lambda^{2r}|\Lambda|^{\frac{r}{3}}}\,.

Moreover, let

(5.197)

\displaystyle\operatorname{err}^{(dis)}_{2,c}(\frac{T}{\lambda^{2}};g[J])

\displaystyle:=

\displaystyle\frac{1}{N\lambda^{2}}\int_{\Delta[T,2]}\,d\mathbf{S}_{2}\,\big(\operatorname{col}_{d}(f_{0})[J](\mathbf{S}_{2}/\lambda^{2}))\,-\,\operatorname{col}_{c}(f_{0})[J](\mathbf{S}_{2}/\lambda^{2}))

\displaystyle+\,\mathrm{abs}_{cub,d}(f_{0})[J](\mathbf{S}_{2}/\lambda^{2}))\,-\,\mathrm{abs}_{cub,c}(f_{0})[J](\mathbf{S}_{2}/\lambda^{2}))\big)

Again, applying Lemma
5.5
together with Proposition
5.2
, yields

(5.198)

\displaystyle|\operatorname{err}^{(dis)}_{2,c}(\frac{T}{\lambda^{2}};g[J])|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{C_{\|\hat{v}\|_{2(\left\lfloor\frac{r}{2}\right\rfloor+1),w,c},\|f_{0}\|_{2(\left\lfloor\frac{r}{2}\right\rfloor+1),c},r}\langle T\rangle^{r+2}\|J\|_{W^{2\left\lfloor\frac{r}{2}\right\rfloor+2,\infty}}}{N\lambda^{2r+2}|\Lambda|^{\frac{r}{3}}}

for all
\lambda>0
small enough, dependent on
\|\hat{v}\|_{2(\left\lfloor\frac{r}{2}\right\rfloor+1),w,c}
. This concludes the proof.
∎

For the next result, observe that

(5.199)

\displaystyle{\Delta_{cub}E}\,=\,E(p_{1})+E(p_{2})-E(p_{1}+p_{2})\,=\,-p_{1}\cdot p_{2}\,.

Let

(5.200)

\displaystyle\operatorname{Bol}_{fec}(f_{0})[J](T)

\displaystyle:=

\displaystyle\frac{\pi T}{(2\pi)^{6}}\int_{p_{1}\perp p_{2}}\,d{\mathcal{H}}^{5}(\mathbf{p}_{2})\,{\Delta_{cub}J}(\mathbf{p}_{2})\frac{(\hat{v}(p_{1})+\hat{v}(p_{2}))^{2}}{|\mathbf{p}_{2}|}

\displaystyle\big(\widetilde{f_{0}}(p_{1})\widetilde{f_{0}}(p_{2})f_{0}(p_{1}+p_{2})\,-\,f_{0}(p_{1})f_{0}(p_{2})\widetilde{f_{0}}(p_{1}+p_{2})\big)

be the Boltzmann operator with energy conserving collision kernel for the free energy dispersion
E(p)=\frac{|p|^{2}}{2}
.

Proposition 5.8
.

We have that

(5.201)

\displaystyle\operatorname{Bol}_{c}(f_{0})[J](T;\lambda)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{\pi T}{(2\pi)^{6}}\int\,d\mathbf{p}_{2}\,\delta_{\frac{2\lambda^{2}}{T}}({\Delta_{cub}\Omega}(\mathbf{p}_{2})){\Delta_{cub}J}(\mathbf{p}_{2})(\hat{v}(p_{1})+\hat{v}(p_{2}))^{2}

\displaystyle\big(\widetilde{f_{0}}(p_{1})\widetilde{f_{0}}(p_{2})f_{0}(p_{1}+p_{2})\,-\,f_{0}(p_{1})f_{0}(p_{2})\widetilde{f_{0}}(p_{1}+p_{2})\big)\,,

where
\delta_{\varepsilon}(x):=\frac{{\varepsilon}\sin^{2}(x/{\varepsilon})}{\pi x^{2}}
. Moreover, approximating
\operatorname{Bol}(f_{0})[J](T\lambda^{-2})
with its continuum counterpart, i.e., replacing
\frac{1}{|\Lambda|}\sum_{{\Lambda^{*}}}
by the (Lebesgue-) integral
\frac{1}{(2\pi)^{3}}\int_{{\mathbb{R}}^{3}}
, we find that

(5.202)

\displaystyle\frac{1}{N}\operatorname{Bol}_{c}(f_{0})[J](T;\lambda)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{1}{N}\operatorname{Bol}_{fec}(f_{0})[J](T)\,+\,\operatorname{err}^{(fec)}_{2,c}(\frac{T}{\lambda^{2}};f[J])\,,

where

(5.203)

\displaystyle|\operatorname{err}^{(fec)}_{2,c}(\frac{T}{\lambda^{2}};f[J])|\,\leq\,C_{\|\hat{v}\|_{2(\left\lfloor\frac{r}{2}\right\rfloor+1),w,c},\|f_{0}\|_{2(\left\lfloor\frac{r}{2}\right\rfloor+1),c},r}\langle T\rangle\|J\|_{W^{2,\infty}}\frac{\lambda}{N}

for all
\lambda>0
small enough, dependent on
\|\hat{v}\|_{2(\left\lfloor\frac{r}{2}\right\rfloor+1),w,c}
.

Proof.

Observe that
{\Delta_{cub}E}=0
is equivalent to
p_{1}\perp p_{2}
, see (
5.199
), and that

(5.204)

\displaystyle|\nabla{\Delta_{cub}E}(\mathbf{p}_{2})|\,=\,\Big|\begin{pmatrix}-p_{2}\\
-p_{1}\end{pmatrix}\Big|\,=\,|\mathbf{p}_{2}|\,.

Moreover, notice that for any
\omega\in{\mathbb{R}}\setminus\{0\}
, we have

(5.205)

\displaystyle\mathrm{Re}\int_{\Delta[t,2]}d\mathbf{s}_{2}\,e^{-i\omega(s_{1}-s_{2})}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{1-\cos(\omega t)}{\omega^{2}}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle 2\frac{\sin^{2}(\frac{\omega t}{2})}{\omega^{2}}\,.

Observe that
\delta_{a}(x)=\frac{a\sin^{2}(x/a)}{\pi x^{2}}
defines an approximate identity in the sense that we have

(5.206)

\displaystyle\delta_{a}(x)

\displaystyle\geq

\displaystyle 0\quad\forall a>0\,,

(5.207)

\displaystyle\int\,dx\,\delta_{a}(x)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle 1\quad\forall a>0\,,

(5.208)

\displaystyle\int_{|x|>\rho}\,dx\,\delta_{a}(x)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{Ca}{\rho}\quad\forall a,\rho>0\,.

With that, we obtain

(5.209)

\displaystyle\operatorname{Bol}(f_{0})[J](T;\lambda)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{\pi T}{(2\pi)^{6}}\int\,d\mathbf{p}_{2}\,\delta_{\frac{2\lambda^{2}}{T}}({\Delta_{cub}\Omega}(\mathbf{p}_{2})){\Delta_{cub}J}(\mathbf{p}_{2})(\hat{v}(p_{1})+\hat{v}(p_{2}))^{2}

\displaystyle\big(f_{0}(p_{1}+p_{2})+f_{0}(p_{1})f_{0}(p_{1}+p_{2})+f_{0}(p_{1}+p_{2})f_{0}(p_{2})

\displaystyle-f_{0}(p_{1})f_{0}(p_{2})\big)

This proves the first part of the statement.

For the second part, assume
\mathbf{p}_{2}
ranges over
{\mathbb{R}}^{6}
. Emphasizing the dependence of
{\mathcal{E}}(\lambda,\mathbf{p}_{2}):={\Delta_{cub}\Omega}_{\lambda}(\mathbf{p}_{2})
on
\lambda>0
, we abbreviate (
5.209
) as

(5.210)

\displaystyle\lambda^{2}\operatorname{Bol}(f_{0})[J](T\lambda^{-2})

\displaystyle=:

\displaystyle\int\,d\mathbf{p}_{2}\,\delta_{\frac{2\lambda^{2}}{T}}({\mathcal{E}}(\lambda,\mathbf{p}_{2}))H(\mathbf{p}_{2})\,,

where we emphasize. Observe that
{\mathcal{E}}(0,\mathbf{p}_{2})={\Delta_{cub}E}(\mathbf{p}_{2})
. Using the Fundamental Theorem of Calculus by the Coarea Formula, we obtain that

(5.211)

\displaystyle N\operatorname{err}^{(fd)}_{2}(\frac{T}{\lambda^{2}};f[J])

\displaystyle:=

\displaystyle\int\,d\mathbf{p}_{2}\,\Big[\delta_{\frac{2\lambda^{2}}{T}}({\mathcal{E}}(\lambda,\mathbf{p}_{2}))\,-\,\delta_{\frac{2\lambda^{2}}{T}}({\mathcal{E}}(0,\mathbf{p}_{2}))\Big]H(\mathbf{p}_{2})

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{\lambda}\,d\tau\,\delta_{\frac{2\lambda^{2}}{T}}^{\prime}({\mathcal{E}}(\tau,\mathbf{p}_{2}))\partial_{\tau}{\mathcal{E}}(\tau,\mathbf{p}_{2})H(\mathbf{p}_{2})

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{\lambda}\,d\tau\,\int\,d\omega\,\delta_{\frac{2\lambda^{2}}{T}}^{\prime}(\omega)

\displaystyle\int_{{\mathcal{E}}(\tau,\mathbf{p}_{2})=\omega}\,d{\mathcal{H}}^{5}\,\frac{\partial_{\tau}{\mathcal{E}}(\tau,\mathbf{p}_{2})H(\mathbf{p}_{2})}{|\nabla_{\mathbf{p}_{2}}{\mathcal{E}}(\tau,\mathbf{p}_{2})|}\,.

We prove in Lemma
C.6
that

(5.212)

\displaystyle|\operatorname{err}^{(fd)}_{2}(t;f[J])|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C_{\|\hat{v}\|_{2(\left\lfloor\frac{r}{2}\right\rfloor+1),w,c},\|f_{0}\|_{2(\left\lfloor\frac{r}{2}\right\rfloor+1),c},r}T\|J\|_{W^{2,\infty}}\frac{\lambda}{N}

for all
\lambda>0
small enough, dependent on
\|\hat{v}\|_{2(\left\lfloor\frac{r}{2}\right\rfloor+1),w,c}
.

Next, using (
5.207
) together with the Coarea Formula, we have that

(5.213)

\displaystyle N\operatorname{err}^{(ec)}_{2}(\frac{T}{\lambda^{2}};f[J])

\displaystyle:=

\displaystyle\int\,d\mathbf{p}_{2}\,\delta_{\frac{2\lambda^{2}}{T}}({\Delta_{cub}E}(\mathbf{p}_{2}))H(\mathbf{p}_{2})

\displaystyle-\,\int_{{\Delta_{cub}E}=0}\,d{\mathcal{H}}^{5}\,\frac{H}{|\nabla{\Delta_{cub}E}|}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\int\,d\omega\,\delta_{\frac{2\lambda^{2}}{T}}(\omega)\Big(\int_{{\Delta_{cub}E}=\omega}\,d{\mathcal{H}}^{5}\,\frac{H}{|\nabla{\Delta_{cub}E}|}

\displaystyle+\,\int_{{\Delta_{cub}E}=0}\,d{\mathcal{H}}^{5}\,\frac{H}{|\nabla{\Delta_{cub}E}|}\Big)\,.

We prove in Lemma
C.7
that

(5.214)

\displaystyle|\operatorname{err}^{(ec)}_{2}(\frac{T}{\lambda^{2}};f[J])|\,\leq\,C_{\|\hat{v}\|_{2(\left\lfloor\frac{r}{2}\right\rfloor+1),w,c},\|f_{0}\|_{2(\left\lfloor\frac{r}{2}\right\rfloor+1),c},r}\sqrt{T}\|J\|_{W^{2,\infty}}\frac{\lambda}{N}

for all
\lambda>0
small enough, dependent on
\|\hat{v}\|_{2(\left\lfloor\frac{r}{2}\right\rfloor+1),w,c}
. In particular, we have that

(5.215)

\displaystyle\operatorname{err}^{(fec)}_{2,c}(\frac{T}{\lambda^{2}};f[J])\,=\,\operatorname{err}^{(fd)}_{2}(\frac{T}{\lambda^{2}};f[J])\,+\,\operatorname{err}^{(ec)}_{2}(\frac{T}{\lambda^{2}};f[J])

satisfies

(5.216)

\displaystyle|\operatorname{err}^{(fec)}_{2,c}(\frac{T}{\lambda^{2}};f[J])|\,\leq\,C_{\|\hat{v}\|_{2(\left\lfloor\frac{r}{2}\right\rfloor+1),w,c},\|f_{0}\|_{2(\left\lfloor\frac{r}{2}\right\rfloor+1),c},r}\langle T\rangle\|J\|_{W^{2,\infty}}\frac{\lambda}{N}\,.

This concludes the proof.
∎

5.3.
Centered expectations

In order to resolve the fluctuations around the HFB dynamics, we have to consider the dynamics relative to the condensate term, i.e.,

(5.217)

\displaystyle f_{t}^{(\Phi)}(p)

\displaystyle:=

\displaystyle f_{t}(p)\,-\,|\Phi_{t}|^{2}\delta(p)\,.

Proposition 5.9
.

Let
T>0
,
\lambda\in(0,1)
and
|\Lambda|\geq 1
. Let
J\in L^{\infty}({\mathbb{R}}^{3};{\mathbb{R}})
. Then the following holds.

(1)

(5.218)

\displaystyle\frac{1}{N^{\frac{1}{2}}\lambda}\operatorname{Con}_{d}(f_{0})(T;\lambda)\,=\,\frac{1}{N^{\frac{1}{2}}\lambda}\operatorname{Con}_{d}(f^{(\Phi)})(T;\lambda)\,+\,\operatorname{err}^{(cen)}_{1,d}(\frac{T}{\lambda^{2}};\Phi)\,,

where

(5.219)

\displaystyle|\operatorname{err}^{(cen)}_{1,d}(\frac{T}{\lambda^{2}};\Phi)|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C_{\|\hat{v}\|_{w,d},\|f_{0}\|_{d}}\langle T\rangle^{3}\frac{|\Lambda|^{\frac{3}{2}}e^{C\|\hat{v}\|_{w,d}|\Lambda|/\lambda T}}{N\lambda^{2}}

\displaystyle\Big(1+\frac{|\Lambda|}{N}\Big)\Big(1\,+\,\frac{1}{N^{\frac{1}{2}}\lambda|\Lambda|^{\frac{3}{2}}}\Big)\,,

(2)

(a)

(5.220)

\displaystyle\frac{1}{N}\big(\operatorname{Bol}_{d}(f_{0})[J](T;\lambda)\,+\,\lambda\operatorname{Bol}_{d}^{(1)}(f_{0})[J](T;\lambda)\,+\,\lambda^{2}\operatorname{Bol}_{d}^{(2)}(f_{0})[J](T;\lambda)\big)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{1}{N}\big(\operatorname{Bol}_{d}(f^{(\Phi)})[J](T;\lambda)\,+\,\lambda\operatorname{Bol}_{d}^{(1)}(f^{(\Phi)})[J](T;\lambda)

\displaystyle+\,\lambda^{2}\operatorname{Bol}_{d}^{(2)}(f^{(\Phi)})[J](T;\lambda)\big)\,+\,\operatorname{err}^{(cen)}_{2,d}(\frac{T}{\lambda^{2}};f[J])\,,

with

(5.221)

\displaystyle|\operatorname{err}^{(cen)}_{2,d}(\frac{T}{\lambda^{2}};f[J])|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C_{\|\hat{v}\|_{w,d},\|f_{0}\|_{d}}\|J\|_{\ell^{\infty}({\Lambda^{*}})}\langle T\rangle^{4}\frac{|\Lambda|^{\frac{3}{2}}e^{C\|\hat{v}\|_{w,d}|\Lambda|/\lambda T}}{N^{\frac{3}{2}}\lambda^{3}}

\displaystyle\Big(1+\frac{|\Lambda|}{N}\Big)\Big(1\,+\,\frac{1}{N^{\frac{1}{2}}\lambda|\Lambda|^{\frac{3}{2}}}\Big)

\displaystyle\Big[1\,+\,\langle T\rangle^{2}\frac{|\Lambda|^{\frac{3}{2}}e^{C\|\hat{v}\|_{w,d}|\Lambda|/\lambda T}}{N^{\frac{1}{2}}\lambda}\Big(1+\frac{|\Lambda|}{N}\Big)\Big(1\,+\,\frac{1}{N^{\frac{1}{2}}\lambda|\Lambda|^{\frac{3}{2}}}\Big)\Big]\,,

(b)

(5.222)

\displaystyle\frac{1}{N}\operatorname{Bol}_{c}(f_{0})[J](T;\lambda)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{1}{N}\operatorname{Bol}_{c}(f^{(\Phi)})[J](T;\lambda)\,+\,\operatorname{err}^{(cen)}_{2,c}(\frac{T}{\lambda^{2}};f[J])\,,

where
\operatorname{err}^{(cen)}_{2,c}(\frac{T}{\lambda^{2}};f[J])
satisfies the same bound as
\operatorname{err}^{(cen)}_{2,d}(\frac{T}{\lambda^{2}};f[J])
,

(3)

(a)

(5.223)

\displaystyle\frac{1}{N}\int_{0}^{T}\,dS\,\mathrm{abs}_{quart,d}(f_{0})[J](S/\lambda^{2})

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{1}{N}\int_{0}^{T}\,dS\,\mathrm{abs}_{quart,d}(f^{(\Phi)})[J](S/\lambda^{2})\,+\,\operatorname{err}^{(fd)}_{1,d}(\frac{T}{\lambda^{2}};g[J])

with

(5.224)

\displaystyle|\operatorname{err}^{(cen)}_{1,d}(\frac{T}{\lambda^{2}};g[J])|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C_{\|\hat{v}\|_{w,d},\|f_{0}\|_{d}}\|J\|_{\ell^{\infty}({\Lambda^{*}})}\langle T\rangle^{3}\frac{|\Lambda|^{\frac{3}{2}}e^{C\|\hat{v}\|_{w,d}|\Lambda|/\lambda T}}{N^{\frac{3}{2}}\lambda}

\displaystyle\Big(1+\frac{|\Lambda|}{N}\Big)\Big(1\,+\,\frac{1}{N^{\frac{1}{2}}\lambda|\Lambda|^{\frac{3}{2}}}\Big)

\displaystyle\Big[1\,+\,\langle T\rangle^{2}\frac{|\Lambda|^{\frac{3}{2}}e^{C\|\hat{v}\|_{w,d}|\Lambda|/\lambda T}}{N^{\frac{1}{2}}\lambda}\Big(1+\frac{|\Lambda|}{N}\Big)\Big(1\,+\,\frac{1}{N^{\frac{1}{2}}\lambda|\Lambda|^{\frac{3}{2}}}\Big)\Big]\,,

(b)

(5.225)

\displaystyle\frac{1}{N\lambda^{2}}\int_{\Delta[T,2]}\,d\mathbf{S}_{2}\,\big(\operatorname{col}_{d}(f_{0})[J](\mathbf{S}_{2}/\lambda^{2})+\mathrm{abs}_{cub,d}(f_{0})[J](\mathbf{S}_{2}/\lambda^{2})\big)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{1}{N\lambda^{2}}\int_{\Delta[T,2]}\,d\mathbf{S}_{2}\,\big(\operatorname{col}_{d}(f^{(\Phi)})[J](\mathbf{S}_{2}/\lambda^{2})

\displaystyle+\mathrm{abs}_{cub,d}(f^{(\Phi)})[J](\mathbf{S}_{2}/\lambda^{2})\big)\,+\,\operatorname{err}^{(cen)}_{2,d}(\frac{T}{\lambda^{2}};g[J])

with

(5.226)

\displaystyle|\operatorname{err}^{(cen)}_{2,d}(\frac{T}{\lambda^{2}};g[J])|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C_{\|\hat{v}\|_{w,d},\|f_{0}\|_{d}}\|J\|_{\ell^{\infty}({\Lambda^{*}})}\langle T\rangle^{3}\frac{|\Lambda|^{\frac{3}{2}}e^{C\|\hat{v}\|_{w,d}|\Lambda|/\lambda T}}{N^{\frac{3}{2}}\lambda^{3}}

\displaystyle\Big(1+\frac{|\Lambda|}{N}\Big)\Big(1\,+\,\frac{1}{N^{\frac{1}{2}}\lambda|\Lambda|^{\frac{3}{2}}}\Big)

\displaystyle\Big[1\,+\,\langle T\rangle^{2}\frac{|\Lambda|^{\frac{3}{2}}e^{C\|\hat{v}\|_{w,d}|\Lambda|/\lambda T}}{N^{\frac{1}{2}}\lambda}\Big(1+\frac{|\Lambda|}{N}\Big)\Big(1\,+\,\frac{1}{N^{\frac{1}{2}}\lambda|\Lambda|^{\frac{3}{2}}}\Big)\Big]\,.

The analogous statements hold true for the continuum approximation if one replaces
(\|\hat{v}\|_{w,d},\|f_{0}\|_{d})
on the RHS of the inequalities by
(C_{r}\|\hat{v}\|_{2(\left\lfloor\frac{r}{2}\right\rfloor+1),w,c},C_{r}\|f_{0}\|_{2(\left\lfloor\frac{r}{2}\right\rfloor+1),c})
.

Proof.

In the notation of the proof, we will focus on the discrete case. The bounds for the continuum approximation follow by replacing
(\|\hat{v}\|_{w,d},\|f_{0}\|_{d})
by

(5.227)

\displaystyle(C_{r}\|\hat{v}\|_{2(\left\lfloor\frac{r}{2}\right\rfloor+1),w,c},C_{r}\|f_{0}\|_{2(\left\lfloor\frac{r}{2}\right\rfloor+1),c})\,,

see Remark
5.6
.

Let
t=T\lambda^{-2}
. Next, Lemma
3.1
and the definition (
5.217
) of
f^{(\Phi)}
imply that

(5.228)

\displaystyle\int\,dp\,J(p)\big(f_{0}(p)-f_{t}^{(\Phi)}(p)\big)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\int\,dp\,J(p)\big(f_{0}(p)-f_{t}(p)\big)\,+\,J(0)|\Phi_{t}|^{2}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-i\int_{0}^{t}\,ds\,\frac{\nu_{s}([f[J],{\mathcal{H}}_{I}(s)])}{|\Lambda|}\,+\,J(0)|\Phi_{t}|^{2}\,.

Using (
5.13
), we have the estimate

(5.229)

\displaystyle\Big|\frac{\nu_{s}([f[J],{\mathcal{H}}_{I}(s)])}{|\Lambda|}\Big|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C_{\|\hat{v}\|_{w,d},\|f_{0}\|_{d}}e^{C\|f_{0}\|_{d}|\Lambda|/\lambda T}\|J\|_{\ell^{\infty}({\Lambda^{*}})}\frac{\lambda|\Lambda|^{\frac{3}{2}}}{N^{\frac{1}{2}}}\Big(1\,+\,\frac{|\Lambda|^{\frac{1}{2}}}{N^{\frac{1}{2}}}\Big)\,.

We have that

(5.230)

\displaystyle\Phi_{t}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-i\int_{0}^{t}\,ds\,\frac{\nu_{s}\big([a_{0},{\mathcal{H}}_{I}(s)]\big)}{|\Lambda|}\,.

Recall from (
5.18
) and (
5.26
) that

(5.231)

\displaystyle[a_{0},{\mathcal{H}}_{cub}(s)]

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle f[J_{1}(s)]+g[J_{2}(s)]+g^{*}[J_{3}(s)]+J_{4}(s)\,,

(5.232)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{1}{N^{\frac{1}{2}}}{\mathcal{H}}_{cub}^{(1)}(s)\,.

Collecting (
5.24
), (
5.25
), and using Lemma
4.4
, (
5.13
) yields

(5.233)

\displaystyle|\Phi_{t}|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C_{\|\hat{v}\|_{w,d},\|f_{0}\|_{d}}Te^{C\|f_{0}\|_{d}|\Lambda|/\lambda T}\frac{1}{N^{\frac{1}{2}}\lambda}\Big(1\,+\,\frac{|\Lambda|^{\frac{1}{2}}}{N^{\frac{1}{2}}}\Big)\,.

Then (
5.228
), (
5.229
), and (
5.233
) yield that

(5.234)

\displaystyle\Big|\int\,dp\,J(p)\big(f_{0}(p)-f_{t}^{(\Phi)}(p)\big)\Big|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C_{\|\hat{v}\|_{w,d},\|f_{0}\|_{d}}\langle T\rangle^{2}\|J\|_{\ell^{\infty}({\Lambda^{*}})}\frac{|\Lambda|^{\frac{3}{2}}e^{C\|\hat{v}\|_{w,d}|\Lambda|/\lambda T}}{N^{\frac{1}{2}}\lambda}

\displaystyle\Big(1+\frac{|\Lambda|}{N}\Big)\Big(1\,+\,\frac{1}{N^{\frac{1}{2}}\lambda|\Lambda|^{\frac{3}{2}}}\Big)\,.

As a consequence of (
5.234
) and recalling definition (
5.49
) of
\operatorname{Con}(f)
, we find that

(5.235)

\displaystyle|\operatorname{err}^{(cen)}_{1}(\frac{T}{\lambda^{2}};\Phi)|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C_{\|\hat{v}\|_{w,d},\|f_{0}\|_{d}}\langle T\rangle^{3}\frac{|\Lambda|^{\frac{3}{2}}e^{C\|\hat{v}\|_{w,d}|\Lambda|/\lambda T}}{N\lambda^{2}}

\displaystyle\Big(1+\frac{|\Lambda|}{N}\Big)\Big(1\,+\,\frac{1}{N^{\frac{1}{2}}\lambda|\Lambda|^{\frac{3}{2}}}\Big)\,.

After substitution and using the notation in the proof of Proposition
5.1
, the terms in
\operatorname{bol}^{(j)}(f_{0})[J](t)-\operatorname{bol}^{(j)}(f^{(\Phi)})[J](t)
and
\operatorname{col}^{(k)}(f_{0})[J](t)-\operatorname{col}^{(k)}(f^{(\Phi)})[J](t)
are of the form

(5.236)

\displaystyle\int\,d\mathbf{p}_{2}\,H_{1}(p_{1})H_{2}(p_{2})H_{3}(p_{1}\pm p_{2})f_{s_{2}}^{(\Phi)}(p_{1})(f_{0}(p_{2})-f_{s_{2}}^{(\Phi)}(p_{2}))\,,

(5.237)

\displaystyle\int\,d\mathbf{p}_{2}\,H_{1}(p_{1})H_{2}(p_{2})H_{3}(p_{1}\pm p_{2})f_{0}(p_{1})(f_{0}(p_{2})-f_{s_{2}}^{(\Phi)}(p_{2}))\,,

(5.238)

\displaystyle\int\,d\mathbf{p}_{2}\,H_{1}(p_{1})H_{2}(p_{2})H_{3}(p_{12})(f_{0}(p_{12})-f_{s_{2}}^{(\Phi)}(p_{12}))\,,

where
H_{j}\in L^{\infty}_{\mathbf{s}_{2}}({\mathbb{R}}^{2};L^{\infty}_{p}({\mathbb{R}}^{3}))
can differ in every line and in the last line, we require
H_{1}\in L^{\infty}_{\mathbf{s}_{2}}({\mathbb{R}}^{2};L^{1}_{p}({\mathbb{R}}^{3}))
. We have that
s_{2}\leq T\lambda^{-2}
with
\lambda\in(0,1)
.

Observe that

(5.239)

\displaystyle\int\,dp\,|H_{1}(p)|f_{0}(p)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{\nu_{0}(f(|H_{1}|))}{|\Lambda|}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{\|H_{1}\|_{\infty}\nu_{0}(\mathcal{N}_{b})}{|\Lambda|}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\|f_{0}\|_{d}\|H_{1}\|_{\infty}

due to Lemma
4.7
and

(5.240)

\displaystyle\frac{\nu_{0}(\mathcal{N}_{b})}{|\Lambda|}\,=\,\int\,dp\,f_{0}(p)\,\leq\,\|f_{0}\|_{d}\,.

(
5.234
) and (
5.239
) imply that the terms of the form (
5.237
) satisfy

(5.241)

\displaystyle|\int\,d\mathbf{p}_{2}\,H_{1}(p_{1})H_{2}(p_{2})H_{3}(p_{1}\pm p_{2})f_{0}(p_{1})(f_{0}(p_{2})-f_{s_{2}}^{(\Phi)}(p_{2}))|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\int\,dp_{1}\,|H_{1}(p_{1})|f_{0}(p_{1})\Big|\int\,dp_{2}\,H_{2}(p_{2})H_{3}(p_{1}\pm p_{2})(f_{0}(p_{2})-f_{s_{2}}^{(\Phi)}(p_{2}))\Big|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C_{\|\hat{v}\|_{w,d},\|f_{0}\|_{d}}\|H_{1}\|_{\infty}\|H_{2}\|_{\infty}\|H_{3}\|_{\infty}\langle T\rangle^{2}\frac{|\Lambda|^{\frac{3}{2}}e^{C\|\hat{v}\|_{w,d}|\Lambda|/\lambda T}}{N^{\frac{1}{2}}\lambda}

\displaystyle\Big(1+\frac{|\Lambda|}{N}\Big)\Big(1\,+\,\frac{1}{N^{\frac{1}{2}}\lambda|\Lambda|^{\frac{3}{2}}}\Big)\,.

For (
5.236
), (
5.234
) and (
5.241
) yield that

(5.242)

\displaystyle|\int\,d\mathbf{p}_{2}\,H_{1}(p_{1})H_{2}(p_{2})H_{3}(p_{1}\pm p_{2})f_{s_{2}}^{(\Phi)}(p_{1})(f_{0}(p_{2})-f_{s_{2}}^{(\Phi)}(p_{2}))|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle|\int\,d\mathbf{p}_{2}\,H_{1}(p_{1})H_{2}(p_{2})H_{3}(p_{1}\pm p_{2})f_{0}(p_{1})(f_{0}(p_{2})-f_{s_{2}}^{(\Phi)}(p_{2}))|

\displaystyle+|\int\,d\mathbf{p}_{2}\,H_{1}(p_{1})H_{2}(p_{2})H_{3}(p_{1}\pm p_{2})\big(f_{s_{2}}^{(\Phi)}(p_{1})-f_{0}(p_{1})\big)(f_{0}(p_{2})-f_{s_{2}}^{(\Phi)}(p_{2}))|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C_{\|\hat{v}\|_{w,d},\|f_{0}\|_{d}}\|H_{1}\|_{\infty}\langle T\rangle^{2}\frac{|\Lambda|^{\frac{3}{2}}e^{C\|\hat{v}\|_{w,d}|\Lambda|/\lambda T}}{N^{\frac{1}{2}}\lambda}

\displaystyle\Big(1+\frac{|\Lambda|}{N}\Big)\Big(1\,+\,\frac{1}{N^{\frac{1}{2}}\lambda|\Lambda|^{\frac{3}{2}}}\Big)

\displaystyle\Big(\|H_{2}\|_{\infty}\|H_{3}\|_{\infty}\,+\,\sup_{p_{1}}\Big|\int\,dp_{2}\,H_{2}(p_{2})H_{3}(p_{1}\pm p_{2})(f_{0}(p_{2})-f_{s_{2}}^{(\Phi)}(p_{2}))\Big|\Big)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C_{\|\hat{v}\|_{w,d},\|f_{0}\|_{d}}\|H_{1}\|_{\infty}\|H_{2}\|_{\infty}\|H_{3}\|_{\infty}\langle T\rangle^{2}\frac{|\Lambda|^{\frac{3}{2}}e^{C\|\hat{v}\|_{w,d}|\Lambda|/\lambda T}}{N^{\frac{1}{2}}\lambda}

\displaystyle\Big(1+\frac{|\Lambda|}{N}\Big)\Big(1\,+\,\frac{1}{N^{\frac{1}{2}}\lambda|\Lambda|^{\frac{3}{2}}}\Big)

\displaystyle\Big[1\,+\,\langle T\rangle^{2}\frac{|\Lambda|^{\frac{3}{2}}e^{C\|\hat{v}\|_{w,d}|\Lambda|/\lambda T}}{N^{\frac{1}{2}}\lambda}\Big(1+\frac{|\Lambda|}{N}\Big)\Big(1\,+\,\frac{1}{N^{\frac{1}{2}}\lambda|\Lambda|^{\frac{3}{2}}}\Big)\Big]\,.

Employing (
5.234
) again, we estimate (
5.238
