) by

(5.243)

\displaystyle|\int\,d\mathbf{p}_{2}\,H_{1}(p_{1})H_{2}(p_{2})H_{3}(p_{1}+p_{2})(f_{0}(p_{1}+p_{2})-f_{s_{2}}^{(\Phi)}(p_{1}+p_{2}))|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C_{\|\hat{v}\|_{w,d},\|f_{0}\|_{d}}\|H_{1}\|_{1}\|H_{2}\|_{\infty}\|H_{3}\|_{\infty}\langle T\rangle^{2}\frac{|\Lambda|^{\frac{3}{2}}e^{C\|\hat{v}\|_{w,d}|\Lambda|/\lambda T}}{N^{\frac{1}{2}}\lambda}

\displaystyle\Big(1+\frac{|\Lambda|}{N}\Big)\Big(1\,+\,\frac{1}{N^{\frac{1}{2}}\lambda|\Lambda|^{\frac{3}{2}}}\Big)\,.

Collecting (
5.241
)–(
5.243
), recalling definitions (
5.50
)–(
5.56
), and denoting
\operatorname{Bol}^{(0)}:=\operatorname{Bol}
, we find the upper bound

(5.244)

\displaystyle\frac{\lambda^{j}}{N}|\operatorname{Bol}^{(j)}(f_{0})[J](T;\lambda)-\operatorname{Bol}^{(j)}(f^{(\Phi)})[J](T;\lambda)|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C_{\|\hat{v}\|_{w,d},\|f_{0}\|_{d}}\frac{\lambda^{2+j}t^{2}\|J\|_{\ell^{\infty}({\Lambda^{*}})}}{N}\langle T\rangle^{2}\frac{|\Lambda|^{\frac{3}{2}}e^{C\|\hat{v}\|_{w,d}|\Lambda|/\lambda T}}{N^{\frac{1}{2}}\lambda}

\displaystyle\Big(1+\frac{|\Lambda|}{N}\Big)\Big(1\,+\,\frac{1}{N^{\frac{1}{2}}\lambda|\Lambda|^{\frac{3}{2}}}\Big)

\displaystyle\Big[1\,+\,\langle T\rangle^{2}\frac{|\Lambda|^{\frac{3}{2}}e^{C\|\hat{v}\|_{w,d}|\Lambda|/\lambda T}}{N^{\frac{1}{2}}\lambda}\Big(1+\frac{|\Lambda|}{N}\Big)\Big(1\,+\,\frac{1}{N^{\frac{1}{2}}\lambda|\Lambda|^{\frac{3}{2}}}\Big)\Big]\,.

In particular, we have that

(5.245)

\displaystyle|\operatorname{err}^{(cen)}_{2,d}(t;f[J])|\,,\,|\operatorname{err}^{(cen)}_{2,c}(t;f[J])|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C_{\|\hat{v}\|_{w,d},\|f_{0}\|_{d}}\|J\|_{\ell^{\infty}({\Lambda^{*}})}\langle T\rangle^{4}\frac{|\Lambda|^{\frac{3}{2}}e^{C\|\hat{v}\|_{w,d}|\Lambda|/\lambda T}}{N^{\frac{3}{2}}\lambda^{3}}

\displaystyle\Big(1+\frac{|\Lambda|}{N}\Big)\Big(1\,+\,\frac{1}{N^{\frac{1}{2}}\lambda|\Lambda|^{\frac{3}{2}}}\Big)

\displaystyle\Big[1\,+\,\langle T\rangle^{2}\frac{|\Lambda|^{\frac{3}{2}}e^{C\|\hat{v}\|_{w,d}|\Lambda|/\lambda T}}{N^{\frac{1}{2}}\lambda}\Big(1+\frac{|\Lambda|}{N}\Big)\Big(1\,+\,\frac{1}{N^{\frac{1}{2}}\lambda|\Lambda|^{\frac{3}{2}}}\Big)\Big]\,.

Finally, it remains to estimate
\operatorname{err}^{(cen)}_{j}(\frac{T}{\lambda^{2}};g[J])
,
j\in\{1,2\}
. Following analogous steps that lead to (
5.245
), Proposition
5.2
implies

(5.246)

\displaystyle|\operatorname{err}^{(cen)}_{1}(\frac{T}{\lambda^{2}};g[J])|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C_{\|\hat{v}\|_{w,d},\|f_{0}\|_{d}}\|J\|_{\ell^{\infty}({\Lambda^{*}})}\langle T\rangle^{3}\frac{|\Lambda|^{\frac{3}{2}}e^{C\|\hat{v}\|_{w,d}|\Lambda|/\lambda T}}{N^{\frac{3}{2}}\lambda}

\displaystyle\Big(1+\frac{|\Lambda|}{N}\Big)\Big(1\,+\,\frac{1}{N^{\frac{1}{2}}\lambda|\Lambda|^{\frac{3}{2}}}\Big)

\displaystyle\Big[1\,+\,\langle T\rangle^{2}\frac{|\Lambda|^{\frac{3}{2}}e^{C\|\hat{v}\|_{w,d}|\Lambda|/\lambda T}}{N^{\frac{1}{2}}\lambda}\Big(1+\frac{|\Lambda|}{N}\Big)\Big(1\,+\,\frac{1}{N^{\frac{1}{2}}\lambda|\Lambda|^{\frac{3}{2}}}\Big)\Big]\,,

(5.247)

\displaystyle|\operatorname{err}^{(cen)}_{2}(\frac{T}{\lambda^{2}};g[J])|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C_{\|\hat{v}\|_{w,d},\|f_{0}\|_{d}}\|J\|_{\ell^{\infty}({\Lambda^{*}})}\langle T\rangle^{3}\frac{|\Lambda|^{\frac{3}{2}}e^{C\|\hat{v}\|_{w,d}|\Lambda|/\lambda T}}{N^{\frac{3}{2}}\lambda^{3}}

\displaystyle\Big(1+\frac{|\Lambda|}{N}\Big)\Big(1\,+\,\frac{1}{N^{\frac{1}{2}}\lambda|\Lambda|^{\frac{3}{2}}}\Big)

\displaystyle\Big[1\,+\,\langle T\rangle^{2}\frac{|\Lambda|^{\frac{3}{2}}e^{C\|\hat{v}\|_{w,d}|\Lambda|/\lambda T}}{N^{\frac{1}{2}}\lambda}\Big(1+\frac{|\Lambda|}{N}\Big)\Big(1\,+\,\frac{1}{N^{\frac{1}{2}}\lambda|\Lambda|^{\frac{3}{2}}}\Big)\Big]\,.

This concludes the proof.
∎

6.
Main order terms in the evolution of
(\Phi,f,g)

In this section, we will prove Theorem
2.2
. We will always assume that
\lambda\in(0,1)
is small enough to comply with the estimates proven in all preceding steps.

6.1.
Discrete case

Fix
L\geq 1
. Recall that we refer to the case of fixed
\Lambda
as the ’discrete case’.

6.1.1.
Main order term of
\Phi

Using Propositions
5.1
, and
5.2
, we have that

(6.1)

\displaystyle\Phi_{\frac{T}{\lambda^{2}}}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{1}{N^{\frac{1}{2}}\lambda}\operatorname{Con}_{d}(f_{0})(T;\lambda)\,+\operatorname{Rem}_{2}(\frac{T}{\lambda^{2}};\Phi)\,+\,\operatorname{err}^{(Bog)}_{1,d}(\frac{T}{\lambda^{2}};\Phi)

with

(6.2)

\displaystyle|\operatorname{Rem}_{2}(\frac{T}{\lambda^{2}};\Phi)|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C_{\|\hat{v}\|_{w,d},\|f_{0}\|_{d}}T^{2}e^{C\|\hat{v}\|_{w,d}|\Lambda|/\lambda T}\frac{|\Lambda|^{\frac{3}{2}}}{N\lambda^{2}}\,,

(6.3)

\displaystyle|\operatorname{err}^{(Bog)}_{1,d}(\frac{T}{\lambda^{2}};\Phi)|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{C_{\|\hat{v}\|_{w,d},\|f_{0}\|_{d}}T}{N^{\frac{1}{2}}}\,.

In particular, the main term is given by

(6.4)

\displaystyle\frac{1}{N^{\frac{1}{2}}\lambda}\operatorname{Con}_{d}(f_{0})(T;\lambda)\,=\,-\frac{iT}{N^{\frac{1}{2}}\lambda}\int_{\Lambda^{*}}\,dp\,\hat{v}(p)f_{0}(p)\,,

see (
5.49
), and thus it is of size
N^{-\frac{1}{2}}\lambda^{-1}
. In order to suppress
\operatorname{Rem}_{2}(\frac{T}{\lambda^{2}};\Phi)
, we choose
\lambda=\log\log N/\log N
.

6.1.2.
Main order term of
f

For
f
, we apply Propositions
5.1
, and
5.2
to obtain that

(6.5)

\displaystyle\int_{\Lambda^{*}}dp\,\big(f_{\frac{T}{\lambda^{2}}}^{(\Phi)}-f_{0}^{(\Phi)}\big)J(p)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{1}{N}\Big(\operatorname{Bol}_{d}(f_{0})[J](T;\lambda)\,+\,\lambda\operatorname{Bol}_{d}^{(1)}(f_{0})[J](T;\lambda)\,+\,\lambda^{2}\operatorname{Bol}_{d}^{(2)}(f_{0})[J](T;\lambda)\Big)

\displaystyle+\,\operatorname{Rem}_{2}(\frac{T}{\lambda^{2}};f[J])\,+\,\operatorname{err}^{(Bog,\operatorname{Bol})}_{2,d}({\frac{T}{\lambda^{2}}};f[J])\,+\,J(0)\operatorname{err}^{(Bog,\operatorname{Con})}_{2}({\frac{T}{\lambda^{2}}};f)\,,

where

(6.6)

\displaystyle|\operatorname{Rem}_{2}({\frac{T}{\lambda^{2}}};f[J])|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C_{\|\hat{v}\|_{w,d},\|f_{0}\|_{d}}\langle T\rangle^{4}\|J\|_{\ell^{\infty}({\Lambda^{*}})}\frac{e^{C\|\hat{v}\|_{w,d}|\Lambda|/\lambda T}|\Lambda|^{6}}{\lambda^{4}N^{2}}\,,

(6.7)

\displaystyle|\operatorname{err}^{(Bog,\operatorname{Bol})}_{2,d}({\frac{T}{\lambda^{2}}};f[J])|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C_{\|\hat{v}\|_{w,d},\|f_{0}\|_{d}}\|J\|_{\ell^{\infty}({\Lambda^{*}})}T^{2}\frac{\lambda}{N}\,,

(6.8)

\displaystyle|\operatorname{err}^{(Bog,\operatorname{Con})}_{2,d}(t;f)|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C_{\|\hat{v}\|_{w,d},\|f_{0}\|_{d}}e^{C\|\hat{v}\|_{w,d}|\Lambda|/\lambda T}\frac{\langle T\rangle^{4}|\Lambda|^{\frac{3}{2}}}{\lambda^{3}N^{\frac{3}{2}}}\Big(1+\frac{|\Lambda|^{\frac{3}{2}}}{\lambda N^{\frac{1}{2}}}\Big)\,.

Due to Lemma
5.3
, we can expand the oscillatory factors in
\operatorname{Bol}_{d}^{(j)}
in powers of
\lambda
. Then the main order term depends on
\hat{v}
,
\lambda(N)
, and
T
. We are interested in terms up to
O(N^{-1})
which, as we will see below, is the size of the main order term in the continuum approximation. For the same reasons as above, we choose
\lambda=\log\log N/\log N
.

6.1.3.
Main order term of
g

Similar to the case of
f
, we need to subtract the dynamics of the condensate in order to resolve the fluctuations defined by
g
. For that, we introduce

(6.9)

\displaystyle g_{t}^{(\Phi)}(p)\,:=\,g_{t}(p)\,-\,\Phi_{t}^{2}\delta(p)\,.

Propositions
5.1
and
5.2
then yield

(6.10)

\displaystyle\int_{\Lambda^{*}}dp\,g_{\frac{T}{\lambda^{2}}}^{(\Phi)}(p)J(p)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{1}{N}\int_{0}^{T}\,dS\,\mathrm{abs}_{quart,d}(f_{0})[J](S/\lambda^{2})

\displaystyle+\,\frac{1}{N\lambda^{2}}\int_{\Delta[T,2]}\,d\mathbf{S}_{2}\,\big(\operatorname{col}_{d}(f_{0})[J](\mathbf{S}_{2}/\lambda^{2})+\mathrm{abs}_{cub,d}(f_{0})[J](\mathbf{S}_{2}/\lambda^{2})\big)

\displaystyle+\,\operatorname{Rem}_{2}(\frac{T}{\lambda^{2}};g[J])\,+\,J(0)\operatorname{err}^{(Bog,\operatorname{Con})}_{2}(\frac{T}{\lambda^{2}};g)

with

(6.11)

\displaystyle|\operatorname{Rem}_{2}(\frac{T}{\lambda^{2}};g[J])|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C_{\|\hat{v}\|_{w,d},\|f_{0}\|_{d}}\langle T\rangle^{4}\|J\|_{2\cap\infty,d}\frac{e^{C\|\hat{v}\|_{w,d}|\Lambda|/\lambda T}|\Lambda|^{6}}{\lambda^{4}N^{2}}\,,

(6.12)

\displaystyle|\operatorname{err}^{(Bog,\operatorname{Con})}_{2,d}(\frac{T}{\lambda^{2}};g)|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C_{\|\hat{v}\|_{w,d},\|f_{0}\|_{d}}e^{C\|\hat{v}\|_{w,d}|\Lambda|/\lambda T}\frac{\langle T\rangle^{4}|\Lambda|^{\frac{3}{2}}}{\lambda^{3}N^{\frac{3}{2}}}\Big(1+\frac{|\Lambda|^{\frac{3}{2}}}{\lambda N^{\frac{1}{2}}}\Big)\,.

As in the case of
f
, we again observe a phenomenon similar to the Talbot effect on
\mathrm{abs}_{quart,d}
,
\operatorname{col}_{d}
, and
\mathrm{abs}_{cub,d}
. We choose
N^{-1}
as reference order.

6.1.4.
Conclusion for
L\sim 1

As described above, we choose
\lambda=\log\log(N)/\log(N)
. Let

(6.13)

\displaystyle\delta_{\Psi,d}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\min\Big\{\frac{\log\frac{1}{\lambda}}{\log N},\frac{1}{2}-\frac{\log\frac{1}{\lambda}}{\log N}-\frac{C\|\hat{v}\|_{w,d}|\Lambda|T}{\log\log N}\Big\}\,,

(6.14)

\displaystyle\delta_{F,d}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\min\Big\{\frac{\log\frac{1}{\lambda}}{\log N},\frac{1}{2}-\frac{3\log\frac{1}{\lambda}}{\log N}-\frac{C\|\hat{v}\|_{w,d}|\Lambda|T}{\log\log N}\Big\}\,,

(6.15)

\displaystyle\delta_{G,d}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{1}{2}-\frac{3\log\frac{1}{\lambda}}{\log N}-\frac{C\|\hat{v}\|_{w,d}|\Lambda|T}{\log\log N}\,.

Then we obtain

(6.16)

\displaystyle\Big|\Phi_{\frac{T}{\lambda^{2}}}\,-\,\frac{1}{N^{\frac{1}{2}}\lambda}\operatorname{Con}_{d}(f_{0})(T;\lambda)\Big|

\displaystyle\hskip 56.9055pt\leq\,\frac{C_{\|\hat{v}\|_{w,d},\|f_{0}\|_{d},|\Lambda|,T}}{N^{\frac{1}{2}+\delta_{\Psi,d}}\lambda}\,,

(6.17)

\displaystyle\Big|\int_{\Lambda^{*}}dp\,\big(f_{\frac{T}{\lambda^{2}}}^{(\Phi)}(p)-f_{0}^{(\Phi)}(p)\big)J(p)

\displaystyle-\,\frac{1}{N}\Big(\operatorname{Bol}_{d}(f_{0})[J](T;\lambda)\,+\,\sum_{j=1}^{2}\lambda^{j}\operatorname{Bol}_{d}^{(j)}(f_{0})[J](T;\lambda)\Big)\Big|

\displaystyle\hskip 56.9055pt\leq\,\frac{C_{\|\hat{v}\|_{w,d},\|f_{0}\|_{d},|\Lambda|,T}\|J\|_{\ell^{\infty}({\Lambda^{*}})}}{N^{1+\delta_{F,d}}}\,,

(6.18)

\displaystyle\Big|\int_{\Lambda^{*}}dp\,g_{\frac{T}{\lambda^{2}}}^{(\Phi)}(p)J(p)\,-\,\frac{1}{N}\int_{0}^{T}\,dS\,\mathrm{abs}_{quart,d}(f_{0})[J](S/\lambda^{2})

\displaystyle-\,\frac{1}{N\lambda^{2}}\int_{\Delta[T,2]}\,d\mathbf{S}_{2}\,\big(\operatorname{col}_{d}(f_{0})[J](\mathbf{S}_{2}/\lambda^{2})+\mathrm{abs}_{cub,d}(f_{0})[J](\mathbf{S}_{2}/\lambda^{2})\big)\Big|

\displaystyle\hskip 56.9055pt\leq\,\frac{C_{\|\hat{v}\|_{w,d},\|f_{0}\|_{d},|\Lambda|,T}\|J\|_{2\cap\infty,d}}{N^{1+\delta_{G,d}}}

for all
N
larger than a universal constant. Notice that for all
N
large enough, we have that
\delta_{j,d}=\delta_{j,d}(N,|\Lambda|,T)>0
for
j\in\{\Psi,F,G\}
.

6.2.
Continuum approximation

We have that errors coming from the tail in the Duhamel expansion grow like
\exp(C_{r}\|\hat{v}\|_{2(\left\lfloor\frac{r}{2}\right\rfloor+1),w,c}T|\Lambda|/\lambda)
. As a consequence, we require

(6.19)

\displaystyle\frac{|\Lambda|}{\lambda}=O(\frac{\log(N)}{\log\log(N)})

as
N\to\infty
. In order to use bounds established for general values of
|\Lambda|
to the limit
|\Lambda|\to\infty
, for specific expressions of interest, we employ Remark
5.6
.

6.2.1.
Main order term of
\Phi

Using Propositions
5.1
and
5.7
, we have that

(6.20)

\displaystyle\Phi_{\frac{T}{\lambda^{2}}}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{1}{N^{\frac{1}{2}}\lambda}\operatorname{Con}_{c}(f_{0})(T;\lambda)\,+\operatorname{Rem}_{2}(\frac{T}{\lambda^{2}};\Phi)\,+\,\operatorname{err}^{(Bog)}_{1,c}(\frac{T}{\lambda^{2}};\Phi)

with

\displaystyle|\operatorname{Rem}_{2}(\frac{T}{\lambda^{2}};\Phi)|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C_{\|\hat{v}\|_{2(\left\lfloor\frac{r}{2}\right\rfloor+1),w,c},\|f_{0}\|_{2(\left\lfloor\frac{r}{2}\right\rfloor+1),c},r}T^{2}e^{C_{r}\|\hat{v}\|_{2(\left\lfloor\frac{r}{2}\right\rfloor+1),w,c}|\Lambda|/\lambda T}\frac{|\Lambda|^{\frac{3}{2}}}{N\lambda^{2}}

\displaystyle\Big(1+\frac{|\Lambda|}{N}\Big)\,,

(6.21)

\displaystyle|\operatorname{err}^{(Bog)}_{1,c}(t;\Phi)|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{C_{\|\hat{v}\|_{2(\left\lfloor\frac{r}{2}\right\rfloor+1),w,c},\|f_{0}\|_{2(\left\lfloor\frac{r}{2}\right\rfloor+1),c},r}T}{N^{\frac{1}{2}}\lambda}\Big(\lambda\,+\,\frac{1}{|\Lambda|^{\frac{r}{6}}}\Big)

for some
r>6
. The main order term is given by

(6.22)

\displaystyle\frac{1}{N^{\frac{1}{2}}\lambda}\operatorname{Con}_{c}(f_{0})(T;\lambda)\,=\,-\frac{iT}{(2\pi)^{3}N^{\frac{1}{2}}\lambda}\int_{{\mathbb{R}}^{3}}\,dp\,\hat{v}(p)f_{0}(p)\,,

and it is of size
N^{\frac{1}{2}}\lambda^{-1}
.

6.2.2.
Main order term of
f

For
f
, we apply Propositions
5.1
,
5.7
, and
5.8
to obtain that

(6.23)

\displaystyle\int_{\Lambda^{*}}dp\,\big(f_{\frac{T}{\lambda^{2}}}^{(\Phi)}(p)-f_{0}^{(\Phi)}(p)\big)J(p)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{1}{N}\operatorname{Bol}_{fec}(f_{0})[J](T)

\displaystyle+\,\operatorname{Rem}_{2}(\frac{T}{\lambda^{2}};f[J])\,+\,\operatorname{err}^{(Bog,\operatorname{Bol})}_{2,c}({\frac{T}{\lambda^{2}}};f[J])

\displaystyle+\,J(0)\operatorname{err}^{(Bog,\operatorname{Con})}_{2,c}({\frac{T}{\lambda^{2}}};f)\,+\,\operatorname{err}^{(fec)}_{2,c}(T\lambda^{-2};f[J])\,,

where

(6.24)

\displaystyle|\operatorname{Rem}_{2}({\frac{T}{\lambda^{2}}};f[J])|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C_{\|\hat{v}\|_{2(\left\lfloor\frac{r}{2}\right\rfloor+1),w,c},\|f_{0}\|_{2(\left\lfloor\frac{r}{2}\right\rfloor+1),c},r}\langle T\rangle^{4}\|J\|_{\infty}

\displaystyle\frac{e^{C_{r}\|\hat{v}\|_{2(\left\lfloor\frac{r}{2}\right\rfloor+1),w,c}|\Lambda|/\lambda T}|\Lambda|^{6}}{\lambda^{4}N^{2}}\Big(1+\frac{|\Lambda|}{N}\Big)^{2}\,,

(6.25)

\displaystyle|\operatorname{err}^{(Bog,\operatorname{Bol})}_{2,c}(\frac{T}{\lambda^{2}};f[J])|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{C_{\|\hat{v}\|_{2(\left\lfloor\frac{r}{2}\right\rfloor+1),w,c},\|f_{0}\|_{2(\left\lfloor\frac{r}{2}\right\rfloor+1),c},r}\langle T\rangle^{r+2}\|J\|_{W^{2\left\lfloor\frac{r}{2}\right\rfloor+2,\infty}}}{N}

\displaystyle\Big(\frac{1}{\lambda^{2(r+1)}|\Lambda|^{\frac{r}{3}}}+\lambda|\log(\lambda)|\Big)\,,

(6.26)

\displaystyle|\operatorname{err}^{(Bog,\operatorname{Con})}_{2,c}(\frac{T}{\lambda^{2}};f[J])|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C_{\|\hat{v}\|_{2(\left\lfloor\frac{r}{2}\right\rfloor+1),w,c},\|f_{0}\|_{2(\left\lfloor\frac{r}{2}\right\rfloor+1),c},r}e^{C_{r}\|\hat{v}\|_{2(\left\lfloor\frac{r}{2}\right\rfloor+1),w,c}|\Lambda|/\lambda T}

\displaystyle\frac{\langle T\rangle^{4}|\Lambda|^{\frac{3}{2}}}{\lambda^{3}N^{\frac{3}{2}}}\Big(1+\frac{|\Lambda|}{N}\Big)^{2}\Big(1+\frac{|\Lambda|^{\frac{3}{2}}}{\lambda N^{\frac{1}{2}}}\Big)\,,

(6.27)

\displaystyle|\operatorname{err}^{(fec)}_{2,c}(\frac{T}{\lambda^{2}};f[J])|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C_{\|\hat{v}\|_{2(\left\lfloor\frac{r}{2}\right\rfloor+1),w,c},\|f_{0}\|_{2(\left\lfloor\frac{r}{2}\right\rfloor+1),c},r}\langle T\rangle\|J\|_{W^{2,\infty}}\frac{\lambda}{N}

for all
\lambda>0
small enough, dependent on
\|\hat{v}\|_{2(\left\lfloor\frac{r}{2}\right\rfloor+1),w,c}
. Recalling definition (
5.200
) of
\operatorname{Bol}_{fec}
, the main term is given by

(6.28)

\displaystyle\frac{\pi T}{(2\pi)^{6}N}\int_{p_{1}\perp p_{2}}\,d{\mathcal{H}}^{5}(\mathbf{p}_{2})\,{\Delta_{cub}J}(\mathbf{p}_{2})\frac{(\hat{v}(p_{1})+\hat{v}(p_{2}))^{2}}{|\mathbf{p}_{2}|}

\displaystyle\big(\widetilde{f_{0}}(p_{1})\widetilde{f_{0}}(p_{2})f_{0}(p_{1}+p_{2})\,-\,f_{0}(p_{1})f_{0}(p_{2})\widetilde{f_{0}}(p_{1}+p_{2})\big)\,,

and it is of size
N^{-1}
. In order to have that the discretization error is negligible, we need to impose
\frac{1}{\lambda}=o(|\Lambda|^{\frac{r}{6(r+1)}})
as
|\Lambda|\to\infty
. In particular, we may choose

(6.29)

\displaystyle L\,=\,\lambda^{-2-\frac{2}{r}-{\varepsilon}}

for any arbitrary
{\varepsilon}>0
. Recall that we required

(6.30)

\displaystyle\frac{|\Lambda|}{\lambda}=O\big(\frac{\log N}{\log\log N}\big)

as
N\to\infty
to suppress the tail in the Duhamel expansion. We may thus choose

(6.31)

\displaystyle\lambda

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\Big(\frac{\log\log N}{\log N}\Big)^{\frac{r}{(7+{\varepsilon})r+6}}\,,

(6.32)

\displaystyle L

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\Big(\frac{\log N}{\log\log N}\Big)^{\frac{(2+{\varepsilon})r+2}{(7+{\varepsilon})r+6}}\,.

6.2.3.
Main order term of
g

Propositions
5.1
and
5.7
imply

(6.33)

\displaystyle\int_{\Lambda^{*}}dp\,g_{\frac{T}{\lambda^{2}}}^{(\Phi)}(p)J(p)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{1}{N}\int_{0}^{T}\,dS\,\mathrm{abs}_{quart,c}(f_{0})[J](S/\lambda^{2})

\displaystyle+\,\frac{1}{N\lambda^{2}}\int_{\Delta[T,2]}\,d\mathbf{S}_{2}\,\big(\operatorname{col}_{c}(f_{0})[J](\mathbf{S}_{2}/\lambda^{2})+\mathrm{abs}_{cub,c}(f_{0})[J](\mathbf{S}_{2}/\lambda^{2})\big)

\displaystyle+\,\operatorname{Rem}_{2}(\frac{T}{\lambda^{2}};g[J])\,+\,\operatorname{err}^{(dis)}_{1,c}(\frac{T}{\lambda^{2}};g[J])

\displaystyle+\,\operatorname{err}^{(dis)}_{2,c}(\frac{T}{\lambda^{2}};g[J])\,+\,J(0)\operatorname{err}^{(Bog,\operatorname{Con})}_{2,c}(\frac{T}{\lambda^{2}};g)

with

(6.34)

\displaystyle|\operatorname{Rem}_{2}(\frac{T}{\lambda^{2}};g[J])|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C_{\|\hat{v}\|_{2(\left\lfloor\frac{r}{2}\right\rfloor+1),w,c},\|f_{0}\|_{2(\left\lfloor\frac{r}{2}\right\rfloor+1),c},r}\langle T\rangle^{4}\|J\|_{2(\left\lfloor\frac{r}{2}\right\rfloor+1),c}

\displaystyle\frac{e^{C_{r}\|\hat{v}\|_{2(\left\lfloor\frac{r}{2}\right\rfloor+1),w,c}|\Lambda|/\lambda T}|\Lambda|^{6}}{\lambda^{4}N^{2}}\Big(1+\frac{|\Lambda|}{N}\Big)^{2}\,,

(6.35)

\displaystyle|\operatorname{err}^{(dis)}_{1,c}(\frac{T}{\lambda^{2}};g[J])|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{C_{\|\hat{v}\|_{2(\left\lfloor\frac{r}{2}\right\rfloor+1),w,c},\|f_{0}\|_{2(\left\lfloor\frac{r}{2}\right\rfloor+1),c},r}\langle T\rangle^{r+1}\|J\|_{W^{2\left\lfloor\frac{r}{2}\right\rfloor+2,\infty}}}{N\lambda^{2r}|\Lambda|^{\frac{r}{3}}}\,,

(6.36)

\displaystyle|\operatorname{err}^{(dis)}_{2,c}(\frac{T}{\lambda^{2}};g[J])|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{C_{\|\hat{v}\|_{2(\left\lfloor\frac{r}{2}\right\rfloor+1),w,c},\|f_{0}\|_{2(\left\lfloor\frac{r}{2}\right\rfloor+1),c},r}\langle T\rangle^{r+2}\|J\|_{W^{2\left\lfloor\frac{r}{2}\right\rfloor+2,\infty}}}{N\lambda^{2(r+1)}|\Lambda|^{\frac{r}{3}}}\,,

(6.37)

\displaystyle|\operatorname{err}^{(Bog,\operatorname{Con})}_{2,c}(\frac{T}{\lambda^{2}};g[J])|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C_{\|\hat{v}\|_{2(\left\lfloor\frac{r}{2}\right\rfloor+1),w,c},\|f_{0}\|_{2(\left\lfloor\frac{r}{2}\right\rfloor+1),c},r}e^{C_{r}\|\hat{v}\|_{2(\left\lfloor\frac{r}{2}\right\rfloor+1),w,c}|\Lambda|/\lambda T}

\displaystyle\frac{\langle T\rangle^{4}|\Lambda|^{\frac{3}{2}}}{\lambda^{3}N^{\frac{3}{2}}}\Big(1+\frac{|\Lambda|}{N}\Big)^{2}\Big(1+\frac{|\Lambda|^{\frac{3}{2}}}{\lambda N^{\frac{1}{2}}}\Big)

for all
\lambda>0
small enough, dependent on
\|\hat{v}\|_{2(\left\lfloor\frac{r}{2}\right\rfloor+1),w,c}
. All the errors are suppressed for the choices of
|\Lambda|
and
\lambda
as in the case of
f
above.

6.2.4.
Conclusion for
L\sim\lambda^{-2-}

Recall that we impose

(6.38)

\displaystyle\lambda

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\Big(\frac{\log\log N}{\log N}\Big)^{\frac{r}{(7+{\varepsilon})r+6}}\,,

(6.39)

\displaystyle L

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\lambda^{-2-\frac{2}{r}-{\varepsilon}}\,=\,\Big(\frac{\log N}{\log\log N}\Big)^{\frac{(2+{\varepsilon})r+2}{(7+{\varepsilon})r+6}}\,.

Let

(6.40)

\displaystyle\delta_{\Psi,c}

\displaystyle:=

\displaystyle\min\Big\{\frac{1}{2}-C_{r,{\varepsilon}}\frac{\log\frac{1}{\lambda}}{\log N}-\frac{C_{r}\|\hat{v}\|_{2(\left\lfloor\frac{r}{2}\right\rfloor+1),w,c}T}{\log\log N},\frac{\log\frac{1}{\lambda}}{\log N}\Big\}\,,

(6.41)

\displaystyle\delta_{F,c}

\displaystyle:=

\displaystyle\min\Big\{\frac{1}{2}-C_{r,{\varepsilon}}\frac{\log\frac{1}{\lambda}}{\log N}-\frac{C_{r}\|\hat{v}\|_{2(\left\lfloor\frac{r}{2}\right\rfloor+1),w,c}T}{\log\log N},\frac{{\varepsilon}r\log\frac{1}{\lambda}}{\log N},

\displaystyle\frac{\log\frac{1}{\lambda}-\log\log\frac{1}{\lambda}}{\log N}\Big\}\,,

(6.42)

\displaystyle\delta_{G,c}

\displaystyle:=

\displaystyle\min\Big\{\frac{1}{2}-C_{r,{\varepsilon}}\frac{\log\frac{1}{\lambda}}{\log N}-\frac{C_{r}\|\hat{v}\|_{2(\left\lfloor\frac{r}{2}\right\rfloor+1),w,c}T}{\log\log N},\frac{{\varepsilon}r\log\frac{1}{\lambda}}{\log N}\Big\}\,.

Observe that we have

(6.43)

\displaystyle\|J\|_{W^{2,\infty}({\mathbb{R}}^{3})}\,\leq\,\|J\|_{2(\left\lfloor\frac{r}{2}\right\rfloor+1),c}\,.

Then we have proved that, for some
N_{0}=N_{0}(\|\hat{v}\|_{2(\left\lfloor\frac{r}{2}\right\rfloor+1),w,c})
,

(6.44)

\displaystyle\Big|\Phi_{\frac{T}{\lambda^{2}}}\,-\,\frac{1}{N^{\frac{1}{2}}\lambda}\operatorname{Con}_{c}(f_{0})(T;\lambda)\Big|

\displaystyle\hskip 56.9055pt\leq\,\frac{C_{\|\hat{v}\|_{2(\left\lfloor\frac{r}{2}\right\rfloor+1),w,c},\|f_{0}\|_{2(\left\lfloor\frac{r}{2}\right\rfloor+1),c},r,{\varepsilon},T}}{N^{\frac{1}{2}+\delta_{\Psi,c}}\lambda}\,,

(6.45)

\displaystyle\Big|\int_{\Lambda^{*}}dp\,\big(f_{\frac{T}{\lambda^{2}}}^{(\Phi)}(p)-f_{0}^{(\Phi)}(p)\big)J(p)\,-\,\frac{1}{N}\operatorname{Bol}_{fec}(f_{0})[J](T)\Big|

\displaystyle\hskip 56.9055pt\leq\,\frac{C_{\|\hat{v}\|_{2(\left\lfloor\frac{r}{2}\right\rfloor+1),w,c},\|f_{0}\|_{2(\left\lfloor\frac{r}{2}\right\rfloor+1),c},r,{\varepsilon},T}\|J\|_{W^{2\left\lfloor\frac{r}{2}\right\rfloor+2,\infty}}}{N^{1+\delta_{F,c}}}\,,

(6.46)

\displaystyle\Big|\int_{\Lambda^{*}}dp\,g_{\frac{T}{\lambda^{2}}}^{(\Phi)}(p)J(p)\,-\,\frac{1}{N}\int_{0}^{T}\,dS\,\mathrm{abs}_{quart,c}(f_{0})[J](S/\lambda^{2})

\displaystyle-\,\frac{1}{N\lambda^{2}}\int_{\Delta[T,2]}\,d\mathbf{S}_{2}\,\big(\operatorname{col}_{c}(f_{0})[J](\mathbf{S}_{2}/\lambda^{2})

\displaystyle+\mathrm{abs}_{cub,c}(f_{0})[J](\mathbf{S}_{2}/\lambda^{2})\big)\Big|

\displaystyle\hskip 56.9055pt\leq\,\frac{C_{\|\hat{v}\|_{2(\left\lfloor\frac{r}{2}\right\rfloor+1),w,c},\|f_{0}\|_{2(\left\lfloor\frac{r}{2}\right\rfloor+1),c},r,{\varepsilon},T}\|J\|_{2(\left\lfloor\frac{r}{2}\right\rfloor+1),c}}{N^{1+\delta_{G,c}}}

for all
N\geq N_{0}(\|\hat{v}\|_{2(\left\lfloor\frac{r}{2}\right\rfloor+1),w,c})
. Observe that for all
N
large enough, we have that
\delta_{j,c}=\delta_{j,c}(N,T)>0
for
j\in\{\Psi,F,G\}
.

7.
Effective equations

After establishing the sizes of the leading order terms, we can derive the effective equations. This will prove Theorem
2.1
.

7.1.
Discrete case

Let
L\geq 1
be fixed. Choose
\lambda=\log\log(N)/\log(N)
as explained in section
6.1.4
.

7.1.1.
Evolution of
\Phi

We have that

(7.1)

\displaystyle\Phi_{\frac{T}{\lambda^{2}}}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{1}{N^{\frac{1}{2}}\lambda}\operatorname{Con}_{d}(f^{(\Phi)})(T;\lambda)

\displaystyle+\operatorname{Rem}_{2}(\frac{T}{\lambda^{2}};\Phi)\,+\,\operatorname{err}^{(Bog)}_{1,d}(\frac{T}{\lambda^{2}};\Phi)\,+\,\operatorname{err}^{(cen)}_{1,d}(\frac{T}{\lambda^{2}};\Phi),

see (
6.1
). Proposition
5.9
implies

(7.2)

\displaystyle|\operatorname{err}^{(cen)}_{1,d}(\frac{T}{\lambda^{2}};\Phi)|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C_{\|\hat{v}\|_{w,d},\|f_{0}\|_{d}}\langle T\rangle^{3}\frac{|\Lambda|^{\frac{3}{2}}e^{C\|\hat{v}\|_{w,d}|\Lambda|/\lambda T}}{N\lambda^{2}}

\displaystyle\Big(1+\frac{|\Lambda|}{N}\Big)\Big(1\,+\,\frac{1}{N^{\frac{1}{2}}\lambda|\Lambda|^{\frac{3}{2}}}\Big)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{C_{\|\hat{v}\|_{w,d},\|f_{0}\|_{2(\left\lfloor\frac{r}{2}\right\rfloor+1),c},|\Lambda|,T}}{N^{\frac{1}{2}+\delta_{\Psi,d}}\lambda}\,.

for all
N
large enough. Observe that

(7.3)

\displaystyle\frac{1}{N^{\frac{1}{2}}\lambda}\operatorname{Con}_{d}(f^{(\Phi)})(T;\lambda)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-\frac{i}{N^{\frac{1}{2}}\lambda}\int_{0}^{T}\,dS\,\int_{\Lambda^{*}}\,dp\,\hat{v}(p)f^{(\Phi)}_{\frac{S}{\lambda^{2}}}(p)\,.

Then, analogously to (
6.16
) and employing (
7.2
), we obtain that

(7.4)

\displaystyle\Big|\Phi_{\frac{T}{\lambda^{2}}}\,-\,\frac{1}{N^{\frac{1}{2}}\lambda}\operatorname{Con}_{d}(f^{(\Phi)})(T;\lambda)\Big|\,\leq\,\frac{C_{\|\hat{v}\|_{w,d},\|f_{0}\|_{d},|\Lambda|,T}}{N^{\frac{1}{2}+\delta_{\Psi,d}}\lambda}\,.

7.1.2.
Evolution of
f

(
6.5
) and Proposition
5.9
imply

(7.5)

\displaystyle\int_{\Lambda^{*}}dp\,\big(f_{\frac{T}{\lambda^{2}}}^{(\Phi)}(p)-f_{0}^{(\Phi)}(p)\big)J(p)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{1}{N}\Big(\operatorname{Bol}_{d}(f^{(\Phi)})[J](T;\lambda)\,+\,\sum_{j=1}^{2}\lambda^{j}\operatorname{Bol}_{d}^{(1)}(f^{(\Phi)})[J](T;\lambda)\Big)

\displaystyle+\,\operatorname{Rem}_{2}(\frac{T}{\lambda^{2}};f[J])\,+\,\operatorname{err}^{(Bog,\operatorname{Bol})}_{2,d}({\frac{T}{\lambda^{2}}};f[J])

\displaystyle+\,J(0)\operatorname{err}^{(Bog,\operatorname{Con})}_{2,d}({\frac{T}{\lambda^{2}}};f)\,+\,\operatorname{err}^{(cen)}_{2,d}(\frac{T}{\lambda^{2}};f[J])\,.

Recall that, due to Proposition
5.9
,

(7.6)

\displaystyle|\operatorname{err}^{(cen)}_{2,d}(\frac{T}{\lambda^{2}};f[J])|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C_{\|\hat{v}\|_{w,d},\|f_{0}\|_{d}}\|J\|_{\ell^{\infty}({\Lambda^{*}})}\langle T\rangle^{4}\frac{|\Lambda|^{\frac{3}{2}}e^{C\|\hat{v}\|_{w,d}|\Lambda|/\lambda T}}{N^{\frac{3}{2}}\lambda^{3}}

\displaystyle\Big(1+\frac{|\Lambda|}{N}\Big)\Big(1\,+\,\frac{1}{N^{\frac{1}{2}}\lambda|\Lambda|^{\frac{3}{2}}}\Big)

\displaystyle\Big[1\,+\,\langle T\rangle^{2}\frac{|\Lambda|^{\frac{3}{2}}e^{C\|\hat{v}\|_{w,d}|\Lambda|/\lambda T}}{N^{\frac{1}{2}}\lambda}\Big(1+\frac{|\Lambda|}{N}\Big)\Big(1\,+\,\frac{1}{N^{\frac{1}{2}}\lambda|\Lambda|^{\frac{3}{2}}}\Big)\Big]

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{C_{\|\hat{v}\|_{w,d},\|f_{0}\|_{d},|\Lambda|,T}\|J\|_{\ell^{\infty}({\Lambda^{*}})}}{N^{1+\delta_{F,d}(N)}}\,,

where we possibly enlarge the constant in the definition of
\delta_{F,d}
. In particular, similar to (
6.17
) we obtain that

(7.7)

\displaystyle\Big|\int_{\Lambda^{*}}dp\,\big(f_{\frac{T}{\lambda^{2}}}^{(\Phi)}(p)-f_{0}^{(\Phi)}(p)\big)J(p)\,-\,\frac{1}{N}\Big(\operatorname{Bol}_{d}(f^{(\Phi)})[J](T;\lambda)

\displaystyle+\,\sum_{j=1}^{2}\lambda^{j}\operatorname{Bol}_{d}^{(j)}(f^{(\Phi)})[J](T;\lambda)\Big)\Big|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{C_{\|\hat{v}\|_{w,d},\|f_{0}\|_{d},|\Lambda|,T}\|J\|_{\ell^{\infty}({\Lambda^{*}})}}{N^{1+\delta_{F,d}}}\,.

7.1.3.
Evolution of
g

(
6.10
) yields

(7.8)

\displaystyle\int_{\Lambda^{*}}dp\,g_{\frac{T}{\lambda^{2}}}^{(\Phi)}(p)J(p)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{1}{N}\int_{0}^{T}\,dS\,\mathrm{abs}_{quart,d}(f^{(\Phi)})[J](S/\lambda^{2})

\displaystyle+\,\frac{1}{N\lambda^{2}}\int_{\Delta[T,2]}\,d\mathbf{S}_{2}\,\big(\operatorname{col}_{d}(f^{(\Phi)})[J](\mathbf{S}_{2}/\lambda^{2})+\mathrm{abs}_{cub,d}(f^{(\Phi)})[J](\mathbf{S}_{2}/\lambda^{2})\big)

\displaystyle+\,\operatorname{Rem}_{2}(\frac{T}{\lambda^{2}};g[J])\,+\,J(0)\operatorname{err}^{(Bog,\operatorname{Con})}_{2,d}(\frac{T}{\lambda^{2}};g)

\displaystyle+\,\operatorname{err}^{(cen)}_{1}(\frac{T}{\lambda^{2}};g[J])\,+\,\operatorname{err}^{(cen)}_{2}(\frac{T}{\lambda^{2}};g[J])\,,

where, due to Proposition
5.9
and analogously to (
7.6
),

(7.9)

\displaystyle|\operatorname{err}^{(cen)}_{1}(\frac{T}{\lambda^{2}};g[J])|\,,\,|\operatorname{err}^{(cen)}_{2}(\frac{T}{\lambda^{2}};g[J])|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{C_{\|\hat{v}\|_{w,d},\|f_{0}\|_{d},|\Lambda|,T}\|J\|_{\ell^{\infty}({\Lambda^{*}})}}{N^{1+\delta_{G,d}}}\,,

where we possibly enlarge the constant in the definition of
\delta_{G,d}
. As a consequence of (
6.18
), we thus obtain that

(7.10)

\displaystyle\Big|\int_{\Lambda^{*}}dp\,g_{\frac{T}{\lambda^{2}}}^{(\Phi)}(p)J(p)\,-\,\frac{1}{N}\int_{0}^{T}\,dS\,\mathrm{abs}_{quart,d}(f^{(\Phi)})[J](S/\lambda^{2})

\displaystyle-\,\frac{1}{N\lambda^{2}}\int_{\Delta[T,2]}\,d\mathbf{S}_{2}\,\big(\operatorname{col}_{d}(f^{(\Phi)})[J](\mathbf{S}_{2}/\lambda^{2})+\mathrm{abs}_{cub,d}(f^{(\Phi)})[J](\mathbf{S}_{2}/\lambda^{2})\big)\Big|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{C_{\|\hat{v}\|_{w,d},\|f_{0}\|_{d},|\Lambda|,T}\|J\|_{2\cap\infty,d}}{N^{1+\delta_{G,d}(N)}}\,.

7.1.4.
Conclusion for
L\sim 1

Recall that
\lambda=\log\log(N)/\log(N)
. We consider the effective quantities

(7.11)

\displaystyle\Psi_{T}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\Phi_{\frac{T}{\lambda^{2}}}\,,

(7.12)

\displaystyle F_{T}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle f_{\frac{T}{\lambda^{2}}}^{(\Phi)}\,,

(7.13)

\displaystyle G_{T}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle g_{\frac{T}{\lambda^{2}}}^{(\Phi)}\,.

We have that

(7.14)

\displaystyle\lambda^{2}\mathrm{Re}\int_{[\Delta[T\lambda^{-2},2]}\,d\mathbf{s}_{2}\,e^{-i\omega(s_{1}-s_{2})}h(s_{2})

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\lambda^{2}\int_{0}^{\frac{T}{\lambda^{2}}}\,ds\,\frac{\sin(\omega(\frac{T}{\lambda^{2}}-s))}{\omega}h(s)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{T}\,dS\,\frac{\sin\Big(\frac{\omega}{\lambda^{2}}(T-S)\Big)}{\omega}h(\frac{S}{\lambda^{2}})\,.

For a function
H_{t}(p)
and
j\in\{1,2\}
, denote

(7.15)

\displaystyle Q_{d;T-S,\lambda}(H_{S})[J]

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\int_{({\Lambda^{*}})^{2}}d\mathbf{p}_{2}\frac{\sin\Big(\frac{\Omega(p_{1})+\Omega(p_{2})-\Omega(p_{1}+p_{2})}{\lambda^{2}}(T-S)\Big)}{\Omega(p_{1})+\Omega(p_{2})-\Omega(p_{1}+p_{2})}

\displaystyle(\hat{v}(p_{1})+\hat{v}(p_{2}))^{2}\big(J(p_{1})+J(p_{2})-J(p_{1}+p_{2})\big)

\displaystyle\big(\widetilde{H_{S}}(p_{1})\widetilde{H_{S}}(p_{2})H_{S}(p_{1}+p_{2})

\displaystyle-\,H_{S}(p_{1})H_{S}(p_{2})\widetilde{H_{S}}(p_{1}+p_{2})\big)\,,

(7.16)

\displaystyle q^{(j)}_{d,F;\mathbf{S}_{2},\lambda}(H_{S_{2}})[J]

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\operatorname{bol}^{(j)}(H_{\cdot\lambda^{2}})[J](\mathbf{S}_{2}/\lambda^{2})\,,

(7.17)

\displaystyle{\mathcal{Q}}_{d,G;T,\lambda}(H)[J]

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\,\frac{1}{\lambda^{2}}\int_{\Delta[T,2]}\,d\mathbf{S}_{2}\,\operatorname{col}_{d}(H_{\cdot\lambda^{2}})[J](\mathbf{S}_{2}/\lambda^{2})\,,

(7.18)

\displaystyle{\mathcal{A}}_{d;T,\lambda}(H)[J]

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{T}\,dS\,\mathrm{abs}_{quart,d}(H_{\cdot\lambda^{2}})[J](S/\lambda^{2})

\displaystyle+\,\frac{1}{\lambda^{2}}\int_{\Delta[T,2]}\,d\mathbf{S}_{2}\mathrm{abs}_{cub,d}(H_{\cdot\lambda^{2}})[J](\mathbf{S}_{2}/\lambda^{2})\,,

where we used Proposition
5.2
.
{\mathcal{Q}}_{d,G;T,\lambda}
denotes a generalized collision operator and
{\mathcal{A}}_{d;T,\lambda}
a generalized absorption operator. We have proved that

(7.19)

\displaystyle\Big|\Psi_{T}+\frac{i}{N^{\frac{1}{2}}\lambda}\int_{0}^{T}\,dS\,\int_{{\Lambda^{*}}}\,dp\,\hat{v}(p)F_{S}(p)\Big|

\displaystyle\hskip 56.9055pt\leq\,\frac{C_{\|\hat{v}\|_{w,d},\|f_{0}\|_{d},|\Lambda|,T}}{N^{\frac{1}{2}+\delta_{\Psi,d}}\lambda}\,,

(7.20)

\displaystyle\Big|\int_{\Lambda^{*}}dp\,\big(F_{T}(p)-F_{0}(p)\big)J(p)\,-\,\frac{1}{N}\Big(\int_{0}^{T}\,dS\,Q_{d;T-S,\lambda}(F_{S})[J]

\displaystyle+\,\int_{\Delta[T,2]}\,d\mathbf{S}_{2}\,\sum_{j=1}^{2}\lambda^{j}q^{(j)}_{d,F;\mathbf{S}_{2},\lambda}(F_{S_{2}})[J]\Big)\Big|

\displaystyle\hskip 56.9055pt\leq\,\frac{C_{\|\hat{v}\|_{w,d},\|f_{0}\|_{d},|\Lambda|,T}\|J\|_{\ell^{\infty}({\Lambda^{*}})}}{N^{1+\delta_{F,d}}}\,,

(7.21)

\displaystyle\Big|\int_{\Lambda^{*}}dp\,G_{T}(p)J(p)\,-\,\frac{1}{N}\Big({\mathcal{A}}_{d;T,\lambda}(F)[J]\,+\,{\mathcal{Q}}_{d,G;T,\lambda}(F)[J]\Big)\Big|

\displaystyle\hskip 56.9055pt\leq\,\frac{C_{\|\hat{v}\|_{w,d},\|f_{0}\|_{d},|\Lambda|,T}\|J\|_{2\cap\infty,d}}{N^{1+\delta_{G,d}}}

for all
N>0
larger than a universal constant.

7.2.
Continuum approximation

Recall that

(7.22)

\displaystyle\lambda

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\Big(\frac{\log\log N}{\log N}\Big)^{\frac{r}{(7+{\varepsilon})r+6}}\,,

(7.23)

\displaystyle L

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\lambda^{-2-\frac{2}{r}-{\varepsilon}}\,=\,\Big(\frac{\log N}{\log\log N}\Big)^{\frac{(2+{\varepsilon})r+2}{(7+{\varepsilon})r+6}}\,.

7.2.1.
Evolution of
\Phi

(
6.20
) and Proposition
5.9
imply that

(7.24)

\displaystyle\Phi_{\frac{T}{\lambda^{2}}}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{1}{N^{\frac{1}{2}}\lambda}\operatorname{Con}_{c}(f^{(\Phi)})(T;\lambda)

\displaystyle+\operatorname{Rem}_{2}(\frac{T}{\lambda^{2}};\Phi)\,+\,\operatorname{err}^{(Bog)}_{1,c}(\frac{T}{\lambda^{2}};\Phi)\,+\,\operatorname{err}^{(cen)}_{1}(t;\Phi)\,.

We have that

(7.25)

\displaystyle|\operatorname{err}^{(cen)}_{1}(t;\Phi)|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C_{\|\hat{v}\|_{2(\left\lfloor\frac{r}{2}\right\rfloor+1),w,c},\|f_{0}\|_{2(\left\lfloor\frac{r}{2}\right\rfloor+1),c},r}\langle T\rangle^{3}\frac{|\Lambda|^{\frac{3}{2}}e^{C_{r}\|\hat{v}\|_{2(\left\lfloor\frac{r}{2}\right\rfloor+1),w,c}|\Lambda|/\lambda T}}{N\lambda^{2}}

\displaystyle\Big(1+\frac{|\Lambda|}{N}\Big)\Big(1\,+\,\frac{1}{N^{\frac{1}{2}}\lambda|\Lambda|^{\frac{3}{2}}}\Big)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{C_{\|\hat{v}\|_{2(\left\lfloor\frac{r}{2}\right\rfloor+1),w,c},\|f_{0}\|_{2(\left\lfloor\frac{r}{2}\right\rfloor+1),c},r,T}}{N^{\frac{1}{2}+\delta_{\Psi,c}}\lambda}

by possibly enlarging the constant
C
in the definition (
6.40
) of
\delta_{\Psi,c}
. Combining this inequality with (
6.44
), we find that,

(7.26)

\displaystyle\Big|\Phi_{\frac{T}{\lambda^{2}}}\,-\,\frac{1}{N^{\frac{1}{2}}\lambda}\operatorname{Con}_{c}(f^{(\Phi)})(T;\lambda)\Big|\,\leq\,\frac{C_{\|\hat{v}\|_{2(\left\lfloor\frac{r}{2}\right\rfloor+1),w,c},\|f_{0}\|_{2(\left\lfloor\frac{r}{2}\right\rfloor+1),c},r,{\varepsilon},T}}{N^{\frac{1}{2}+\delta_{\Psi,c}}\lambda}\,.

7.2.2.
Evolution of
f

As a consequence of (
6.23
), and Proposition
5.8
,
5.9
, we have that

(7.27)

\displaystyle\int_{\Lambda^{*}}dp\,\big(f_{\frac{T}{\lambda^{2}}}^{(\Phi)}(p)-f_{0}^{(\Phi)}(p)\big)J(p)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{1}{N}\operatorname{Bol}_{c}(f^{(\Phi)})[J](T;\lambda)

\displaystyle+\,\operatorname{Rem}_{2}(\frac{T}{\lambda^{2}};f[J])\,+\,\operatorname{err}^{(Bog,\operatorname{Bol})}_{2,c}({\frac{T}{\lambda^{2}}};f[J])

\displaystyle+\,J(0)\operatorname{err}^{(Bog,\operatorname{Con})}_{2}({\frac{T}{\lambda^{2}}};f)\,+\,\operatorname{err}^{(cen)}_{2,c}(\frac{T}{\lambda^{2}};f[J])\,.

Observe that

(7.28)

\displaystyle|\operatorname{err}^{(cen)}_{2,c}(\frac{T}{\lambda^{2}};f[J])|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C_{\|\hat{v}\|_{2(\left\lfloor\frac{r}{2}\right\rfloor+1),w,c},\|f_{0}\|_{2(\left\lfloor\frac{r}{2}\right\rfloor+1),c},r}\|J\|_{\infty}\langle T\rangle^{4}\frac{|\Lambda|^{\frac{3}{2}}e^{C_{r}\|\hat{v}\|_{2(\left\lfloor\frac{r}{2}\right\rfloor+1),w,c}|\Lambda|/\lambda T}}{N^{\frac{3}{2}}\lambda^{3}}

\displaystyle\Big(1+\frac{|\Lambda|}{N}\Big)\Big(1\,+\,\frac{1}{N^{\frac{1}{2}}\lambda|\Lambda|^{\frac{3}{2}}}\Big)

\displaystyle\Big[1\,+\,\langle T\rangle^{2}\frac{|\Lambda|^{\frac{3}{2}}e^{C_{r}\|\hat{v}\|_{2(\left\lfloor\frac{r}{2}\right\rfloor+1),w,c}|\Lambda|/\lambda T}}{N^{\frac{1}{2}}\lambda}\Big(1+\frac{|\Lambda|}{N}\Big)\Big(1\,+\,\frac{1}{N^{\frac{1}{2}}\lambda|\Lambda|^{\frac{3}{2}}}\Big)\Big]

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{C_{\|\hat{v}\|_{2(\left\lfloor\frac{r}{2}\right\rfloor+1),w,c},\|f_{0}\|_{2(\left\lfloor\frac{r}{2}\right\rfloor+1),c},r,T}\|J\|_{\infty}}{N^{1+\delta_{F,c}}}

by possibly enlarging the constants in the definition (
6.41
) of
\delta_{F,c}(N,\lambda)
. Then, (
6.45
) yields

\displaystyle\Big|\int_{\Lambda^{*}}dp\,\big(f_{\frac{T}{\lambda^{2}}}^{(\Phi)}(p)-f_{0}^{(\Phi)}(p)\big)J(p)\,-\,\frac{1}{N}\operatorname{Bol}_{c}(f^{(\Phi)})[J](T;\lambda)\Big|

(7.29)

\displaystyle\,\leq\,\frac{C_{\|\hat{v}\|_{2(\left\lfloor\frac{r}{2}\right\rfloor+1),w,c},\|f_{0}\|_{2(\left\lfloor\frac{r}{2}\right\rfloor+1),c},r,{\varepsilon},T}\|J\|_{W^{2\left\lfloor\frac{r}{2}\right\rfloor+2,\infty}}}{N^{1+\delta_{F,c}}}\,.

7.2.3.
Evolution of
g

(
6.33
) and Proposition
5.9
imply

(7.30)

\displaystyle\int_{\Lambda^{*}}dp\,g_{\frac{T}{\lambda^{2}}}^{(\Phi)}(p)J(p)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{1}{N}\int_{0}^{T}\,dS\,\mathrm{abs}_{quart,c}(f^{(\Phi)})[J](S/\lambda^{2})

\displaystyle+\,\frac{1}{N\lambda^{2}}\int_{\Delta[T,2]}\,d\mathbf{S}_{2}\,\big(\operatorname{col}_{c}(f^{(\Phi)})[J](\mathbf{S}_{2}/\lambda^{2})+\mathrm{abs}_{cub,c}(f^{(\Phi)})[J](\mathbf{S}_{2}/\lambda^{2})\big)

\displaystyle+\,\operatorname{Rem}_{2}(\frac{T}{\lambda^{2}};g[J])\,+\,\operatorname{err}^{(dis)}_{1}(\frac{T}{\lambda^{2}};g[J])\,+\,\operatorname{err}^{(dis)}_{2}(\frac{T}{\lambda^{2}};g[J])

\displaystyle+\,J(0)\operatorname{err}^{(Bog,\operatorname{Con})}_{2}(\frac{T}{\lambda^{2}};g)\,+\,\operatorname{err}^{(cen)}_{1}(\frac{T}{\lambda^{2}};g[J])\,+\,\operatorname{err}^{(cen)}_{2}(\frac{T}{\lambda^{2}};g[J])\,.

Analogously to (
7.28
), we have that

(7.31)

\displaystyle|\operatorname{err}^{(cen)}_{1}(\frac{T}{\lambda^{2}};g[J])|\,,\,|\operatorname{err}^{(cen)}_{2}(\frac{T}{\lambda^{2}};g[J])|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{C_{\|\hat{v}\|_{2(\left\lfloor\frac{r}{2}\right\rfloor+1),w,c},\|f_{0}\|_{2(\left\lfloor\frac{r}{2}\right\rfloor+1),c},r,{\varepsilon},T}\|J\|_{\infty}}{N^{1+\delta_{G,c}}}\,,

by again possibly enlarging the constants in the definition (
6.42
) of
\delta_{G,c}
. Applying (
6.46
), we obtain that

(7.32)

\displaystyle\Big|\int_{\Lambda^{*}}dp\,g_{\frac{T}{\lambda^{2}}}^{(\Phi)}(p)J(p)\,-\,\frac{1}{N}\int_{0}^{T}\,dS\,\mathrm{abs}_{quart,c}(f^{(\Phi)})[J](S/\lambda^{2})

\displaystyle-\,\frac{1}{N\lambda^{2}}\int_{\Delta[T,2]}\,d\mathbf{S}_{2}\,\big(\operatorname{col}_{c}(f^{(\Phi)})[J](\mathbf{S}_{2}/\lambda^{2})

\displaystyle+\mathrm{abs}_{cub,c}(f^{(\Phi)})[J](\mathbf{S}_{2}/\lambda^{2})\big)\Big|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{C_{\|\hat{v}\|_{2(\left\lfloor\frac{r}{2}\right\rfloor+1),w,c},\|f_{0}\|_{2(\left\lfloor\frac{r}{2}\right\rfloor+1),c},r,{\varepsilon},T}\|J\|_{2(\left\lfloor\frac{r}{2}\right\rfloor+1),c}}{N^{1+\delta_{G,c}}}\,.

7.2.4.
Conclusion for
L\sim\lambda^{-2-}

Recall that we impose

(7.33)

\displaystyle\lambda

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\Big(\frac{\log\log N}{\log N}\Big)^{\frac{r}{(7+{\varepsilon})r+6}}\,,

(7.34)

\displaystyle L

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\lambda^{-2-\frac{2}{r}-{\varepsilon}}\,=\,\Big(\frac{\log N}{\log\log N}\Big)^{\frac{(2+{\varepsilon})r+2}{(7+{\varepsilon})r+6}}\,.

Again, we consider the effective quantities

(7.35)

\displaystyle\Psi_{T}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\Phi_{\frac{T}{\lambda^{2}}}\,,

(7.36)

\displaystyle F_{T}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle f_{\frac{T}{\lambda^{2}}}^{(\Phi)}\,,

(7.37)

\displaystyle G_{T}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle g_{\frac{T}{\lambda^{2}}}^{(\Phi)}\,.

Let
Q_{c;T-S,\lambda}
,
Q_{c,G;T,\lambda}
and
{\mathcal{A}}_{c;T,\lambda}
be defined analogously to (
2.18
), (
2.15
) respectively (
2.17
) with sums over
{\Lambda^{*}}
replaced by integrals
\frac{1}{(2\pi)^{3}}\int_{{\mathbb{R}}^{3}}
over
{\mathbb{R}}^{3}
. Collecting (
7.26
), (
7.29
), and (
7.32
), we have proved that, for some possibly larger constant
N_{0}=N_{0}(\|\hat{v}\|_{2(\left\lfloor\frac{r}{2}\right\rfloor+1),w,c})
,

(7.38)

\displaystyle\Big|\Psi_{T}+\frac{i}{(2\pi)^{3}N^{\frac{1}{2}}\lambda}\int_{0}^{T}\,dS\,\int_{{\mathbb{R}}^{3}}\,dp\,\hat{v}(p)F_{S}(p)\Big|

\displaystyle\hskip 56.9055pt\leq\,\,\frac{C_{\|\hat{v}\|_{2(\left\lfloor\frac{r}{2}\right\rfloor+1),w,c},\|f_{0}\|_{2(\left\lfloor\frac{r}{2}\right\rfloor+1),c},r,{\varepsilon},T}}{N^{\frac{1}{2}+\delta_{\Psi,c}}\lambda}\,,

(7.39)

\displaystyle\Big|\int_{\Lambda^{*}}dp\,\big(F_{T}(p)-F_{0}(p)\big)J(p)-\frac{1}{N}\int_{0}^{T}\,dS\,Q_{c;T-S,\lambda}(F_{S})\Big|

\displaystyle\hskip 56.9055pt\leq\,\frac{C_{\|\hat{v}\|_{2(\left\lfloor\frac{r}{2}\right\rfloor+1),w,c},\|f_{0}\|_{2(\left\lfloor\frac{r}{2}\right\rfloor+1),c},r,{\varepsilon},T}\|J\|_{W^{2\left\lfloor\frac{r}{2}\right\rfloor+2,\infty}}}{N^{1+\delta_{F,c}}}\,,

(7.40)

\displaystyle\Big|\int_{\Lambda^{*}}dp\,G_{T}(p)J(p)\,-\,\frac{1}{N}\Big({\mathcal{A}}_{c;T,\lambda}(F)[J]\,+\,{\mathcal{Q}}_{c,G;T,\lambda}(F)[J]\Big)\Big|

\displaystyle\hskip 56.9055pt\leq\,\frac{C_{\|\hat{v}\|_{2(\left\lfloor\frac{r}{2}\right\rfloor+1),w,c},\|f_{0}\|_{2(\left\lfloor\frac{r}{2}\right\rfloor+1),c},r,{\varepsilon},T}\|J\|_{2(\left\lfloor\frac{r}{2}\right\rfloor+1),c}}{N^{1+\delta_{G,c}}}

for all
N\geq N_{0}
.

Appendix A
Calculus for creation and annihilation operators

Lemma A.1
.

Let
\nu
be a translation invariant state, i.e.,

(A.1)

\displaystyle\nu(A)\,=\,\nu(e^{ix\cdot{\mathcal{P}}}Ae^{-ix\cdot{\mathcal{P}}})

for all
x\in{\mathbb{R}}^{3}
and all observables
A
. Then we have

(A.2)

\displaystyle\nu(\prod_{i=1}^{m}a_{p_{i}}^{(\sigma_{i})})\,=\,\frac{\delta(\sum_{i=1}^{m}\sigma_{i}p_{i})}{|\Lambda|}\nu(\prod_{i=1}^{m}a_{p_{i}}^{(\sigma_{i})})\,.

Proof.

By translation invariance, we have that

(A.3)

\displaystyle\nu(\prod_{i=1}^{m}a_{p_{i}}^{(\sigma_{i})})

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\nu(e^{ix\cdot{\mathcal{P}}}\prod_{i=1}^{m}a_{p_{i}}^{(\sigma_{i})}e^{-ix\cdot{\mathcal{P}}})

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle e^{ix\cdot(\sum_{i=1}^{m}\sigma_{i}p_{i})}\nu(\prod_{i=1}^{m}a_{p_{i}}^{(\sigma_{i})})

for all
x\in{\mathbb{R}}^{3}
. Integrating both sides
\int_{\Lambda}dx
, we obtain

(A.4)

\displaystyle|\Lambda|\nu(\prod_{i=1}^{m}a_{p_{i}}^{(\sigma_{i})})\,=\,\delta(\sum_{i=1}^{m}\sigma_{i}p_{i}))\nu(\prod_{i=1}^{m}a_{p_{i}}^{(\sigma_{i})})\,,

which yields the statement.
∎

Lemma A.2

(Cumulant Formula)
.

Recall from (
4.13
) that

(A.5)

\displaystyle\kappa_{n}\,=\,(-\partial_{\mu})^{n}\big|_{\mu=0}\int_{{\Lambda^{*}}}dp\log\big(1-e^{-(K(p)-\mu)}\big)\,.

Then there are constants
a_{n,k}\in{\mathbb{R}}
such that

(A.6)

\displaystyle\kappa_{n}\,=\,\sum_{k=1}^{n-1}a_{n,k}\int_{\Lambda^{*}}dp\,f_{0}(p)^{k}(1+f_{0}(p))^{n-k}\,+\,\delta_{n,1}\int_{\Lambda^{*}}dp\,f_{0}(p)\,,

where
f_{0}(p)=(e^{K(p)}-1)^{-1}
.

Proof.

We will show, in more generality that

(A.7)

\displaystyle\kappa_{n}(\mu)

\displaystyle:=

\displaystyle(-\partial_{\mu})^{n}\int_{{\Lambda^{*}}}dp\log\big(1-e^{-(K(p)-\mu)}\big)

(A.8)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\sum_{k=1}^{n-1}a_{n,k}\int_{\Lambda^{*}}dp\,f_{\mu}(p)^{k}(1+f_{\mu}(p))^{n-k}\,+\,\delta_{n,1}\int_{\Lambda^{*}}dp\,f_{\mu}(p)

for all
\mu\leq 0
, where
f_{\mu}(p)=(e^{K(p)-\mu}-1)^{-1}
. A straightforward calculation yields

(A.9)

\displaystyle\kappa_{1}(\mu)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\int_{\Lambda^{*}}dp\,f_{\mu}(p)\,,

(A.10)

\displaystyle\kappa_{2}(\mu)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\int_{\Lambda^{*}}dp\,f_{\mu}(p)(1+f_{\mu}(p))\,.

Observe that

(A.11)

\displaystyle-\partial_{\mu}f_{\mu}\,=\,f_{\mu}(1+f_{\mu})\,.

Now assume that (
A.8
) for some fixed
n\in{\mathbb{N}}
,
n\geq 2
. By definition, we have that

(A.12)

\displaystyle\kappa_{n+1}(\mu)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle(-\partial_{\mu})\kappa_{n}(\mu)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\int_{\Lambda^{*}}dp\,\sum_{k=1}^{n-1}a_{n,k}\big(kf_{\mu}(p)^{k}(1+f_{\mu}(p))^{n+1-k}

\displaystyle+\,(n-k)f_{\mu}(p)^{k+1}(1+f_{\mu}(p))^{n-k}\big)\,.

After an index shift, we can further simplify this to

(A.13)

\displaystyle\kappa_{n+1}(\mu)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle a_{n,1}\int_{\Lambda^{*}}dp\,f_{\mu}(p)(1+f_{\mu}(p))^{n}

\displaystyle+\,a_{n,n-1}\int_{\Lambda^{*}}dp\,f_{\mu}(p)^{n}(1+f_{\mu}(p))

\displaystyle+\,\sum_{k=2}^{n-1}(ka_{n,k}+(n+1-k)a_{n,k-1})

\displaystyle\int_{\Lambda^{*}}dp\,f_{\mu}(p)^{k}(1+f_{\mu}(p))^{n+1-k}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\sum_{k=1}^{n}a_{n+1,k}\int_{\Lambda^{*}}dp\,f_{\mu}(p)^{k}(1+f_{\mu}(p))^{n+1-k}\,,

for some
a_{n+1,k}\in{\mathbb{R}}
. This finishes the proof.
∎

For the following standard result, we need to introduce some notation. For a proof of the statement, we refer, e.g., to
[
34
,
35
]
. Given a finite ordered subset
J=\{j_{1}<j_{2}<\ldots<j_{r}\}\subset{\mathbb{N}}
and
\sigma_{j_{k}}\in\{\pm 1\}
, we define the ordered product

(A.14)

\displaystyle\prod_{j\in J}a_{p_{j}}^{(\sigma_{j})}\,:=\,a_{p_{j_{1}}}^{(\sigma_{j_{1}})}\ldots a_{p_{j_{r}}}^{(\sigma_{j_{r}})}\,.

In addition, we abbreviate

(A.15)

\displaystyle\mathbf{p}_{J}\,:=\,(p_{j_{k}})_{k=1}^{r}\,,

as well as

(A.16)

\displaystyle a^{(\sigma)}(\mathbf{p}_{J})\,:=\,\prod_{j\in J}a^{(\sigma)}_{p_{j}}\,.

Furthermore, we define the sets

(A.17)

\displaystyle J_{\pm}\,:=\,\{j\in J\mid\sigma_{j}=\pm 1\}

and the Wick-ordered product

(A.18)

\displaystyle:\prod_{j\in J}a_{p_{j}}^{(\sigma_{j})}:\,:=\,a^{+}(\mathbf{p}_{J_{+}})a(\mathbf{p}_{J_{-}})

with all creation operators to the left, and all annihilation operators to the right.

Finally, in order to keep track of the correct scaling, it is useful to work with the rescaled
\ell^{2}({\Lambda^{*}})
-norm

(A.19)

\displaystyle\|H\|_{L^{2}({\Lambda^{*}})}\,=\,\frac{1}{\sqrt{|\Lambda|}}\|H\|_{\ell^{2}({\Lambda^{*}})}\,,

see (
2.1
). More generally, we also define

(A.20)

\displaystyle\|H\|_{L^{\infty}_{\mathbf{p}_{m}}L^{2}_{\mathbf{k}_{n}}(({\Lambda^{*}})^{m+n})}\,:=\,\sup_{\mathbf{p}_{m}\in({\Lambda^{*}})^{m}}\Big(\int_{({\Lambda^{*}})^{n}}d\mathbf{k}_{n}|H(\mathbf{p}_{m},\mathbf{k}_{n})|^{2}\Big)^{\frac{1}{2}}\,,

(A.21)

\displaystyle\|H\|_{L^{2}_{\mathbf{k}_{n}}L^{\infty}_{\mathbf{p}_{m}}(({\Lambda^{*}})^{m+n})}\,:=\,\Big(\int_{({\Lambda^{*}})^{n}}d\mathbf{k}_{n}\sup_{\mathbf{p}_{m}\in({\Lambda^{*}})^{m}}|H(\mathbf{p}_{m},\mathbf{k}_{n})|^{2}\Big)^{\frac{1}{2}}\,,

where in the case
n=0
, this norm reduces to
\|H\|_{L^{\infty}_{\mathbf{p}_{m}}(({\Lambda^{*}})^{m})}
, and in the case
m=0
, to
\|H\|_{L^{2}_{\mathbf{k}_{n}}(({\Lambda^{*}})^{n})}
.

Lemma A.3

(Wick’s Theorem)
.

Let
\sigma_{j}\in\{\pm 1\}
,
p_{j}\in{\mathbb{R}}^{3}
for all
j\in\{1,\ldots,n\}
,
n\in{\mathbb{N}}
. Then we have that

(A.22)

\displaystyle\prod_{j=1}^{n}a_{p_{j}}^{(\sigma_{j})}\,=\,\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}J\subseteq\\
\{1,\ldots,n\}\end{subarray}}\langle\Omega_{0},\prod_{\begin{subarray}{c}j\in\\
\{1,\ldots,n\}\setminus J\end{subarray}}a_{p_{j}}^{(\sigma_{j})}\Omega_{0}\rangle:\prod_{j\in J}a_{p_{j}}^{(\sigma_{j})}:\,.

Lemma A.4

(Wick-ordered operator bound)
.

Let
M\in{\mathbb{N}}_{0}
,
n\in{\mathbb{N}}
,
J:=\{1,\ldots,n\}
,
\sigma_{j}\in\{\pm 1\}
for all
j\in J
. Let
H:({\Lambda^{*}})^{n}\to{\mathbb{C}}
, and
g_{j}:{\Lambda^{*}}\to{\mathbb{C}}
be given functions. Then the following holds true

(1)

If
J_{\pm}\neq\emptyset
, we have that

\displaystyle\Big\|\int_{({\Lambda^{*}})^{n}}d\mathbf{p}_{n}H(\mathbf{p}_{n})\delta(\sum_{j=1}^{n}p_{j}\sigma_{j})\prod_{j=1}^{n}g_{j}(p_{j}):\prod_{j=1}^{n}a_{p_{j}}^{(\sigma_{j})}:P_{M}\Big\|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\||H|^{\frac{1}{2}}\prod_{j\in J_{-}}g_{j}(p_{j})\delta(\sum_{j=1}^{n}\sigma_{j}p_{j})^{\frac{1}{2}}\|_{L^{\infty}_{\mathbf{p}_{J_{+}}}L^{2}_{\mathbf{p}_{J_{-}}}}\||H|^{\frac{1}{2}}\prod_{j\in J_{+}}g_{j}(p_{j})\delta(\sum_{j=1}^{n}\sigma_{j}p_{j})^{\frac{1}{2}}\|_{L^{\infty}_{\mathbf{p}_{J_{-}}}L^{2}_{\mathbf{p}_{J_{+}}}}

\displaystyle(M+\sum_{j=1}^{n}\sigma_{j})_{|J_{+}|}^{\frac{1}{2}}(M)_{|J_{-}|}^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathds{1}_{M\geq|J_{-}|}\,,

where

(A.23)

\displaystyle(x)_{m}\,:=\,\prod_{k=0}^{m-1}(x-k)

denotes the falling factorial.

(2)

If
J_{+}=\emptyset
and
n\geq 2
, we find that

(A.24)

\displaystyle\Big\|\int_{({\Lambda^{*}})^{n}}d\mathbf{p}_{n}H(\mathbf{p}_{n})\delta(\sum_{j=1}^{n}p_{j})\prod_{j=1}^{n}a_{p_{j}}P_{M}\Big\|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\big(\|H\|_{L^{2}_{\mathbf{p}_{n-2}L^{\infty}_{p_{n-1},p_{n}}}}^{2}(M-n+1)+|\Lambda|\|\delta(\sum_{j=1}^{n}p_{j})^{\frac{1}{2}}H\|_{L^{2}_{\mathbf{p}_{n}}}^{2}\big)^{\frac{1}{2}}(M)_{n-1}^{\frac{1}{2}}\,.

Similarly, in the case
J_{-}=\emptyset
, we have that

(A.25)

\displaystyle\Big\|\int_{({\Lambda^{*}})^{n}}d\mathbf{p}_{n}\,H(\mathbf{p}_{n})\delta(\sum_{j=1}^{n}p_{j})a^{+}(\mathbf{p}_{n})P_{M}\Big\|

(A.26)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\big(\|H\|_{L^{2}_{\mathbf{p}_{n-2}}L^{\infty}_{p_{n-1},p_{n}}}^{2}(M+1)+|\Lambda|\|\delta(\sum_{j=1}^{n}p_{j})^{\frac{1}{2}}H\|_{L^{2}_{\mathbf{p}_{n}}}^{2}\big)^{\frac{1}{2}}(M+n)_{n-1}^{\frac{1}{2}}\,.

(3)

If
n=1
, we obtain

(A.27)

\displaystyle\|a_{0}P_{M}\|\,=\,\sqrt{M|\Lambda|}\,.

Proof.

Let
\Phi_{+}\in{\mathcal{F}}_{M+\sum_{j=1}^{n}\sigma_{j}}
and
\Phi_{-}\in{\mathcal{F}}_{M}
be two normalized test functions.

In the case
J_{\pm}\neq\emptyset
, we have that

(A.28)

\displaystyle\Big|\Big\langle\Phi_{+},\int_{({\Lambda^{*}})^{n}}d\mathbf{p}_{n}H(\mathbf{p}_{n})\delta(\sum_{j=1}^{n}p_{j}\sigma_{j})\prod_{j=1}^{n}g_{j}(p_{j}):\prod_{j=1}^{n}a_{p_{j}}^{(\sigma_{j})}:\Phi_{-}\Big\rangle\Big|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\int_{({\Lambda^{*}})^{n}}d\mathbf{p}_{n}|H(\mathbf{p}_{n})|\delta(\sum_{j=1}^{n}p_{j}\sigma_{j})\prod_{j=1}^{n}|g_{j}(p_{j})|\|a(\mathbf{p}_{J_{+}})\Phi_{+}\|\|a(\mathbf{p}_{J_{-}})\Phi_{-}\|\,,

where we applied Cauchy-Schwarz w.r.t. the inner product on
{\mathcal{F}}
. Let
\alpha\in[0,1]
be arbitrary. Then Cauchy-Schwarz w.r.t.
d\mathbf{p}_{n}
implies that we can estimate the last expression by

(A.29)

\displaystyle\Big(\int_{({\Lambda^{*}})^{n}}d\mathbf{p}_{n}\delta(\sum_{j=1}^{n}p_{j}\sigma_{j})|H(\mathbf{p}_{n})|\prod_{j\in J_{-}}|g_{j}(p_{j})|^{2}\|a(\mathbf{p}_{J_{+}})\Phi_{+}\|^{2}\Big)^{\frac{1}{2}}

\displaystyle\Big(\int_{({\Lambda^{*}})^{n}}d\mathbf{p}_{n}\delta(\sum_{j=1}^{n}p_{j}\sigma_{j})|H(\mathbf{p}_{n})|\prod_{j\in J_{+}}|g_{j}(p_{j})|^{2}\|a(\mathbf{p}_{J_{-}})\Phi_{-}\|^{2}\Big)^{\frac{1}{2}}\,.

Observe that the Pull-Through Formula implies that

(A.30)

\displaystyle\int d\mathbf{p}_{m}a^{+}(\mathbf{p}_{m})a(\mathbf{p}_{m})

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\int d\mathbf{p}_{m-1}a^{+}(\mathbf{p}_{m-1})a(\mathbf{p}_{m-1})(\mathcal{N}_{b}-m+1)_{+}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle(\mathcal{N}_{b})_{m}\,,

where we define
(\mathcal{N}_{b})_{m}\big|_{{\mathcal{F}}_{j}}:=0
for all
j\in\{0,1,\ldots,m-1\}
, in accordance with (
3.3
). In particular, we have that
(\mathcal{N}_{b})_{m}\geq 0
as a quadratic form on
{\mathcal{F}}
.

Then, integrating the first factor first w.r.t. the momenta
p_{J_{-}}
and then
p_{J_{+}}
, and opposite for the second factor, (
A.29
) has the upper bound

(A.31)

\displaystyle\||H|^{\frac{1}{2}}\prod_{j\in J_{-}}g_{j}(p_{j})\delta(\sum_{j=1}^{n}\sigma_{j}p_{j})^{\frac{1}{2}}\|_{L^{\infty}_{\mathbf{p}_{J_{+}}}L^{2}_{\mathbf{p}_{J_{-}}}}\|(\mathcal{N}_{b})_{|J_{+}|}^{\frac{1}{2}}\Phi_{+}\|

\displaystyle\||H|^{\frac{1}{2}}\prod_{j\in J_{+}}g_{j}(p_{j})\delta(\sum_{j=1}^{n}\sigma_{j}p_{j})^{\frac{1}{2}}\|_{L^{\infty}_{\mathbf{p}_{J_{-}}}L^{2}_{\mathbf{p}_{J_{+}}}}\|(\mathcal{N}_{b})_{|J_{-}|}^{\frac{1}{2}}\Phi_{-}\|\,.

Using the fact that
\Phi_{+}\in{\mathcal{F}}_{M+\sum_{j=1}^{n}\sigma_{j}}
,
\Phi_{-}\in{\mathcal{F}}_{M}
and that both are normalized with norm 1, we have that

(A.32)

\displaystyle\|(\mathcal{N}_{b})_{|J_{+}|}^{\frac{1}{2}}\Phi_{+}\|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle(M+\sum_{j=1}^{n}\sigma_{j})_{|J_{+}|}^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathds{1}_{M+\sum_{j=1}^{n}\sigma_{j}\geq|J_{+}|}\,,

(A.33)

\displaystyle\|(\mathcal{N}_{b})_{|J_{-}|}^{\frac{1}{2}}\Phi_{-}\|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle(M)_{|J_{-}|}^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathds{1}_{M\geq|J_{-}|}\,.

Observe that
\sum_{j=1}^{n}\sigma_{j}=|J_{+}|-|J_{-}|
. Collecting (
A.28
)–(
A.33
), we have proved that

(A.34)

\displaystyle\Big|\Big\langle\Phi_{+},\int_{({\Lambda^{*}})^{n}}d\mathbf{p}_{n}H(\mathbf{p}_{n})\delta(\sum_{j=1}^{n}p_{j}\sigma_{j})\prod_{j=1}^{n}g_{j}(p_{j}):\prod_{j=1}^{n}a_{p_{j}}^{(\sigma_{j})}:\Phi_{-}\Big\rangle\Big|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\||H|^{\frac{1}{2}}\prod_{j\in J_{-}}g_{j}(p_{j})\delta(\sum_{j=1}^{n}\sigma_{j}p_{j})^{\frac{1}{2}}\|_{L^{\infty}_{\mathbf{p}_{J_{+}}}L^{2}_{\mathbf{p}_{J_{-}}}}\||H|^{\frac{1}{2}}\prod_{j\in J_{+}}g_{j}(p_{j})\delta(\sum_{j=1}^{n}\sigma_{j}p_{j})^{\frac{1}{2}}\|_{L^{\infty}_{\mathbf{p}_{J_{-}}}L^{2}_{\mathbf{p}_{J_{+}}}}

\displaystyle(M+\sum_{j=1}^{n}\sigma_{j})_{|J_{+}|}^{\frac{1}{2}}(M)_{|J_{-}|}^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathds{1}_{M\geq|J_{-}|}\,.

In the case
J_{-}=\emptyset
, we have that

(A.35)

\displaystyle\Big\|\int_{({\Lambda^{*}})^{n}}d\mathbf{p}_{n}\,H(\mathbf{p}_{n})\delta(\sum_{j=1}^{n}p_{j})a^{+}(\mathbf{p}_{n})P_{M}\Big\|

(A.36)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\Big\|\int_{({\Lambda^{*}})^{n}}d\mathbf{p}_{n}\,\overline{H}(\mathbf{p}_{n})\delta(\sum_{j=1}^{n}p_{j})a(\mathbf{p}_{n})P_{M+n}\Big\|\,,

which reduces to the case
J_{+}=\emptyset
.

In the case
J_{+}=\emptyset
and
n\geq 2
, we find that

(A.37)

\displaystyle\Big\|\int_{({\Lambda^{*}})^{n}}d\mathbf{p}_{n}\,H(\mathbf{p}_{n})\delta(\sum_{j=1}^{n}p_{j})a(\mathbf{p}_{n})\Phi_{-}\Big\|^{2}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\int_{({\Lambda^{*}})^{2n}}d\mathbf{p}_{n}d\mathbf{q}_{n}\,\overline{H}(\mathbf{p}_{n})H(\mathbf{q}_{n})\delta(\sum_{j=1}^{n}p_{j})\delta(\sum_{j=1}^{n}q_{j})\langle\Phi_{-},a^{+}(\mathbf{p}_{n})a(\mathbf{q}_{n})\Phi_{-}\rangle

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\int_{({\Lambda^{*}})^{2n}}d\mathbf{p}_{n}d\mathbf{q}_{n}\,\overline{H}(\mathbf{p}_{n})H(\mathbf{q}_{n})\delta(\sum_{j=1}^{n}p_{j})\delta(\sum_{j=1}^{n}q_{j})

\displaystyle\langle\Phi_{-},a^{+}(\mathbf{p}_{n-1})a_{q_{n}}a^{+}_{p_{n}}a(\mathbf{q}_{n-1})\Phi_{-}\rangle

\displaystyle-\int_{({\Lambda^{*}})}dp_{n}\,\|A_{p_{n}}\Phi_{-}\|^{2}\,,

where

(A.38)

\displaystyle A_{p_{n}}:=\int_{({\Lambda^{*}})^{n-1}}d\mathbf{p}_{n-1}\,H(\mathbf{p}_{n})\delta(\sum_{j=1}^{n}p_{j})a(\mathbf{p}_{n-1})\,.

Using Cauchy-Schwarz first on the inner product on
{\mathcal{F}}
, and then w.r.t.
d\mathbf{p}_{n}d\mathbf{q}_{n}
, we find the upper bound

(A.39)

\displaystyle\Big\|\int_{({\Lambda^{*}})^{n}}d\mathbf{p}_{n}\,H(\mathbf{p}_{n})\delta(\sum_{j=1}^{n}p_{j})a(\mathbf{p}_{n})\Phi_{-}\Big\|^{2}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\int_{({\Lambda^{*}})^{2n}}d\mathbf{p}_{n}d\mathbf{q}_{n}\,|H(\mathbf{p}_{n})||H(\mathbf{q}_{n})|\delta(\sum_{j=1}^{n}p_{j})\delta(\sum_{j=1}^{n}q_{j})

\displaystyle\|a_{q_{n}}^{+}a(\mathbf{p}_{n-1})\Phi_{-}\|\|a_{p_{n}}^{+}a(\mathbf{q}_{n-1})\Phi_{-}\|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\Big(\int_{({\Lambda^{*}})^{2n}}d\mathbf{p}_{n}d\mathbf{q}_{n}\,\delta(\sum_{j=1}^{n}p_{j})\delta(\sum_{j=1}^{n}q_{j})|H(\mathbf{q}_{n})|^{2}\|a_{q_{n}}^{+}a(\mathbf{p}_{n-1})\Phi_{-}\|^{2}\Big)^{\frac{1}{2}}

\displaystyle\Big(\int_{({\Lambda^{*}})^{2n}}d\mathbf{p}_{n}d\mathbf{q}_{n}\,\delta(\sum_{j=1}^{n}p_{j})\delta(\sum_{j=1}^{n}q_{j})|H(\mathbf{p}_{n})|^{2}\|a_{p_{n}}^{+}a(\mathbf{q}_{n-1})\Phi_{-}\|^{2}\Big)^{\frac{1}{2}}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\int_{({\Lambda^{*}})^{2n-1}}d\mathbf{p}_{n-1}d\mathbf{q}_{n}\,\delta(\sum_{j=1}^{n}q_{j})|H(\mathbf{q}_{n})|^{2}\|a_{q_{n}}a(\mathbf{p}_{n-1})\Phi_{-}\|^{2}

\displaystyle+\,|\Lambda|\int_{({\Lambda^{*}})^{n}}d\mathbf{q}_{n}\,\delta(\sum_{j=1}^{n}q_{j})|H(\mathbf{q}_{n})|^{2}\|(\mathcal{N}_{b})_{n-1}^{\frac{1}{2}}\Phi_{-}\|^{2}\,,

where we used that
[a_{q_{n}},a_{q_{n}}^{+}]=|\Lambda|
together with (
A.30
). Using (
A.30
) again, we conclude

(A.40)

\displaystyle\Big\|\int_{({\Lambda^{*}})^{n}}d\mathbf{p}_{n}\,H(\mathbf{p}_{n})\delta(\sum_{j=1}^{n}p_{j})a(\mathbf{p}_{n})\Phi_{-}\Big\|^{2}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\|H\|_{L^{2}_{\mathbf{p}_{n-2}}L^{\infty}_{p_{n-1},p_{n}}}^{2}\|(\mathcal{N}_{b})_{n}^{\frac{1}{2}}\Phi_{-}\|^{2}+|\Lambda|\|\delta(\sum_{j=1}^{n}p_{j})^{\frac{1}{2}}H\|_{L^{2}_{\mathbf{p}_{n}}}^{2}\|(\mathcal{N}_{b})_{n-1}^{\frac{1}{2}}\Phi_{-}\|^{2}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\big(\|H\|_{L^{2}_{\mathbf{p}_{n-2}}L^{\infty}_{p_{n-1},p_{n}}}^{2}(M-n+1)+|\Lambda|\|\delta(\sum_{j=1}^{n}p_{j})^{\frac{1}{2}}H\|_{L^{2}_{\mathbf{p}_{n}}}^{2}\big)(M)_{n-1}\,.

Finally, we have

(A.41)

\displaystyle\|a_{0}P_{M}\|^{2}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\sup_{\|\Phi\|=1}\langle P_{M}\Phi,a_{0}^{+}a_{0}P_{M}\Phi\rangle

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\langle P_{M}\Phi,a_{0}a_{0}^{+}P_{M}\Phi\rangle-|\Lambda|\|P_{M}\|

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\|a_{0}^{+}P_{M}\|^{2}\,-\,|\Lambda|\,,

where we used that
\|P_{M}\|=1
. In addition, we have that

(A.42)

\displaystyle\|a_{0}^{+}P_{M}\|\,=\,\|P_{M+1}a_{0}^{+}\|\,=\,\|a_{0}P_{M+1}\|\,.

(
A.41
) and (
A.42
) imply

(A.43)

\displaystyle\|a_{0}P_{M}\|^{2}\,=\,M|\Lambda|\,.

This finishes the proof.
∎

Appendix B
Propagation of approximate restricted quasifreeness

Lemma B.1
.

Let

(B.1)

\displaystyle{\mathcal{U}}_{N}(t)\;:=\;{\mathcal{V}}_{HFB}^{*}(t){\mathcal{W}}^{*}[\sqrt{N|\Lambda|}\phi_{0}]e^{-it{\mathcal{H}}_{N}}{\mathcal{W}}[\sqrt{N|\Lambda|}\phi_{0}]\,,

where for the definition of
{\mathcal{V}}_{HFB}
, we refer to (
3.7
). Then fluctuation dynamics
{\mathcal{U}}_{N}
obeys

(B.2)

\begin{cases}i\partial_{t}{\mathcal{U}}_{N}(t)\,&=\,({\mathcal{H}}_{cub}(t)+{\mathcal{H}}_{quart}(t)){\mathcal{U}}_{N}(t),\\
{\mathcal{U}}_{N}(0)\,&=\,{\bf 1}\,,\end{cases}

where
{\mathcal{H}}_{cub}(t)
is defined in (
2.8
),
{\mathcal{H}}_{quart}(t)
in (
2.9
).

Proof.

We start by defining the auxiliary dynamics

(B.3)

\displaystyle\widetilde{\mathcal{U}}_{N}(t)\,:=\,{\mathcal{W}}^{*}[\sqrt{N|\Lambda|}\phi_{0}]e^{-i{\mathcal{H}}_{N}t}{\mathcal{W}}[\sqrt{N|\Lambda|}\phi_{0}].

We have that

(B.4)

\displaystyle i\partial_{t}\widetilde{\mathcal{U}}_{N}(t)\,

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\,{\mathcal{W}}^{*}[\sqrt{N|\Lambda|}\phi_{0}]{\mathcal{H}}_{N}{\mathcal{W}}[\sqrt{N|\Lambda|}\phi_{0}]\widetilde{\mathcal{U}}_{N}(t)\,.

Using
\phi_{0}=|\Lambda|^{-1/2}
, the explicit expressions for the terms on the right hand side are given by

(B.5)

\displaystyle{\mathcal{W}}^{*}[\sqrt{N|\Lambda|}\phi_{0}]\,{\mathcal{H}}_{N}\,{\mathcal{W}}[\sqrt{N|\Lambda|}\phi_{0}]

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{N|\Lambda|\lambda}{2}\int_{\Lambda}dx\,v(x)\,+\,\frac{1}{2}\int_{\Lambda}dx\,a_{x}^{+}(-\Delta_{x})a_{x}

\displaystyle+\lambda\sqrt{N}\int_{\Lambda^{2}}dx\,dy\,v(x-y)(a^{+}_{y}+a_{y})\,+\,\lambda\int_{\Lambda^{2}}dx\,dy\,v(x-y)a^{+}_{y}a_{y}

\displaystyle+\lambda\int_{\Lambda^{2}}dx\,dy\,v(x-y)\,\big(a^{+}_{x}a_{y}\,+\,\frac{1}{2}(a^{+}_{x}a^{+}_{y}\,+\,a_{y}a_{x})\big)

\displaystyle+\frac{\lambda}{\sqrt{N}}\int_{\Lambda^{2}}dx\,dy\,v(x-y)a_{x}^{+}(a_{y}+a^{+}_{y})a_{y}

\displaystyle+\frac{\lambda}{2N}\int_{\Lambda^{2}}dx\,dy\,v(x-y)a_{x}^{+}a_{y}^{+}a_{y}a_{x}\,.

Using the fact that
\hat{v}(0)=0
, and recalling definitions (
1.34
)–(
1.35
), we thus obtain

(B.6)

\begin{cases}i\partial_{t}\widetilde{\mathcal{U}}_{N}(t)&=({\mathcal{H}}_{HFB}+{\mathcal{H}}_{cub}+{\mathcal{H}}_{quart})\widetilde{\mathcal{U}}_{N}(t),\\
\widetilde{\mathcal{U}}_{N}(0)&={\bf 1}\,.\end{cases}

In particular, using

(B.7)

\displaystyle{\mathcal{U}}_{N}(t)\,=\,{\mathcal{V}}_{HFB}^{*}(t)\widetilde{\mathcal{U}}_{N}(t)\,,

a straight-forward calculation yields that
{\mathcal{U}}_{N}
satisfies (
B.2
). This concludes the proof.
∎

Next, we adjust Proposition 3.1 in
[
74
]
to our present context.

Lemma B.2
.

Assume
|\Lambda|\geq 1
. Let
\widetilde{{\mathcal{U}}}_{N}
be defined as in (
B.6
). Then there are
C_{\ell},K_{\ell}>0
such that

(B.8)

\displaystyle\Big\|(\mathcal{N}_{b}+|\Lambda|)^{\frac{\ell}{2}}\widetilde{{\mathcal{U}}}_{N}(t)(\mathcal{N}_{b}+|\Lambda|)^{-\frac{\ell}{2}}\big(1+\frac{\mathcal{N}_{b}}{N|\Lambda|}\big)^{-\frac{1}{2}}\Big\|\,\leq\,C_{\ell}e^{K_{\ell}\|\hat{v}\|_{w,d}\lambda|\Lambda|t}

for all
\ell\in{\mathbb{N}}
.

Proof.

We follow the steps of the proof in
[
74
]
and point out the differences. We show the statement by induction. Let
\psi\in{\mathcal{F}}
be arbitrary.

Step 1:

(
B.6
) implies that

(B.9)

\displaystyle\partial_{t}\langle\widetilde{{\mathcal{U}}}_{N}(t)\psi,(\mathcal{N}_{b}+|\Lambda|)\widetilde{{\mathcal{U}}}_{N}(t)\psi\rangle

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-i\langle\widetilde{{\mathcal{U}}}_{N}(t)\psi,[\mathcal{N}_{b},{\mathcal{H}}_{HFB}+{\mathcal{H}}_{cub}+{\mathcal{H}}_{quart}]\widetilde{{\mathcal{U}}}_{N}(t)\psi\rangle

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-i\langle\widetilde{{\mathcal{U}}}_{N}(t)\psi,[\mathcal{N}_{b},{\mathcal{H}}_{HFB}+{\mathcal{H}}_{cub}]\widetilde{{\mathcal{U}}}_{N}(t)\psi\rangle\,.

Recalling (
1.34
) and (
1.35
), we have that

(B.10)

\displaystyle[\mathcal{N}_{b},{\mathcal{H}}_{HFB}]

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\lambda\int d\mathbf{p}_{2}\,\hat{v}(p_{1})\delta(p_{1}-p_{2})(a_{p_{1}}^{+}a_{-p_{2}}^{+}-a_{p_{1}}a_{-p_{2}})\,,

(B.11)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{\lambda}{\sqrt{N}}\int d\mathbf{p}_{3}\,\hat{v}(p_{2})\delta(p_{1}+p_{2}-p_{3})(a_{p_{1}}^{+}a_{p_{2}}^{+}a_{p_{3}}\,-\,a_{p_{3}}^{+}a_{p_{2}}a_{p_{1}})\,.

Employing Lemma
A.4
, (
B.9
) thus yields

(B.12)

\displaystyle|\partial_{t}\langle\widetilde{{\mathcal{U}}}_{N}(t)\psi,(\mathcal{N}_{b}+|\Lambda|)\widetilde{{\mathcal{U}}}_{N}(t)\psi\rangle|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C\|\hat{v}\|_{w,d}\lambda\Big(\langle\widetilde{{\mathcal{U}}}_{N}(t)\psi,(\mathcal{N}_{b}+|\Lambda|)\widetilde{{\mathcal{U}}}_{N}(t)\psi\rangle

\displaystyle+\,\frac{1}{\sqrt{N}}\langle\widetilde{{\mathcal{U}}}_{N}(t)\psi,\mathcal{N}_{b}^{\frac{3}{2}}\widetilde{{\mathcal{U}}}_{N}(t)\psi\rangle\Big)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C\|\hat{v}\|_{w,d}\lambda\Big(\langle\widetilde{{\mathcal{U}}}_{N}(t)\psi,(\mathcal{N}_{b}+|\Lambda|)\widetilde{{\mathcal{U}}}_{N}(t)\psi\rangle

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{N}\langle\widetilde{{\mathcal{U}}}_{N}(t)\psi,\mathcal{N}_{b}^{2}\widetilde{{\mathcal{U}}}_{N}(t)\psi\rangle\Big)\,.

Using

(B.13)

\displaystyle{\mathcal{W}}[\sqrt{N|\Lambda|}\phi_{0}]a_{p}{\mathcal{W}}^{*}[\sqrt{N|\Lambda|}\phi_{0}]\,=\,a_{p}\,-\,\sqrt{N}\delta(p)\,,

we derive that

(B.14)

\displaystyle[\mathcal{N}_{b},{\mathcal{W}}^{*}[\sqrt{N|\Lambda|}\phi_{0}]]

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-\sqrt{N}{\mathcal{W}}^{*}[\sqrt{N|\Lambda|}\phi_{0}](a_{0}+a_{0}^{+})+N|\Lambda|{\mathcal{W}}^{*}[\sqrt{N|\Lambda|}\phi_{0}]

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-\big(\sqrt{N}(a_{0}+a_{0}^{+})+N|\Lambda|\big){\mathcal{W}}^{*}[\sqrt{N|\Lambda|}\phi_{0}]\,,

(B.15)

\displaystyle]

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle{\mathcal{W}}[\sqrt{N|\Lambda|}\phi_{0}]\big(\sqrt{N}(a_{0}+a_{0}^{+})+N|\Lambda|\big)\,.

From these identities and using
[\mathcal{N}_{b},{\mathcal{H}}_{N}]=0
, we obtain that

(B.16)

\displaystyle[\mathcal{N}_{b},\widetilde{{\mathcal{U}}}_{N}(t)]

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle[\mathcal{N}_{b},{\mathcal{W}}^{*}[\sqrt{N|\Lambda|}\phi_{0}]]e^{-it{\mathcal{H}}_{N}}{\mathcal{W}}[\sqrt{N|\Lambda|}\phi_{0}]

\displaystyle+\,{\mathcal{W}}^{*}[\sqrt{N|\Lambda|}\phi_{0}]e^{-it{\mathcal{H}}_{N}}[\mathcal{N}_{b},{\mathcal{W}}[\sqrt{N|\Lambda|}\phi_{0}]]

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-\sqrt{N}(a_{0}+a_{0}^{+})\widetilde{{\mathcal{U}}}_{N}(t)\,+\,\sqrt{N}\widetilde{{\mathcal{U}}}_{N}(t)(a_{0}+a_{0}^{+})\,.

As a consequence, we have that

(B.17)

\displaystyle\langle\widetilde{{\mathcal{U}}}_{N}(t)\psi,\mathcal{N}_{b}^{2}\widetilde{{\mathcal{U}}}_{N}(t)\psi\rangle

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\langle\mathcal{N}_{b}\widetilde{{\mathcal{U}}}_{N}(t)\psi,\widetilde{{\mathcal{U}}}_{N}(t)\mathcal{N}_{b}\psi\rangle\,-\,\sqrt{N}\langle\mathcal{N}_{b}\widetilde{{\mathcal{U}}}_{N}(t)\psi,(a_{0}+a_{0}^{+})\widetilde{{\mathcal{U}}}_{N}(t)\psi\rangle\,

\displaystyle+\,\sqrt{N}\langle\mathcal{N}_{b}\widetilde{{\mathcal{U}}}_{N}(t)\psi,\widetilde{{\mathcal{U}}}_{N}(t)(a_{0}+a_{0}^{+})\psi\rangle\,.

Using Cauchy-Schwarz, we thus obtain

(B.18)

\displaystyle\langle\widetilde{{\mathcal{U}}}_{N}(t)\psi,\mathcal{N}_{b}^{2}\widetilde{{\mathcal{U}}}_{N}(t)\psi\rangle

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\|\mathcal{N}_{b}\widetilde{{\mathcal{U}}}_{N}(t)\psi\|\Big(\|\widetilde{{\mathcal{U}}}_{N}(t)\mathcal{N}_{b}\psi\|+\sqrt{N}\big(\|(a_{0}+a_{0}^{+})\widetilde{{\mathcal{U}}}_{N}(t)\psi\|\,+\,\|\widetilde{{\mathcal{U}}}_{N}(t)(a_{0}+a_{0}^{+})\psi\|\big)\Big)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\|\mathcal{N}_{b}\widetilde{{\mathcal{U}}}_{N}(t)\psi\|\Big(\|\mathcal{N}_{b}\psi\|+\sqrt{N}\big(\|a_{0}\widetilde{{\mathcal{U}}}_{N}(t)\psi\|\,+\,\|a_{0}^{+}\widetilde{{\mathcal{U}}}_{N}(t)\psi\|

\displaystyle+\,\|a_{0}\psi\|\,+\,\|a_{0}^{+}\psi\|\big)\Big)\,,

where we also applied that
\widetilde{{\mathcal{U}}}_{N}(t):{\mathcal{F}}\to{\mathcal{F}}
is a unitary transformation. Lemma
A.4
implies

(B.19)

\displaystyle\|a_{0}\psi\|^{2}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\sum_{M=0}\|P_{M}a_{0}\psi\|^{2}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\sum_{M=0}\|a_{0}P_{M-1}\psi\|^{2}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\sum_{M=0}(M-1)|\Lambda|\|P_{M-1}\psi\|^{2}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle|\Lambda|\|\sqrt{\mathcal{N}_{b}}\psi\|^{2}\,.

Similarly, we have that

(B.20)

\displaystyle\|a_{0}^{+}\psi\|\,\leq\,\sqrt{|\Lambda|}\|\sqrt{\mathcal{N}_{b}+1}\psi\|

Employing (
B.19
) and (
B.20
), (
B.18
) implies

(B.21)

\displaystyle\langle\widetilde{{\mathcal{U}}}_{N}(t)\psi,\mathcal{N}_{b}^{2}\widetilde{{\mathcal{U}}}_{N}(t)\psi\rangle

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\|\mathcal{N}_{b}\widetilde{{\mathcal{U}}}_{N}(t)\psi\|\big(\|\mathcal{N}_{b}\psi\|+2\sqrt{N|\Lambda|}(\|\sqrt{\mathcal{N}_{b}+1}\widetilde{{\mathcal{U}}}_{N}(t)\psi\|\,+\,\|\sqrt{\mathcal{N}_{b}+1}\psi\|)\big)\,.

Using Young’s inequality implies

(B.22)

\displaystyle\langle\widetilde{{\mathcal{U}}}_{N}(t)\psi,\mathcal{N}_{b}^{2}\widetilde{{\mathcal{U}}}_{N}(t)\psi\rangle

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{1}{2}\langle\widetilde{{\mathcal{U}}}_{N}(t)\psi,\mathcal{N}_{b}^{2}\widetilde{{\mathcal{U}}}_{N}(t)\psi\rangle+C\Big(\langle\psi,\big(\mathcal{N}_{b}^{2}+N|\Lambda|(\mathcal{N}_{b}+1)\big)\psi\rangle

\displaystyle N|\Lambda|\langle\widetilde{{\mathcal{U}}}_{N}(t)\psi,(\mathcal{N}_{b}+1)\widetilde{{\mathcal{U}}}_{N}(t)\psi\rangle\Big)

As a consequence, we find that

(B.23)

\displaystyle\frac{1}{N}\langle\widetilde{{\mathcal{U}}}_{N}(t)\psi,\mathcal{N}_{b}^{2}\widetilde{{\mathcal{U}}}_{N}(t)\psi\rangle

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C|\Lambda|\Big(\langle\widetilde{{\mathcal{U}}}_{N}(t)\psi,(\mathcal{N}_{b}+1)\widetilde{{\mathcal{U}}}_{N}(t)\psi\rangle+\langle\psi,\big(\mathcal{N}_{b}+1+\frac{\mathcal{N}_{b}^{2}}{N|\Lambda|}\big)\psi\rangle\Big)\,.

Plugging this into (
B.12
) and using
|\Lambda|\geq 1
, we obtain that

(B.24)

\displaystyle|\partial_{t}\langle\widetilde{{\mathcal{U}}}_{N}(t)\psi,(\mathcal{N}_{b}+|\Lambda|)\widetilde{{\mathcal{U}}}_{N}(t)\psi\rangle|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C\|\hat{v}\|_{w,d}\lambda|\Lambda|\Big(\langle\widetilde{{\mathcal{U}}}_{N}(t)\psi,(\mathcal{N}_{b}+|\Lambda|)\widetilde{{\mathcal{U}}}_{N}(t)\psi\rangle\,+\,\langle\psi,\big(\mathcal{N}_{b}+1+\frac{\mathcal{N}_{b}^{2}}{N|\Lambda|}\big)\psi\rangle\Big)\,.

Gronwall’s Lemma then implies

(B.25)

\displaystyle\langle\widetilde{{\mathcal{U}}}_{N}(t)\psi,(\mathcal{N}_{b}+|\Lambda|)\widetilde{{\mathcal{U}}}_{N}(t)\psi\rangle\,\leq\,e^{C\|\hat{v}\|_{w,d}\lambda|\Lambda|t}\langle\psi,\big(\mathcal{N}_{b}+|\Lambda|+\frac{\mathcal{N}_{b}^{2}}{N|\Lambda|}\big)\psi\rangle\,.

Step 2:

Assume that

(B.26)

\displaystyle\langle\widetilde{{\mathcal{U}}}_{N}(t)\psi,(\mathcal{N}_{b}+|\Lambda|)^{j}\widetilde{{\mathcal{U}}}_{N}(t)\psi\rangle

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C_{j}e^{K_{j}\|\hat{v}\|_{w,d}\lambda|\Lambda|t}\langle\psi,(\mathcal{N}_{b}+|\Lambda|)^{j}\big(1+\frac{\mathcal{N}_{b}}{N|\Lambda|}\big)\psi\rangle

for all
1\leq j\leq\ell
and some constants
C_{j},K_{j}
, and any
\psi\in{\mathcal{F}}
. We compute

(B.27)

\displaystyle i\partial_{t}\langle\widetilde{{\mathcal{U}}}_{N}(t)\psi,(\mathcal{N}_{b}+|\Lambda|)^{\ell+1}\widetilde{{\mathcal{U}}}_{N}(t)\psi\rangle

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\langle\widetilde{{\mathcal{U}}}_{N}(t)\psi,\big[(\mathcal{N}_{b}+|\Lambda|)^{\ell+1},{\mathcal{H}}_{cor}^{\phi_{0}}+{\mathcal{H}}_{cub}^{\phi_{0}}]\widetilde{{\mathcal{U}}}_{N}(t)\psi\rangle

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\sum_{j=1}^{\ell+1}\langle\widetilde{{\mathcal{U}}}_{N}(t)\psi,(\mathcal{N}_{b}+|\Lambda|)^{j-1}\big[\mathcal{N}_{b},{\mathcal{H}}_{cor}^{\phi_{0}}+{\mathcal{H}}_{cub}^{\phi_{0}}\big](\mathcal{N}_{b}+|\Lambda|)^{\ell+1-j}\widetilde{{\mathcal{U}}}_{N}(t)\psi\rangle\,.

Let

(B.28)

\displaystyle A_{cub}[\hat{v}]\,:=\,\int d\mathbf{p}_{3}\,\hat{v}(p_{2})\delta(p_{1}+p_{2}-p_{3})a_{p_{1}}^{+}a_{p_{2}}^{+}a_{p_{3}}\,.

Applying (
B.10
) and (
B.11
), (
B.27
) yields

(B.29)

\displaystyle i\partial_{t}\langle\widetilde{{\mathcal{U}}}_{N}(t)\psi,(\mathcal{N}_{b}+|\Lambda|)^{\ell+1}\widetilde{{\mathcal{U}}}_{N}(t)\psi\rangle

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle 2\sum_{j=1}^{\ell+1}\mathrm{Im}\langle\widetilde{{\mathcal{U}}}_{N}(t)\psi,(\mathcal{N}_{b}+|\Lambda|)^{j-1}(g[\hat{v}]-A_{cub}[\hat{v}])(\mathcal{N}_{b}+|\Lambda|)^{\ell+1-j}\widetilde{{\mathcal{U}}}_{N}(t)\psi\rangle

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle 2\sum_{j=1}^{\ell+1}\mathrm{Im}\sum_{m,n=0}^{\infty}(m+|\Lambda|)^{j-1}(n+|\Lambda|)^{\ell+1-j}

\displaystyle\langle\widetilde{{\mathcal{U}}}_{N}(t)\psi,P_{m}(\lambda g[\hat{v}]-\frac{\lambda}{\sqrt{N}}A_{cub}[\hat{v}])P_{n}\widetilde{{\mathcal{U}}}_{N}(t)\psi\rangle\,.

Observe that we have

(B.30)

\displaystyle P_{m}g[\hat{v}]P_{n}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle P_{m}g[\hat{v}]P_{m+2}\delta_{n,m+2}\,,

(B.31)

\displaystyle P_{m}A_{cub}[\hat{v}]P_{n}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle P_{m}A_{cub}[\hat{v}]P_{m-1}\delta_{n,m-1}\,.

Lemma
A.4
and (
B.29
) then imply

(B.32)

\displaystyle|\partial_{t}\langle\widetilde{{\mathcal{U}}}_{N}(t)\psi,(\mathcal{N}_{b}+|\Lambda|)^{\ell+1}\widetilde{{\mathcal{U}}}_{N}(t)\psi\rangle|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C\lambda\sum_{j=1}^{\ell+1}\sum_{m,n=0}^{\infty}(m+|\Lambda|)^{j-1}(n+|\Lambda|)^{\ell+1-j}\|P_{m}\widetilde{{\mathcal{U}}}_{N}(t)\psi\|\|P_{n}\widetilde{{\mathcal{U}}}_{N}(t)\psi\|

\displaystyle\big(\|g[\hat{v}]P_{m+2}\|\delta_{n,m+2}\,+\,\frac{\|A_{cub}[\hat{v}]P_{m-1}\|}{\sqrt{N}}\delta_{n,m-1}\big)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C\|\hat{v}\|_{w,d}\lambda\sum_{j=1}^{\ell+1}\sum_{m,n=0}^{\infty}(m+|\Lambda|)^{j-1}(n+|\Lambda|)^{\ell+1-j}

\displaystyle\big(\|P_{m}\widetilde{{\mathcal{U}}}_{N}(t)\psi\|^{2}+\|P_{n}\widetilde{{\mathcal{U}}}_{N}(t)\psi\|^{2}\big)\big((m+2+|\Lambda|)\delta_{n,m+2}\,+\,\frac{m^{\frac{3}{2}}}{\sqrt{N}}\delta_{n,m-1}\big)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C\|\hat{v}\|_{w,d}\lambda\sum_{j=1}^{\ell+1}\sum_{m=0}^{\infty}(m+|\Lambda|)^{j-1}(m+2+|\Lambda|)^{\ell+1-j}\|P_{m}\widetilde{{\mathcal{U}}}_{N}(t)\psi\|^{2}

\displaystyle\big(m+2+|\Lambda|+\frac{(m+1)^{\frac{3}{2}}}{\sqrt{N}}\big)\,.

We can further estimate this by

(B.33)

\displaystyle|\partial_{t}\langle\widetilde{{\mathcal{U}}}_{N}(t)\psi,(\mathcal{N}_{b}+|\Lambda|)^{\ell+1}\widetilde{{\mathcal{U}}}_{N}(t)\psi\rangle|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C(\ell+1)\|\hat{v}\|_{w,d}\lambda\big(\langle\widetilde{{\mathcal{U}}}_{N}(t)\psi,(\mathcal{N}_{b}+|\Lambda|+2)^{\ell+1}\widetilde{{\mathcal{U}}}_{N}(t)\psi\rangle

\displaystyle+\,\langle\widetilde{{\mathcal{U}}}_{N}(t)\psi,\frac{(\mathcal{N}_{b}+|\Lambda|+2)^{\ell+\frac{3}{2}}}{\sqrt{N}}\widetilde{{\mathcal{U}}}_{N}(t)\psi\rangle\big)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C_{\ell}\|\hat{v}\|_{w,d}\lambda\big(\langle\widetilde{{\mathcal{U}}}_{N}(t)\psi,(\mathcal{N}_{b}+|\Lambda|)^{\ell+1}\widetilde{{\mathcal{U}}}_{N}(t)\psi\rangle

\displaystyle+\,\frac{1}{N}\langle\widetilde{{\mathcal{U}}}_{N}(t)\psi,(\mathcal{N}_{b}+|\Lambda|)^{\ell+2}\widetilde{{\mathcal{U}}}_{N}(t)\psi\rangle

We claim that

(B.34)

\displaystyle\frac{1}{N|\Lambda|}\langle\widetilde{{\mathcal{U}}}_{N}(t)\psi,(\mathcal{N}_{b}+|\Lambda|)^{j+1}\widetilde{{\mathcal{U}}}_{N}(t)\psi\rangle

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C_{j}\Big(e^{K_{j}\|\hat{v}\|_{w,d}\lambda|\Lambda|t}\langle\psi,(\mathcal{N}_{b}+|\Lambda|)^{j}\big(1+\frac{\mathcal{N}_{b}}{N|\Lambda|}\big)\psi\rangle

\displaystyle+\,\langle\widetilde{{\mathcal{U}}}_{N}(t)\psi,(\mathcal{N}_{b}+|\Lambda|)^{j}\widetilde{{\mathcal{U}}}_{N}(t)\psi\rangle\Big)

for all
1\leq j\leq\ell+1
and all
\psi\in{\mathcal{F}}
. (
B.34
) for
j=\ell+1
together with (
B.33
) implies

(B.35)

\displaystyle|\partial_{t}\langle\widetilde{{\mathcal{U}}}_{N}(t)\psi,(\mathcal{N}_{b}+|\Lambda|)^{\ell+1}\widetilde{{\mathcal{U}}}_{N}(t)\psi\rangle|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C_{\ell}\|\hat{v}\|_{w,d}\lambda|\Lambda|\Big(\langle\widetilde{{\mathcal{U}}}_{N}(t)\psi,(\mathcal{N}_{b}+|\Lambda|)^{\ell+1}\widetilde{{\mathcal{U}}}_{N}(t)\psi\rangle

\displaystyle+\ e^{K_{\ell}\|\hat{v}\|_{w,d}\lambda|\Lambda|t}\langle\psi,(\mathcal{N}_{b}+|\Lambda|)^{\ell+1}\big(1+\frac{\mathcal{N}_{b}}{N|\Lambda|}\big)\psi\rangle\Big)\,.

Gronwall’s Lemma then implies

(B.36)

\displaystyle\langle\widetilde{{\mathcal{U}}}_{N}(t)\psi,(\mathcal{N}_{b}+|\Lambda|)^{\ell+1}\widetilde{{\mathcal{U}}}_{N}(t)\psi\rangle|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle e^{K_{\ell}\|\hat{v}\|_{w,d}\lambda|\Lambda|t}\langle\psi,(\mathcal{N}_{b}+|\Lambda|)^{\ell+1}\big)\psi\rangle

\displaystyle+\,C_{\ell}\|\hat{v}\|_{w,d}\lambda|\Lambda|te^{K_{\ell}\|\hat{v}\|_{w,d}\lambda|\Lambda|t}\langle\psi,(\mathcal{N}_{b}+|\Lambda|)^{\ell+1}\big(1+\frac{\mathcal{N}_{b}}{N|\Lambda|}\big)\psi\rangle

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C_{\ell}e^{K_{\ell}\|\hat{v}\|_{w,d}\lambda|\Lambda|t}\langle\psi,(\mathcal{N}_{b}+|\Lambda|)^{\ell+1}\big(1+\frac{\mathcal{N}_{b}}{N|\Lambda|}\big)\psi\rangle\,.

Thus, proving (
B.34
) for
j=\ell+1
concludes the proof. We have proved (
B.34
) for
j=1
in Step 1, (
B.23
). We have that (
B.34
) also holds for
j=0
, observing that

(B.37)

\displaystyle{\mathcal{W}}[\sqrt{N|\Lambda|}\phi_{0}]\mathcal{N}_{b}{\mathcal{W}}^{*}[\sqrt{N|\Lambda|}\phi_{0}]

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\mathcal{N}_{b}-\sqrt{N}(a_{0}+a_{0}^{+})+N|\Lambda|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle 2(\mathcal{N}_{b}+1+N|\Lambda|)\,,

which then commutes with
e^{-it{\mathcal{H}}_{N}}
.

Suppose (
B.34
) holds up to some
1\leq j\leq\ell-1
. Applying (
B.16
), we have that

(B.38)

\displaystyle\frac{1}{N|\Lambda|}\langle\widetilde{{\mathcal{U}}}_{N}(t)\psi,(\mathcal{N}_{b}+|\Lambda|)^{j+2}\widetilde{{\mathcal{U}}}_{N}(t)\psi\rangle

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{1}{N|\Lambda|}\langle(\mathcal{N}_{b}+|\Lambda|)^{j+1}\widetilde{{\mathcal{U}}}_{N}(t)\psi,\widetilde{{\mathcal{U}}}_{N}(t)(\mathcal{N}_{b}+|\Lambda|)\psi\rangle

\displaystyle-\frac{1}{\sqrt{N}|\Lambda|}\langle(\mathcal{N}_{b}+|\Lambda|)^{j+1}\widetilde{{\mathcal{U}}}_{N}(t)\psi,(a_{0}+a_{0}^{+})\widetilde{{\mathcal{U}}}_{N}(t)\psi\rangle

\displaystyle+\,\frac{1}{\sqrt{N}|\Lambda|}\langle(\mathcal{N}_{b}+|\Lambda|)^{j+1}\widetilde{{\mathcal{U}}}_{N}(t)\psi,\widetilde{{\mathcal{U}}}_{N}(t)(a_{0}+a_{0}^{+})\psi\rangle\,.

We can bound the second term by

(B.39)

\displaystyle\frac{1}{\sqrt{N}|\Lambda|}\big|\langle(\mathcal{N}_{b}+|\Lambda|)^{j+1}\widetilde{{\mathcal{U}}}_{N}(t)\psi,(a_{0}+a_{0}^{+})\widetilde{{\mathcal{U}}}_{N}(t)\psi\rangle\big|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\alpha\langle\widetilde{{\mathcal{U}}}_{N}(t)\psi,(\mathcal{N}_{b}+|\Lambda|)^{j+1}\widetilde{{\mathcal{U}}}_{N}(t)\psi\rangle

\displaystyle+\,\frac{1}{\alpha N|\Lambda|^{2}}\langle\widetilde{{\mathcal{U}}}_{N}(t)\psi,(a_{0}+a_{0}^{+})(\mathcal{N}_{b}+|\Lambda|)^{j+1}(a_{0}+a_{0}^{+})\widetilde{{\mathcal{U}}}_{N}(t)\psi\rangle\,.

Employing (
B.19
) and (
B.20
), we find that, for any
\tilde{\psi}\in{\mathcal{F}}
,

(B.40)

\displaystyle\langle\tilde{\psi},(a_{0}+a_{0})^{+}(\mathcal{N}_{b}+|\Lambda|)^{k}(a_{0}+a_{0}^{+})\tilde{\psi}\rangle

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle(\|(\mathcal{N}_{b}+|\Lambda|)^{\frac{k}{2}}a_{0}\tilde{\psi}\|+\|(\mathcal{N}_{b}+|\Lambda|)^{\frac{k}{2}}a_{0}^{+}\tilde{\psi}\|)^{2}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle(\|a_{0}(\mathcal{N}_{b}+|\Lambda|-1)^{\frac{k}{2}}\tilde{\psi}\|+\|a_{0}^{+}(\mathcal{N}_{b}+|\Lambda|+1)^{\frac{k}{2}}\tilde{\psi}\|)^{2}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C_{k}|\Lambda|\langle\tilde{\psi},(\mathcal{N}_{b}+|\Lambda|)^{k+1}\tilde{\psi}\rangle\,,

i.e.,

(B.41)

\displaystyle(a_{0}+a_{0})^{+}(\mathcal{N}_{b}+|\Lambda|)^{k}(a_{0}+a_{0}^{+})\,\leq\,C_{k}|\Lambda|(\mathcal{N}_{b}+|\Lambda|)^{k+1}\,.

Employing (
B.41
) and choosing
\alpha>0
sufficiently large, (
B.39
) implies

(B.42)

\displaystyle\frac{1}{\sqrt{N}|\Lambda|}\big|\langle(\mathcal{N}_{b}+|\Lambda|)^{j+1}\widetilde{{\mathcal{U}}}_{N}(t)\psi,(a_{0}+a_{0}^{+})\widetilde{{\mathcal{U}}}_{N}(t)\psi\rangle\big|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C_{j}\langle\widetilde{{\mathcal{U}}}_{N}(t)\psi,(\mathcal{N}_{b}+|\Lambda|)^{j+1}\widetilde{{\mathcal{U}}}_{N}(t)\psi\rangle

\displaystyle+\,\frac{1}{4N|\Lambda|}\langle\widetilde{{\mathcal{U}}}_{N}(t)\psi,(\mathcal{N}_{b}+|\Lambda|)^{j+2}\widetilde{{\mathcal{U}}}_{N}(t)\psi\rangle\,.

We bound the third term in (
B.38
) by

\displaystyle\frac{1}{\sqrt{N}|\Lambda|}\big|\langle(\mathcal{N}_{b}+|\Lambda|)^{j+1}\widetilde{{\mathcal{U}}}_{N}(t)\psi,\widetilde{{\mathcal{U}}}_{N}(t)(a_{0}+a_{0}^{+})\psi\rangle\big|

(B.43)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{4}{|\Lambda|}\langle\widetilde{{\mathcal{U}}}_{N}(t)(a_{0}+a_{0}^{+})\psi,(\mathcal{N}_{b}+|\Lambda|)^{j}\widetilde{{\mathcal{U}}}_{N}(t)(a_{0}+a_{0}^{+})\psi\rangle

\displaystyle+\,\frac{1}{4N|\Lambda|}\langle\widetilde{{\mathcal{U}}}_{N}(t)\psi,(\mathcal{N}_{b}+|\Lambda|)^{j+2}\widetilde{{\mathcal{U}}}_{N}(t)\psi\rangle\,.

The induction hypothesis (
B.26
) and (
B.41
) hence imply

(B.44)

\displaystyle\frac{1}{\sqrt{N}|\Lambda|}\big|\langle(\mathcal{N}_{b}+|\Lambda|)^{j+1}\widetilde{{\mathcal{U}}}_{N}(t)\psi,\widetilde{{\mathcal{U}}}_{N}(t)(a_{0}+a_{0}^{+})\psi\rangle\big|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{C_{j}}{|\Lambda|}e^{K_{j}\|\hat{v}\|_{w,d}\lambda|\Lambda|t}\langle(a_{0}+a_{0}^{+})\psi,(\mathcal{N}_{b}+|\Lambda|)^{j}\big(1+\frac{\mathcal{N}_{b}}{N|\Lambda|}\big)(a_{0}+a_{0}^{+})\psi\rangle

\displaystyle+\,\frac{1}{4N|\Lambda|}\langle\widetilde{{\mathcal{U}}}_{N}(t)\psi,(\mathcal{N}_{b}+|\Lambda|)^{j+2}\widetilde{{\mathcal{U}}}_{N}(t)\psi\rangle

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C_{j}e^{K_{j}\|\hat{v}\|_{w,d}\lambda|\Lambda|t}\langle\psi,(\mathcal{N}_{b}+|\Lambda|)^{j+1}\big(1+\frac{\mathcal{N}_{b}}{N|\Lambda|}\big)\psi\rangle

\displaystyle+\,\frac{1}{4N|\Lambda|}\langle\widetilde{{\mathcal{U}}}_{N}(t)\psi,(\mathcal{N}_{b}+|\Lambda|)^{j+2}\widetilde{{\mathcal{U}}}_{N}(t)\psi\rangle

For the first term in (
B.38
), we apply (
B.16
) to the left and obtain

(B.45)

\displaystyle\frac{1}{N|\Lambda|}\langle(\mathcal{N}_{b}+|\Lambda|)^{j+1}\widetilde{{\mathcal{U}}}_{N}(t)\psi,\widetilde{{\mathcal{U}}}_{N}(t)(\mathcal{N}_{b}+|\Lambda|)\psi\rangle

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{1}{N|\Lambda|}\langle\widetilde{{\mathcal{U}}}_{N}(t)(\mathcal{N}_{b}+|\Lambda|)\psi,(\mathcal{N}_{b}+|\Lambda|)^{j}\widetilde{{\mathcal{U}}}_{N}(t)(\mathcal{N}_{b}+|\Lambda|)\psi\rangle

\displaystyle-\frac{1}{\sqrt{N}|\Lambda|}\langle(\mathcal{N}_{b}+|\Lambda|)^{j}(a_{0}+a_{0}^{+})\widetilde{{\mathcal{U}}}_{N}(t)\psi,\widetilde{{\mathcal{U}}}_{N}(t)(\mathcal{N}_{b}+|\Lambda|)\psi\rangle

\displaystyle+\,\frac{1}{\sqrt{N}|\Lambda|}\langle(\mathcal{N}_{b}+|\Lambda|)^{j}\widetilde{{\mathcal{U}}}_{N}(t)(a_{0}+a_{0}^{+})\psi,\widetilde{{\mathcal{U}}}_{N}(t)(\mathcal{N}_{b}+|\Lambda|)\psi\rangle\,.

For the first term in (
B.45
), we use the induction hypothesis (
B.34
), and obtain

(B.46)

\displaystyle\frac{1}{N|\Lambda|}\langle\widetilde{{\mathcal{U}}}_{N}(t)(\mathcal{N}_{b}+|\Lambda|)\psi,(\mathcal{N}_{b}+|\Lambda|)^{j}\widetilde{{\mathcal{U}}}_{N}(t)(\mathcal{N}_{b}+|\Lambda|)\psi\rangle

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C_{j}\Big(e^{K_{j}\|\hat{v}\|_{w,d}\lambda|\Lambda|t}\langle\psi,(\mathcal{N}_{b}+|\Lambda|)^{j+1}\big(1+\frac{\mathcal{N}_{b}}{N|\Lambda|}\big)\psi\rangle

\displaystyle+\,\langle\widetilde{{\mathcal{U}}}_{N}(t)(\mathcal{N}_{b}+|\Lambda|)\psi,(\mathcal{N}_{b}+|\Lambda|)^{j-1}\widetilde{{\mathcal{U}}}_{N}(t)(\mathcal{N}_{b}+|\Lambda|)\psi\rangle\Big)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C_{j}e^{K_{j}\|\hat{v}\|_{w,d}\lambda|\Lambda|t}\langle\psi,(\mathcal{N}_{b}+|\Lambda|)^{j+1}\big(1+\frac{\mathcal{N}_{b}}{N|\Lambda|}\big)\psi\rangle\,,

where in the last step we employed (
B.26
). Using Cauchy-Schwarz, followed by Young’s inequality, the second term in (
B.45
) can be bounded by

(B.47)

\displaystyle\frac{1}{\sqrt{N}|\Lambda|}\big|\langle(\mathcal{N}_{b}+|\Lambda|)^{j}(a_{0}+a_{0}^{+})\widetilde{{\mathcal{U}}}_{N}(t)\psi,\widetilde{{\mathcal{U}}}_{N}(t)(\mathcal{N}_{b}+|\Lambda|)\psi\rangle\big|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{1}{\sqrt{N}|\Lambda|}\|(\mathcal{N}_{b}+|\Lambda|)^{\frac{j}{2}}(a_{0}+a_{0}^{+})\widetilde{{\mathcal{U}}}_{N}(t)\psi\|\|(\mathcal{N}_{b}+|\Lambda|)^{\frac{j}{2}}\widetilde{{\mathcal{U}}}_{N}(t)(\mathcal{N}_{b}+|\Lambda|)\psi\|

\displaystyle\leq
