\displaystyle\frac{1}{|\Lambda|}\Big(\langle\widetilde{{\mathcal{U}}}_{N}(t)\psi,(a_{0}+a_{0}^{+})(\mathcal{N}_{b}+|\Lambda|)^{j}(a_{0}+a_{0}^{+})\widetilde{{\mathcal{U}}}_{N}(t)\psi\rangle

\displaystyle+\,\frac{1}{N}\langle\widetilde{{\mathcal{U}}}_{N}(t)(\mathcal{N}_{b}+|\Lambda|)\psi,(\mathcal{N}_{b}+|\Lambda|)^{j}\widetilde{{\mathcal{U}}}_{N}(t)(\mathcal{N}_{b}+|\Lambda|)\psi\rangle\Big)\,.

Employing (
B.41
) and then (
B.46
), we thus obtain

(B.48)

\displaystyle\frac{1}{\sqrt{N}|\Lambda|}\big|\langle(\mathcal{N}_{b}+|\Lambda|)^{j}(a_{0}+a_{0}^{+})\widetilde{{\mathcal{U}}}_{N}(t)\psi,\widetilde{{\mathcal{U}}}_{N}(t)(\mathcal{N}_{b}+|\Lambda|)\psi\rangle\big|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C_{j}\langle\widetilde{{\mathcal{U}}}_{N}(t)\psi,(\mathcal{N}_{b}+|\Lambda|)^{j+1}\widetilde{{\mathcal{U}}}_{N}(t)\psi\rangle

\displaystyle+\,\frac{1}{N|\Lambda|}\langle\widetilde{{\mathcal{U}}}_{N}(t)(\mathcal{N}_{b}+|\Lambda|)\psi,(\mathcal{N}_{b}+|\Lambda|)^{j}\widetilde{{\mathcal{U}}}_{N}(t)(\mathcal{N}_{b}+|\Lambda|)\psi\rangle

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C_{j}\langle\widetilde{{\mathcal{U}}}_{N}(t)\psi,(\mathcal{N}_{b}+|\Lambda|)^{j+1}\widetilde{{\mathcal{U}}}_{N}(t)\psi\rangle

\displaystyle+\,C_{j}e^{K_{j}\|\hat{v}\|_{w,d}\lambda|\Lambda|t}\langle\psi,(\mathcal{N}_{b}+|\Lambda|)^{j+1}\big(1+\frac{\mathcal{N}_{b}}{N|\Lambda|}\big)\psi\rangle\,.

Similarly, the third term in (
B.45
) can be estimated using

(B.49)

\displaystyle\frac{1}{\sqrt{N}|\Lambda|}\big|\langle(\mathcal{N}_{b}+|\Lambda|)^{j}\widetilde{{\mathcal{U}}}_{N}(t)(a_{0}+a_{0}^{+})\psi,\widetilde{{\mathcal{U}}}_{N}(t)(\mathcal{N}_{b}+|\Lambda|)\psi\rangle\big|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{1}{|\Lambda|}\langle\widetilde{{\mathcal{U}}}_{N}(t)(a_{0}+a_{0}^{+})\psi,(\mathcal{N}_{b}+|\Lambda|)^{j}\widetilde{{\mathcal{U}}}_{N}(t)(a_{0}+a_{0}^{+})\psi\rangle

\displaystyle+\,\frac{1}{N|\Lambda|}\langle\widetilde{{\mathcal{U}}}_{N}(t)(\mathcal{N}_{b}+|\Lambda|)\psi,(\mathcal{N}_{b}+|\Lambda|)^{j}\widetilde{{\mathcal{U}}}_{N}(t)(\mathcal{N}_{b}+|\Lambda|)\psi\rangle

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{C_{j}}{|\Lambda|}e^{K_{j}\|\hat{v}\|_{w,d}\lambda|\Lambda|t}\langle\psi,(a_{0}+a_{0}^{+})(\mathcal{N}_{b}+|\Lambda|)^{j}\big(1+\frac{\mathcal{N}_{b}}{N|\Lambda|}\big)(a_{0}+a_{0}^{+})\psi\rangle

\displaystyle+\,C_{j}e^{K_{j}\|\hat{v}\|_{w,d}\lambda|\Lambda|t}\langle\psi,(\mathcal{N}_{b}+|\Lambda|)^{j+1}\big(1+\frac{\mathcal{N}_{b}}{N|\Lambda|}\big)\psi\rangle

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C_{j}e^{K_{j}\|\hat{v}\|_{w,d}\lambda|\Lambda|t}\langle\psi,(\mathcal{N}_{b}+|\Lambda|)^{j+1}\big(1+\frac{\mathcal{N}_{b}}{N|\Lambda|}\big)\psi\rangle\,,

where we used (
B.26
) and (
B.46
), followed by (
B.41
). Inserting (
B.46
), (
B.48
), and (
B.49
) into (
B.45
), we obtain that

(B.50)

\displaystyle\frac{1}{N|\Lambda|}\langle(\mathcal{N}_{b}+|\Lambda|)^{j+1}\widetilde{{\mathcal{U}}}_{N}(t)\psi,\widetilde{{\mathcal{U}}}_{N}(t)(\mathcal{N}_{b}+|\Lambda|)\psi\rangle

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C_{j}e^{K_{j}\|\hat{v}\|_{w,d}\lambda|\Lambda|t}\langle\psi,(\mathcal{N}_{b}+|\Lambda|)^{j+1}\big(1+\frac{\mathcal{N}_{b}}{N|\Lambda|}\big)\psi\rangle

\displaystyle+\,C_{j}\langle\widetilde{{\mathcal{U}}}_{N}(t)\psi,(\mathcal{N}_{b}+|\Lambda|)^{j+1}\widetilde{{\mathcal{U}}}_{N}(t)\psi\rangle\,.

Plugging (
B.42
), (
B.44
), and (
B.50
) into (
B.38
), we find that

(B.51)

\displaystyle\frac{1}{N|\Lambda|}\langle\widetilde{{\mathcal{U}}}_{N}(t)\psi,(\mathcal{N}_{b}+|\Lambda|)^{j+2}\widetilde{{\mathcal{U}}}_{N}(t)\psi\rangle

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C_{j}e^{K_{j}\|\hat{v}\|_{w,d}\lambda|\Lambda|t}\langle\psi,(\mathcal{N}_{b}+|\Lambda|)^{j+1}\big(1+\frac{\mathcal{N}_{b}}{N|\Lambda|}\big)\psi\rangle

\displaystyle+\,C_{j}\langle\widetilde{{\mathcal{U}}}_{N}(t)\psi,(\mathcal{N}_{b}+|\Lambda|)^{j+1}\widetilde{{\mathcal{U}}}_{N}(t)\psi\rangle\,.

This concludes the proof.
∎

Appendix C
Proof of convergence to mean field equations

Lemma C.1
.

Let
H,F\in C^{1}({\mathbb{R}}^{n})
be such that

(C.1)

\displaystyle\Big\|\nabla\cdot\Big(\frac{H\nabla F}{|\nabla F|}\Big)\Big\|_{1}\,<\,\infty\,.

Then the following holds true for all
\omega\in{\mathbb{R}}
and
g\in C^{1}_{0}({\mathbb{R}})
:

(1)

\int_{F=\omega}\frac{d{\mathcal{H}}^{n-1}}{|\nabla F|}H=\int_{F<\omega}dp\,\nabla\cdot\Big(\frac{H\nabla F}{|\nabla F|^{2}}\Big)
,

(2)

\int d\omega\,g(\omega)\partial_{\omega}\int_{F=\omega}\frac{d{\mathcal{H}}^{n-1}}{|\nabla F|}H=\int d\omega\,g(\omega)\int_{F=\omega}\frac{d{\mathcal{H}}^{n-1}}{|\nabla F|}\nabla\cdot\Big(\frac{H\nabla F}{|\nabla F|^{2}}\Big)
.

Proof.

The first statement is a direct consequence of the Divergence Theorem together with the fact that
\nabla F/|\nabla F|
is the outer normal for
\{F<\omega\}
. For the second statement, we have that the divergence theorem implies that

(C.2)

\displaystyle\int d\omega\,g(\omega)\partial_{\omega}\int_{F=\omega}\frac{d{\mathcal{H}}^{n-1}}{|\nabla F|}H

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-\,\int d\omega\,g^{\prime}(\omega)\int_{F=\omega}\frac{d{\mathcal{H}}^{n-1}}{|\nabla F|}H\,,

where we used the assumptions
g\in C^{1}_{0}({\mathbb{R}})
, (
C.1
), and the first statement. Employing the Coarea Formula, we have that

(C.3)

\displaystyle\int d\omega\,g(\omega)\partial_{\omega}\int_{F=\omega}\frac{d{\mathcal{H}}^{n-1}}{|\nabla F|}H

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-\,\int dp\,g^{\prime}(F)H\,.

Expanding with the factor
\frac{\nabla F\cdot\nabla F}{|\nabla F|^{2}}
, the Divergence Theorem, together with
g\in C^{1}_{0}({\mathbb{R}})
, (
C.1
), then implies

(C.4)

\displaystyle\int d\omega\,g(\omega)\partial_{\omega}\int_{F=\omega}\frac{d{\mathcal{H}}^{n-1}}{|\nabla F|}H

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\,\int dp\,g(F)\nabla\cdot\Big(\frac{H\nabla F}{|\nabla F|^{2}}\Big)\,.

Finally, the Coarea Formula implies

(C.5)

\displaystyle\int d\omega\,g(\omega)\partial_{\omega}\int_{F=\omega}\frac{d{\mathcal{H}}^{n-1}}{|\nabla F|}H

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\,\int d\omega\,g(\omega)\int_{F=\omega}\frac{d{\mathcal{H}}^{n-1}}{|\nabla F|}\nabla\cdot\Big(\frac{H\nabla F}{|\nabla F|^{2}}\Big)\,.

This finishes the proof.
∎

Lemma C.2
.

Let
H,F_{1},F_{2}\in C^{2}({\mathbb{R}}^{6})
be s.t.

(C.6)

\displaystyle H\,,\,\nabla\cdot\frac{\nabla F_{2}|H|}{|\nabla F_{2}|^{2}}\in L^{1}({\mathbb{R}}^{6})\,.

Then, for all
t\geq 0
, we have that

(C.7)

\displaystyle\Big|\int\,d\mathbf{p}_{2}\,\int_{\Delta[t,2]}\,d\mathbf{s}_{2}\,e^{-i(F_{1}(\mathbf{p}_{2})s_{1}+F_{2}(\mathbf{p}_{2})s_{2})}H(\mathbf{p}_{2})\Big|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle Ct\Big(\log(t+1)\Big\|\nabla\cdot\frac{\nabla F_{2}|H|}{|\nabla F_{2}|^{2}}\Big\|_{1}+\|H\|_{1}\Big)\,.

Proof.

Observe that

(C.8)

\displaystyle\Big|\int_{\Delta[t,2]}\,d\mathbf{s}_{2}\,e^{-i(\omega_{1}s_{1}+\omega_{2}s_{2})}\Big|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{t}\,ds_{1}\,\Big|\int_{0}^{s_{1}}\,ds_{2}\,e^{-i\omega_{2}s_{2}}\Big|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{t}\,ds_{1}\,\frac{2}{|\omega_{2}|+\frac{1}{s_{1}}}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{2t}{|\omega_{2}|+\frac{1}{t}}

for all
\omega_{1},\omega_{2}\in{\mathbb{R}}
,
t\geq 0
, due to the fact that

(C.9)

\displaystyle\Big|\int_{x_{1}}^{x_{2}}\,dy\,e^{-iay}\Big|\,\leq\,\min\{x_{2}-x_{1};\frac{2}{|a|}\}\,\leq\,\frac{2}{|a|+\frac{1}{x_{2}-x_{1}}}

for all
x_{1},x_{2},a\in{\mathbb{R}}
,
x_{2}\geq x_{1}
.

Then (
C.8
) followed by the Coarea Formula implies

(C.10)

\displaystyle\Big|\int\,d\mathbf{p}_{2}\,\int_{\Delta[t,2]}\,d\mathbf{s}_{2}\,e^{-i(F_{1}(\mathbf{p}_{2})s_{1}+F_{2}(\mathbf{p}_{2})s_{2})}H(\mathbf{p}_{2})\Big|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle Ct\int\,d\mathbf{p}_{2}\,\frac{|H(\mathbf{p}_{2})|}{|F_{2}(\mathbf{p}_{2})|+\frac{1}{t}}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle Ct\int\,d\omega\,\frac{1}{|\omega|+\frac{1}{t}}\int_{F_{2}=\omega}\,d{\mathcal{H}}^{5}(\mathbf{p}_{2})\frac{|H(\mathbf{p}_{2})|}{|\nabla F_{2}(\mathbf{p}_{2})|}\,.

Lemma
C.1
yields

(C.11)

\displaystyle\Big|\int_{F_{2}=\omega}\,d{\mathcal{H}}^{5}(\mathbf{p}_{2})\frac{|H(\mathbf{p}_{2})|}{|\nabla F_{2}(\mathbf{p}_{2})|}\Big|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\int_{F_{2}>\omega}d\mathbf{p}_{2}\Big|\nabla\cdot\frac{\nabla F_{2}(\mathbf{p}_{2})|H(p_{2})|}{|\nabla F_{2}(\mathbf{p}_{2})|^{2}}\Big|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\Big\|\nabla\cdot\frac{\nabla F_{2}|H|}{|\nabla F_{2}|^{2}}\Big\|_{1}\,.

Then, after splitting the domain of integration w.r.t.
d\omega
into
(-1,1)\cup(-1,1)^{c}
, (
C.10
) can be bounded by

(C.12)

\displaystyle\int_{-1}^{1}d\omega\frac{1}{|\omega|+\frac{1}{t}}\sup_{\omega}\Big|\int_{F_{2}=\omega}\,d{\mathcal{H}}^{5}(\mathbf{p}_{2})\frac{|H(\mathbf{p}_{2})|}{|\nabla F_{2}(\mathbf{p}_{2})|}\Big|

\displaystyle+\,\int d\mathbf{p}_{2}\,\mathds{1}_{|F_{2}(\mathbf{p}_{2})|\geq 1}|H(\mathbf{p}_{2})|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle Ct\Big(\log(t+1)\Big\|\nabla\cdot\frac{\nabla F_{2}|H|}{|\nabla F_{2}|^{2}}\Big\|_{1}+\|H\|_{1}\Big)\,,

where, again, we used the Coarea Formula.
∎

Lemma C.3
.

Let
h\in C^{n}_{b}([0,\|\hat{v}\|_{2(\left\lfloor\frac{r}{2}\right\rfloor+1),w,c}])
,
n\in{\mathbb{N}}
,
n\leq 3
. Then the following holds for all
\lambda\in(0,1]
. We have that

(C.13)

\displaystyle\Big\|D^{n}\Big[h\Big(\frac{\lambda\hat{v}}{E}\Big)\Big]\Big\|_{\infty}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C_{n}\lambda\|h\|_{C^{n}[0,\|\hat{v}\|_{2(\left\lfloor\frac{r}{2}\right\rfloor+1),w,c}\lambda]}\|\hat{v}\|_{2(\left\lfloor\frac{r}{2}\right\rfloor+1),w,c}^{n}\,.

Moreover,
\Omega
satisfies

(C.14)

\displaystyle\nabla\Omega(p)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle(1+\lambda m_{\Omega}(p))p\,

(C.15)

\displaystyle\|m_{\Omega}\|_{\infty}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C\|\hat{v}\|_{2(\left\lfloor\frac{r}{2}\right\rfloor+1),w,c}\,,

\displaystyle\|D^{2}\Omega-I\|_{\infty}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C\lambda\|\hat{v}\|_{2(\left\lfloor\frac{r}{2}\right\rfloor+1),w,c}^{2}\,,

(C.16)

\displaystyle\|D^{3}\Omega\|_{\infty}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C\lambda\|\hat{v}\|_{2(\left\lfloor\frac{r}{2}\right\rfloor+1),w,c}^{3}\,.

Proof.

By the Faà di Bruno formula, we have that

(C.17)

\displaystyle|D^{n}\Big[h\Big(\frac{\lambda\hat{v}}{E}\Big)\Big]|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C_{n}\sum_{\mathbf{r}_{n}\in R(n)}\Big|h^{(S(\mathbf{r}_{n}))}\Big(\frac{\lambda\hat{v}}{E}\Big)\Big|\lambda^{S(\mathbf{r}_{n})}\prod_{j=1}^{n}\Big[\big|D^{j}\big(\frac{\hat{v}}{E}\big)\big|\Big]^{r_{j}}\ \,,

where,
R(n)
is defined as in (
5.151
) and

(C.18)

\displaystyle S(\mathbf{r}_{n})\,:=\,\sum_{k=1}^{n}r_{k}\,,

see (
5.152
). Notice that
S(\mathbf{r}_{n})
satisfies

(C.19)

\displaystyle 1\,\leq\,S(\mathbf{r}_{n})\,\leq\,n

due to the summation condition

(C.20)

\displaystyle\sum_{j=1}^{n}jr_{j}\,=\,n\,.

(
C.19
) allows us to extract a factor
\lambda
in (
C.17
) since it appears within the sum with power
S(\mathbf{r}_{n})
. Then (
C.17
) implies

(C.21)

\displaystyle\Big\|D^{n}\Big[h\Big(\frac{\lambda\hat{v}}{E}\Big)\Big]\Big\|_{\infty}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C_{n}\lambda\|h\|_{C^{n}[0,\|\hat{v}\|_{2(\left\lfloor\frac{r}{2}\right\rfloor+1),w,c}\lambda]}\|\hat{v}\|_{2(\left\lfloor\frac{r}{2}\right\rfloor+1),w,c}^{n}\,.

With analogous steps, we have that

(C.22)

\displaystyle\Big\|\partial_{|p|}^{n}\Big[h\Big(\frac{\lambda\hat{v}}{E}\Big)\Big]\Big\|_{\infty}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C_{n}\lambda\|h\|_{C^{n}[0,\|\hat{v}\|_{2(\left\lfloor\frac{r}{2}\right\rfloor+1),w,c}\lambda]}\|\hat{v}\|_{2(\left\lfloor\frac{r}{2}\right\rfloor+1),w,c}^{n}\,.

We have that
\Omega=E\sqrt{1+\frac{2\lambda\hat{v}}{E}}
, see (
3.19
), is radial since
\hat{v}
is radial. Thus we have that

(C.23)

\displaystyle\nabla\Omega(p)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle p\Big[\sqrt{1+\frac{2\lambda\hat{v}(p)}{E(p)}}\,+\,\frac{|p|}{2}\Big(\sqrt{1+\frac{2\lambda\hat{v}}{E}}\Big)^{\prime}(p)\Big]\,.

Observe that

(C.24)

\displaystyle\Big|\sqrt{1+\frac{2\lambda\hat{v}}{E}}-1\Big|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C\frac{\lambda\|\hat{v}\|_{2(\left\lfloor\frac{r}{2}\right\rfloor+1),w,c}}{\sqrt{1+\frac{2\lambda\hat{v}}{E}}+1}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C\lambda\|\hat{v}\|_{2(\left\lfloor\frac{r}{2}\right\rfloor+1),w,c}\,.

Then (
C.22
), and (
C.24
) imply that

(C.25)

\displaystyle\nabla\Omega(p)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle(1+\lambda m_{\Omega}(p))p\,

(C.26)

\displaystyle\|m_{\Omega}\|_{\infty}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C\|\hat{v}\|_{2(\left\lfloor\frac{r}{2}\right\rfloor+1),w,c}\,.

Next, using the multivariate Leibniz rule together with (
C.24
), we then find that

(C.27)

\displaystyle D^{2}\Omega

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\sqrt{1+\frac{2\lambda\hat{v}}{E}}I+2p\vee D\sqrt{1+\frac{2\lambda\hat{v}}{E}}+ED^{2}\sqrt{1+\frac{2\lambda\hat{v}}{E}}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle I\,+\,\lambda A

for some bounded matrix
A
. Using (
C.21
) with
h(x)=\sqrt{1+2x}
, and (
C.24
), we have that

(C.28)

\displaystyle\|A\|_{\infty}\,\leq\,C\|\hat{v}\|_{2(\left\lfloor\frac{r}{2}\right\rfloor+1),w,c}^{2}

for all
\lambda\in(0,1]
. Here, we used the fact that
\|h\|_{C^{2}([0,\|\hat{v}\|_{2(\left\lfloor\frac{r}{2}\right\rfloor+1),w,c}])}\leq C
.

Finally, the multivariate Leibniz rule implies

(C.29)

\displaystyle\|D^{3}\Omega\|_{\infty}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C\sum_{k=0}^{3}\Big\|D^{k}(|p|^{2})D^{3-k}\sqrt{1+\frac{2\lambda\hat{v}}{E}}\Big\|_{\infty}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C\sum_{k=0}^{2}\Big\||p|^{2-k}D^{3-k}\sqrt{1+\frac{2\lambda\hat{v}}{E}}\Big\|_{\infty}\,.

Using (
C.17
) and the fact that

(C.30)

\displaystyle\||p|^{2-k}D^{j}\big(\frac{\hat{v}}{E}\big)\|_{\infty}\,\leq\,C\|\hat{v}\|_{2(\left\lfloor\frac{r}{2}\right\rfloor+1),w,c}

for
j\in\{1,2,3\}
, we find that

(C.31)

\displaystyle\|D^{3}\Omega\|_{\infty}\,\leq\,C\lambda\|\hat{v}\|_{2(\left\lfloor\frac{r}{2}\right\rfloor+1),w,c}^{3}\,.

This concludes the proof.
∎

Lemma C.4
.

Let

(C.32)

\displaystyle F_{2}(\mathbf{p}_{2})\,:=\,\sigma_{1}\Omega(p_{1})+\sigma_{2}\Omega(p_{2})+\sigma_{12}\Omega(p_{12})

and

(C.33)

\displaystyle T\,:=\,\begin{pmatrix}(\sigma_{1}+\sigma_{12})I&\sigma_{12}I\\
\sigma_{12}I&(\sigma_{2}+\sigma_{12})I\end{pmatrix}\,.

F_{2}
satisfies

(C.34)

\displaystyle\nabla F_{2}(0)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle 0\,,

(C.35)

\displaystyle\|\det(D^{2}F_{2})-\sigma_{12}(\sigma_{12}\sigma_{1}\sigma_{2}+\sigma_{1}+\sigma_{2})^{3}\|_{\infty}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C\lambda\|\hat{v}\|_{2(\left\lfloor\frac{r}{2}\right\rfloor+1),w,c}^{2}\,,

(C.36)

\displaystyle\|D^{2}F_{2}\|_{\infty}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C

For all
\mathbf{p}_{2},\mathbf{k}_{2}\in{\mathbb{R}}^{6}
, and all
\lambda>0
small enough, dependent on
\|\hat{v}\|_{2(\left\lfloor\frac{r}{2}\right\rfloor+1),w,c}
, we have that

(C.37)

\displaystyle|D^{2}F_{2}(\mathbf{k}_{2})\mathbf{p}_{2}|\in\Big[\frac{|T\mathbf{p}_{2}|}{2},\frac{3|T\mathbf{p}_{2}|}{2}\Big]\,.

In particular, in this case, we have

\displaystyle|D^{2}F_{2}(\mathbf{k}_{2})\mathbf{p}_{2}|\,\geq\,\begin{cases}|\mathbf{p}_{2}|/2\,,\mbox{\ if\ }\sigma_{1}=\sigma_{2}=\pm\sigma_{12}\,,\\
|p_{1}|/2\,,\mbox{\ if\ }\sigma_{1}=-\sigma_{2}=\sigma_{12}\,,\\
|p_{2}|/2\,,\mbox{\ if\ }-\sigma_{1}=\sigma_{2}=\sigma_{12}\,.\end{cases}

Proof.

Thanks to Lemma
C.3
, we have that

(C.38)

\displaystyle\nabla\Omega(p)\,=\,p(1+\lambda m_{\Omega}(p))

with

(C.39)

\displaystyle\|m_{\Omega}\|_{\infty}\,\leq\,C\|\hat{v}\|_{2(\left\lfloor\frac{r}{2}\right\rfloor+1),w,c}\,.

This immediately implies (
C.34
).

Next, again due to Lemma
C.3
, we have that

(C.40)

\displaystyle D^{2}\Omega\,=\,I+\lambda A

for some matrix
A\in{\mathbb{R}}^{3\times 3}
with

(C.41)

\displaystyle\|A\|_{\infty}\,\leq\,C\|\hat{v}\|_{2(\left\lfloor\frac{r}{2}\right\rfloor+1),w,c}^{2}

for all
\lambda\in(0,1)
. Denoting
A_{j}:=A(p_{j})
, we obtain that

(C.42)

\displaystyle D^{2}F_{2}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\begin{pmatrix}\sigma_{1}(I+\lambda A_{1})+\sigma_{12}(I+\lambda A_{12})&\sigma_{12}(I+\lambda A_{12})\\
\sigma_{12}(I+\lambda A_{12})&\sigma_{2}(I+\lambda A_{2})+\sigma_{12}(I+\lambda A_{12})\end{pmatrix}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\begin{pmatrix}(\sigma_{1}+\sigma_{12})I&\sigma_{12}I\\
\sigma_{12}I&(\sigma_{2}+\sigma_{12})I\end{pmatrix}+\lambda\begin{pmatrix}\sigma_{1}A_{1}+\sigma_{12}A_{12}&\sigma_{12}A_{12}\\
\sigma_{12}A_{12}&\sigma_{2}A_{2}+\sigma_{12}A_{12}\end{pmatrix}

\displaystyle=:

\displaystyle T+\lambda\tilde{B}\,.

This identity together with (
C.41
) immediately implies (
C.36
). Notice that due to (
C.41
), we have that

(C.43)

\displaystyle\|\tilde{B}\|_{\infty}\,\leq\,C\|\hat{v}\|_{2(\left\lfloor\frac{r}{2}\right\rfloor+1),w,c}^{2}\,.

Now, observe that

(C.44)

\displaystyle\det(T)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\det\big(\begin{pmatrix}0&-(\sigma_{12}\sigma_{1}\sigma_{2}+\sigma_{1}+\sigma_{2})I\\
\sigma_{12}I&(\sigma_{2}+\sigma_{12})I\end{pmatrix}\big)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-\det\big(\begin{pmatrix}\sigma_{12}I&(\sigma_{2}+\sigma_{12})I\\
0&-(\sigma_{12}\sigma_{1}\sigma_{2}+\sigma_{1}+\sigma_{2})I\end{pmatrix}\big)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\sigma_{12}(\sigma_{12}\sigma_{1}\sigma_{2}+\sigma_{1}+\sigma_{2})^{3}\,\neq\,0\,.

Thus we may rewrite
T+\lambda\tilde{B}=:(I+\lambda B)T
with

(C.45)

\displaystyle\|B\|_{\infty}\,\leq\,C\|\hat{v}\|_{2(\left\lfloor\frac{r}{2}\right\rfloor+1),w,c}^{2}\,.

due to (
C.43
) and (
C.44
). Then, we have that

(C.46)

\displaystyle\|\det(I+\lambda B)-1\|_{\infty}\,\leq\,C\lambda\|B\|_{\infty}

for all
\lambda\in(0,1)
.

Finally, Gershgorin’s Circle Theorem implies that

(C.47)

\displaystyle\sigma(|I+\lambda B|^{2})

\displaystyle\subseteq

\displaystyle[1-2\lambda\|B\|_{\infty}-\lambda^{2}\|B\|_{\infty}^{2},1+2\lambda\|B\|_{\infty}+\lambda^{2}\|B\|_{\infty}^{2}]

\displaystyle\subseteq

\displaystyle\Big[\frac{1}{4},\frac{9}{4}\Big]

for all
\lambda\in(0,1)
small enough, dependent on
\|\hat{v}\|_{2(\left\lfloor\frac{r}{2}\right\rfloor+1),w,c}
. Then we have that

(C.48)

\displaystyle|D^{2}F_{2}(\mathbf{k}_{2})\mathbf{p}_{2}|^{2}\,=\,(T\mathbf{p}_{2})^{T}|I+\lambda B(\mathbf{k}_{2})|^{2}T\mathbf{p}_{2}\,,

which together with (
C.47
) implies that

(C.49)

\displaystyle|D^{2}F_{2}(\mathbf{k}_{2})\mathbf{p}_{2}|\in\Big[\frac{|T\mathbf{p}_{2}|}{2},\frac{3|T\mathbf{p}_{2}|}{2}\Big]\,.

This finishes the proof.
∎

C.1.
Error bounds due to HFB evolution

Lemma C.5
.

We have that

(C.50)

\displaystyle\frac{\lambda^{j}}{N}|\operatorname{Bol}_{c}^{(j)}(f_{0})[J](T;\lambda)|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C_{\|\hat{v}\|_{2(\left\lfloor\frac{r}{2}\right\rfloor+1),w,c},\|f_{0}\|_{2(\left\lfloor\frac{r}{2}\right\rfloor+1),c},r}T(1+\log(T))\|J\|_{W^{1,\infty}}\frac{\lambda|\log(\lambda)|}{N}

for
j\in\{1,2\}
, and all
\lambda>0
small enough, dependent on
\|\hat{v}\|_{2(\left\lfloor\frac{r}{2}\right\rfloor+1),w,c}
.

Proof.

\frac{\lambda^{j}}{N}|\operatorname{Bol}_{c}^{(j)}(f_{0})[J](T;\lambda)|
are of the form

(C.51)

\displaystyle\frac{\lambda^{j-2}}{N}\int_{{\mathbb{R}}^{6}}\,d\mathbf{p}_{2}\,\int_{\Delta[T,2]}\,d\mathbf{S}_{2}\,e^{-i(F_{1}(\mathbf{p}_{2})S_{1}+F_{2}(\mathbf{p}_{2})S_{2})/\lambda^{2}}H(\mathbf{p}_{2})

with

(C.52)

\displaystyle F_{2}(\mathbf{p}_{2})\,=\,\sigma_{1}\Omega(p_{1})+\sigma_{2}\Omega(p_{2})+\sigma_{12}\Omega(p_{12})

for some
\sigma_{j}\in\{\pm 1\}
and
H:{\mathbb{R}}^{6}\to{\mathbb{R}}
. Employing the bound on
D^{2}F_{2}
given in Lemma
C.4
and the diamagnetic inequality
|\nabla|h||\leq|\nabla h|
, we find the estimate

(C.53)

\displaystyle\Big\|\nabla\cdot\frac{\nabla F_{2}|H|}{|\nabla F_{2}|^{2}}\Big\|_{1}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\Big\|\frac{\Delta F_{2}H}{|\nabla F_{2}|^{2}}\Big\|_{1}\,+\,\Big\|\frac{\nabla F_{2}^{T}D^{2}F_{2}\nabla F_{2}H}{|\nabla F_{2}|^{4}}\Big\|_{1}\,+\,\Big\|\frac{\nabla|H|}{|\nabla F_{2}|}\Big\|_{1}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C\Big(\Big\|\frac{H}{|\nabla F_{2}|^{2}}\Big\|_{1}+\Big\|\frac{\nabla H}{|\nabla F_{2}|}\Big\|_{1}\Big)\,.

Let
B_{F_{2}}:=\{|\nabla F_{2}|\leq 1\}
denote the unit ball induced by
\nabla F_{2}
, and let
J_{\nabla F_{2}}:=|\det(D^{2}F_{2})|
. Notice that for any
m\in[0,4)
and test function
h
, we have that

(C.54)

\displaystyle\Big\|\frac{h}{|\nabla F_{2}|^{m}}\Big\|_{1}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\Big\|\frac{{J_{\nabla F_{2}}}^{\frac{12-m}{24-3m}}}{|\nabla F_{2}|^{m}}\Big\|_{L^{\frac{24-3m}{12-m}}(B_{F_{2}})}\Big\|\frac{h}{{J_{\nabla F_{2}}}^{\frac{12-m}{24-3m}}}\Big\|_{L^{\frac{24-3m}{12-2m}}(B_{F_{2}})}

\displaystyle+\,\Big\|\frac{h}{|\nabla F_{2}|^{m}}\Big\|_{L^{1}(B_{F_{2}}^{c})}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C\big(\|h\|_{\frac{24-3m}{12-2m}}+\|h\|_{1}\big)

for all
\lambda>0
small enough, dependent on
\|\hat{v}\|_{2(\left\lfloor\frac{r}{2}\right\rfloor+1),w,c}
. Here, we used Lemma
C.4
to bound
J_{\nabla F_{2}}\geq C
for
\lambda\in(0,1)
small enough, substitution, and the fact that

(C.55)

\displaystyle\int_{B_{1}}\,\frac{d{\bf x}_{6}}{|{\bf x}_{6}|^{m\frac{24-3m}{12-m}}}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C\int_{0}^{1}\frac{d|{\bf x}_{6}|}{|{\bf x}_{6}|^{m\frac{24-3m}{12-m}-5}}\,<\,\infty

since, due to
m<4
,

(C.56)

\displaystyle m\frac{24-3m}{12-m}-5\,=\,-3\frac{(m-5)^{2}-1}{12-m}+1\,<\,1.

In particular, (
C.53
) and (
C.54
) imply

(C.57)

\displaystyle\Big\|\nabla\cdot\frac{\nabla F_{2}|H|}{|\nabla F_{2}|^{2}}\Big\|_{1}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C\big(\|H\|_{W^{1,1}}\,+\,\|H\|_{\frac{9}{4}}\,+\,\|\nabla H\|_{\frac{21}{10}}\big)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C\big(\|H\|_{W^{1,1}}\,+\,\|H\|_{W^{1,\frac{9}{4}}}\big)\,,

where we employed the fact that
m\mapsto\frac{24-3m}{12-2m}
is an increasing function, and interpolation. With that, we apply Lemma
C.2
to obtain

(C.58)

\displaystyle\Big|\int\,d\mathbf{p}_{2}\,\int_{\Delta[t,2]}\,d\mathbf{s}_{2}\,e^{-i(F_{1}(\mathbf{p}_{2})s_{1}+F_{2}(\mathbf{p}_{2})s_{2})}H(\mathbf{p}_{2})\Big|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle Ct\Big(\log(1+t)\Big\|\nabla\cdot\frac{\nabla F_{2}|H|}{|\nabla F_{2}|^{2}}\Big\|_{1}+\|H\|_{1}\Big)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle Ct(1+\log(1+t))\big(\|H\|_{W^{1,1}}\,+\,\|H\|_{W^{1,\frac{9}{4}}}\big)\,.

As a consequence of (
C.58
) and using the chain rule, we obtain that

(C.59)

\displaystyle\frac{\lambda^{j}}{N}|\operatorname{Bol}_{c}^{(j)}(f_{0})[J](T;\lambda)|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C_{\|\hat{v}\|_{2(\left\lfloor\frac{r}{2}\right\rfloor+1),w,c},\|f_{0}\|_{2(\left\lfloor\frac{r}{2}\right\rfloor+1),c},r}T(1+\log(T))\|J\|_{W^{1,\infty}}\frac{\lambda|\log(\lambda)|}{N}

for
j\in\{1,2\}
, and all
\lambda>0
small enough, dependent on
\|\hat{v}\|_{2(\left\lfloor\frac{r}{2}\right\rfloor+1),w,c}
. This finishes the proof.

∎

C.2.
Mollified energy conservation

Lemma C.6
.

Let
\operatorname{err}^{(fd)}_{2}(t;f[J])
be defined as in (
5.211
). Then

(C.60)

\displaystyle|\operatorname{err}^{(fd)}_{2}(t;f[J])|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C_{\|\hat{v}\|_{2(\left\lfloor\frac{r}{2}\right\rfloor+1),w,c},\|f_{0}\|_{2(\left\lfloor\frac{r}{2}\right\rfloor+1),c},r}T\|J\|_{W^{2,\infty}}\frac{\lambda}{N}

for all
\lambda>0
small enough, dependent on
\|\hat{v}\|_{2(\left\lfloor\frac{r}{2}\right\rfloor+1),w,c}
.

Proof.

We want to apply integration by parts to

(C.61)

\displaystyle\operatorname{err}^{(fd)}_{2}(t;f[J])

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{1}{N}\int_{0}^{\lambda}\,d\tau\,\int\,d\omega\,\delta_{\frac{2\lambda^{2}}{T}}^{\prime}(\omega)

\displaystyle\int_{{\mathcal{E}}(\tau,\mathbf{p}_{2})=\omega}\,d{\mathcal{H}}^{5}\,\frac{\partial_{\tau}{\mathcal{E}}(\tau,\mathbf{p}_{2})H(\mathbf{p}_{2})}{|\nabla_{\mathbf{p}_{2}}{\mathcal{E}}(\tau,\mathbf{p}_{2})|}\,.

For that, we need to establish a uniform bound on

(C.62)

\displaystyle\int_{{\mathcal{E}}(\tau,\mathbf{p}_{2})=\omega}\,d{\mathcal{H}}^{5}\,\frac{\partial_{\tau}{\mathcal{E}}(\tau,\mathbf{p}_{2})H(\mathbf{p}_{2})}{|\nabla_{\mathbf{p}_{2}}{\mathcal{E}}(\tau,\mathbf{p}_{2})|}\,.

The divergence theorem implies that

(C.63)

\displaystyle\Big|\int_{{\mathcal{E}}(\tau,\mathbf{p}_{2})=\omega}\,d{\mathcal{H}}^{5}\,\frac{\partial_{\tau}{\mathcal{E}}(\tau,\mathbf{p}_{2})H(\mathbf{p}_{2})}{|\nabla_{\mathbf{p}_{2}}{\mathcal{E}}(\tau,\mathbf{p}_{2})|}\Big|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\Big\|\nabla_{\mathbf{p}_{2}}\cdot\Big(\frac{\nabla_{\mathbf{p}_{2}}{\mathcal{E}}(\tau)\partial_{\tau}{\mathcal{E}}(\tau)H}{|\nabla_{\mathbf{p}_{2}}{\mathcal{E}}(\tau)|^{2}}\Big)\Big\|_{1}\,.

We compute

(C.64)

\displaystyle\partial_{\tau}\Omega_{\tau}\,=\,\frac{2\hat{v}}{\sqrt{1+\frac{2\tau\hat{v}}{E}}}\,.

Then
\partial_{\tau}\Omega_{\tau}
satisfies

(C.65)

\displaystyle\|\partial_{\tau}\Omega_{\tau}\|_{\infty}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C_{\|\hat{v}\|_{2(\left\lfloor\frac{r}{2}\right\rfloor+1),w,c}}\,,

(C.66)

\displaystyle\|\nabla_{p}\partial_{\tau}\Omega_{\tau}\|_{\infty}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C\Big(\|\hat{v}\|_{2(\left\lfloor\frac{r}{2}\right\rfloor+1),w,c}\,+\,\|\hat{v}\|_{\infty}\Big\|D\Big(1+\frac{2\tau\hat{v}}{E}\Big)^{-\frac{1}{2}}\Big\|_{\infty}\Big)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C_{\|\hat{v}\|_{2(\left\lfloor\frac{r}{2}\right\rfloor+1),w,c}}\,,

(C.67)

\displaystyle\|D_{p}^{2}\partial_{\tau}\Omega_{\tau}\|_{\infty}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C\Big(\|D^{2}\hat{v}\|_{\infty}\,+\,\|D\hat{v}\|_{\infty}\Big\|D\Big(1+\frac{2\tau\hat{v}}{E}\Big)^{-\frac{1}{2}}\Big\|_{\infty}

\displaystyle+\,\|\hat{v}\|_{\infty}\Big\|D^{2}\Big(1+\frac{2\tau\hat{v}}{E}\Big)^{-\frac{1}{2}}\Big\|_{\infty}\Big)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C_{\|\hat{v}\|_{2(\left\lfloor\frac{r}{2}\right\rfloor+1),w,c}}

for all
\tau\in[0,\lambda]
,
\lambda\in(0,1]
. Here we applied the Leibniz Formula, Lemma
C.3
. Then (
C.65
)-(
C.67
) imply

(C.68)

\displaystyle\|\partial_{\tau}{\mathcal{E}}(\tau,\mathbf{p}_{2})\|_{W^{2,\infty}}\,\leq\,C_{\|\hat{v}\|_{2(\left\lfloor\frac{r}{2}\right\rfloor+1),w,c}}

for all
\tau\in[0,\lambda]
,
\lambda\in(0,1)
. Following the steps of (
C.53
) in Lemma
C.5
, we find that

(C.69)

\displaystyle\Big\|\nabla_{\mathbf{p}_{2}}\cdot\Big(\frac{\nabla_{\mathbf{p}_{2}}{\mathcal{E}}(\tau)\partial_{\tau}{\mathcal{E}}(\tau)H}{|\nabla_{\mathbf{p}_{2}}{\mathcal{E}}(\tau)|^{2}}\Big)\Big\|_{1}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C\Big(\Big\|\frac{\partial_{\tau}{\mathcal{E}}(\tau)H}{|\nabla_{\mathbf{p}_{2}}{\mathcal{E}}(\tau)|^{2}}\Big\|_{1}

\displaystyle+\,\Big\|\frac{\nabla_{\mathbf{p}_{2}}\big(\partial_{\tau}{\mathcal{E}}(\tau)H\big)}{|\nabla_{\mathbf{p}_{2}}{\mathcal{E}}(\tau)|}\Big\|_{1}\Big)

due to Lemma
C.4
. Using the Mean-Value Theorem together with
\nabla_{\tau,\mathbf{p}_{2}}{\mathcal{E}}(\tau,0)=0
, see Lemma
C.4
, we find that, for some
\zeta_{\tau,\mathbf{p}_{2}}\in[0,\mathbf{p}_{2}]
,

(C.70)

\displaystyle\nabla_{\mathbf{p}_{2}}{\mathcal{E}}(\tau,\mathbf{p}_{2})

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle D_{\mathbf{p}_{2}}^{2}{\mathcal{E}}(\tau,\zeta_{\tau,\mathbf{p}_{2}})\mathbf{p}_{2}\,.

Using
C.4
again, we conclude that

(C.71)

\displaystyle|\nabla_{\mathbf{p}_{2}}{\mathcal{E}}(\tau,\mathbf{p}_{2})|\,\geq\,\frac{1}{2}\Big|\begin{pmatrix}p_{2}\\
p_{1}\end{pmatrix}\Big|\,=\,\frac{|\mathbf{p}_{2}|}{2}

for all
\lambda>0
small enough, dependent on
\|\hat{v}\|_{2(\left\lfloor\frac{r}{2}\right\rfloor+1),w,c}
. Thus, we obtain that

(C.72)

\displaystyle\Big|\frac{D^{\ell}\big[(\hat{v}(p_{1})+\hat{v}(p_{2}))^{2}f_{0}(p_{12})\big]}{|\nabla_{\mathbf{p}_{2}}{\mathcal{E}}(\tau,\mathbf{p}_{2})|^{m}}\Big|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C_{\|\hat{v}\|_{2(\left\lfloor\frac{r}{2}\right\rfloor+1),w,c},\|f_{0}\|_{2(\left\lfloor\frac{r}{2}\right\rfloor+1),c}}\Big(\frac{1}{|\mathbf{p}_{2}|^{m}}\mathds{1}_{B_{1}}(\mathbf{p}_{2})

\displaystyle+\,D^{\ell}\big[(\hat{v}(p_{1})+\hat{v}(p_{2}))^{2}f_{0}(p_{12})\big]\mathds{1}_{B_{1}^{c}}(\mathbf{p}_{2})\Big)\,

for all
\ell,m\in{\mathbb{N}}_{0}
,
\ell\leq 2
. Hence,

(C.73)

\displaystyle\Big\|\frac{D^{\ell}\big[(\hat{v}(p_{1})+\hat{v}(p_{2}))^{2}f_{0}(p_{12})\big]}{|\nabla_{\mathbf{p}_{2}}{\mathcal{E}}(\tau)|^{m}}\Big\|_{1}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C_{\|\hat{v}\|_{2(\left\lfloor\frac{r}{2}\right\rfloor+1),w,c},\|f_{0}\|_{2(\left\lfloor\frac{r}{2}\right\rfloor+1),c}}

for all
\ell,m\in{\mathbb{N}}_{0}
,
\ell\leq 2
,
m\leq 5
, and all
\lambda>0
small enough, dependent on
\|\hat{v}\|_{2(\left\lfloor\frac{r}{2}\right\rfloor+1),w,c}
. With analogous arguments, we may replace
f_{0}(p_{12})
in (
C.73
) by
f_{0}(p_{12})f_{0}(p_{1})
,
f_{0}(p_{12})f_{0}(p_{2})
, or
f_{0}(p_{1})f_{0}(p_{2})
and obtain an analogous inequality. Then, (
C.63
), (
C.68
), (
C.69
), (
C.73
), and the definition (
5.210
) of
H
imply

(C.74)

\displaystyle\Big|\int_{{\mathcal{E}}(\tau,\mathbf{p}_{2})=\omega}\,d{\mathcal{H}}^{5}\,\frac{\partial_{\tau}{\mathcal{E}}(\tau,\mathbf{p}_{2})H(\mathbf{p}_{2})}{|\nabla_{\mathbf{p}_{2}}{\mathcal{E}}(\tau,\mathbf{p}_{2})|}\Big|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C_{\|\hat{v}\|_{2(\left\lfloor\frac{r}{2}\right\rfloor+1),w,c},\|f_{0}\|_{2(\left\lfloor\frac{r}{2}\right\rfloor+1),c}}T\|J\|_{W^{1,\infty}}

for all
\omega\in{\mathbb{R}}
,
\tau\in[0,\lambda]
,
\lambda
small enough, dependent on
\|\hat{v}\|_{2(\left\lfloor\frac{r}{2}\right\rfloor+1),w,c}
.

Next, by integration by parts w.r.t.
d\omega
,
\delta_{\frac{2\lambda^{2}}{T}}^{\prime}(\omega)\to 0
as
|\omega|\to\infty
, (
C.74
), and employing Lemma
C.1
, (
C.61
) implies that

(C.75)

\displaystyle\operatorname{err}^{(fd)}_{2}(t;f[J])

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-\frac{1}{N}\int_{0}^{\lambda}\,d\tau\,\int\,d\omega\,\delta_{\frac{2\lambda^{2}}{T}}(\omega)\int_{{\mathcal{E}}(\tau,\mathbf{p}_{2})=\omega}\,\frac{d{\mathcal{H}}^{5}}{|\nabla_{\mathbf{p}_{2}}{\mathcal{E}}(\tau,\mathbf{p}_{2})|}\,

\displaystyle\nabla_{\mathbf{p}_{2}}\cdot\Big(\frac{\nabla_{\mathbf{p}_{2}}{\mathcal{E}}(\tau,\mathbf{p}_{2})\partial_{\tau}{\mathcal{E}}(\tau,\mathbf{p}_{2})H(\mathbf{p}_{2})}{|\nabla_{\mathbf{p}_{2}}{\mathcal{E}}(\tau,\mathbf{p}_{2})|^{2}}\Big)

Lemma
C.1
implies

(C.76)

\displaystyle\Big|\int_{{\mathcal{E}}(\tau,\mathbf{p}_{2})=\omega}\,\frac{d{\mathcal{H}}^{5}}{|\nabla_{\mathbf{p}_{2}}{\mathcal{E}}(\tau,\mathbf{p}_{2})|}\,\nabla_{\mathbf{p}_{2}}\cdot\Big(\frac{\nabla_{\mathbf{p}_{2}}{\mathcal{E}}(\tau,\mathbf{p}_{2})\partial_{\tau}{\mathcal{E}}(\tau,\mathbf{p}_{2})H(\mathbf{p}_{2})}{|\nabla_{\mathbf{p}_{2}}{\mathcal{E}}(\tau,\mathbf{p}_{2})|^{2}}\Big)\Big|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\Big\|\nabla_{\mathbf{p}_{2}}\cdot\Big[\frac{\nabla_{\mathbf{p}_{2}}{\mathcal{E}}(\tau,\mathbf{p}_{2})}{|\nabla_{\mathbf{p}_{2}}{\mathcal{E}}(\tau,\mathbf{p}_{2})|^{2}}\nabla_{\mathbf{p}_{2}}\cdot\Big(\frac{\nabla_{\mathbf{p}_{2}}{\mathcal{E}}(\tau,\mathbf{p}_{2})\partial_{\tau}{\mathcal{E}}(\tau,\mathbf{p}_{2})H(\mathbf{p}_{2})}{|\nabla_{\mathbf{p}_{2}}{\mathcal{E}}(\tau,\mathbf{p}_{2})|^{2}}\Big)\Big]\Big\|_{1}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C\Big(\|D^{2}_{\mathbf{p}_{2}}{\mathcal{E}}(\tau)\|_{\infty}^{2}\Big\|\frac{\partial_{\tau}{\mathcal{E}}(\tau)H}{|\nabla_{\mathbf{p}_{2}}{\mathcal{E}}(\tau,\mathbf{p}_{2})|^{4}}\Big\|_{1}\,+\,\|D^{3}_{\mathbf{p}_{2}}{\mathcal{E}}(\tau)\|_{\infty}\Big\|\frac{\partial_{\tau}{\mathcal{E}}(\tau)H}{|\nabla_{\mathbf{p}_{2}}{\mathcal{E}}(\tau,\mathbf{p}_{2})|^{3}}\Big\|_{1}

\displaystyle+\,\|D^{2}_{\mathbf{p}_{2}}{\mathcal{E}}(\tau)\|_{\infty}\Big\|\frac{D_{\mathbf{p}_{2}}\big(\partial_{\tau}{\mathcal{E}}(\tau)H\big)}{|\nabla_{\mathbf{p}_{2}}{\mathcal{E}}(\tau,\mathbf{p}_{2})|^{3}}\Big\|_{1}\,+\,\Big\|\frac{D_{\mathbf{p}_{2}}^{2}\big(\partial_{\tau}{\mathcal{E}}(\tau)H\big)}{|\nabla_{\mathbf{p}_{2}}{\mathcal{E}}(\tau,\mathbf{p}_{2})|^{2}}\Big\|_{1}\Big)\,.

Lemma
C.3
implies

(C.77)

\displaystyle\|D^{2}_{\mathbf{p}_{2}}{\mathcal{E}}(\tau)\|_{\infty}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle 1+C\|\hat{v}\|_{2(\left\lfloor\frac{r}{2}\right\rfloor+1),w,c}^{2}\tau\,,

(C.78)

\displaystyle\|D^{3}_{\mathbf{p}_{2}}{\mathcal{E}}(\tau)\|_{\infty}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C\|\hat{v}\|_{2(\left\lfloor\frac{r}{2}\right\rfloor+1),w,c}^{3}\tau\,.

Collecting (
C.68
), (
C.73
), (
C.76
), (
C.77
), and (
C.78
), we find that

\displaystyle\Big|\int_{{\mathcal{E}}(\tau,\mathbf{p}_{2})=\omega}\frac{d{\mathcal{H}}^{5}}{|\nabla_{\mathbf{p}_{2}}{\mathcal{E}}(\tau,\mathbf{p}_{2})|}\,\nabla_{\mathbf{p}_{2}}\cdot\Big(\frac{\nabla_{\mathbf{p}_{2}}{\mathcal{E}}(\tau,\mathbf{p}_{2})\partial_{\tau}{\mathcal{E}}(\tau,\mathbf{p}_{2})H(\mathbf{p}_{2})}{|\nabla_{\mathbf{p}_{2}}{\mathcal{E}}(\tau,\mathbf{p}_{2})|^{2}}\Big)\Big|

(C.79)

\displaystyle\,\leq\,C_{\|\hat{v}\|_{2(\left\lfloor\frac{r}{2}\right\rfloor+1),w,c},\|f_{0}\|_{2(\left\lfloor\frac{r}{2}\right\rfloor+1),c}}T\|J\|_{W^{2,\infty}}\,.

Employing (
C.73
), (
C.75
), and (
C.79
), we have proved that

(C.80)

\displaystyle|\operatorname{err}^{(fd)}_{2}(t;f[J])|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C_{\|\hat{v}\|_{2(\left\lfloor\frac{r}{2}\right\rfloor+1),w,c},\|f_{0}\|_{2(\left\lfloor\frac{r}{2}\right\rfloor+1),c}}\frac{T\|J\|_{W^{2,\infty}}}{N}\int_{0}^{\lambda}\,d\tau\,\int\,d\omega\,\delta_{\frac{2\lambda^{2}}{T}}(\omega)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C_{\|\hat{v}\|_{2(\left\lfloor\frac{r}{2}\right\rfloor+1),w,c},\|f_{0}\|_{2(\left\lfloor\frac{r}{2}\right\rfloor+1),c}}\frac{T\lambda\|J\|_{W^{2,\infty}}}{N}\,,

where in the last step, we applied the normalization (
5.207
) of
\delta_{\frac{2\lambda^{2}}{T}}
. This finishes the proof.
∎

Lemma C.7
.

Recall that, due to (
5.213
),

(C.81)

\displaystyle\operatorname{err}^{(ec)}_{2}(t;f[J])

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{1}{N}\int\,d\omega\,\delta_{\frac{2\lambda^{2}}{T}}(\omega)\Big(\int_{{\Delta_{cub}E}=\omega}\,d{\mathcal{H}}^{5}\,\frac{H}{|\nabla{\Delta_{cub}E}|}

\displaystyle+\,\int_{{\Delta_{cub}E}=0}\,d{\mathcal{H}}^{5}\,\frac{H}{|\nabla{\Delta_{cub}E}|}\Big)

with
H
as defined in (
5.210
). Then we have that

(C.82)

\displaystyle|\operatorname{err}^{(ec)}_{2}(t;f[J])|\,\leq\,C_{\|\hat{v}\|_{2(\left\lfloor\frac{r}{2}\right\rfloor+1),w,c},\|f_{0}\|_{2(\left\lfloor\frac{r}{2}\right\rfloor+1),c}}\sqrt{T}\|J\|_{W^{2,\infty}}\frac{\lambda}{N}

for all
\lambda>0
small enough, dependent on
\|\hat{v}\|_{2(\left\lfloor\frac{r}{2}\right\rfloor+1),w,c}
.

Proof.

We start by writing

(C.83)

\displaystyle\operatorname{err}^{(ec)}_{2}(t;f[J])

\displaystyle=:

\displaystyle\frac{1}{N}\int\,d\omega\,\delta_{\frac{2\lambda^{2}}{T}}(\omega){\mathcal{I}}(\omega)\,.

Analogously to (
C.74
), we have that

(C.84)

\displaystyle|{\mathcal{I}}(\omega)|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C_{\|\hat{v}\|_{2(\left\lfloor\frac{r}{2}\right\rfloor+1),w,c},\|f_{0}\|_{2(\left\lfloor\frac{r}{2}\right\rfloor+1),c}}T\|J\|_{W^{1,\infty}}\frac{1}{N}\,.

Notice that, due to Lemma
C.1
, we have that

(C.85)

\displaystyle{\mathcal{I}}(\omega)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{\omega}\,d\tau\,\int_{{\Delta_{cub}E}=\tau}\,\frac{d{\mathcal{H}}^{5}}{|\nabla{\Delta_{cub}E}|}\,\nabla\cdot\Big(\frac{\nabla{\Delta_{cub}E}H}{|\nabla{\Delta_{cub}E}|^{2}}\Big)\,,

where the integral respects the orientation of
[0,\omega]
resp.
[\omega,0]
. Using Lemma
C.1
, we thus obtain

(C.86)

\displaystyle|{\mathcal{I}}(\omega)|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle|\omega|\Big\|\nabla\cdot\Big[\frac{\nabla{\Delta_{cub}E}}{|\nabla{\Delta_{cub}E}|^{2}}\nabla\cdot\Big(\frac{\nabla{\Delta_{cub}E}H}{|\nabla{\Delta_{cub}E}|^{2}}\Big)\Big]\Big\|_{1}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C|\omega|\Big(\|D^{2}{\Delta_{cub}E}\|_{\infty}^{2}\Big\|\frac{H}{|\nabla{\Delta_{cub}E}|^{4}}\Big\|_{1}\,+\,\Big\|\frac{|D^{3}{\Delta_{cub}E}|H}{|\nabla{\Delta_{cub}E}|^{3}}\Big\|_{1}

\displaystyle+\,\|D^{2}{\Delta_{cub}E}\|_{\infty}\Big\|\frac{DH}{|\nabla{\Delta_{cub}E}|^{3}}\Big\|_{1}\,+\,\Big\|\frac{D^{2}H}{|\nabla{\Delta_{cub}E}|^{2}}\Big\|_{1}\Big)\,,

for all
\lambda>0
small enough, dependent on
\|\hat{v}\|_{2(\left\lfloor\frac{r}{2}\right\rfloor+1),w,c}
, analogously to (
C.76
). Notice that
D^{3}{\Delta_{cub}E}=0
and
\|D^{2}{\Delta_{cub}E}\|_{\infty}\leq C
. Analogously to (
C.80
), we thus obtain the upper bound

(C.87)

\displaystyle|{\mathcal{I}}(\omega)|\,\leq\,C_{\|\hat{v}\|_{2(\left\lfloor\frac{r}{2}\right\rfloor+1),w,c},\|f_{0}\|_{2(\left\lfloor\frac{r}{2}\right\rfloor+1),c}}|\omega|T\|J\|_{W^{2,\infty}}\,.

We split the integral in (
C.83
) into the regions
(-\omega_{0},\omega_{0})\cup(-\omega_{0},\omega_{0})^{c}
with
\omega_{0}
to be determined below. Then (
C.84
) and (
C.87
) yield

(C.88)

\displaystyle|\operatorname{err}^{(ec)}_{2}(t;f[J])|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{1}{N}\int_{(-\omega_{0},\omega_{0})}\,d\omega\,\delta_{\frac{2\lambda^{2}}{T}}(\omega)|{\mathcal{I}}(\omega)|\,+\,\int_{(-\omega_{0},\omega_{0})^{c}}\,d\omega\,\delta_{\frac{2\lambda^{2}}{T}}(\omega)|{\mathcal{I}}(\omega)|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{C_{\|\hat{v}\|_{2(\left\lfloor\frac{r}{2}\right\rfloor+1),w,c},\|f_{0}\|_{2(\left\lfloor\frac{r}{2}\right\rfloor+1),c}}T}{N}\Big(\|J\|_{W^{2,\infty}}\int_{(-\omega_{0},\omega_{0})}\,d\omega\,\delta_{\frac{2\lambda^{2}}{T}}(\omega)|\omega|

\displaystyle+\,\|J\|_{W^{1,\infty}}\int_{(-\omega_{0},\omega_{0})^{c}}\,d\omega\,\delta_{\frac{2\lambda^{2}}{T}}(\omega)\Big)\,.

Employing the normalization condition (
5.207
) and the decay condition (
5.208
) on
\delta_{\frac{2\lambda^{2}}{T}}
, we thus obtain

(C.89)

\displaystyle|\operatorname{err}^{(ec)}_{2}(t;f[J])|\,\leq\,\frac{C_{\|\hat{v}\|_{2(\left\lfloor\frac{r}{2}\right\rfloor+1),w,c},\|f_{0}\|_{2(\left\lfloor\frac{r}{2}\right\rfloor+1),c}}T\|J\|_{W^{2,\infty}}}{N}\Big(\omega_{0}\,+\,\frac{\lambda^{2}}{\omega_{0}T}\Big)\,.

By now choosing
\omega_{0}=\frac{\lambda}{\sqrt{T}}
, we have hence proved that

(C.90)

\displaystyle|\operatorname{err}^{(ec)}_{2}(t;f[J])|\,\leq\,\frac{C_{\|\hat{v}\|_{2(\left\lfloor\frac{r}{2}\right\rfloor+1),w,c},\|f_{0}\|_{2(\left\lfloor\frac{r}{2}\right\rfloor+1),c}}\sqrt{T}\|J\|_{W^{2,\infty}}\lambda}{N}\,.

∎
